# Release Note

## Important Changes

### Core Cryptographic and Protocol Library

- Make new NID allocation use a ThreadSanitizer-compatible path when available to avoid false reports of concurrent access from analysis tools. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/29c80c6004de8bfd1792e421bbe03ab5f075f21d)
- Add lock protection for OBJ signature algorithm identifier related calls to avoid concurrent read/write of object mapping state. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c568900c9ac02e92c54bd3168773d54d7350a580)
- Add SHA-512 assembly implementation support for RISC-V architecture, enabling corresponding optimized implementations on that platform. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/611685adc04a7c7e9612d51e743044fdcd9d1846)
- Support BTI and pointer authentication in AArch64 assembly implementations, adapting to systems with these hardware security features enabled. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/19e277dd19f2897f6a7b7eb236abe46655e575bf)
- Add SHA-256 assembly implementation support for RISC-V architecture, enabling corresponding optimized implementations on that platform. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/657d1927c68bdc3fb0250d16df2a8439e8e043f1)
- Add object identifiers for SM4-GCM and SM4-CCM algorithms, supporting identification of these combined modes via OID. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a596d38a8cddca4af3416b2664e120028d96e6a9)
- Support carrying new extensions in certificate requests, allowing requests to transmit previously uncovered extension fields. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cbb862fbaaa1ec5a3e33836bc92a6dbea97ceba0)
- Add missing compile macro for enabling x86 AES-NI, so x86 builds correctly enable AES-NI optimized implementations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d92c696d82b55552da62d6fb71942645315e307a)
- Check return values of BN_new(), sk_RSA_PRIME_INFO_new_reserve(), EVP_PKEY_CTX_new_from_pkey(), and EVP_CIPHER_CTX_new() to avoid operating on null pointers after allocation failure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9dddcd90a1350fa63486cbf3226c3eee79f9aff5)
- Add Provider parameter to ossl_method_construct() to allow algorithm method construction to be limited to a specified Provider. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cfce50f791511c8fee7dec90c57f02d9410d039f)
- Add internal function evp_generic_fetch_from_prov() to fetch generic EVP methods from a specified Provider. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2fd3392c8f4e2f3481fa4d7e6a683dc19c6c1cd2)
- Add internal function evp_keymgmt_fetch_from_prov() to fetch key management methods from a specified Provider. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/33561e0d5b89a06d1c03b952196d008b5014914a)
- Add internal function to fetch EVP methods by method type from a Provider, for reuse by specific algorithm operations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ff7781462dd04ab99c159136b47672252bad7fa8)
- Add checks conforming to SM2 digital signature algorithm requirements, rejecting non-conforming signature inputs. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e81c81c9af8a5d22658110d2dc753582eb87a58e)
- Add object identifier for RPKI id-ct-ASPA, enabling OpenSSL to recognize this certificate content type. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b0c1214e1e82bc4c98eadd11d368b4ba9ffa202c)
- Add missing NULL return value checks to avoid dereferencing or using null objects after resource creation failure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ed5b26ce0b34ec00bdd53d15854a22bccbb4d415)
- Enable BIO_gets() for BIO_s_connect(), allowing connection BIO to read text lines via this interface. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7a9b09feaa07f79522f7affccbca4236da2443e5)
- Enable Brainpool curves in TLS 1.3, allowing negotiation of corresponding elliptic curve groups. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0a10825a009c830125fef94c81d34e41300a24a5)
- Add BSD PPC, PPC64, and PPC64LE configuration support, enabling OpenSSL builds on these BSD architectures. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f5485b97b6c9977c0d39c7669b9f97a879312447)
- Add RNG assembly implementation for AArch64, enabling optimized code for random number generator on that architecture. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/efa1f22483ee43d84e1aee01b08c0bda04060c1c)
- Add static length check in BN_hex2bn() to reject hexadecimal input exceeding the target bignum representation range. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7c78bd4be810ddceb8f13585a921946cc98f5fbd)
- Explain in ec.h why EVP_EC_gen() uses strstr() to parse parameter strings, and add string.h declaration. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1d8f18dce1c8ba99693dfaeb1696d625d9f4b7e0)
- Add missing trace category entry in trace.c so that category can be correctly identified and enabled. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e06c0a2870c55aa4e66108ca071e7da7fd00b922)
- Add internal function ossl_X509_ALGOR_from_nid() to create algorithm identifiers from NID and simplify ASN.1 algorithm code. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9944df112ffbe4b6855b6a9bf88720803277cc23)
- Add several error reason codes to the error queue for more accurate failure diagnosis. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/826da1451b2525b70f93fcc57ed5dbab61a19591)
- Add error messages for built-in parameter type conversion errors, explaining the parameter and reason for conversion failure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ac1082f00f991aca1c6e8282717fece16e9bb41f)
- Make parameter construction operations push error information before returning on failure, aiding callers in diagnosing construction failures. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3831351da50b7ce07edba88056394a7a33c5e5d5)
- Make parameter construction set operations push error information before returning on failure, uniformly reporting set insertion errors. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3ee3a2bd1e5763b0df5c0a2cba3b06edc26f5276)
- Make parameter copy operations push error information before returning on failure, providing diagnosable reasons for copy failure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a10a576090022e583a06271ceced8e38dd509657)
- Add comments to invalid macros, clarifying that their expansion will not take effect, to avoid maintainers mistakenly thinking it performs protection. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/79704a88eb5aa70fa506e3e59a29fcda21f428af)
- Add summary and password context copy functions, allowing copying of EVP_MD_CTX and EVP_CIPHER_CTX states. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4e62f1a3af36512a1f5e1273d2dc54e3ce7f5fca)
- Improve SSL support for ThreadSanitizer, fixing TSAN synchronization annotations in concurrent operations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/acce055778ecbf72e06a254b3a9bf2a2907e5170)
- Document the storage order of internal data blocks for large numbers in the bignum_st structure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/99d3349d6f4b62c89d0cbcd6200cbc9bda388c52)
- Add encoding and decoding functions between signed BIGNUM and byte arrays. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f5e8050fdcf2083825ef450d51bfacac21d2730e)
- Check whether X509_STORE_lock() succeeds in X509 store operations, avoiding access to shared storage when lock acquisition fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/814999cb44135fd197945693a7c00cf0af784206)
- Support reading and writing signed BIGNUM parameters in the OSSL_PARAM API. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f1719858a05a9568ccbd052f160746cf4c027a9e)
- Support constructing signed BIGNUM parameters in the OSSL_PARAM_BLD API. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/17898ec6011cc583c5af69ca8f25f5d165ff3e6a)
- Add -no_ems option to s_client and s_server, allowing disabling of the TLS Extended Master Secret extension. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a829d53a14eeae2b0bc783b7952b4212cf31d918)
- Add SSL_get0_iana_groups() and SSL_client_hello_get_extension_order(), providing interfaces to query the client-supported group list and ClientHello extension order. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/13a53fbf13bc6fa09c95ad4bdc6ec70fa15aa16d)
- Add missing CRYPTO_THREAD_cleanup_local() cleanup for default context thread-local storage, avoiding resource leaks on thread exit. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8e012cdc896ec6a98b45119b127b230cbbb6e93b)
- Add ChaCha20-Poly1305 support for FreeBSD KTLS. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/77f3936928068bee9d7e0c6939709ac179cb1059)
- Check the return value of BN_BLINDING_lock() in the RSA path, safely handling blinding operations when lock acquisition fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/aefbcde29166caf851cf388361d70fd0dcf17d87)
- Add do_updatedb test, covering certificate database update behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/065121ff198a84106023013420dedd57ac4ff53a)
- Restore DH public key length checks, rejecting overly short public key inputs. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d5530efada83825ef239a8458db541adc4b422ec)
- openssl enc adds handling for wrap-type cipher modes: allows setting the wrap flag and restricts input as non-streamable mode, avoiding applying ordinary stream cipher IV and data processing logic. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7850cc8307b9105f37dde864d5c8c881c522b28a)
- Random number implementation checks the return value of rand_get_global, avoiding continued access when the global random context is unavailable. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/09dca557332a2187598932388ac7bd7bbf16172b)
- When CMP request does not provide a certificate or explicit subject name, use the subject from CSR as the message sender name, and document this fallback rule. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cd7ec0bca00ceb6e8d4af46a57c6c096a7ed8947)
- X.509 extension helper code checks the allocation result of OPENSSL_strndup and returns via error path on failure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/366a16263959c0b6599f0b9ec18124d75560c6ef)
- Include DHE_PSK and ECDHE_PSK cipher suites in forward secrecy suite determination for security level 3 and above, avoiding incorrect rejection of suites meeting PFS conditions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b139a95665eb023b38695d62d9dfc28f3fb89972)
- CMS signing and verification now support public key types implemented by Providers, no longer relying on signature algorithm mapping paths applicable only to built-in algorithms. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d15d561844d8989e50896724d89681ae7ba81a74)
- Add tests for OBJ_find_sigid_by_algs, verifying lookup results of signature algorithm and digest algorithm combinations to object identifiers. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/065442165a3d339a7de469b4cd18a3f902c73443)
- Add TCP Fast Open support for socket BIO, s_client, and s_server, and include platform build and test coverage for enabling this feature. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a3e53d56831adb60d6875297b3339a4251f735d2)
- Check the return value of BIO_read in PEM reading path, avoiding parsing invalid data when read fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2823e2e1d39479a7835d176862ec15e47a1bdecd)
- PKCS12_parse adds parsing support for PKCS#12 files without MAC password protection, compatible with such files where password is empty/unset. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cfd24cde81aa5f63dba41ddcde0fa3c5d64e1db0)
- Add using_ktls state in TLS record reading path, distinguishing records processed by OpenSSL and KTLS. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/031132c297e54cbc20404a0bf8de6ed863196399)
- Extend KTLS receive offload to support TLS 1.3, enabling kernel to handle TLS 1.3 receive records. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7c78932b9a4330fb7c8db72b3fb37cbff1401f8b)
- Add get0 APIs for reading verification certificate store and certificate chain store on SSL and SSL_CTX, and add corresponding manual pages. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/948cf521798a801cfde47a137343e6f958d71f04)
- Add feature detection for new-generation s390 processors, enabling related hardware optimizations on new models. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/42f111ad41141e2ecd67f0a6954625a5ad01890b)
- X509V3_parse_list checks the return value of X509V3_add_value, reporting errors when extension value allocation or appending fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bcd5645b34c319b8e4d72d6850ead80e85f18921)
- Add new error code for "explicit curve parameters not supported", so EC Provider returns clear diagnostics when rejecting such parameters. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/53137462f42f8673fbd5b0831f8ea051ddea509f)
- Add MD5 assembly implementation for AArch64, improving MD5 computation performance on ARM64 platforms. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/04904a0fff639c058d38b355d75485ca5dde0a89)
- Add new object identifiers required for CMP updates, enabling extended CMPv2 to recognize new protocol objects. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/34959f7a2256eadd23d56f0efe855be7fde282b2)
- Extend query cache flushing and method removal capabilities to non-EVP operations, including encoding, decoding, and key exchange Provider interfaces. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/32e3c071373280b69be02ba91fc3204495e2e1bf)
- Add assembly architecture detection for Linux riscv64 configuration targets, and integrate corresponding RISC-V optimized assembly implementations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cb2764f2a8165421dc5ab52159af99cbf766fa2c)
- Always allow construction methods during algorithm fetching, to discover algorithms provided by Providers loaded after the query cache. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4b1b629725970384d6cf4dafe9e83e54859574cd)
- Add fallback handling when locale initialization fails, avoiding crashes in string comparison due to unsupported locale environment. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/92d050167713f9a094c149c38435b07512c68936)
- Check the return value of EVP_PKEY_copy_parameters in ssl_set_cert_and_key, aborting certificate and private key setting when copying public key parameters fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6646e015a50e5455117c22a27032011689db710f)
- PBES2 adds support for SHA-3-based PRF, allowing use of HMAC-SHA3 and corresponding SHA3 digests for password key derivation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c73ba81899c291d60851321e6de8913d4800c456)
- Add RISC-V 64-bit general assembly AES implementation, providing hardware instruction acceleration path for supported RISC-V platforms. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b3504b600c028a00f36cdbfedc928a48df9818ff)
- Add RISC-V CPU capability detection code and OPENSSL_riscvcap flag, identifying available instruction sets at runtime. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/360f6dcc5aa1a86ec3ff9a94612b88e3d960ee2e)
- Add GHASH implementation based on carry-less multiply for riscv64 with Zbb/Zbc extensions, accelerating GCM authentication operations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/999376dcf33986c468361ede16fa9de409dc4e2e)
- Extend optional encryption protection suites for SRTP configuration, adding profiles defined by RFC 8723 and RFC 8269, and update interface documentation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a425c0fec6eb74c942ca5bca8e27ff0c9f126d48)
- Implement UnsafeLegacyServerConnect option as documented, making it truly control legacy server connection compatibility behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/65b2bb9ca0cff5e65938dc0d9dcd71c251bd67db)
- Add OSSL_QUIC method interface declarations in public headers and manual pages, providing API skeleton for QUIC implementation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/770ea54b58769bae07e22a92e0c12ece9bdbc8e2)
- Add empty implementations for QUIC method functions, enabling optional QUIC code to link and provide initial interface framework. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/99e1cc7bcae2e3707913881d7108c92b7a9bf7a1)
- Add test cases for SSL_new, covering creation and initialization of new QUIC protocol objects. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/08e4901298df12931b45c7115254a0e159727683)
- Allow enabling SSL_CERT_FLAG_TLS_STRICT via SSL configuration commands, enabling strict certificate chain validation in configuration files. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/336d92eb206946293a50db667fdc44ab7d69f8ad)
- Add AES assembly implementation based on RISC-V Zkn instruction extension, providing hardware acceleration for CPUs supporting this extension. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/608cadfbdbdba076a07e172f834a0afb6aafa59b)
- Integrate RISC-V Zkn AES generator into build system, enabling configuration targets to generate and compile corresponding assembly files. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9912c38ed69c97ca737c66c68ae454c5cd265133)
- Revert PPC64 fixed-length Montgomery multiplication assembly implementation, returning to verified generic implementation to avoid incorrect results. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/712d9cc90e355b2c98a959d4e9398610d2269c9e)
- Clear sensitive temporary memory after private key encoding, preventing private key material from remaining in released buffers. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/16249341bb64329c2542c3d1e23b97ed3c44fad3)
- Add an extra reduction step in RSAZ modular exponentiation implementation, ensuring modular exponentiation results fall within valid range. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6d702cebfce3ffd9d8c0cb2af80a987d3288e7a3)
- ASN.1 time string parsing checks OPENSSL_malloc result when allocating temporary buffer, correctly returning error on allocation failure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8547cd6790881cbba0f20aa4ce048243065a24bf)
- Add generic priority queue implementation, supporting insertion, extraction, and maintenance of queued elements by priority. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c8003ad5e939a6c5fc0049c9b7ed1e99fbe9511b)
- X509 store lookup path checks the return value of OPENSSL_strdup, terminating processing when string copy fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e163969d3580e5b797fcebde0d3000302912ef18)
- Add OpenSSL 3.1 deprecation macros and mark OPENSSL_LH_stats as deprecated, prompting callers to migrate statistics interfaces. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5317b6ee1fc3db20de5976fbb46cc49a45c0768a)
- Add SM3 assembly implementation for RISC-V Zksh extension, using scalar cryptography instructions to accelerate SM3 compression function. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7ae2bc9df6e0916a8f16183f07dfa1815dd4b66d)
- Add inline assembly rotation instruction support for SM3 implementation, enabling RISC-V optimized code to generate round functions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/eea820f3e239a4c11d618741fd5d00a6bc877347)
- HTTP client checks OPENSSL_strdup allocation result, avoiding null pointer dereference when copying request status text fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/816d6e578ccc4d8ae41de77e3069762d03079d18)
- Add object identifiers required for CAdES-Processing, supporting recognition of related CMS/CAdES attributes and algorithms. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5f7d4e9111dcd2a91429ecab807c4f282164ea46)
- Export CMS_EnvelopedData type and add CMS_EnvelopedData_decrypt API, allowing callers to directly access and decrypt EnvelopedData content. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e2f6960fc5fe1d6eb2178adf51db1ed206ff9e90)
- When Store Provider does not directly provide keymgmt, fall back to keymgmt from the same Provider to fetch keys, supporting key references returned by Store. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4cfcc7e1213d39c78852a614894ebcd2e2be095c)
- Adjust default MAC salt length and add corresponding configuration option, allowing users to control MAC salt length for algorithms like SIV. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e393064ee78a7ea07e2d63493579eab95384afe4)
- CMP adds version 3 support while keeping version 2 as default, avoiding unexpected changes to existing interoperability behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c4ad4e5bf67dae6f7729de5438c9a96a2abd0f92)
- Implement encoding, parsing, and configuration of the optional hashAlg field in CMPv3 certConf messages. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/74107c4428edbe8d6797ac6a700e0ea2c9e14952)
- In v3_sxnet, check the conversion result of i2s_ASN1_INTEGER, and stop generating the extension when integer-to-string conversion fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9ef1f848a646565d4dd86e56542cf921d4921ad9)
- Integrate the sparse array implementation into libssl, allowing the SSL library to use the public internal sparse array structure to store index data. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5067c18910d817e1a25976362ae75aa34f4d26a3)
- Implement an event queue for SSL internal use, supporting scheduling, querying, and canceling pending events by time. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e6be47e427fb6650f274c418947e7665fbe08889)
- Add the OPENSSL_NO_UNIX_SOCK configuration switch, allowing disabling related implementations on platforms that do not support Unix domain sockets. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/081f3484593cdd3be2b7fdd8818c3f928ce729bc)
- Check the result of BN_dup before copying TLS SRP parameters, avoiding null pointer access on allocation failure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/12e488367d34657a5c0e1bc322e66c48463d2a0c)
- Check the return value of OBJ_new_nid, and stop establishing object mappings when NID allocation fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a0ff8e413e94ba46720a4bf3a5032c50531c526c)
- Add RISC-V Zbb/Zbkb inline rotation assembly for ChaCha20, using bit manipulation instructions to improve round function efficiency. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ca6286c382a7eb527fac9aba2a018354acb27b16)
- Add CMS_SignedData_verify, allowing callers to perform SignedData verification with extended parameters while retaining the standard CMS_verify interface. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d7d3dae694fa4611c1cd953dccf81b3d2b4121c6)
- DH parameter generation uses the minimum key size that meets security level requirements, avoiding unnecessarily large default parameters. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ddb13b283be84d771deba1e964610b1670641f03)
- Add X509_PUBKEY_set0_public_key, splitting the public key object setting logic into a standalone API, making it easier for callers to directly transfer public key ownership. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9df71587f1897c3b282b3fe1b47c01656b58531e)
- Add and use the ossl_asn1_string_set_bits_left helper function to uniformly set the number of unused bits in ASN.1 BIT STRING. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7c310e872e72977432b3520c5d27641e13815548)
- Add OSSL_CMP_CTX_get0_validatedSrvCert, and fix OSSL_CMP_validate_msg to return the validated server certificate. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b6fbef1159c9aeb1590c116a9426e169d2203506)
- Extend the memory BIO to support datagram mode, allowing BIO_s_mem to read and write messages by datagram boundaries. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5a4ba72f00f9b336a4d65abff822699ceb9617c6)
- Add manual lock protection for InterlockedOr64 for legacy 32-bit VC++, compatible with compilers that do not provide this intrinsic and Windows XP. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2d46a44ff24173d2cf5ea2196360cb79470d49c7)
- Add RISC-V Zbb/Zbkb inline rotation assembly for DES, using bit manipulation instructions to improve round function efficiency. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6136408e6abf10672e399bf95be064868f2f7ca6)
- Re-enable PPC multi-precision Montgomery multiplication optimization, restoring the fixed-length six-limb unrolled path after fixing the implementation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/eae70100fadbc94f18ba7a729bf065cb524a9fc9)
- Add code signing certificate verification purpose, allowing X509 validation to check certificate extensions for code signing purposes. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/178696d6020878361a088086243d56203e0beaa9)
- Add header files from the recordmethod draft design, defining the initial structure of the TLS record layer method interface. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/79a1f3e4bb62c10d9604718f6814bb8bdde4ffd6)
- Add a TLS record method skeleton, defining creation, read/write, and destruction entry points for a replaceable record layer implementation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/34a4068cc402c38e2134a6b46d9633ad3112bfa5)
- Migrate the read responsibilities of ssl3_read_n to the new record layer, splitting record reading and buffering from the old SSL code. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e2d5742b1460c45bf39094ea08e4e85a8f507ea8)
- Move the record encryption/decryption implementations for TLS 1.0/1.1/1.2 into the new record layer, isolating protocol record processing from the handshake state machine. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/aedbb71b6334a6cb616cf31cbb5de02109a2c5ed)
- Make the SSLv3 read flow use the new record layer interface, completing the migration of the old SSLv3 read path. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/10560aed15dd71601b89c8f0308f30b70744c914)
- Make the TLS 1.3 record read flow use the new record layer, unifying the read-side interfaces for old and new protocols. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2b891e30ce1839a27f6a47f4c668d5810a15f847)
- Implement the KTLS receive path in the new read-side record layer, so that record decryption and kernel receive offload are uniformly managed by the record layer. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cc110a0aaebd627a9e61e2c8d68b02e3e0a4e76b)
- Support transferring record data from one epoch to the next, meeting the key phase switching needs of DTLS/QUIC. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/359affdead3af497f1673204c5c34061d28dfa7b)
- Allow the record layer to call SSL security callbacks, so that application security policies can continue to check record layer algorithms and parameters. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ed0e298fb8a3864b232e1d3801e849935a7a7f7e)
- Fix the default password algorithm name in the openssl pkcs12 help, making the help text consistent with the actual default. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d0b17ea025477ce13ebe5d802ada232a57e1a2f2)
- Release reclaimable session cache space before adding a new session, avoiding refusal to save new sessions when the cache is full. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a16f9d3366a4b4e8c8014bbf39b86baaf1a04047)
- Update SHAKE documentation, explaining the default output length and how to request variable-length output. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/52f616990537b22b0ec81475207caef25fdc0886)
- Treat EXFLAG_NO_FINGERPRINT as a status flag for certificates without fingerprints, no longer misreporting it as an extension cache error. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e128eaa094fc0e95c93081c914c85bd6962a9a42)
- Adjust the layout of the default X509_VERIFY_PARAM table for easier viewing of default verification parameter configurations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c0f6792b81784be05c5e51156767a873bca1b374)
- Migrate TLS 1.3 record reading code to the new read record layer interface. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/664e096cf94b1a2f72b3c562dd93db7e13b235f4)
- Fix compilation issues when building with the compression option disabled, ensuring no-comp configuration builds correctly. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f69ec4b484c08e67e863707eab4af4a4e6f4fc95)
- Remove the record layer's call to query the handshake state machine's error state, reducing coupling between the two layers. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b1b889d1b3fc92a56ead5536bee06f3415b78482)
- Move record sequence numbers into OSSL_RECORD_LAYER, so that read and write sequence numbers are maintained by the record layer. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/61170642b1ad084ae4f52e43d39c5c1e471b323a)
- Migrate early data counting from the SSL structure to the record layer, centrally managing 0-RTT data volume. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b733ce73a423b99c0354b42e268216e0656e556b)
- Add null parameter checks for X509 request and general certificate operations, and unify error handling and code style. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8e39049d38ebe8b8398d6c4aa8a6f7cef9712132)
- Expand OSSL_TIME time arithmetic helper functions and add documentation for them, supporting time comparison, addition/subtraction, and conversion. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/364c3b7b1ac3172dbe2108be23ae215b86ef8e08)
- Improve SSL_CERT_DIR URI loading, and add the SSL_CERT_URI environment variable to specify the certificate URI source. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/021859bf810a3614758c2f4871b9cd7202fac9b2)
- Add support for password-based encryption using HMAC-SM3, allowing PBES2/PBE to use SM3 as the PRF. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/48963ff6d0d07648e09e63d2dca9fb6069241f42)
- Add trace API tests, verifying trace channel enablement status, output, and configuration behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fcff5bd43c85418cc4aa8052e3dc3dba344d763e)
- Add direct error reporting for x509_vpm.c and tidy code formatting, ensuring that default parameter parsing failures are diagnosable. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/38ebfc3f5f83cbbd01011636d159ad3ed23e9765)
- Add missing error reporting for stack.c and tidy code style, making it easier to locate stack operation failures. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/30eba7f35983a917f1007bce45040c0af3442e42)
- Add a cap for late-arriving datagrams in the QUIC receive record layer, limiting the memory usage of pending UDP data backlog. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0ff98137445ec63249eed3c1e40cf01dc5190c65)
- Move TLS write-side record state into a separate record layer structure, preparing for decoupling write record code from SSL connection state. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a566864b607317fc95cbe190bbf0b8b928fcfa77)
- Add missing data type declarations for FreeBSD, fixing compilation failures of record layer code on that platform. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/23bf52a4b40deb033de0a257b724012afe32b169)
- Fix message length and structure handling in FreeBSD BIO_sendmmsg/BIO_recvmmsg calls, supporting batch datagram send/receive on that platform. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/69ed6760f938975d9cdcc12ec756d58c83ac6b90)
- Adjust code in BIO datagram tests to be compatible with strict warnings about function prototypes in older Clang. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8a163641c1d94c877a46ade8ff2ecefdf5cbbeeb)
- Add test skip conditions in evp_test based on FIPS Provider version, supporting different test results for different versions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fcae2ae4f675def607d338b7945b9af1dd9bb746)
- Update input format documentation, noting that standard input is also processed as binary data. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9251c3c4c7695b6268fcd122e18643d61f02b5dd)
- Uniformly read command input files as binary, avoiding character format conversion corrupting input bytes. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1e76110b7214a4fb39dc1397cbc4771538d06f39)
- Check the result of SSL_CIPHER stack access, avoiding null pointer dereference when entries are missing. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a8572674f12ceb39f7e66ccbaa8918b922c76739)
- Remove unnecessary conditional macro definitions, simplifying branches in tests or implementations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/16f0e91cf82e13c327f0b0402459dfbf78ef787c)
- Create write record layer objects and methods, and connect the TLS record sending path to this object. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7f2d6188c7b16ef7a4deeeedb56f42014156b9f8)
- Move TLS alert handling out of the record layer, letting the upper-layer state machine dispatch alerts parsed by the record layer. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ef917549f5867d269d359155ff67b8ccb5e66a76)
- The write record layer uses its own message callback, no longer relying on the SSL object's msg_callback field. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2c50d7fb06e34c5ab562bf890c49cc00cbd52a56)
- Make the write record layer return accurate success, retry, or failure status as agreed, fixing caller status determination. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/757ef3bab02e976500ffd2b1ae2229ed62f85a61)
- Add a post-processing step after record layer encryption, allowing the record layer to perform finalization required for MAC, sequence numbers, or KTLS after encryption completes. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2a354d54632cccf7d76130712d068a3ef188a356)
- Add a BIO preparation step before TLS record writing, ensuring the output BIO is ready before submitting encrypted records. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ace3819506d8d6bd298dd8448fefcbd62b63580c)
- Add name mappings for ECX curve group parameters, so that related Provider parameters can recognize Curve25519/Curve448 groups. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c048779520d47962316ddb436d08a050d5659666)
- Add preemptive thread support, allowing OpenSSL internal work to be scheduled by a thread pool on supported platforms. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4574a7fd8dda070b129d76defca07703cab53842)
- Add support for the Brotli certificate compression algorithm, implementing handshake certificate compression/decompression as defined in RFC 7924. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/12e96a23604a7aa1cd8f83486b02f1bcab6d468f)
- Add support for the Zstandard certificate compression algorithm, providing another compression option in the RFC 8478 series. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/caf9317d7d75213990014e07048384be15688889)
- Implement the RFC 8879 certificate compression extension, negotiating, compressing, and decompressing certificate messages in the TLS handshake. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b67cb09f8ddf258cf326f3e7b20be095fb53457c)
- Added the for_comp flag when obtaining certificates to be compressed, to select a certificate loading path suitable for certificate compression. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/72620ac79133ca7a4553b70573fd100257e8269d)
- Check the return value of sk_SRP_gN_new_null and immediately report a memory error when stack allocation fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f44d32fdfbd2a249dae74dc24478f31fca69d288)
- Out-of-bounds access in OPENSSL_sk_pop/shift/value returns a null result without adding an error, clarifying the lenient behavior of these stack read interfaces. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a8086e6bfc37355626393751a94bc5c92df7e9d3)
- Initialize the public/private DRBG state in the global default context to avoid referencing uninitialized objects when the random number generator is first used. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7c8187d43d043c6a66559ed341ff1e01b8711093)
- Added TLS write pipeline tests to verify the functionality of batch encryption and sending of multiple records. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b718f6fcc4bbf48cfc6ab3fa64e6cb95453299b3)
- Added d2i_PUBKEY_ex_fp and d2i_PUBKEY_ex_bio to support decoding public keys from FILE/BIO with specified libctx and property queries. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/820723dde0c9ec9a4fc68406a0e5aee1dc83f836)
- Added a one-shot compression interface for zlib, allowing compression methods to directly process a single input segment without maintaining stream state. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3840271e984010132380892817c1e1173f4a1576)
- Added a GHASH fallback implementation in the x86_64 GCM function selection logic, continuing to use the 4-bit generic algorithm when the CPU does not support dedicated acceleration. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/be0161ff100bf10c9549fc09ce4513681011da1c)
- KTLS send/receive supports CCM mode, allowing CCM TLS ciphers that meet kernel interface conditions to enable kernel offloading. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/34c2f90d8ed325a892618ce0e42ebe916966d4d8)
- Compile thread pool-specific functions only when the thread pool is enabled, avoiding unused functions or dependency issues when the thread pool is disabled. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f5a3669c8bc9cd1ea00f2bb7d058a752e6d2f152)
- Added APIs to get libctx and property query from CMP_CTX, enabling CMP operations to query their algorithm context and property configuration. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2da163cb73eabac7af093747ecee26ed76aa364a)
- Added Windows on Arm build target, supporting clang-cl as assembler or compiler, and adjusted ARM capability detection and assembly generation scripts. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b863e1e4c69068e4166bdfbbf9f04bb07991dd40)
- Linux KTLS adds zerocopy sendfile support, allowing s_server with KTLS enabled to send file contents via zero-copy. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cd715b7e7fdd2aeb0fd80220d2df5187b291f87a)
- EVP_PKEY_Q_keygen adds SM2 support, allowing SM2 key pair generation with the unified convenience API. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3f32d29ad464591ed968a1e430111e1525280f4c)
- Added OSSL_CMP_CTX_reset_geninfo_ITAVs to explicitly clear the CMP general message ITAV list. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a2ede0396addd13f7fe9a629b450a14892152a83)
- Added SM4-XTS object identifier, allowing SM4-XTS to be identified in ASN.1 encoding. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/de8f6a3e293a43f364cddefdf734b13486ec4cc9)
- Added OSSL_PROVIDER_get0_default_search_path, exposing a read-only API to query the default Provider search directory, with documentation and tests. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d3db25f568087bc9dc89b6720f0b4213cd5585c3)
- Added implicit rejection in PKCS#1 v1.5 RSA decryption: when padding is invalid, generate synthetic plaintext and delay failure exposure to mitigate padding oracle attacks. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7fc67e0a33102aa47bbaa56533eeecb98c0450f7)
- QUIC ACK Manager supports marking packets as pseudo-lost, allowing scenarios like Retry to reschedule sending without misinterpreting actual packet loss. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e5d575686efb280af08c3fd307a649ed2a942ce3)
- QUIC RX packets use reference counting and remove the extra wrapper, unifying packet lifecycle and ownership. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6d5d5fc9a9f6b701fc5e17f05d3df464fe0bc56e)
- Implemented QUIC Retry Integrity Tag calculation, generating a verification tag based on the initial DCID and Retry packet bytes. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/70d45893d0decc1ac2431a20db6750bc70cbaea5)
- Added QUIC transport parameter IDs and other protocol constant definitions for use in handshake extensions and parameter encoding/decoding. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/56a1a0ad2438d8cacdedb9413cc5dd8823e7b805)
- QUIC TXP adds a query interface to inform the caller whether the sender currently has pending frames that can be immediately assembled into a packet. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/04e5226f6549683a8362ae1af2445987d699540a)
- Added Connection ID encoding/decoding support for QUIC transport parameters, and allows omitting optional ID parameters as per the specification. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a64d82485d52c6ae1075217e611a92522fbe6560)
- Added a PTO query interface for QUIC ACK Manager, returning the next time a probe timeout should be triggered. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4ed9e0a1e36eaa8f07a4a5371f9d13912a3f9da8)
- Added BIO poll descriptor API, allowing non-blocking BIOs to expose waitable file descriptors and event directions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/68801bcb766806a04e95e8ef714a0b836b1d7069)
- Added QUIC reactor, scheduling connection read/write, timers, and protocol events based on BIO poll descriptors. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/69523214ee5a718a0f24803a93bedf0795578173)
- QUIC can be built even when disabled, ensuring related headers, tests, and source files do not force dependencies on optional modules. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6292519cd8102983e9924b6b0d3f298ac5f93e80)
- Updated TODO items in QUIC design, removing completed items and marking not-yet-implemented features. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c8b3fdc2e4833b065c0f7f0ff6ab771c6ff6b1a9)
- Marked CCM record protection as not yet supported in QUIC documentation as a TODO item. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c41c7ee976aad76f63ce42c1ea883e4d075e2f0e)
- PKCS#12 import/export APIs add libctx and property query parameter support, ensuring algorithm acquisition follows the caller's Provider configuration. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fe2a7341b50450dc6acd6f8a17d4420511a5aefe)
- QUIC demux adds a default packet handler, allowing server packets not associated with existing connections to be handed to the server callback to create connections. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/93e9b6cc4e2b47a5fb32f093c38b7963e9c270aa)
- Added server state machine for QUIC dummy handshake, supporting server-side handshake prototype flow. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b83cf3fcf1149326f215cffd37f5c9725a4b61de)
- QUIC QRX supports manual injection of received UDP datagrams by the server, for processing by the demux/connection dispatch path. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4e392f601db9a5a131d0db8fa3fa2e3808d2770a)
- QUIC_CHANNEL includes optional reason text when sending transport parameters, and adds server-side transport parameter processing. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3c567a52c25980f99a7353457d6285dc633f366b)
- Added basic server-side logic for QUIC_CHANNEL, supporting server paths beyond connection parameters and client initial handshake. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b1b06da2a3968abc552c1a440cba1b91bd6e34c2)
- Added CMP client RR and GENM tests to confirm that end_time is correctly initialized during request processing. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b908ec0f217da0a23f9d81442f81d44c94c98f23)
- Added support for setting custom TLS Record Layer, allowing SSL_CTX/SSL to register and create application-provided record layer implementations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bea8d70498c9ad0e2cca3652c748d327be7b841e)
- Allow adding custom TLS extensions on SSL objects, for integrated protocols like QUIC-TLS to inject extension data. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f6da3bbfb7342f3931d36e0c67bd9f79169fac2b)
- Added initial QUIC-TLS implementation, connecting QUIC channel with TLS handshake, key update, and encryption levels. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/19863d497dd1f74099998d4e5788d270de6423d6)
- Integrated SSL message callbacks into QUIC-TLS, allowing applications to observe TLS handshake messages used by QUIC. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1d57dbac190ac6082de7865ed9205cd1f41bfd97)
- Added QUIC-TLS server-side handshake support, enabling QUIC servers to complete TLS handshake and export transport keys. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4e3a55fd14cb4424fd62516345d918cdf0d9cdcc)
- Added OSSL_TRACE_STRING, OSSL_TRACE_STRING_MAX, and OSSL_trace_string for safe output of string content in tracing. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0243e821473ef6dedc8d5f3d6ebefc1b06f2e46f)
- Improved QUIC SSL_shutdown behavior and began implementing SSL_shutdown_ex with connection/stream shutdown parameters. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e8043229ead9b44e2883a80ce256c219a1171cbb)
- QUIC front-end I/O API adds setting and detection of EOS signal, allowing read/write interfaces to express stream end state. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a9979965bf2b74ca79e4bf3fa13539ab90728eeb)
- Removed direct support for datagram mode in BIO_s_mem, delegating datagram semantics to the dedicated BIO_s_dgram_mem. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6e193d4d03f6c7bdf95e82e226c5fccbd67562f2)
- Added BIO_s_dgram_mem based on BIO_s_dgram_pair, providing a single-ended in-memory datagram BIO and reusing message queue implementation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3a857b9532169b1ffaa739ba29cd67a5d93cbe8a)
- Enabled AES optimization implementation for Apple Silicon M2, selecting hardware acceleration path based on processor capabilities. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d79bb5316e1318bd776d6b2d6723a36778e07f9d)
- SSL_CONF_cmd adds IgnoreUnexpectedEOF option, corresponding to SSL_OP_IGNORE_UNEXPECTED_EOF; only enable when upper-layer protocol can detect truncation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/51cf034433d528876f3c235c5150c5acfe88f24d)
- Made PKCS7_decrypt support decryption of signedAndEnveloped content, not just ordinary EnvelopedData. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/35da6af1f82e3d02338aabe28cab744a63728fd8)
- Restored several __owur markers to remind callers to check return values of important APIs. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/98283a61f5b792dde0f0e1d9a616ec6e232b57b6)
- Extended Punycode test coverage and removed the no longer needed ossl_a2ucompare comparison helper function. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/23e65561e28f705f8f59128470aaf89bdbdb84fa)
- Added EC_GROUP_to_params, converting EC_GROUP curve parameters to OSSL_PARAM array for Provider export. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a8aad913ecc632405096b2b61942b2c782cc74f4)
- Added Montgomery exponentiation and CRT operation offloading for s390x, using CPACF to accelerate RSA operations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/79040cf29e011c21789563d74da626b7465a0540)
- Client sets PKI rejection status when rejecting newly issued certificates, ensuring CMP certConf response reflects the rejection result. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e7041bfea77cc7e6bab1fe8d2745b6969a8c78aa)
- Added intrinsic implementations needed for RSA assembly on non-x64 MSVC platforms, supporting ARM and other MSVC targets. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/075652f224479dad2e64b92e791b296177af8705)
- Added test callback to modify packets before writing out QUIC packets, supporting injection of controlled network faults. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/14e314093943ffd89633746179c2c8f0b5c631a4)
- Added QUIC fault injector skeleton, defining packet mutation callbacks and test fault scenario interfaces. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/adef87a2c6a0136aa3d965162932f961daf28411)
- QUIC test server exposes connection termination reason, allowing tests to assert protocol closure or transport error categories. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/149a8e6c0a279b0dbbced72ffa6c5ed870a1bbc0)
- Added mutation capability before writing out TLS handshake messages, for QUIC tests to inject handshake-layer faults. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d03fe5de8d1b78dd8190a9bce04bb228719b9947)
- Added test for server not sending QUIC transport parameters, covering the case where handshake lacks required extensions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f10e5885f01582c449eff8df70b61c916d9224cf)
- Declared some test server helper parameters as const, indicating functions do not modify input configuration. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/45bb98bfa223efd3258f445ad443f878011450f0)
- Extended keymgmt import/export types callback to pass Provider context, allowing dynamic provision of importable/exportable parameter types per instance. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5e3b84505e44377b183e7529dab7585674b83936)
- stream_frame_new checks frame object allocation result, no longer dereferences null pointer on memory exhaustion. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bf762f9203d3b5541c21f2b376750e32ebf36651)
- Added missing copyright header to source files. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/93b0a1ea614f9ce3931373fd3d1d1af04795e6d7)
- Reorder the X509_STORE_CTX_get_error manual page entries and add missing verification error code descriptions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1caa4835eb140682ba091bf328758fc6535e70bc)
- Add assignment after successful digest lookup in EVP_get_digestbynid to ensure the function returns the actual digest object. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c09c202e9bc66f0300ee598ca94f2b3fa5a5899d)
- Add FINALIZED flag to prevent erroneous reuse of digest contexts that have completed final processing. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3fc2b7d6b8f961144905330dfd4689f5bd515199)
- Improve error messages when reading register operands in riscv.pm, indicating invalid register names or formats. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ac97cd6005878700653d8e50b6e7319728c523a1)
- Add GHASH assembly implementation for RISC-V Zbc, using carry-less multiply to accelerate GCM authentication hashing. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f3fed0d5fc11a3406951884b9739a93639697a56)
- Provide more specific error codes and diagnostic information for some QUIC failure scenarios, such as remote address not set. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/44a1ac5de0cb422bc65089e1e3bf1b46bb8ab141)
- Add basic QUIC blocking mode tests to verify that blocking reads and writes can wait for protocol events and complete data transfer. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0c593328fe811583da68d25b0c8bf87ba842acbb)
- Add CERTIFICATE_VERIFY_MAX_LENGTH constant to uniformly limit the maximum length of certificate verification chains or certificate data. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c6d14bfd5f16a103181c04614492be03e137d1a4)
- Add SSL_inject_net_dgram API to inject specified UDP datagrams into the QUIC connection receive path for testing or custom network stacks. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/553a4e00aab3e2cc04f47678cba3cd8345e7b0e3)
- Add condition variable wait function with timeout, supporting timed waits in thread pools and reactors. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2b2b26788e7e46abb8fb340d49a088184fbc0b9b)
- Add mutex to QUIC_CHANNEL to protect concurrent access to handshake, record layer, and connection state. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fb2245c44b58b41a378eb47422221edd49ba9091)
- Add lock protection to QUIC front-end SSL API to avoid races from concurrent application thread reads and writes to connection state. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a8489257e69fab643d22932dfa27afb945e78c5a)
- Add locks to QUIC test server to protect server-side connection and event loop state. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e053505f0ce1a6d15cbcd42e49dabc844610b65a)
- Add channel-only tick mode and use it when driving in the background to only advance the channel without repeatedly driving the reactor. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ccd31037713ad1cdfd88c85a169bd18b08579813)
- Add Windows XP compatible implementation for Thread Assisted Mode, adapting to older Windows condition variable and thread APIs. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1dd04a0fe2ffc4104db5198543ed0ec5895e9651)
- Implement receive stream get_record/release_record interface in QUIC-TLS layer, allowing TLS consumers to read QUIC stream data records. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7257188b7054cf8acfc4837e38486459e0930718)
- Fix Win32 UEFI build support, adding required system types, assembly, or platform configurations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c0e090bd61d2e46a1d8f60f39f10152c87e87753)
- Add `OSSL_DISPATCH_END` macro as dispatch array end marker, giving a clear name to the terminating entry of provider function tables. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/23e648962e04af132c0841bec950b8a89b87fb2d)
- Add allocation and copy helper functions for OSSL_PARAM, centralizing memory management for parameter arrays for internal callers. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/79523d55923e7f61104cc7269131fd6a975b579f)
- Reject encoding negative integers as ASN.1 BIT STRING, avoiding silent conversion of invalid integer inputs to bit strings. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1258a8e4361320cd3cfaf9ede692492ce01034c8)
- Add library context parameter to X9.31 RSA key generation, allowing algorithm implementations to resolve providers and properties in the caller-specified library context. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bcd94b6335e37304a170d89977a2382fae370a97)
- Add support for big-endian environments in ARMv8 AES-GCM assembly implementation, ensuring input blocks and authenticated data are processed in correct byte order. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/507356598bc1e17a08f9737df50e1a19525efa88)
- Fix CMP client logic for checking newly issued certificates when using old certificates without providing private key, so certificate registration results can be correctly verified. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e0f1ec3b2ec1b137695abc3199a62def5965351f)
- Introduce `QUIC_XSO` object to encapsulate QUIC SSL objects and their stream state, providing a unified object representation for connection and stream APIs. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f8636c7e85229bf780da7cf61c234695952f8cad)
- Add interface to determine whether an SSL object belongs to QUIC, allowing generic SSL callers to safely distinguish QUIC objects. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3e5a47d4de5754a2d2f42b3402bfe887010357ae)
- Change `ssl_lib.c` front-end to call QUIC implementation through new dispatch interface, centralizing operation dispatch between SSL and QUIC. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6d495cc4de9efac980df0a70be5981fd94831d33)
- Add QUIC stream creation API, allowing applications to explicitly create and use streams on a connection. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2dbc39deacf9d5850eecef515cbc50331750dd22)
- Fix initialization of state and flow control credits for new QUIC streams, avoiding new streams with incorrect state or wrong send/receive credit limits. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e8fe7a21ea253408930d570ce00e6d3a78652162)
- Make QUIC test server support multiple streams on the same connection, facilitating testing of multi-stream transmission behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b757beb5f326ce4a7da021d0f4c52e03e37e1945)
- Implement `SSL_get0_connection()` in QUIC dispatch, allowing callers to obtain the owning connection from a stream object. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/020d0389396d0ee01041188a3d1b211a1d6b6c6a)
- Implement `SSL_is_connection()` QUIC dispatch path to determine whether an SSL object represents a connection or a stream. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e1dee2e37971e068d6aff25dbfc92ef4db5adbd9)
- Implement `SSL_get_stream_type()` QUIC dispatch path, allowing applications to query whether a QUIC stream is unidirectional or bidirectional. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1bca3f1b2d139c2306fd65d23583e4d16bdc11f9)
- Implement `SSL_get_stream_id()` QUIC dispatch path, allowing applications to read the QUIC protocol identifier of a stream. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/19cb0887722b66e5db7ec0d339526608444a11ef)
- Implement QUIC default stream mode setting API and organize default stream object creation logic, allowing connections to configure the type of subsequent default streams. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8b7be3aa7e90d85441f5012624cece4dca33291e)
- Add API declaration for setting QUIC inbound stream rejection policy, providing an interface for applications to control acceptance of new streams from peers. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8a90df343edb194920b7a01c8b5e47d8b6e952c5)
- Extend QUIC receive flow control state to also track and constrain the maximum number of streams created by the peer. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5bd9ddd86e714705840215b8d2bbb0aedc598e96)
- Add QUIC `SSL_stream_reset()` and stream state query APIs, allowing applications to reset streams and query their termination state. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c3a04ea2fdd073e55b57e70e4f17f3ccbaa8c8a6)
- Add frame acknowledgment callback to QUIC frame processing layer, allowing stream objects to be notified when their sent frames are acknowledged by the peer. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9cacba434b027bc6f3a3f3c4255c2453935e5357)
- Add basic handling of `NEW_CONNECTION_ID` frames in QUIC send path, enabling endpoints to receive and record new connection IDs provided by the peer. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/eff046524b970243196d4622d20ffb8e0aeb208b)
- Make QUIC test server support changing connection IDs, to test connection ID updates and their impact on subsequent packet processing. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bbc9754026e815429b55c92cf2a70e4ac59464cf)
- Add QUIC `NEW_CONNECTION_ID` frame processing test, verifying that endpoints can correctly receive and use new connection IDs. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/80b9eca279772185c32bb8d639af874b00217d6f)
- Add name aliases for Microsoft enterprise OIDs and unify references to related OIDs, facilitating identification of these object identifiers by consistent names. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f3afe15fb7d3a1ed4397252d7615e7d788be662a)
- Make QUIC test server able to detect newly arrived inbound streams, allowing tests to verify stream arrival and acceptance queue behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1df479a9f95d2862e32c43c89d17d3e094fb2292)
- Add width description for Windows `LONG` type, avoiding code assuming incorrect bit width for this platform integer type. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a2c61e414332fa7162bb0f9ab991983e0d8cb438)
- Add preliminary message callback support for QUIC, allowing application callbacks to observe QUIC TLS handshake messages. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/63dfde87c46f8ad037ad5b5e635e609f4909578e)
- Add trace output for QUIC datagrams, enabling debuggers to observe information about sent and received datagrams. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/45aac10717479b5c2445e7704cd742b0d754aaa8)
- Add trace output for QUIC packets, facilitating diagnosis of packet sending, receiving, and processing. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2a35a1bec0845d314f06a88d703a9eb30dbed10e)
- Add trace output for QUIC frames, allowing debug information to display frame types and their processing status. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/70f0ea280af0bed9fb48b20b61c7a12c7f03e6d9)
- Enable tracing of sent QUIC packets, filling the gap where previously only received packets could be observed for diagnostics. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e8528c95a0543a218b432d2ea02e6bd0c1e7ab19)
- Enable tracing of sent QUIC datagrams, so datagrams in both directions can be used for troubleshooting. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8aff8f89f7bec3865b14b550a4c1a7ec7786e3f3)
- Add trace details for remaining QUIC frame types, making diagnostic output cover more protocol frame content. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cc87010d27f4dc3645ea718144bf387d8833e14c)
- Add `id-it-caCerts` support for CMP genm/genp messages, allowing clients to request or receive CA certificate lists via generic messages. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d477484d33b7b3572150e21562cf4209c8dd9ef5)
- Add documentation for CMP client certificate confirmation phase and mark function parameters used only for conditional compilation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/afe7a4311d7c0350bc65764b6f642149099a3e0a)
- Skip write lock when certificate store has no new entries, reducing lock overhead for no-change operations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/50001e0e15d4a96213c2eea7c56f80087afa89fd)
- Add `SSL_get0_group_name()` to allow applications to query the group name actually used in key exchange. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/68668243b176cd2bc53a83c6768d4f39930ba8ed)
- Introduce `HAVE_MADVISE` and `NO_MADVISE` configuration macros to explicitly control whether `madvise()` can be used in build configuration. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/78634e8ac253a8edf338d329965724dfa8e033ab)
- Add `BN_CTX_end()` in SM2 signature function to ensure temporary big number context is correctly freed at end of operation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/050dddb06162a8016c004317273f8f01b72ac20a)
- Partially revoke Windows CA certificate store support, restoring previously available certificate lookup behavior for affected scenarios. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/dfdbc113eefb80712fefc3187367fe6050610da5)
- Adjust QUIC receive-side key update callback to also provide the packet number that triggered the update. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/256eee3f3f500a50a434615a054b35a42a2f78b1)
- Make QUIC receive path report the key phase used by packets, enabling connection layer to verify consistency of key rotation and packet number phases. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/754d2282cd50fef14971605d7151623bb11e3fd6)
- Ensure newly sent QUIC packets are not protected by old keys, avoiding continued use of expired keys after key rotation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/54fb0072c6f14a35808f3bb837517f053aff3847)
- Add QUIC key update interface for internal white-box testing only, allowing tests to directly verify key rotation state. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/16f3b542f89dbdd6029400c740a55d49d4af8e53)
- Fix spelling errors newly discovered by codespell. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6ea4da6e4d03cd9591805e166cfd94a374c33628)
- Make `SSL_OP_CLEANSE_PLAINTEXT` clear plaintext buffers on QUIC streams, reducing residual sensitive application data in memory. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a02571a02473889d13fe7996e0d2d052328f3199)
- Add QUIC test server tool, providing a repeatable local test endpoint for QUIC clients and multi-stream behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/37f27b91deda5b6537883c06e845f0d2c28c5d5c)
- Supplement missing PKI/PMI OBJECT IDENTIFIER definitions so that related certificate object identifiers can be recognized. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bac2f6db06e1e606a3f26c8667aebe4d1a0dc583)
- Add `extern "C"` to HPKE and thread header files so that these C APIs can be correctly linked by C++ source files. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ed5c0dfdf4f2d386fadfadcd8692a521061c772d)
- Implement QUIC's `SSL_rstate_string()` and long format variants, allowing applications to query the text representation of QUIC handshake record state. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9ea0e7299223d10f61eee4db62ed0d4aec8f52e4)
- Implement `SSL_has_pending()` for QUIC connections, allowing applications to query whether there is still QUIC data to be read. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9280d26a3a14e2aa79ad26cc25e4f41fbaa828ec)
- Add a way to specify certificates to be revoked by issuer name and certificate serial number for CMP revocation requests. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1d32ec20feae7320ddb2b929441688377b912a40)
- Continue fixing WebAssembly/WASI build issues, adjusting platform dependencies to support the compilation environment for this target. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/66f61ece724a54253da36f70274bc320faf9f4e2)
- Add support for requesting root CA certificates for CMP general messages, and add support for root CA key update for general responses. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/01b048513153bdbee3efc82389d38d353352a7f1)
- Add a ciphertext length calculation function for the QUIC send queue, allowing the scheduler to estimate the final packet size before encryption. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/41d39984e948322700a9b48ed6c6e8426bed3a9d)
- Check the return value of `ASN1_STRING_set()` to ensure that errors are reported to the upper layer when X.509 string allocation or copying fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/46e95903762f0cc478d8a3c252390fa7312bba6e)
- Make `DH_check()` set the corresponding error flag when new error types occur, making it easier for callers to distinguish the cause of parameter validation failures. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/81d10e61a4b7d5394d08a718bf7d6bae20e818fc)
- Add an internal interface to adjust the QUIC send buffer size, making it easier to control the pending data cache according to actual connection needs. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3415677eec8e0b474973115ad871430f11ced3fd)
- Always set an appropriate error message on decoding failure, so that callers no longer receive failure results without an error reason. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/564e5b754a4680dfad38585dd73bcf025567b448)
- Allow the QUIC test server to use a pre-created `SSL_CTX`, making it easier for tests to reuse a unified TLS configuration. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/829eec9f86f94ca81de920a1b61e9b636792d3c2)
- Supplement the explanation of the persistent congestion time requirement in RFC 9000 Section 10.2, clarifying the time range for determining persistent congestion. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a441d08b1b10f259c8fa9eb9cd836ffe19b23b0f)
- Add compliance comments for QUIC regarding the requirements in RFC 9000 Section 10.2.3, explaining the protocol constraints during the congestion handling phase. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d15d5ea6a6dbc98dce76ea40287d5e65fe3c0be8)
- Add assembly implementations for elliptic curve field squaring and multiplication for PowerPC64 little-endian, accelerating corresponding curve operations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/966047ee13188e8634af25af348940acceb9316d)
- Check the return value of `ASN1_STRING_set()` to ensure that ASN.1 string setting failures are not treated as successful. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c791e399abba8394833a2f88abaeb69f27f33b42)
- Make the QUIC-based SSL BIO support most control operations, allowing general BIO users to manage QUIC connections through the SSL BIO. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a2ca189e273584a7af3fcb90d893df9439e96659)
- Complete the missing unlock operations in the QUIC application layer, avoiding deadlocks due to mutexes remaining locked on exception paths or subsequent calls. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4669a3d79b59d037ccb5b4a30bc522ebe55d3eec)
- Allow applications to trigger sending NewSessionTicket on demand, enabling the server to control when to issue session tickets to clients. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/614c08c23999e39945b556851eabff157aef833f)
- Add a test for receiving CertificateRequest after the handshake, verifying that QUIC reports an error according to the protocol when receiving this disabled message. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/644ef0bb696eeaf3572e858b2beeca17b0621a3f)
- Add `X509_STORE_CTX_set_get_crl()` and `X509_STORE_CTX_set_current_reasons()`, allowing verification callbacks to customize CRL retrieval methods and query the current revocation check reason. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4a469cba27cf778f1d97ddeefd3a3a80cd623553)
- Complete the two missing reason codes in the OCSP CRLReason table, so that the encoding and display of revocation reasons cover all defined values. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1c8a7f5091e2c5aebc043be86bcbedc6947e1c6f)
- Add `ERR_raise()` for EVP call failure paths in QUIC, providing diagnosable cryptographic operation reasons to the error stack. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cb19528b932d66e4e90c9365ed67acaec79fe9ad)
- Add a unified QUIC non-I/O error reporting macro and apply it to related error paths to distinguish network retries from non-retryable failures. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8ee3ee10e39fd6fe1323187c63ce41460bd4f9d4)
- Allow the QUIC test server to set `SSL_trace()` as a message callback to observe its handshake trace output. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cb931288730d3ad3e3a6ad9a9db13a8180d31ed9)
- Add braces to QUIC initialization code to be compatible with strict parsing of conditional statement formats by older Clang versions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d848520afed1d3a4e4c38307d3bf21e14bff096f)
- Implement backpressure when creating QUIC streams, making callers wait when the concurrent stream limit is reached instead of continuing to create excess streams. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9d6bd3d30f8068a5558efa0bda2db570500ff364)
- Improve the backpressure implementation for QUIC stream creation, so that when the stream limit is reached, the API waits as expected and continues after quota is restored. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/96fe5e5f964d44dfff8667fb3c0111a25be58c87)
- Add the ability to wait for the peer to initiate shutdown for the QUIC multi-stream API, allowing applications to synchronously wait for peer shutdown to complete. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/25a0c4b907b0dbef4f0e70bf35cd84c85aaee3ad)
- Add datagram mode to `BIO_s_connect`, allowing connection BIOs to be used with UDP datagram sockets. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7841dbabec50eb701022154d9639a01c2a875eaa)
- Refactor the blocking mode configuration in the QUIC application layer so that it can detect at runtime whether the underlying BIO supports blocking operations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/51e671e204ede3a56c3e1c38d834240020800dfa)
- Introduce addressed and non-addressed modes for QUIC channels, distinguishing sockets bound to network addresses from addressless datagram transports. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/617b459ddfabe5c2fbfc28808126999d936218fe)
- Allow the datagram connect BIO to support configuring non-blocking mode to adapt to QUIC's event-driven connection flow. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/533254eeb31efa46a9011665712e47b56a2fe720)
- Allow the datagram BIO to support setting non-blocking mode, so that its read/write operations can work with the event loop via poll/retry. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f3295bd80c44f0e202026ec856c73d8c9bb04670)
- Add `BIO_dgram_detect_peer_addr()`, allowing callers to query whether the datagram BIO has learned the peer address. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/000ef7818b24a61611825670299ab82b283e0501)
- Implement addressed and non-addressed mode handling in the QUIC application layer, so that socket BIOs and memory datagram BIOs can work according to their respective address capabilities. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/62665fc2430cb3d3c9e59a133e67ab9941222017)
- Make the QUIC application layer return the correct unsupported result for `SSL_clear()`, avoiding generic SSL callers mistakenly thinking the connection has been reset. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b139f7a26d0158e42b0f4b9e7364111a8fd17fa2)
- Implement `SSL_want()` for QUIC, allowing applications to query whether the current connection or stream needs reading, writing, or waiting for other events. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5debf070103131cff97a2fc78c93cae391099842)
- Implement condition variable broadcast in the Windows XP compatibility layer, so that operations waking multiple waiting threads at once work correctly. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/425a780462d387a81c83fd2b87d0efd91d6b154b)
- Add a ChaCha20 module to the LoongArch64 assembly implementation, enabling the ChaCha20 hardware optimization path on this architecture. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9a41a3c6a453a90d6c6cf106480a4a72b08b14f9)
- Add a test for QUIC using pre-shared keys, verifying that PSK identity and key negotiation can be used for the QUIC handshake. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1e4fc0b2e57d08a90a6d8e30981fce2007d21109)
- Complete handling of duplicate transport parameter combinations in QUIC channels, avoiding inconsistent state initialization due to duplicate parameters. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/70e809b08a3fe70fed7f7ecdad88e5bb9fc3af1c)
- Add a public `BIO_ADDR_copy()`, allowing applications to copy BIO_ADDR objects and their network address contents. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a18c9f80916134bd7122cc1ba204bb5cdca752a3)
- Make X.509v3 configuration support all extensions represented with NULL syntax, meeting standard null extension configuration requirements. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/91bc783a93a2a695fe6a2f8da93cf5b5e086ba42)
- Add an independent error code for the random number generator, making it easier to distinguish new random number error paths. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a9483b8aa00753a2a9665273c0e376f3c1d36e65)
- Add a mapping for ECDSA to the SHA-3 digest algorithm in object cross-references, so that algorithm name and NID lookups are compatible with this combination. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/98e0755511fbb5e2563dfe0017f011803d57f51d)
- Enable additional optimizations for GHASH, RAND, and AES on Windows ARM64, improving hardware acceleration performance for these cryptographic operations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/636ee1d0b864f29a70573a4894958958e940c01e)
- Add error reporting for `CMS_add1_signer()` failure paths, making signer addition failures diagnosable. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/72a99ef665b26fa207c0eee6e7e4842d1e42752c)
- Validate the EVP context pointer in the SHAKE control function to avoid null pointer dereference. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/410c80dc7bf2085167553ab9fa517189eed2b3a6)
- Implement support for `SSL_get_shutdown()` in QUIC, allowing callers to query connection close flags. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7757f5ef731ad4e8d6c0f59ef752e4f726ba4f90)
- Supplement usage instructions for memory allocation and deallocation of `strdup`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/26f75c2d604014069b5ff32cdf7f13f9e6aec5e6)
- Add parameter validation required by SP800-56B Rev.2 for RSA key generation, rejecting candidate values that do not meet the standard. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8b268541d9aabee51699aef22963407362830ef9)
- Add an API to query the frame type that caused the QUIC connection to close, for applications to identify the close reason. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/55abe7486089ffa24b52e68a56b7eaed9a60a8ee)
- Check for null pointer before dereferencing SSL_CONNECTION in `ossl_ctrl_internal()`, avoiding crashes on invalid control calls. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/54fa5b3911ead0e1ba7d32bc5732ed2a60b38a99)
- Validate both SSL and SSL_CONNECTION pointers in control dispatch, avoiding access to null objects. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6f1d3e130ed9f79335e5c9bd518f835b71417564)
- Complete null pointer checks for SSL and connection objects, protecting control calls on uninitialized connections. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1c03353511238251f48152c09b523322c1de54ad)
- Confirm the internal connection is valid before executing SSL control operations, avoiding null connection dereference. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4230172a0556e1d99dea9469905d18cb64e7c799)
- Add error stack entries for failures in EVP public key bit length, security bits, and size queries. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/51f4115dcc818a35e2c8838c01b3b08740d0c1e1)
- Add an unbiased random range integer generation interface, avoiding probability bias caused by modulo. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3fe56baf936373daa39b944e3194a6f234fbe8bf)
- Supplement comments for the uniform random number algorithm, explaining the steps to eliminate modulo bias. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7a78528bc540db41ca6834810766a62b640a09c7)
- Add strengthened checks for HPKE internal parameter validation based on code audit. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2a4f8da45c73cff771ae45de46ef73095a6ca29e)
- Do not append other stream frames after an implicit-length STREAM frame, respecting its termination position requirement. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fc0dce399aed2c8d2aea8ea675aa3d50f51b6ef4)
- Deduct QUIC connection-level flow control quota synchronously when writing stream data, avoiding exceeding MAX_DATA. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/915ec623eca7b413db6f54ec4aa64585e81ac4df)

### Provider Plugin Framework

- Add an encoder in the Provider encoder that outputs EncryptedPrivateKeyInfo, supporting the export of encrypted private key information structures. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0195cdd28fde7d0897e368fdcd4e92509425faad)
- Added Keccak-224, Keccak-256, Keccak-384, and Keccak-512 digest implementations in the default Provider. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/524f12611040de64cda13dd148ad1b8ca559c730)
- Added the PVK key derivation function implementation to the Provider, making PVK KDF available through the Provider algorithm interface. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/722fe8edf224ecc0921481b47fdd06a54d82e4ff)
- Added SM4-GCM encryption implementation in the Provider, supporting use of this mode through the Provider interface. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c2ee608a234340aa735f894f8d84ead0ce58286e)
