- Added an empty digest implementation in the default Provider, supporting operations that request no digest algorithm. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bef9b48e5071cdd2b41a4f486d1bcb5e14b2a5c3)
- Added HAS_PREFIX() and CHECK_AND_SKIP_PREFIX() string prefix helper macros, and used them to unify literal prefix detection. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2ff286c26c29b69b02ca99656d26d2f8cfd54682)
- Added case-insensitive prefix and suffix detection macros, and replaced duplicate string checks in related code. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/747adb6a0134e3b707fbc47d0f0c52d6ff9c4223)
- Moved common internal definitions from cryptlib.h to internal/common.h, separating public internal interfaces from library core definitions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/af16097febcd4fa31cd5fcd05ad09cf8b53659ea)
- Made KBKDF support R_BITS parameters of different lengths, adapting to derivation configurations with different output block bit sizes. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0e9a265e42890699dfce82f1ff6905de6aafbd41)
- Added RNDRRS Provider support, enabling OpenSSL to call the ARM RNDRRS random number instruction through the Provider interface. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/eb28fda79748c303d88a8af48de5187100f2c64c)
- Check the return value of OSSL_PARAM_BLD_new() in dsa_kmgmt.c, and exit safely if the parameter builder creation fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0da3b39af3d961486758262ca71d2135d7013048)
- Added DRBG lock handling for cases where ThreadSanitizer is unavailable, ensuring safe access to random number generator state. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8ff861dcee38a41ce93374753e8c462e4b9012e2)
- Reimplemented ERR base marking operations in the legacy Provider using upcalls, avoiding direct dependency on the core error stack. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8c2e588bcf0de61880ec8a956ef57ad6b8a50163)
- Optimized AES-GCM for different microarchitectures using loop unrolling and new instructions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/954f45ba4c504570206ff5bed811e512cf92dc8e)
- Added return value check for BN_bn2hex(), correctly reporting errors when hexadecimal conversion allocation fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4dd085c03a885580cc945f71187131ea7fb39b70)
- Added a safe memdup helper function for copying memory regions during Provider context duplication. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5b030ec0800d4ad6022ecd00e18a19f77ada0b04)
- Implemented Provider context duplication for PBKDF1. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6585d3aa7638c8cea2d4bb9f10e7298002f652e5)
- Implemented Provider context duplication for PBKDF2. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0a10f71d3071bae0183cd4277da64d100f6b48eb)
- Implemented Provider context duplication for HKDF. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/95bd5ff65985e992827f7178deda84d95b1e6f66)
- Implemented Provider context duplication for KBKDF. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d54c52c28ebb780e2ffc5b7752d35359215cf0a6)
- Implemented Provider context duplication for Kerberos KDF. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4c1a841c3de645674ed2af92da25f7f5736fae1c)
- Implemented Provider context duplication for PVK KDF. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fe01052f775d1b5dff86ff9b405b6b0df5efd3cf)
- Implemented Provider context duplication for scrypt. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cdcdcf5c6fa382c879cb3503609519d56fa62e81)
- Implemented Provider context duplication for SSH KDF. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/59558f9d8824747024b6ab756f3798a577ecae48)
- Implemented Provider context duplication for SSKDF. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2722eeceaa993f4488b295a22d2e1178f5ba1ce1)
- Implemented Provider context duplication for K94 KDF. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/769cd46540b2ec2a2d91ee3886b9e4f9d78e9a51)
- Implemented Provider context duplication for PKCS#12 KDF. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d3aaf4e9e71944d869ae47821d7b5a8402234ee8)
- Implemented Provider context duplication for TLS1-PRF. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b9d8ad3f157fa816c423bec6f7b4328ef894577c)
- Check the return value of OPENSSL_strdup() in dh_exch.c, and handle failures when duplicating parameter names correctly. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c920020f0bb13f0d2bf0fcad5c7ee63458b633b4)
- Added macro guards to header files to avoid duplicate declarations or definitions from multiple inclusion. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3d27ac8d92ef89c202b518cf6c4e15477eb594b2)
- Implemented cross-platform strcasecmp and strncasecmp, comparing strings according to C locale rules and handling length-limited comparisons. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fb4cdca053fb9d3f0e11eeaf31f4b4ff87f69a95)
- Added VERSIONINFO resource to the FIPS Provider DLL on Windows, enabling the system to identify component version and product information. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/18f0870d39eb055c8c67af8025fd1b9e01ce6a43)
- Added Windows VERSIONINFO resource to the legacy Provider DLL for non-built-in builds. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9510661400470d357c74c5c4d0ff01c7b813b974)
- Added RISC-V Zkn hardware implementation selection in the AES Provider, supporting related AES-ECB/CBC/CTR and other cipher modes. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ee11118deb65d2b22b94721125a5649d05591e7b)
- RSA DER writer validates saltlen and trailerfield ranges, rejecting parameters outside the allowed encoding format or algorithm range. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/48320997b49b07b5abadec89c7fbe5d5f3d41da4)
- Implemented RFC 8452 AES-GCM-SIV, adding corresponding key derivation, synthetic IV, and authenticated encryption/decryption logic. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0113ec8460a918f8bc782130db8f75540b3b1ab2)
- Corrected the OPENSSL_MALLOC_FD manual description to accurately describe the memory debugging output target. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/08ae9fa627e858b9f8e96e0c6d3cf84422a11d75)
- Allowed the record layer to call the SSL security policy callback, continuing application security checks on record layer algorithms. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5ccee69b1384fa9377986a6f7730e0d9a372b42b)
- Added support for loading system trusted CAs from the Windows CryptoAPI root certificate store. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/606e0426a148034c8c131de9f31f7d3e38be99ea)
- Added HPKE DHKEM Provider support for EC, X25519, and X448, implementing corresponding DHKEM key encapsulation/decapsulation algorithms. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/78c44b05945be07eae86f0164b9b777e2de2295b)
- Fixed a null pointer dereference reported by Coverity, exiting after object allocation or lookup failure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c9ee6e3646258f79a9970be96394cb2b93b7eddd)
- Implemented SM4-XTS data unit encryption in the Provider, providing corresponding algorithm registration and key/IV parameter handling. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2788b56f0c8306c89c97a6599484120afddfa14a)
- Registered the AES-GCM-SIV algorithm in the FIPS Provider, providing this mode in corresponding FIPS build configurations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/edaab86dc001603741f5b5e406afc1cc3a1c4e6e)
- Implemented RFC 6979 deterministic ECDSA signing, using private key and message digest to determine nonce, avoiding reliance on random nonce. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f3090fc710e30a749acaf9e5dfbe20dd163cf15d)
- Added a configurable switch for RSA implicit rejection, allowing callers to enable or disable this protection behavior according to compatibility needs. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5ab3ec1bb1eaa795d775f5896818cfaa84d33a1a)
- Added KMAC128 and KMAC256 as MAC options in KBKDF, extending key derivation support based on KMAC. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/211c47ca1b1ac129dcee59d383cae44e36532bb9)
- Removed duplicate definition of __NR_getrandom and added usage notes for system call numbers. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c8a9b26d6ed7a62d26a013c21e62ba4a0a2d6dd1)
- Added support for the five EdDSA instances defined in RFC 8032, including pure EdDSA and variants with context/prehash. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/836080a89a1f5e45dac4e0df76b9270587f65d5b)
- Added -ems-check configuration option in the FIPS module, enforcing Extended Master Secret (EMS) check in the TLS1_PRF KDF. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/50ea5cdcb735916591e35a04c1f5a659bf253ddc)
- Enabled VPSM4 extension capability detection for ARMv8 SM4 GCM, allowing GCM mode to use vector SM4 instructions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/738d43634a5192b1be0869f151682bb8e9157d5a)
- BLAKE2b Provider supports extensible output length mode, allowing output length to be set via the EVP XOF interface. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/786b9a8d3f8e203c5536e36b9a9bab83bde0311a)
- Added Provider error reason codes to distinguish invalid memory size and invalid thread pool size configurations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/232dd87c55f66ecae906299cbea1ea7782241b64)
- Added Argon2d, Argon2i, and Argon2id KDF Provider implementations, and used an optional thread pool for parallel computation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6dfa998f7ea150f9c6d4e4727cf6d5c82a68a8da)
- Enabled hardware acceleration for SM4-CCM, selecting optimized key setup and block processing functions based on CPU capabilities. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fdfa63dfd6f1e6d7aff175fba56486a0cf79713f)
- Allowed reading some DRBG parameters without acquiring a lock, reducing lock contention between read-only parameter queries and random number generation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/61f11cad7a0dffc7abd234164a0e74c6ae8e7c2a)
- Added trie-based fast indexing for OSSL_PARAM lookup, reducing lookup overhead when querying parameters by name. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e84b5fcc1b1d599173eaab07790c06a532981e50)
- Made GCM implementation use the new fast parameter location mechanism, accelerating algorithm parameter lookup. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/79d702250b76cb88947158d4f4d0786fbe96eeac)
- Added Cygwin to CI, continuously verifying build and tests on that platform. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e3b01eb6b25e76d4832d81023d056948edc2cb36)
- Added `no-ecx` configuration option to remove ECX algorithm family features at build time. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4032cd9a1434610e4dc2bbde01f98d04faa615e5)
- Use thread sanitizer-compatible counter operations to increment FIPS counters, avoiding `tsan_add` from triggering issues in thread detection builds. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ff934cfdc85a7b8ddb4bdebf9ab68d518bf68b7f)
- Added SHA-256/192 support, enabling corresponding digest length configurations to be recognized and used. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/81bafac5cbbd195ff9c53a06aaca7c3eacbb2fc0)
- Added limited support for WebAssembly WASI targets, allowing available OpenSSL components to build and run in WASI environments. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d88a0f5f3944535dd83d55bedc4d239544c5678d)
- Enabled hardware-accelerated SHA-3 implementation on compatible Apple ARMv8.2 CPUs, improving SHA-3 digest computation performance. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f6484de23df5f04e1f9fa8418e942c45c1a65578)
- Registered MSBLOB/PVK decoders only when applicable to the current input, avoiding providing mismatched decoding paths. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6207f2b657b5ba1823681b49c7c34c619da0dd00)
- Implemented `dupctx` for ChaCha20 cipher, enabling EVP to correctly duplicate its algorithm context. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/61cfc22b60e33bc77b1e1944759af48c8e58f0d2)
- Added `dupctx` support for AEAD cipher contexts, enabling EVP to correctly duplicate AEAD algorithm state. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0239fb3db77e9de2031c5054854cba8e417c1b72)
- Implement `dupctx` for the AES-WRAP method, supporting copying the context of the wrapped key cipher. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2c021e7d11f03ede2330398c4fd8e8c7bd8768ee)
- Implement `dupctx` for ChaCha20-Poly1305, allowing EVP to safely copy the context of this AEAD algorithm. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/df93b3c9e72571876bd01e5a50a5ba8368c6c77f)
- Add `dupctx` support for the RC4-HMAC-MD5 algorithm, enabling correct copying of legacy cipher contexts. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/123c85864fa7fe97d8ae3a09989d410501d957a5)
- Fix spelling errors newly discovered by codespell. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/10fe5e29cad9a9dbaeda3cdc7c62470b21dd7d38)
- Add cleanup callbacks for user entropy and nonce, ensuring that caller-provided data is cleaned up at the end of its lifecycle. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5516d20226c496c2b22fa741698b4d48dad0428f)

### Cross-cutting / Other Architecture-related Changes

- Add a check for the latest run-checker fuzzing results in GitHub Actions, continuously validating the fuzzing CI tasks. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f92bfddc1d4c4957c57337d7f4192c586cc09a5c)
- Add tests for KEM dupctx, verifying KEM context copying behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d7b5f06ede163851d39f5a8b507bd0670deeaa21)
- Add validation tests for mixed PEM files, covering scenarios where the same input file contains multiple certificate objects. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d4458e59f62b0d102069e53da41f1d5305a66912)
- Add OpenVMS x86_64 build configuration support, enabling OpenSSL to be built on that platform with the corresponding compiler and target settings. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6929c8fb5b46c9c2a383a7c212ee052e0dcef021)
- Add the -latomic link parameter for the linux-x86-clang build target, satisfying the dependency on the atomic operations runtime library for that target. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7ea01f521d08d6585a62c7cfd9358c0f191bd903)
- Adjust the random number API usage for systems below macOS 10.12, avoiding random number issues caused by incompatible old system API behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/24cdb1bfecbd765e829b9932a5a60ff63a7dff4b)
- Add the ktls option to s_server, allowing the server command to configure kernel TLS data transmission. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e2ef7f1265e727567e8963aa2756a387a621ef71)
- Add the ktls option to s_client, allowing the client command to configure kernel TLS data transmission. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e396c114eb7233e24ba6a920606cfdd6bc6cff7c)
- Add a note that OSSL_FUNC_keymgmt_has is a required function, clarifying that Provider Key Management implementations must provide this callback. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d270a6c9ea9f240f653a98c7eb0a5c2134a5d63a)
- Add copyright headers to CI scripts, unifying the copyright notices in continuous integration scripts. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/08d8c2d87ec782e95c28ff795e096c2f6f590d63)
- Update gost-engine so that its name output configuration is consistent with the new default nameopt settings added in OpenSSL. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7a27bdbdce3d6d6548d5878a30aecc989fcab574)
- Add ThreadSanitizer annotation macros for concurrent code to mark thread-safe accesses that require special handling. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b0b456f8c8b628c3d7e212339e31cbfd06ac4ec8)
- Note in the documentation that OBJ_ functions do not yet have thread safety guarantees in OpenSSL 3.0. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b66b024cf7124c9639011b27b70a082e3bc3d269)
- Add concurrent object creation tests, verifying the safety of creating OBJ objects from multiple threads simultaneously. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0855591e1f3559313641c13e4b7ce900ce42321c)
- Add more build tasks for different operating systems in CI, expanding platform build coverage. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a16ba5f37547eb6ef38a9e623e42b21b35ce47fb)
- Add SM2 encryption test cases from GM/T 0003.5-2012, verifying the encryption behavior specified by the standard. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8ba65c35ea3af347c3b2adc8e665066b541a1c35)
- Add a PVK KDF manual page, explaining its purpose and calling interface. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fc9eda53bcf7d41c8159b7161c663db86dda5481)
- Add PVK KDF unit tests, covering the algorithm's derived input and output behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2d34e5b2ecf6a5db982c53bb56c62249b7791051)
- Add a note in the SSL_set_fd() manual about Windows compiler warnings, helping callers understand platform compilation hints. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f8dd5869bca047a23599ac925aace70efcf487ad)
- Add attribute parsing failure tests, covering error handling for duplicate or invalid attribute inputs. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/747d142318c5c9ecd80de3f061f54d7af4189039)
- Update test cases to comply with the algorithm and certificate restrictions after the default security level is raised to 2. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/61cab65029e787d59d3f3138e0160adb8df85f99)
- No longer output extra warnings when generating a new certificate request due to reading from standard input. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/58608487a44b3991ecc6d431d6273b2ca8c980a6)
- Add SM2 signature test cases based on GM/T 0003.5-2012, verifying the standard signature process. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f087ebcb2eb516a424245fcb93642e57ba024cc4)
- Add ENGINE-related regression tests, covering engine reference issues during key copying and Provider interaction. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0299094c52ddb66f9a22cfff4e7d70c139112832)
- Add a note in the EVP_DigestSign() documentation, clarifying the requirements for input and output buffers in the signing interface. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/dc3f057ce1701d5fd77cc4fcc1d14afe3e3122a3)
- Add X509_dup tests based on ENGINE keys, checking the lifecycle and reference behavior of the associated key after certificate duplication. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bf585c9c071ec606ebb4606e749e63354140ca30)
- Add unit tests for weak keys and key parity checks, covering valid and invalid key inputs. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cc350c882218b1053a636d01eb36573b3e7b20c2)
- Make the -sans option of the cmp command support RFC822Name email addresses, extending the SAN name input format. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/03ee2e5b1ecd1832d99d07fc459ecf62f5a0b168)
- Add a daily Coverity build task in CI, continuously scanning for static analysis issues in the code. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7267769c28fb90d990a9d789093e83699bf4c5a0)
- Add SKID/AKID combination tests for self-issued and self-signed certificates, systematically verifying extension behavior during request and certificate generation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/00cf3a2d30fc7642bf9f816a7c545115985a8c0c)
- Add integer overflow helper functions for safely checking the boundaries of integer addition and multiplication. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b037e3637a492fefe22b5fb12d7206afe6754ccd)
- Add unit tests for integer overflow helper functions, covering boundary values and overflow detection results. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bc4efcb0d0740467f1b8b536677a2886c2445c80)
- Add safe_math tests in the run-checker CI for cases where the compiler does not support the relevant built-in functions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d362db7cd1cc46462e0dd3bbccd5c279f2b2ccc8)
- Add tests for repeated initialization of EVP_DigestSignInit(), verifying that the same context can restart the signing process. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/816f72d08834ee35ba2615f624b4a29f2717d1c7)
- Add DigestSign re-initialization tests for SIPHASH MAC, verifying the signing output after repeated initialization of the algorithm. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8cbfc4f67b4e97d423ab4784dbbb54d454c6342a)
- Add tests for creating overly short ECX private keys, verifying that invalid length inputs are rejected. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8c08c8b37cab0eb66ca74fc65a40af3ccec77c00)
- Add an RSA encryption example, demonstrating public key encryption using the OpenSSL EVP interface. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/35530b117fcf54cf733c485e9e2e267963c081ee)
- Add documentation for several previously undocumented environment variables, explaining their impact on OpenSSL build and runtime. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7f6496275157f8e40f544f75a223c2c0dc6b389e)
- Add test cases for repeatedly loading an ENGINE, verifying that the same dynamic engine is not initialized twice. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2595eef82c2b67ea75cc3368529078b643a1ecb6)
- Add tests for EVP_PKEY_sign_init_ex() with RSA-PSS padding, verifying extended signing initialization options. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5321333520b95a4f355916923af6c24dd10ed5dc)
- Add manual pages for functions that were previously documented but lacked actual manual pages. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4db1df8e824733b6289dc86dcc78bfe325031969)
- Add tests for copying an uninitialized EVP_MD_CTX, verifying that the context can continue to be used independently before and after copying. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8c86529fe1b9ade0794c6f557ca8936f0c0de431)
- Identify arm64 BSD targets and enable assembly optimizations, allowing related systems to use AArch64 optimized implementations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8e22f9d6d956ad583afe10b986519731c113ac80)
- Fix ambiguous descriptions in the BIO_push manual and add calling behavior for edge cases. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7a37fd09a8f3607ed8acf55e03479861595be069)
- Enable and fix the test_bn2padded() test, covering the behavior of the big number padding encoding function. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/23750f677ef61b6bea4e81f23f335ad08fc49b51)
- Check memory allocation results in bio_enc.c to avoid continuing to use a null pointer after encoding BIO allocation failure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/684326d3bd3131debcdc410790e8dcf16f96103f)
- Add EVP_PKEY comparison in test_EC_priv_pub(), verifying the matching relationship of imported EC public and private keys. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/edc8566f475d63278d5f85cd25f324cf2fe9aaf9)
- Add a case for -untrusted certificate mismatch in CMP verification tests, covering certificate chain verification failure behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/22dd3f8b273b18fc20f0650b5a19166eda1950ee)
- Add Name Constraints test certificates to verify certificate name constraint checking behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3269c8bd9489cf9b03abceab5dee24f831a5e492)
- Add TLS name constraint tests, covering scenarios where the end entity certificate has no SAN extension. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/752aa4a6f0f3098258fb6be5592fd18929da59c0)
- Add regression cases for name constraint defects, verifying that previously erroneous paths have been fixed. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0fcf2351ecff5db21cba431704e4da631b74904a)
- Add RNDR tests and merge them with RDRAND tests, covering the generation behavior of the hardware random number Provider. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1f8ce0c9faee59ac51a5db7a8ec42c38866be090)
- Add regression tests verifying that HMAC and digest copying paths no longer suffer from use-after-free. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/27f7f527652e403177335eb2e3ba1ff6df13f193)
- Add support for BSD RISC-V 64-bit targets. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c2d1ad0e048dd3bfa60e6aa0b5ee343cc6d97a15)
- Add unit tests for the property index to string conversion function, covering registered and unknown properties. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9f6841e9d8964943cf5f616543750cee85c4911c)
- Add the -ref_cert option and corresponding API to the CMP mock server, allowing specification of the referenced certificate in responses. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b971d4198def0b29654e8fbf7987f7157741aed2)
- Add test cases for short passwords, verifying handling behavior when the password length is insufficient. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/21095479c016f2ceaca0f71078fd27f0e9ba9375)
- Make check_format.pl check blank line formatting after and between local variable declarations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d8662f2f8716645164a9d4d8795a9c353fe315fb)
- Add tests for creating custom digests using EVP_MD_meth_new(), verifying that legacy digest methods can be properly initialized and called. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fbbe7202eba9fba243c18513f4f0316dafb3496d)
- Detect multiple unknown options in OpenSSL commands, avoiding silent acceptance of multiple unrecognized parameters. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2c2724476ef50b8926b033f009bdfc85ac3f1816)
- Add write lock checks in thread tests, verifying the write protection state of mutexes and read-write locks. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1fc97807d3a3b5e3065a7df80d1ad3601ccc5e2f)
- Added DH parameter setting tests for EVP_PKEY_CTX_set_dh_nid(), verifying behavior when specifying named groups. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/59d3fd1cc8c938daa6384783a7e5847d6f5201f7)
- Added digest context copy tests, verifying that copied digest operations can continue independently. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0be4b0403d2f65adf0d037581223dbebd0fa135e)
- Added cipher context copy tests, verifying that copied encryption operations remain consistent and do not interfere with each other. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ed16b0fc282d29f755e656043e8a70553ef7bea5)
- Added test/bntest.c cases to verify boundary and round-trip behavior of signed BN conversion functions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5288303da96084b41b062d99eb37177fb4cf471e)
- In evp_test, check the return value of OPENSSL_malloc() to avoid continuing with a null buffer after allocation failure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2208ba56ebefe4cf7d924e2ac7044ccd3307250b)
- In ct_test.c, check the return value of sk_SCT_new_null() to avoid continuing operations after test stack allocation failure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7625d70ad9e7be0588dd9453e89892c2b24b8175)
- Added signed BIGNUM parameter tests covering encoding and conversion with OSSL_PARAM and parameter builders. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/748a2967ffd52cf86696582fb1074d513493f469)
- Added X509_STORE_CTX_set_purpose() tests to verify purpose setting and inheritance logic. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8447f2e3912c810a02ed1c8641db27ff70ded5ba)
- Added -verbose and -quiet options to dhparam to control diagnostic and progress output during parameter generation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a414fd6765bbc9bb0d630dbb4d780f44f825c8a2)
- Added context copy operation to the KDF test syntax in evp_test to support verifying derived context copy behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c8adf19d2da318cd7b007753d6c8a7f9dc94d4ed)
- In evp_test, check return values of OPENSSL_strdup() and sk_OPENSSL_STRING_new_null() to avoid using null objects after allocation failure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b2f90e93a07d992515782511a5770aa7cf7dc28f)
- Documented the use of EVP_PKEY_get_bn_param() for reading large integer parameters. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f6f4d1cc00a557232955867b6c04f767e8b5a12e)
- Added EVP_PKEY_set1_encoded_public_key() tests covering encoded public key setting and reading behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/eafd3e9d07e99583a1439bb027e4d6af43e2df27)
- Check the return value of OPENSSL_strdup() and exit safely if string duplication fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/09030ee73693411c19b596cb0e0f43eb512ac0e6)
- Check whether MAX_MISALIGNMENT compensation calculation causes buflen overflow, and reject out-of-bounds lengths. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/378c50f63dceb3a85bb4937a3499283b10d295b6)
- Added a non-interactive run flag for s_client, allowing the client to run without terminal input. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bb2fb5d7cc6c4abc888c3fd6df4366b6dfde25a6)
- In openssl rehash, check the return value of OPENSSL_strdup() to avoid accessing null paths on allocation failure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/79cda38cff834224fb9d86dc7433b4f60688ce49)
- In test/helpers/handshake.c, check the return value of OPENSSL_strdup() to avoid crashes in test helper functions on allocation failure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/17da5f2af833ef16cc2e431359139a4a2e3775b9)
- In s_server, check the return value of OPENSSL_strdup() and report an error if allocating server configuration strings fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0c5905581e9d1d79d62cac56a0e3c2ed487afecf)
- The OCSP command-line tool checks allocation results after duplicating strings to avoid continuing with null pointers on memory exhaustion. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8f084b43803d53e15d83ed130210f026f84679ff)
- s_server adds failure checks for newly created BIOs and releases existing resources before error exit if buffered BIO creation fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ba0b60c632ae9c5590b59184281baaf0a39f0c24)
- mdc2test checks the return value after loading the default Provider and does not continue executing tests if Provider loading fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/78c5f1266fdd859df04b0ce89e4dd849d9b590d7)
- ASN.1 fuzz test entry checks the allocation result of BIO_new and takes the error handling path on creation failure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d43597c718dd6e4f2b18d5cec1eb791503a18988)
- sslapitest checks the result of SSL_CTX_new to avoid continuing tests after context allocation failure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b0317df2311769e02d9ceb4e7afe19521f8ffbf1)
- pkcs7 command adds -quiet; when used with -print_certs, it omits the additional textual digest for each certificate and only outputs certificate content. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/632e8be2b570959dc3781c6956171e7e49f1aa58)
- Added failure checks for calls creating memory input BIOs for BIO encryption filters to prevent continuing filter initialization on memory allocation failure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cf21d1c62dcd92be624ea0fb8a86d91e4fbeed93)
- crltest checks the return value of glue2bio and correctly terminates the test path if BIO assembly fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/18cb1740cc0fd11940836fa2fcaf6d3634c00e90)
- The random number fuzz test checks the return value after creating OSSL_LIB_CTX to avoid continuing on allocation failure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/885d97fbf84fb9de7548a5f6d4e90798f719022a)
- Added forward secrecy determination tests for TLS security level 3 and above, covering acceptance and rejection conditions for DHE/ECDHE and PSK suites. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d71151ae704847f4ac3f4a5f394ea64f1d229815)
- openssl req -x509 can now generate certificates using a CSR: when no private key is provided, it allows providing a CA certificate and CA private key to complete signing, and adds tests for missing signing material. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/df5c86e9f80d14d699bad4c8889292fd9b4bd7ba)
- Added X25519 EVP key exchange example demonstrating the process of creating keys and deriving shared secrets using the EVP interface. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2cc7c9b6981d683711e76c3483f813701b686eb9)
- Added ARIA-256-CBC EVP example showing how to perform CBC encryption via the EVP encryption interface. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/376972773469e59a19acb9ebdecd3ddc290e391b)
- Added Poly1305-AES message authentication example with build instructions, demonstrating EVP usage for Poly1305 MAC. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3dafeacef8d7bf82e462cc52659681108db42e43)
- Added two RSA-PSS examples: one signs an existing digest, the other computes a digest over input data and signs it, and both demonstrate verification flow. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e9492d1cecf459261f1f5ac0eb03e9c631600537)
- Added oqs-provider submodule and test targets to CI and external test configurations, covering interoperability between OpenSSL and post-quantum algorithm Providers. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fa66f62ebbb878bef5c34591efc82b24b9b88dff)
- Added libatomic link dependency to Android x86 build target, fixing unresolved atomic operation symbols. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b420e249370c4a85c4edd65fb445d20f9c19c44d)
- Added migration_guide man page link to README for easy access to migration instructions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/96a77661723b4ba8fe9eb7cef009d735c97e2aa6)
- Added missing OpenSSL 3.0 man page link to README, fixing missing documentation entry. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a20c9b6c13afb71e1dd03bf122673e3093d6c437)
- Added RSA key generation EVP example demonstrating the process of creating RSA key pairs via EVP_PKEY_CTX. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ad083f9b0ab81d094c2dbb8f5e2a5fb7738a0bfe)
- Added standalone man pages for SSL_get_certificate and SSL_get_private_key, explaining how to read certificates and private keys from SSL objects. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2a9219514263454896bdda800b4b811843338bc7)
- Added tests for DH key exchange with X9.42 KDF Provider path, verifying padded shared secret and KDF output. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4413fe3520da3ad42c417828b1785eeedcde50d3)
- Added SipHash EVP example showing usage of creating MAC context, setting key, and computing authentication tag. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/864853988e80517a563d2423d4fb742323995433)
- Added BN_mod_sqrt man page explaining modular square root calculation interface, parameter requirements, return behavior, and error conditions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/03eccd2663e36f8b95ba3ae8c30a63313a38ec0a)
- Added negative tests for BN_mod_sqrt covering failure behavior for inputs without square roots or invalid moduli. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3469282ed2faee747868150089e07a187891b5ee)
- Added RSA EVP encoding/decoding example showing key conversion between PEM/DER representations via Provider encoders and decoders. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1483b37e7a2c952eed5f6c7f5c0be9635aa3a6ea)
- Added required configuration and build notes to Windows ARM64 build instructions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/288e9680399d3a755861d309058dda2fb48af8bf)
- openssl rand changes output length parameter to long and adds optional chunk length, allowing generation of larger random outputs and controlling buffer processing size. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2aa645bca435759fa01e4e5827b7d93ad4e06673)
- Added safe mathematical helper function for ceiling division to avoid overflow when using a+b-1 calculation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4157a32867e6643da8daee94e836aaa18b9feed6)
- Added loongarch64 configuration target, enabling OpenSSL to be configured and built on LoongArch 64-bit platforms. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b625e21e67666213ffd3ba6b1f2cd8a847ba6b66)
- Added -static-libgcc to shared library link parameters for Solaris SPARC v7, resolving dependency on GCC runtime library during linking. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1c529128f53ed1cb33a829dafe7fb4201c7ce69a)
- Added SM2 Dist ID reset test verifying behavior of resetting identifier to empty value after setting. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/707d4e06eba71fb8a8b2faa77a2072511189544d)
- Added test for ecparam when both FIPS and base Providers are loaded, verifying curve parameter command works correctly. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/269c349a7688daae48d95e582e62ff181888c854)
- Added QUIC technical requirements document summarizing protocol behavior, interfaces, and design constraints needed for implementation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cb629550cdab518c925e9b402e11b86497a03845)
- Added simple SSL echo client and server examples demonstrating establishing TLS connection and sending/receiving data. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/801c638c50406c93d683c1ab8bd1d430cff4b6d0)
- Added Tomas Mraz's public key fingerprint to the release key fingerprint list, updating release signature verification information. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2c0a944c69dc92cb280147997696cd88acd7b395)
- Added support configuration to bypass release commit review requirements, facilitating handling of specific release commit review processes per project policy. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a625354d82aad711141172efb3b39b7689318fe2)
- Documented in security level documentation that DTLS 1.0, TLS 1.1, and earlier protocols are unavailable by default at level 1 and above. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/54b0c534eeb283878092e006e7f1e9315ec62ad6)
- Added key checking API documentation explaining differences in EVP_PKEY_*_check validation scope between default and FIPS Providers. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0b3d2594d060dc19269d3740ad672f065ec6398a)
- Added Provider unload effect test confirming that algorithms provided by an unloaded Provider can no longer be returned by cached queries. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4b4d0ded6df357f76f580b7218abb3fe55f64463)
- Quoted file names in Perl script invocations to support build environments with spaces in paths. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3b85d9de443c31e49a0215c1fe3c80828d609062)
- Added Provider load query invalidation test confirming that newly added Providers update previously cached 'algorithm unavailable' results. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/70dc0b6d27a11a7f64fe914a3f376988ad1b1720)
- mkdef.pl adds command-line flags to distinguish shared library export symbols from DSO export symbols, supporting correct export lists for different modules. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e5f831a065df1d6e4640ef389f8594a5f10c9c8e)
- Added PBKDF2 test vectors using SHA3-224/256/384/512, verifying SHA-3 PRF derivation results. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5702392f73e679fd9ed9dd912cf4c9dc613c4d71)
- Added 32/64-bit byte swap assembly routines for riscv64 with Zbb extension for reuse in cryptographic algorithm implementations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e4fd3fc379d76d9cd33ea6699268485606447737)
- Configure regenerates configdata.pm only when its content actually changes, avoiding unnecessary rebuilds on unchanged configurations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/764cf5b26306a8712e8b3d41599c44dc5ed07a25)
- Mark the assembly architecture for the BSD riscv64 build target, enabling RISC-V-specific assembly code. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d1460afdfcb3c90df612896d40d35c6627a5967c)
- Add a QUIC build option that is disabled by default and can be enabled or disabled via configuration, restricting the incomplete QUIC implementation to development features. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/30b013291a502dce406708474a60fe58d5803e66)
- Enable enable-quic in some CI build jobs to continuously compile and test the optional QUIC code. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b7873f92b0f79bdf576795c86d6520656568d672)
- Extend RISC-V CPU capability detection to identify Zb* and Zk* scalar cryptography extensions and provide capability flags to the algorithm dispatch layer. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d5dd608364074fadbf4776142ccd8c7b268845cc)
- Recognize the RISC-V Zkn assembly implementation in AES platform dispatch logic and select the accelerated path based on detected CPU capabilities. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/77d29ff041edcdc6a3d33251d6270a4cfe0be9b3)
- ssl_old_test checks the return value of OPENSSL_zalloc, stopping the test step on allocation failure instead of continuing with a null pointer. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4f4942a133bd57c4940fb1bc6ed7c8b67da4d8f0)
- ssl_old_test adds a check for OPENSSL_malloc failure, ensuring safe exit when test helper buffer allocation fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b2feb9f0e394da6570346598837f1b01eb58c028)
- Add design discussion for the Datagram BIO API, documenting interface requirements and behavioral constraints for datagram BIOs. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b80395efc91e97fdd0ec724c3e3b814195affe21)
- Add unit tests for the priority queue, covering insertion order, priority sorting, and deletion behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f0a4935827db5527d23da61805a1e73f0c660d39)
- Supplement documentation for the new SSL internal time API, explaining the purpose and calling method for obtaining time values. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9f40251da8a6bd66c36a33fe41e52c87ec14ca64)
- s_server checks the return value of OPENSSL_strdup to avoid continuing with a null pointer after failing to copy configuration text. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a6a2dd9f60b3f3e93de1337ee84f9e8f33bc86a8)
- Add timestamp certificate chain verification tests, covering TSA certificate validity period and purpose validation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/386ab7f1fefdd77521e670d9593e9894e2774be0)
- evp_test checks the result of OPENSSL_strdup when copying test label strings, avoiding crashes when memory is insufficient during tests. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5203a8dfdc209f05c7dbd9c1e5208743fcaa6752)
- v3nametest checks the allocation result of OPENSSL_malloc, cleaning up and reporting test failure on memory exhaustion. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b147b9daf17744d529f23b5da40397a6071a88aa)
- Add CI builds with RFC 3779 support disabled, checking that compilation still succeeds when optional address extensions are turned off. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a09adac311975afcb5ad099b2e1cfc7eb1e72865)
- Add manual pages for PEM_X509_INFO_read and PEM_X509_INFO_read_bio, describing the interface for reading certificates, CRLs, and private key collections from files or BIOs. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9454423bf1eac4c75e70ff4fd67456e4cfb05a92)
- Add example-driven initial design demonstration materials, using runnable examples to showcase API design and usage scenarios. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ec36534cbbf57999b90cbb36404d9daa599a9ae4)
- Add GCC 11 to the CI compilation matrix, covering builds and tests under that compiler version. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6332f4c4a2c153869b169d250d9736962abe12c6)
- Upgrade the CI Ubuntu environment to 22.04, thereby adding compilation coverage for GCC 12, Clang 13, and Clang 14. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/712c13c57b97e2e25ca23048f3ba6f50115cacd7)
- Add negative tests for all-zero RSA keys, ensuring invalid public/private key parameters fail key validation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/995eccb611431a4857cac3283e2442c01109d428)
- ASN.1 fuzz testing checks the return value of ASN1_item_i2d, and on encoding failure no longer passes ungenerated data to the fuzzing logic. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1cb35ce06a968dc82e7cd9502ecce8e89eca9580)
- Add tests for the next epoch in DTLS, verifying data handling after the handshake switches record epochs. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e1c153d31d4f913ebe2202a4bc20305919274d1f)
- Supplement the manual for OSSL_FUNC_store_export_object(), describing the callback interface for Store Provider object export. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/13941d438458068d4150c5899f6bbc0add393cc4)
- Add tests for try_key_ref fallback logic, verifying that when Store returns a key in reference form, the key object can still be obtained via the fallback path. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/dca637f50cf71372c46a9cf6022ad4eb9970ab7f)
- dsaparam and gendsa add support for property query settings, allowing specification of Provider properties to select parameter generation and key algorithm implementations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/30b2c3592e8511b60d44f93eb657a1ecb3662c08)
- Add required header files for compilation, fixing build issues caused by missing declarations in related source files. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/924c814a8a6a9dcfeb8e366705c6ef5f078d0628)
- Add an SSL internal zero time constant, unifying the expression of zero time values and simplifying timeout and time comparison logic. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/16612c19290d712de65a7adcb043ba91165f3e0f)
- Add unit tests for the event queue, covering event insertion, time-based triggering, cancellation, and queue clearing behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0eb27659435768d1a2370858d340a9c6793ed244)
- Explicitly include the header file required for memcmp declarations, fixing implicit declaration issues in strict build environments. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f9e578e720bb35228948564192adbe3bc503d5fb)
- Add the cmp command to the openssl-cmds manual index, ensuring the command overview lists the CMP client. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/33478aedafaff1f414cabd67fb30970c41996f5c)
- Add an HMAC EVP example, demonstrating key setup, message updates, and authentication tag generation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e269d8af79de7b0dcc1b72687eed340cc3822a9e)
- Add the LibreOffice Draw source file for the QUIC architecture overview diagram, facilitating maintenance of the design diagram. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b4ba4c8ee8f75ac60d1e26eb80110b68527b44d8)
- Add configuration options to the speed command to control how algorithms and features are built for performance testing. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8403c7350fd836ea44baf69c0b7dc3af1189253f)
- Add HMAC-SM3 test cases from GM/T 0042-2015 Appendix D.3, verifying national standard test vectors. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0648ec1c35a54fb2b7ead34a215691fe9e38516d)
- Explain the purpose of the CA certificate variant without basicConstraints in certificate test scripts, facilitating maintenance of test materials. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a148a9b4f49b0c456d45a39c0d63a52405aa5ea9)
- Add required token permissions to GitHub Actions workflows, ensuring CI can perform corresponding repository operations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c6e7f427c82dfa17416a39af7661c40162d57aaf)
- Add a CMAC EVP example, demonstrating how to set keys and compute CMAC tags via the EVP_MAC interface. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cdf0a5c46032ba4c39d93a7bec52494b4808830f)
- Fix the issue where openssl x509 ignored -CAfile in -new or -in modes, so that certificate issuance looks up certificates according to the specified CA file. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/10c7887330bb6ca136cd16fe081639f4462a072e)
- When -CA is not specified, openssl x509 warns about ignored CA options, avoiding silent configuration neglect. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c54a6a4b0ef664313fb07617d6a8c26a808719e0)
- Clarify that objects allocated by X509V3_add1_i2d need to be freed after calling it, supplementing resource management documentation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/17004adacf74f9f7036b623dab31a6d12c32daf1)
- Add API tests for CMS_EnvelopedData_decrypt, verifying decrypted content and failure scenarios. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/98b183d3c65e56b0f21f4e77b2cd3d2aa62374f2)
- Add dhparam tests, verifying that the private key length is written when generating DH keys and preserved in parameter text output. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2b11a8ecc8ed1355b99a6d88b8e7e7a75a67bd0a)
- Supplement the X509_STORE_CTX_purpose_inherit manual, explaining how verification purpose and trust attributes are inherited from the parent context. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c00fd2dece8ba54b2597a61c8db6a001025d05d5)
- Add regression tests for re-signing certificates with different key sizes, covering cases where the certificate public key and new signing key lengths differ. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/29fcd2e79ef739ff9211edd88d99ba03dd201299)
- Add tests for BIO_s_mem datagram mode, verifying that the memory BIO saves and reads data by message boundaries. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3bfc58ad6f150e343c75565e2b162b80ec39a28d)
- Supplement documentation for BIO_s_mem datagram mode, explaining message boundaries and buffering behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ce602bb0a20589e5a84c48a55ce13219ab881e84)
- Add tests for EC_KEY_set_private_key, verifying that private keys can be correctly set, read, and used in related operations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d93f154d5a524e6ed71ff276447de7fe11d85949)
- Add a standalone timing program for measuring startup phase durations such as certificate and private key loading. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6212fc6814e8a8968bb35239cd454afd22b6a083)
- The dhparam command supports setting Provider property queries, selecting DH parameter generation implementations by specified Provider. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ae3c30acac17271693e91dcae42c804cd96e8f93)
- The ecparam command supports setting Provider property queries, selecting elliptic curve parameter algorithm implementations by properties. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2b8f687d7627a4b15bba6a820825944185980376)
- The pkeyparam command supports setting Provider property queries, controlling the Provider algorithms used for parameter generation/checking. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0185538799803a1a98823f42ac2402ede04f56da)
- Add tests for pkey application property queries, verifying that each key parameter command can obtain algorithms by specified properties. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/33b9bb45a4d463052aef763cc7c64c91384d4249)
- dgst supports Provider property queries in signing operations, allowing selection of digest and signing implementations via properties. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/653a7706781ebbe8a6a4b84d29b39d001c395ffe)
- genrsa supports setting Provider property queries, selecting RSA key generation Provider implementations according to configuration. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b2ccfd81025fa115f1138123b9aa61657e779352)
- Add tests for EVP_PKEY_get1_encoded_public_key, covering successful reading and error handling of EC key encoded public keys. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3a1596f4e3d710c163279a20e6b844d371886e73)
- Add tests for code signing purposes in verification applications, confirming that the corresponding purpose correctly accepts/rejects certificate chains. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/61a97676914df358dd014a9b6fe2ba01b0ebe508)
- Add CODE-OF-CONDUCT.md, explaining the community participation and collaboration code of conduct for the OpenSSL project. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/63df86b041aaafba3e4998b2e3872fa8695a2377)
- Supplement the X509_gmtime_adj manual, explaining parameters and return values for certificate time offsets relative to current time. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/425e972dfaf867affb5b3d438d9ca67bb6aeed65)
- Add tests for DTLSv1_listen, verifying that the new record reading flow does not break DTLS listening and client verification behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/26dad42e9ca609569073463165263173ab2a27ab)
- Add tests for read_ahead data spanning key update boundaries, verifying correct segmentation of old and new key records in the buffer. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f7565348c22785f69239883feb1f3c91d1cfd675)
- Rename the timing tool to timing_load_creds and integrate it into the test build, clarifying its purpose of measuring credential loading time. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a9389c0b75e69ebaf74fdc8fee0c983809e45931)
- pkeyparam supports Provider property queries, controlling the algorithm implementations used for key parameter related operations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/83529f07ca66ec288f1c506a673569b9d8de8368)
- EC keymgmt safely handles null keys/encoding results when querying encoded public key parameters, fixing crashes in OSSL_PKEY_PARAM_ENCODED_PUBLIC_KEY access. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/47dc828c6b652feb9cef5b0e4186d010986f197c)
- Import the recordmethod design draft header file, defining the new TLS record layer method interface. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3c1f8fb13e064ad7f42e9b65c601c68e1aa79f7d)
- Make the settings and options parameters of the record method interface const, preventing implementations from accidentally modifying caller configuration. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6347b86778a392c955b60b1ce107951d3552aec2)
- Move the TLS fragment length check out of the SSL object reference, using the record layer's own configuration and state. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/42ee6e7be43c57136d71e5612fed22a06f7f5d0e)
- The record layer now uses a dedicated message callback and no longer reads the callback field in the SSL object. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cbb15b31b98f47276cf9e87453831d96274baf66)
- Merge read and write encryption context references, removing duplicate state of enc_read_ctx and enc_write_ctx. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f5eac259a03c68c96c77f9b998b1b9c16a8439e7)
- Add the function prototype declaration for OSSL_TRACE() to the OSSL_trace_enabled manual, fully listing the tracing interface. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5d0a327020d238949ffbcff56e1c32aa4a436f52)
- Add an EC key encoding EVP example, demonstrating the key serialization flow of the EC key encoder. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a4b7136ebfd154636f607c50aaeec778a75b2d26)
- Add TLS 1.3 record padding API test, verifying that the padding length set by the caller takes effect in sent records. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f3f8e53c852f07d38c124e45f7c678e854be4a54)
- openssl list adds an option to list all available algorithms, supporting viewing algorithm instances provided by different providers. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/35b670702466b91b3baa724635e5aecbc2061fa7)
- Add a CI task to test the legacy FIPS provider, continuously verifying cross-version compatibility behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/65080a3e1ebced54af838481e6d40e1c0cb7991e)
- Add documentation and tests for EVP_PBE_alg_add, explaining the registration of PBE algorithm mappings and their call results. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/181167b6d0e5cd896847f7538adf28878b81b0b2)
- Add an AES key wrap EVP example, demonstrating the encryption and unwrapping of the RFC 3394 key wrap mode. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bebc6c899943cc3f519501aee221c9d0eb10fcfd)
- Fix KTLS capability detection in Linux cross-compilation environments, no longer using host features to incorrectly judge target kernel support. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9b25f52a4425d03fee1364d3531331d5d37ef742)
- Add more message types to the DTLS dropped record test, covering handshake state and retransmission handling after record drops. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a29ad912b82f50ef876bef99c66522dccd41b6f8)
- Add a QUIC connection ID cache design document, explaining connection ID lookup, many-to-one mapping, and cache management. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/538ee4e0977492009f8ca39d577d8a1aeb8d27fd)
- Fix GCC compilation warnings in crypto/conf, avoiding build failures of configuration modules due to warnings. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/28a5aa0cbdddfdf4d82a437d72407d4f52d4e54a)
- Update oqsprovider and liboqs submodules to v0.7.2, adapting external post-quantum algorithm tests to the new dependency versions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a9c474dc98233ce6e64b898874e3604cc151f461)
- Fix the AES-GCM cipher implementation on Power 8, restoring the correct computation path for that processor. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/67e0735f66971635508d9ab3f07e8b05a95491d5)
- Support handling certificate URIs from SSL_CERT_DIR, and add the SSL_CERT_URI environment variable to configure the URI certificate source. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f51169514d71115f610c9e216dcab03b89b17340)
- Add support for loading Windows CryptoAPI root certificates, enabling the TLS default trust store to read the system certificate store. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/71beab84e6e2c5bda417b309a16c3ec4eee38859)
- Skip corresponding tests when the 3.0.0 FIPS provider does not support explicit curve handling, avoiding reporting known capability limitations as failures. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/511c49189ef600d41f44cd7c5d204e9ea27b5f48)
- The QUIC RX record layer limits the number of delayed datagrams, preventing infinite cache accumulation while the receiver waits. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3f76339a3fe7be412b754ffe7b0a5438a1297f28)
- Add BIO_s_dgram_pair paired datagram BIO, enabling two-end message transmission without a real socket. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fb8bdbe3eba83265586a44cda2ffc783611680d7)
- Add a test for clients sending non-TLS 1.3 key_share, verifying the server's rejection behavior for version-mismatched extensions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/04719b208992750ffe2b9232c62ca9179185f3e5)
- Add a test for TLS 1.3-only clients sending a valid key_share, confirming that normal handshakes still complete. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7b141d4934ab1254d65fd1859ca1c6eff1113b50)
- Add thread-pool and default-thread-pool configuration options, allowing buildable replaceable thread pool implementations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fdb11e1bcb38e33d5804f25dba176a901a19688a)
- Enable thread pool configuration in CI, continuously compiling and verifying this optional thread pool feature. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b1372197496650c3cb318cade911a3bd6af14adc)
- Add the required header file for DH_get0_priv_key, fixing the missing function declaration when compiling the corresponding source file. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bbaa24b7c5ca4d712ad539d4c5ed16af0dd908f4)
- Add CI builds with Brotli and Zstandard enabled, verifying that optional certificate compression algorithms compile correctly. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b540aae97d6b80f9040874b9c56259a85ba46f36)
- Add a blank line after each group of commands or algorithms in openssl list output, improving readability when multiple groups are output consecutively. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ec1d5970be596daed15a3fa723cfa2ac726b0dba)
- Add Clang 15 to the CI compilation matrix, covering the new compiler version. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/75ecda930e0a961f9605ce090af64d95c98ed161)
- Enable Windows QUIC builds in GitHub Actions, verifying that optional QUIC code compiles on the Windows platform. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/76228352d80250801a00d50beeab7dc786336689)
- Add Dependabot configuration to automatically track and update GitHub Actions dependency versions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/225f94e818d9f8cb9e272fb9128b4b0ef88a0cbc)
- Add Punycode decoder unit tests, covering normal domain names, boundary lengths, and malformed inputs, verifying out-of-bounds fixes. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a0af4a3c8b18c435a5a4afb28b3ad1a2730e6ea8)
- fipsinstall adds -self_test_oninstall, allowing FIPS self-tests to be executed at installation time instead of only at load time. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7057dddbcb5e053470121adeff0b6595fa6da0d8)
- Add a regression test for X509 signature TBS caching, verifying that the signature cache is correctly refreshed after modifying a certificate. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/29d4d8e80e72c458501888d41bdfa00f51914909)
- Add Alpine/musl builds in CI, covering non-glibc Linux environments. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e8cec34c39cd2f30b98d1d98e9a297066c918ab3)
- Add a fuzzing target for the Punycode decoder, exploring malformed inputs and covering security boundaries. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8aa82b337081b7a22c35dddad8d62fb1ca9ea884)
- Add tests for generating safe prime DH parameters, verifying the safeprime parameter generation path. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/10119e7475bb198e13b1722b186303b8a7528dfe)
- Add the first-phase design document for the thread pool, describing task submission, worker threads, and pool management interfaces. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d55fc027b9af85b1054cdbc017046a9070935086)
- Check the return value of fdopen in the speed tool, no longer dereferencing a null pointer when file stream creation fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c9a542e41837ea65671dcd75c448d7113d34a4fd)
- The OCSP application checks whether make_ocsp_response succeeds, stopping output and cleaning up state when response generation fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/93e1271eedfe3af0a1c1b14d26899d2c8bde98e9)
- Add EVP_PKEY_eq tests, covering equality and inequality comparisons for keys of the same type. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e5202fbd461cb6c067874987998e91c6093e5267)
- Add two version comparison options to the FIPS version test helper code, supporting comparison of provider version numbers and build version differences. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fe84acc22757e77d48fb6ccc31abe4c72264c877)
- Add explanation for bit 64+17 in the CPUID documentation, listing the processor features corresponding to that capability bit. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ec7689186f3ea5c1a4d1564089cd8df287dfcf3c)
- Add an empty-list check function for the generic linked list, so callers do not need to directly inspect the internal head node. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b6f1b059eefb493d02913e9b32bd267d9017ee73)
- Add structure consistency assertions for the generic linked list in debug builds, detecting corrupted forward/backward links early. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/30773411264dca0a791a068759ec625bd0d4f34b)
- Add IPAddressFamily_check_len tests, covering scenarios where incorrect lengths are correctly rejected. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7489ada9f3fd902c5bc3c58cc03a90de2800d0ab)
- Add a TLS record layer design document, recording read/write paths, method interfaces, and state ownership. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/14b54475d141bf92390934a30ff406a0551e36e9)
- Add EVP_ASYM_CIPHER-RSA provider documentation, and organize the RSA asymmetric cipher description in the FIPS provider manual. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ad60cd522b4f717a69c690f68f1591371a048591)
- Add missing HISTORY sections to OpenSSL 3.0 related manuals, explaining the introduction versions of each API. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4741c80c0556653c74252ec91425dcb74066b2ec)
- Add a DSA EVP signing example, demonstrating creation of a DSA key context, signing, and verification. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/858b5d12b85b0639519d21206c9da7e1bb976a00)
- Add API introduction history information to the EVP_KEM-EC manual. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f5060f9b31654d7ca3b015d2b803e17dda760190)
- Add EVP_PKEY_Q_keygen tests, covering parameter and algorithm selection for the convenience key generation API. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/667a8501f0b6e5705fd611d5bb3ca24848b07154)
- Add SM4-XTS test cases, verifying data unit encryption, decryption, and boundary length results. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6cdf83eaabda63f7c5cf9d69d51d931308da471e)
- Add SM4-XTS documentation, introducing the algorithm's purpose, parameters, and provider fetch names. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e44b34185268e99ee2dc499073cf56d0465d60e2)
- Add known-answer tests for RFC 6979 deterministic DSA, verifying deterministic signatures under different digests and keys. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6b3d28757620e0781bb1556032bb6961ee39af63)
- Add OSSL_CMP_CTX_get_status coverage to CMP client tests, checking PKI status reading after request processing. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6ea44d07a7d0acb4af9eab15d9b4a76227f55f4e)
- Provide a timing_load_creds compatibility implementation for platforms lacking the timersub macro, computing timing intervals. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f2e4629608c3a2f5d93a91ef95abc25726eec44c)
- Add a regression test for the X509 verification deadlock report, verifying that concurrent certificate lookup and policy processing do not stall. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/61203c2c59df5d0022e316a4fe614e5d18907715)
- Add PKCS#1 v1.5 implicit rejection test vectors, covering decryption results for valid padding and malformed ciphertexts. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8ae4f0e68ebb7435be494b58676827ae91695371)
- Test the option to disable RSA implicit rejection, verifying that after disabling, decryption still reports invalid padding in the traditional error manner. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/455db0c94c0b83083ce8b792982c03aa56fc866f)
- Add manual and notes for BIO_s_dgram, explaining message truncation, retry, and buffering risks of datagram BIOs. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/408622b73a18dd1d8eeb6629bdd91b06715823cc)
- Add CMS invalid encryption algorithm tests, verifying that clear errors are returned when decoding or initialization encounters unknown algorithms. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5a8fcd27bb1be7db7945adb77125015553efdbdd)
- Add OSSL_trace_set_callback tests, verifying callback registration, triggering, and cleanup behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e64a169fc678b5e57db28d06c25020d69bc61e4c)
- Add DSA parameter generation and DSA key generation demos, showcasing EVP parameter and key interfaces. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/de1164102083730298e4f53eb465c9324aa6a0c0)
- Add multibin support for NonStop, allowing multiple binary models to coexist and select the corresponding target. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d793a3253bada9b61e5ccd5f8caaa4bfc4f4faa6)
- Added OpenSSL 3.1 blank migration guide file as the documentation entry point for this version's migration notes. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/174d166bc3e5e27d4aef08a5fa4e932e22e8f3a7)
- Supplemented QUIC Connection State Machine new API documentation, explaining state progression, event handling, and calling requirements. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c572bed9f56b48937f58e75cf8132696ba19a6ad)
- Supplemented constant definitions required for QUIC protocol implementation, shared by frame, stream, and packet processing. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/198d97c14e60ef112d443a619378233bd789e743)
- Added portable EINTR test support for sockets.h, allowing simulation of system call interruptions in cross-platform tests. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8c94cf38a2a82d8b4f7ebab2e75c2110f16c9e2f)
- Improved documentation for QUIC tick function and added DTLSv1 tick API description. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8ccc567ef0af03a312d7742f451556eb788d5286)
- Added negative tests for unquoted attribute strings, confirming that non-conforming values are rejected. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/543ac2f0191f10d8a3774727fa691543de8b15bb)
- Added EBNF definition links in attribute documentation for easy reference to full attribute query syntax. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/69d6ecb7c320bafe0d30a88949eb22e19704221a)
- Added tests for public bn2bin variants, covering output length and byte order of different serialization interfaces. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/15192335c8bbfb78bc02086bcd77a0d82efffbce)
- Added large TLS application data tests, verifying that the record layer segments and sends/receives data larger than the regular record buffer. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3ff0a48af4135feeae83f8888fac88298f55d921)
- Added DTLS coverage for large application data tests, verifying fragmentation and reassembly behavior in datagram mode. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2fda45d5eb85e5d939fbbba0dd6562ebf01abd2a)
- Added RV64 cross-compilation targets with Zb* and Zk* extensions, covering RISC-V bit manipulation and cryptography extension builds. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e787c57c538d0922004e49a10be0d403af773272)
- Clarified that OSSL_trace_set_channel takes ownership of the passed BIO, and callers should not independently free that BIO. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bfd5680e6be789fd554acf2ad34428816a644eec)
- Reverted RV64 Zb*/Zk* cross-compilation CI targets to avoid CI failures due to unstable target toolchains. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/45972000b44ce0d97adacfddb38f28710b49cfec)
- Supplemented notes about ignoring context initialization failures, explaining the handling convention where specific APIs defer error reporting. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d4c5d8ff483d99f94d649fb67f1f26fce9694c92)
- Added QUIC stream and connection close API documentation, explaining how to end a single stream or the entire connection. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/522fb49dbcd283c00c77ebcc7a650c54ac6eba5b)
- Added name constraint matching type confusion tests, verifying that incorrectly typed SAN inputs are not mistakenly accepted. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/96e77bd32786209a7c7975eb8aedd6485b79e4e0)
- Added PEM empty payload regression tests, covering pointer state and error handling when the decoder reads an empty certificate body. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/dc341a46677fe19f055bd2eea0e3a2af21053903)
- Added d2i_PKCS7 empty data pointer regression tests, verifying that decoding content-less objects does not trigger NULL dereference. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3436f9c24ab90c1661e4798e7944f028d5d251ce)
- Added parameter-less DSA public key import and check tests, confirming that the Provider import/decode path correctly rejects such objects. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/adf77d6657e836fc87391fad53d857927a764297)
- Added regression tests for missing return value checks of BIO_set_md, confirming that digest BIO initialization failures are propagated. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c47b6fe9e6c014df009c55221b6c08391c361ba6)
- Added DJGPP build target for CI, covering configuration and compilation compatibility in DOS environments. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f9171a06416c6dd9b7b8cd7e4bc08e23a4fab242)
- Added tests for RSA_sign_ASN1_OCTET_STRING and RSA_verify_ASN1_OCTET_STRING, verifying ASN.1 OCTET STRING signing and verification behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/416a9286859d444e5a77bbdcc73f0c35b34e574b)
- Added RSA FIPS 186-4 parameter derivation coverage tests, verifying helper functions that derive RSA key parameters from p and q. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b1ce6a23f8f61cc2f2f48368a97493498c026aa7)
- Added FIPS RSA/DSA/EC key generation self-test failure tests, verifying that pairwise consistency test failures prevent key return. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/dcd20cc139d1a26cd94c66cc5ebc8ab85d928356)
- Updated internal test helper function names in the CMP mock server manual to align documentation with current test interfaces. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a6e1e9ebc216f8b1d07782e631657a7ddb73fb99)
- Added CMP client tests, verifying that the client reports appropriate status when the server returns errors in subsequent transaction requests. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6f88876d4ea66d1f0b9217fec18b9dcc760a451a)
- Added default configuration file uninitialized tests, verifying that custom libctx operations do not accidentally load the system openssl.cnf. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0aa7d7f42bc757a0993739b6cfdc8819a70d22ef)
- Implemented plaintext packet mutation for QUIC tests, allowing insertion, truncation, or modification of packet content before record encryption. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2f1d8f858decda2d604abf3347c8e17237f90387)
- Added tests for receiving unknown QUIC frame types, verifying that the server reports a protocol error and closes the connection. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/71587f2b6a711bc8cd18521575910291f637dfcf)
- Implemented mutation support for TLS handshake messages in the QUIC fault injector, allowing message modification before generating handshake records. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6d1f6933595ea66c2e8367fef01e2824b4f3ce6b)
- The fault injector can modify QUIC packets after record encryption, covering ciphertext corruption and transport integrity tests. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/de60deb258c4b52502da372a61344b83428fc970)
- Added corrupted QUIC packet tests, verifying that the receiver correctly detects authentication failures and terminates or discards data. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/be5b3b3787271d6b9057639ce42145fc66c11732)
- Added QUIC datagram listening interface, enabling the server to wait for and receive connectionless UDP datagrams. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e4cb6583efa11decfa8d4d539c6cc2f08c99a067)
- Added qtest_check_server_transport_err helper function to uniformly assert that the QUIC server reports expected transport errors. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c88de5607829f8d98427ba3fa3d465c4e66e07fb)
- Supplemented explanatory comments for copying, preserving, and updating capacity fields during buffer expansion. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/47d905fdc635dcf92a2de4d1d4eb4cb47a4adcec)
- Added helper function to insert QUIC frames before a packet, used for packet assembly and fault injection tests. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7eaaaaaa559d56edc9732d768dc374a4f829b187)
- CI added Clang 16 compilation and test coverage. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6de73f5d795b74815740088274069b8778264bb8)
- genpkey -pkeyopt help now lists available option values to help users choose algorithm parameters. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2c1ec72a7abb29f2d91eda6f93942670f1cbdb9e)
- Completed manual section numbers in CMS_add0_cert manual cross-references. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e6657e585b60601d89e507b6140b84074649e429)
- Added S/MIME fuzzing target, covering MIME encoding, certificates, and CMS message parsing. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/359d6a26d64c32e7c2bebf5655c70c074f6c805b)
- Added documentation for NULL cipher and NULL digest algorithms, explaining their purpose and limitations of not performing encryption or digest. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f7d76c3d7d09d95a9ceb5b69c8f951f53237ef78)
- Supplemented Provider prefetch algorithm documentation, explaining how to prefetch algorithm implementations after loading a Provider. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e798248c8461893ba29d97410b7c0dcecbf23d82)
- Added X.509 v3 name constraints fuzzing target, covering name constraints extension parsing and matching inputs. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/55aab29c1ea2b8103aa0f0ecb20c058ff200fe27)
- Added make help target, listing common make commands and descriptions supported by the build system. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d108082377aa5f1c5420ec76ca2e9a5b8fb12a32)
- Added QUIC_RSTREAM API tests, covering receive stream read/write, data offsets, and FIN end states. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/330ce4a39bd6d0c1fd5ba7426574fb1f36cef961)
- Added EVP context copy failure tests, confirming that Providers that do not support copying can still complete signing/digest operations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f3c0dd4f0cd3bc282575a98181f8190d81189a78)
- Added RSA X9.31 padding signature tests, verifying signing, verification, and error padding behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/513b20a982e237fbd4c281c1c7ba817864f9a827)
- Added Windows HybridCRT build target, supporting hybrid configurations where applications statically link CRT while OpenSSL DLLs use dynamic CRT. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/18891efdf4813547bc4e5b3791ac7af72fa277c8)
- Explained runtime library linking and applicable scenarios for VC-WIN*-HYBRIDCRT targets in Windows build documentation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4a3b6266604ca447e0b3a14f1dbc8052e1498819)
