- evp_test now supports specifying output length for XOF digests, verifying results of variable-length digests like SHAKE. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c8ebdd6a85a0cefe5542dba41180571fa5f198a0)
- EVP test framework added multi-threading test capability, verifying behavior of Provider digest or algorithm contexts under concurrent use. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ae1792e3d94bbd9f6c535f1784438011097adb4a)
- Added static assertion helper macro to verify at compile time whether two types are the same. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0e200d2a19185dab9d73eee90bd6cd0246416a9e)
- Added OSSL_sleep reasonable duration tests, verifying that extremely large sleep values are limited and normal short sleeps are effective. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6821acbffda908ec69769ed7f110cfde57d8ca58)
- Updated Windows on Arm configuration documentation, explaining the newly added clang-cl compiler/assembler combination. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e9460bb45b38e9edb6a57b79daeefdc80eb9e81f)
- Added Argon2 KDF example, demonstrating key derivation via EVP_KDF with salt, iteration count, and memory cost settings. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c77fb9af5595fa416637f775e51722699ea3c78b)
- Added simple interoperability test with Cloudflare quiche, verifying that OpenSSL QUIC exchanges handshake and data with external implementations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fc11028089c374bb24655895c90eaf069c3cee6f)
- Added SSL_inject_net_dgram tests, covering normal parsing, failure, and connection state changes for injected datagrams. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3cc376c91e0e9d55fd3903f203dc38d0a5788380)
- Added HISTORY documentation section for SSL_inject_net_dgram, explaining the version in which this interface was introduced. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/03fa5127ded6ba0dc9f178090eca0dbe70769c0e)
- Added certificate policy constraints resource exhaustion tests, verifying that oversized policy trees fail safely when reaching node limits. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cca6c050dbf8e377794303ec37aa43cb567afda1)
- DSA key generation added option to set subprime q bit length, allowing control of p/q parameter sizes. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/535ddd37524217143eb710bc880ee8c60b7a6cf8)
- Added command-line help and tests for DSA q bit length option, verifying that specified subprime length is used in generation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/438068674b95b38892d2d1790b3fd14e3112d0cb)
- Added certificate policy validation tests, covering policy matching, inheritance, and invalid policy results. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/591feddc61f113827883ad8bae05109ee01ccd41)
- Added documentation writing guidelines for public functions and macros, specifying requirements for synopsis, parameters, return values, and HISTORY. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c3637cafd378f2dacc70018499fec4619082051b)
- Added advanced command mode for s_client, allowing interactive sessions to accept additional control commands. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d07b763bb9073945ba5e9912e56bc51fe18bdcb5)
- Supplemented documentation and command list for s_client advanced interactive command mode. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b21306b9300996b0e69947d6b4cfa64e4c62ec07)
- Add basic Thread Assisted Mode tests to verify background threads advance connections and application I/O. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bbc646e91a2fccf45b0cd2030b2de7f0ef828c58)
- Add QUIC Thread Assisted Mode design document explaining background driving, locking, and application call models. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/27c49c06f173ce009a00778206a95dfc81618470)
- Add DTLS tests covering reordering scenarios where application data records arrive before epoch switch messages. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8189fe242bba319dfccd8805fd7703d973bf9649)
- Add tests for SSL_version, SSL_get_version, and other APIs in QUIC connections. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/843f6e277f2905d95f0c2d0804deb3ea62cef1c1)
- Add CI cross-version validation for FIPS Provider, automatically comparing current code with supported historical Provider versions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3b38f3d86923530be80e73175abfa07ad6dd2d4a)
- Add openssl/fuzz-corpora submodule to centrally obtain fuzzing corpora. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3565cc2c5bb25847f9de99926ad9959c8b645390)
- Add `-pedantic` option for `openssl fipsinstall` to require stricter FIPS parameter checks during installation configuration. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bc2a4225a4a03f70bb0154a72c2889aa80c1b0f6)
- Adjust configuration used in SSL new connection tests so extended master secret scenarios comply with strict FIPS provider policy requirements. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4454c20f026bb47f158ea05c207f143c81d674d8)
- Allow passing additional assembler options via Configure, enabling cross-builds or specific assembler environments to supplement required ASFLAGS. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d9b811dc01992adcc19ce400f38adc0eb7de2a0b)
- Add KDF `info` parameter concatenation tests to verify KBKDF, SSKDF, and X9.63 KDF combine context data according to their specifications. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8d18f20800077913231c6b1d5c6630ff56047036)
- Add structure diagram and adjust explanatory text in QUIC I/O architecture document to make component relationships and data flow easier to understand. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/dda864793e2de00159f268d910b95e98f21525ae)
- Clarify in SMIME command that six operations are mutually exclusive and reject specifying multiple operations simultaneously during argument parsing; documentation synchronously notes this limitation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2786160731257540a957216aeb6431970bbce95f)
- Add `SSL_rstate_string*()` API tests covering conversion of SSL record layer state values to text. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/79ee017220651d50d345af0e3093f091d5155890)
- Add QUIC client mode for `s_client`, enabling command-line tool to establish QUIC connections and perform TLS handshakes. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f34e5d7a12775ce2fb84e4c5d8b830b5a9f06566)
- Add ability to send FIN in QUIC mode for `s_client`, allowing clients to explicitly end the sending direction of their QUIC stream. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c4f74e7fc90b02cbee59b46783222467b48491ff)
- Supplement command-line documentation for new QUIC mode of `s_client`, explaining connection parameters and usage. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/90ae2c13c1cc318568c65d6ad18409741cc54eae)
- Add documentation for new APIs supporting QUIC multi-stream, explaining usage of stream creation, acceptance, and state management interfaces. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1e4a9d882f77977f518dcad418b05822efb172fe)
- Add reference counting query capability for QUIC internal objects to facilitate debugging and inspection of shared object lifecycles. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/008a61a544e16d20595731f614b2fbc1d20f793e)
- Add invalid frame cases to QUIC new connection ID tests, confirming invalid sequences or parameters are rejected. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/219db5e43c4f030a1c9c4a2f28249fd89b05ea0d)
- Make OpenSSL command-line program support `--version` and its synonyms, allowing direct version information queries like the already supported `--help`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/831ef5347253a9381c2ab6bd3ca74cbe10995939)
- Supplement OpenVMS IA64 porting notes, recording relevant information about this platform port. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d500f04400d0acc83fe5270da860764a7d19deee)
- Add CPUID and related source files required by other architectures to FIPS module source list, ensuring generated modules on these platforms include corresponding implementations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c509c040223aebd2a681fb64b60177c3c21f76d1)
- Add OpenSSL management committee PGP public key fingerprints to fingerprint documentation for users to verify release signing identity. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f925bfebbb287321133b9251e72bee869a0f58b4)
- Adjust QUIC multi-threading tests to support concurrent execution, ensuring tests cover multi-threaded access without relying on serial execution. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a350db7318cba3566014ac02a5caa5f4884f00ba)
- Add basic QUIC multi-threading tests verifying basic behavior when multiple threads concurrently use connections and streams. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/274bb489cb800552cdf6d15ef5e7481551c14544)
- Add stress testing and repeated execution capabilities to QUIC multi-threading tests to repeatedly trigger race conditions in concurrent stream operations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fca44cfc1c930afab94fac08ad5a3a303f1724fe)
- Add QUIC multi-threading test coverage for `MAX_STREAMS`, verifying enforcement and updates of stream count limits during concurrent send/receive. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0554f723c1b13f66e56d892b332ccd36aee498ad)
- Add no-thread builds to CI, continuously verifying OpenSSL can still build when thread support is disabled. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d4cb369f2dae284fa30df7d84616c150f8f03a01)
- Add definition of QCTX to QUIC glossary, unifying the meaning of this context object in design documents. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2b5427027b74d6ea14638c25c1efe028a99aaf54)
- Add QUIC builds to thread sanitizer CI to automatically detect concurrent data races. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5cccc0afdb67a77f9c6eeeba2140748782c011c9)
- Add tests verifying QUIC tracing functionality can report protocol activity. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2e1da9693a7de72643acdf3da4816c4edf96ca29)
- Add `app_conf_try_string()` and switch application configuration reads to use this helper function, reducing repetitive and error-prone optional string reading logic. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/da7f81d39308f9ecab6fde1f9116ff673ef3f3b3)
- Add `app_conf_try_number()` and unify optional numeric reads in application configuration, avoiding missing optional items being misreported as parsing failures. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b77826877be3bdd56e3e86887cb78ea010db90be)
- Add PEM fuzzer, continuously providing mutated inputs to PEM decoding paths to discover parsing defects. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bc07d371865095643ec4f7190f26b174830a2f02)
- Add explanation of related issues next to X.509 signature helper code, recording signature behavior and compatibility background. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/586b5407d6138ce23416d4101168ab0c2b1651b6)
- Introduce dedicated syntax for script module dependencies for `build.info`, enabling build system to correctly express dependencies between generated scripts. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b684ee2ce4bbf2c877d2cdc39e095d52ea3fe2a3)
- Add OpenSSL 3.0.9 to FIPS release version list to execute FIPS-related processes for that version. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/247f307f7201b5cf7ebfc17758f4cc7ffae14536)
- Extend regular expression rules for `find-doc-nits` so documentation checking tools can recognize new `OPT_INFORM` parameter forms. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0ef024a492212b6cac30d62485a3b29632af2ee2)
- Add minimal blocking TLS client example demonstrating how applications establish TLS connections and exchange data via synchronous I/O. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a5a0c6a372d2cb9fbcec627e5a338c6f79aa0b16)
- Add tutorial for writing blocking TLS clients, explaining basic steps from context creation to handshake and data reading/writing. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f7b04ae8b4f58b3ce9b0562565f5fffa7a1c87e7)
- Add `no-http` configuration alias allowing builders to explicitly disable HTTP support. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6b1f763c698cd9967250dacb1aadca6a6a9e9afe)
- Add TLS 1.3 tests verifying HelloRetryRequest does not request invalid groups, ensuring illegal group negotiation is correctly rejected. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/adf33f9e268b17ec1b4739707abb40b03b21ea6a)
- Add decoder fuzzer, continuously inputting mutated samples to encoded data decoding paths to discover parsing defects. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a9e6100bc98439ca787aa1fce541550ad1ff3e84)
- Add corrupted PKCS#12 file import tests, verifying password prompts only appear when MAC verification is needed. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1a27cc3626bd15f8fd9a26a2dbc59a681d505321)
- Add explanation on how to read OpenSSL error stack, helping callers obtain detailed error reasons after operation failures. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a7c54dde5189f11c046f638e5aaf2004aee34202)
- Add tests for SM2-based `pkeyutl` encryption and decryption, verifying end-to-end support of the algorithm in command-line tools. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/810f7dc1c7cc5441097b398f753e33652848a4cc)
- Add FFDHE group coverage to SSL new connection tests, verifying finite field Diffie–Hellman parameter negotiation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2c59d54cd7cb741c4547311ca1b8479e08dce0b7)
- Add FAQ and answers to QUIC API overview, and revise details in interface descriptions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/88e3a640d90943db051bea427afbf86eec79e80e)
- Add SSL API classification list to QUIC API design document, distinguishing interfaces applicable to connections, streams, and ordinary TLS. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b7956859cca7f4fe9dfed8e319da1bf5112fe206)

- Add non-blocking QUIC client example demonstrating event-loop-driven connection and data sending/receiving. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/23fe02e59785f0cfaa6d50aa3fa0d82bffe22a8d)
- Add tutorial for writing blocking QUIC clients, explaining synchronous connection, handshake, and data exchange call flows. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9f0cc5d09a89731f1cd9b111f12aa3ac9667b6a0)
- Include newly added OIDs in fuzzing samples, covering their encoding and decoding paths. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/58cd83f83cb0fb4c0eaf97aef1c65996c0936a7d)
- Add GCC 13 to CI toolchain tests, checking build compatibility under new compilers. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5d16169964b66ddedd078ce0bc959bdb92a62827)

- Fix spelling errors in documentation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6a2b8269a8fb31a0c04b5f4f1ac1074c53319135)
- Fix spelling errors in demo examples. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4409e1522f026defaf326a65c0887dfd31ca4e13)

- Add QUIC multi-stream error handling tests, verifying that errors on one stream do not incorrectly affect other streams. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0345cac6d29da328739e8b06b02260b63d4a91e9)
- Add QUIC SSL BIO tests, verifying binding and I/O behavior of QUIC SSL objects with datagram BIOs. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0a3733babbbb4e297ccfbc3ece29e95cafca5f2d)
- Fix documentation and add usage tips for encapsulate/decapsulate APIs, explaining parameters and output handling for EVP_PKEY encapsulate/decapsulate. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5be8233d2be306a2906d3da16e59aa15a4559dd2)

- Add AES-SIV empty associated data test case, verifying zero-length AAD items are not silently ignored. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3993bb0c0c87e3ed0ab4274e4688aa814e164cfc)
- In CMP application and API documentation, explain that server authentication should use critical flag when receiving trust anchor certificates, reminding callers to verify server identity. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1a9e28607e29a1dc996024f03f445ca67b49a44f)
- Added tests for retries during handshake, verifying that non-blocking handshake can correctly continue after WANT_READ/WANT_WRITE. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/149c4f98168ba19432986e82d30d15bd41bae475)
- Documented the `salt` and `info` parameters in the `openssl kdf` command documentation, and improved related command option descriptions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7b2a3a1e9d5246fb0f2935f152d0daec715f79f9)
- Removed duplicate macro definitions and added comments to avoid maintenance ambiguity caused by repeated macro value declarations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/157c40e4d0a2c901e2dc0ee29b80a079a548d008)
- Added regression test for CVE-2023-3446, covering inputs that cause abnormal DH parameter check time. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ede782b4c8868d1f09c9cd237f82b6f35b7dba8b)
- Added boundary cases for the QUIC send path, verifying packet construction handling under extreme length conditions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/833840be9784205691105e197d71529ed0ddfdc4)

- Added test for DH parameters where q equals p+1, verifying that this boundary input is correctly rejected by `DH_check()`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ad5d35572695d7b5748b2bd4fb1afaa189b29e28)

- Added `PEM_read_bio_Parameters()` test, verifying that reading parameter files does not trigger password prompts and parameters are parsed correctly. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/df3d609030bdb0868d1ccca14227bb6829ad954c)
- Added QUIC backpressure test, verifying that when the receiver's read buffer is limited, the sender and stream processing pause and resume correctly. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a1c87f64dd6d6b0f1c8b276dc415f69e1102f930)
- Added blocking mode support for qtest shutdown, so connection close tests also cover synchronous waiting behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c9fb65b8c8b82a8aa60a118342ec4ee58352db89)

- Added build target for Linux x86 using `-latomic`, supporting toolchains that require atomic runtime libraries. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e1b6ecbab41514f45e9c355d01400691f74041c4)

- Re-enabled previously accidentally disabled QUIC send path tests, restoring corresponding regression coverage. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ec2b45f59bda565bc1b8525ab8d508325dc612de)

- Added `-trace` option to `quicserver`, allowing users to enable QUIC communication tracing from the command line. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f430713c8c5e579b513ffa16133b8c178978c5b6)
- Added preliminary guide for writing QUIC multi-stream clients, introducing basic call structure for connections and multiple streams. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/344ae0f39afa0b4cc352eac64d6e7d25bb37e9bd)
- Added QUIC multi-stream client example, demonstrating creating and handling multiple data streams on the same connection. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/584140fa4b0a037c85b58722a08ba6fd0ee086ce)

- Added explanatory comments to examples, helping readers understand the purpose of connection and stream operations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8d74a1316025a4730f18674ccf187c3d630d7c92)
- Linked to QUIC multi-stream client tutorial from OpenSSL guide homepage, allowing users to directly find multi-stream example documentation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/675481ee7a8a7fe55aa387b1ccc4ba3b579bc2ff)
- Added in-place encryption test option for `evp_test`, verifying cipher behavior when input and output buffers overlap. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d57d0b818935c20a7b468c0e717773ea8a3373e6)
- Added Ed25519 signature example, demonstrating generating Ed25519 keys and using EVP interfaces to sign and verify data. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b544c72f3755c0ea51408d3118821a1ac126c070)
- Added thread-assisted version baseline for non-blocking QUIC connection example, for later comparison with implementations driving QUIC via background threads. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e1d0c930c1a7913a2d67c3b8426cd6376d26bd46)
- Added QUIC API manual page, completing command reference documentation for public connection and stream interfaces. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4426c47d662768b0f087c9099b76cabef4c1f540)
- Added tests verifying that QUIC non-I/O retry errors are correctly returned and interpreted. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/48724e8a205c732705c3f54a3bd43d7049e77774)
- Added `saltlen` option to `openssl pkcs8`, allowing users to control the salt length used for PBES2 derived keys. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9f679bdc71aac83e89cc5aacb42855f3657ace39)
- Added `saltlen` option to `openssl enc`, enabling command-line encryption to set KDF salt length. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e3994583a1e4bde9a589c379520d216bc0a0c515)
- Provided fallback definition for `socklen_t` on VMS, allowing systems without this type to still compile network code. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3ae4686bf6cfb9889efeecbc8e80b279afbe1e28)
- Added fallback definitions for `UINT32_C` and `UINT64_C` in internal numeric header files, compatible with platform compilers that do not provide these macros. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/09a4b4b72204f973804d60096c513f5ed6b39e4f)
- Added comments to busy-wait strategy in QUIC multi-stream tests, explaining its waiting behavior and test purpose. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/025535ecd11bdebd8eb28ed4f0f6b509b1b54577)
- Adjusted decoding key format types to represent and use key decoding implementations provided by ENGINE. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b9a189ce87fde1de4bf691031624538262f005c5)
- Added regression test covering issue #21986, verifying that CMS control failure paths do not continue processing invalid states. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c870a465864259f0bdec0953ad085da31941bc27)
- Added non-blocking TLS client example, demonstrating how to continue driving handshake and data read/write when encountering WANT_READ/WANT_WRITE. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0295364548bbab92b7002451c432489b55229ab8)
- Added non-blocking QUIC client example, showing QUIC connection and stream I/O based on poll/event loop. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b71784f741d5a90712607f57a45912292fba9573)
- Added guide for writing non-blocking TLS clients, explaining retry states, polling, and handshake/read/write steps. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5bd2f66a848049d34fe5852e68b67e6c4e06b524)
- Added guide for writing non-blocking QUIC clients, explaining how QUIC event handling and underlying network I/O cooperate. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e8a5b06bdc280355f5c6703849868708ba83454c)
- Added `BIO_closesocket()` to demo exit paths, ensuring examples close opened sockets. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cdedecd50351a3624b074e6a425d8dfb3af5fa6a)
- Enabled C99 extensions for NonStop configuration, allowing `uintptr_t` to be used in that platform configuration. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e9f8e92645361e3596ccc43fa7cbca5245492cd2)
- Added tests for `SSL_CIPHER_find()` used with QUIC SSL objects, verifying cipher suite query paths. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9912dfb98c9c2b10c83c5ca4b5136232568ad664)
- Deferred support for further copying cipher contexts, avoiding exposing or using not-yet-fully-implemented copy capabilities. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/19937db0f2769bc9e4882b476901e446eaadb384)
- Added tests verifying that `OSSL_STORE_open_ex()` uses password callbacks to check passwords. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/64129008fb822758778f7dd29cec6a0a4582e4d2)
- Added provider documentation for new `OSSL_STORE_open_ex()` password callback parameters. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e40d538ad72c8e496b1dfe7d93c6002ce48351f5)
- Added function documentation for `SSL_CONF_CTX_finish()`, explaining when to call it to complete SSL configuration parsing. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/955c133ccccd2b6e3f5a1b1342045111fe8b3e86)
- Allowed setting test random seed without changing test execution order, making failures reproducible without disturbing test ordering. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/44fbe0de34137c7834dc81c1116d7538a2b4f773)
- Added provider selection command-line option for `evp_test`, allowing tests to specify which provider to fetch algorithms from. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1bebf4b0417303895a6cc350da97beb0d1534b60)
- Added attribute query command-line option for `evp_test`, allowing tests to filter provider algorithms by specified propquery. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f34878d846de43a6f760e506f440b5fef85afba6)
- Added ability to actively drop datagrams for noisy datagram BIO, to test protocol recovery behavior under packet loss. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c6bb25fab062738d22bea38462d14bd1c7de22e5)
- Used `test_random()` to generate controllable random perturbations for noisy datagram BIO, making network noise repeatable and covering more combinations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d3a8daca587157dda52991448258800e9cf1f657)
- Added optional debug output for noisy datagram BIO, facilitating viewing of dropped and passed datagram test behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fbfcc3fe3458c50a11aa414a1e499a2eee0eb2e9)
- Added timeout support for QUIC test library, allowing network operations to set deadlines and end waiting after timeout. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a2026db2643db1e34cc25960e9ef74d08bcdee5a)
- Added packet splitting BIO, splitting one datagram into multiple fragments for output, to test QUIC handling of fragmentation and incomplete input. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/35bd8a60043bde500f777e465530076524d2534a)
- Added client-side tracing switch for QUIC test library, allowing recording of client connection and transmission processes for diagnostics. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8d8c0a901e5d65d68070fbe812d7e8c1449381e1)
- Added public API documentation for `BIO_ADDR_copy()`, explaining usage for copying source and destination addresses. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e55843ab50acde02cac8821d4552db9cba2d2e14)
- Added `BIO_ADDR_copy()` test, verifying that IPv4/IPv6 address contents can be fully copied. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/30224a248495ad604a06b8977fa3aa1cc75b9d0d)
- Added certificate chain verification cases, covering EC certificates signed with SHA-3 digest. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fd27a7e4f829e6a9b0d240efcabc39a971b8f511)
- Output CPU information before running `make test` in CI, preserving hardware environment for failure diagnosis. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2d374e1c665a79af6e0939afe37fcc657af91357)
- Also run QUIC `SSL_trace()` tests when zlib is enabled, covering compressed builds combined with protocol tracing. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d2751ee3932e72b848c22ee2ebddce2e9c93a7ed)
- Added PEM public header file for `d2i_PKCS8PrivateKey()`, ensuring callers can access function prototypes after including relevant declarations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cda2e7c914bffc904e3ee79f360953fd370e19e9)
- Recorded functional changes in `SSL_CTX_set_msg_callback()` history documentation, helping users understand compatibility evolution. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/74f8d9ce081a512777ae1f4abe015b7fd1a4a686)
- Added QUIC header bit flip tests, verifying that protocol parsing detects and rejects illegal packets when corrupted or reserved bits are modified. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6dfc57f8a901f2cb40664a9f2060a91943a7982c)
- Complete the check of the return value on failure in `test_provider_ex()`, so that when provider test initialization fails, it stops promptly. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/50b3c47b65e47a4f52ed1c47a0f248beb890193e)
- Check the return status of `RAND_bytes()`, and on random number acquisition failure, return an error instead of continuing to use uninitialized bytes. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8d120aef951d7bb7deac0b8b559f8003f5ea6384)
- Add tests for non-default configuration options in Windows CI, verifying that build switches can be combined as expected. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/10767fd9db14b6eedfb0827f9e404c0d4b94424b)
- Check for null pointers in CMP tear_down tests to avoid dereferencing uncreated objects during cleanup. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/91a5c0e40cf272d18b65c9e4c9a0268f244758a8)
- Supplement the `CMS_add1_signer()` documentation with an explanation of the return value of `CMS_SignerInfo_sign()`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2c581eb2fd92e997dfe0761b086eb690271cefc2)
- Add regression tests to confirm that legacy RSA keys can continue to be used. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e62097f48c3d0b8b61ca6a061b8098b0086b3fbc)
- Add a QUIC HTTP/3 client example based on nghttp3, demonstrating sending HTTP/3 requests over a QUIC connection. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e33af8000f2e2f4700d9845dce5bbcad9bf77c92)
- Add comments to the HTTP/3 example explaining the call relationship between nghttp3 and the QUIC transport. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/47f8cfead0fbacc9151e4ade72bc00427eaf62b7)
- Add copyright notices to the new examples. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/444d18fc7a3d2ddaef9ed51d90cad06a97af4939)
- Add a link to the nghttp3 project in the HTTP/3 example documentation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/10c04246be386b88fb98129b43b454ebf2d633e6)
- Set SNI for the HTTP/3 example so that the handshake uses the target server hostname. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f92d4a07ccdcce9c446db26e8ad01e880f007f39)
- Add tests for converting OSSL_TIME to `timeval`, covering rounding boundaries. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/039119a0f3eeb610689f21834ea04cc1f0efe8df)
- Add a QUIC client fuzzer covering client handshake and packet parsing paths. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ee7729ed4cfcfb95a3fc0aaa184ed624f3fb7eaa)
- Document in the fuzz README the C++ standard library required to build the fuzzer. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3714a735acba3a0b3c18259950fc80e9940a3e3d)
- Add a test recipe for the QUIC client fuzzer to include it in automated testing. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c327ebbe86c2f98f2204a54f33e21eef2e5de480)
- Document the newly added ERR_STATE save and restore functions in the historical documentation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b13f3f1aa709b6c31136b48af51ed22c30f82cb2)
- Add a `OSSL_ERR_STATE_save_to_mark()` test verifying that the error stack can be saved and restored to a marker. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d3bb8fe73df16e5a96dc94f1ab770b35b6694931)
- Update the fuzz corpus directory to include the QUIC client test subdirectory. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/49f5d95c052130882842a9df2b988ae6303c14bd)
- Skip QUIC record, API, and fault tests that do not support fuzzing mode in the fuzz build. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fac61ea4618c83826b51aebf03cbc2bc3ac7b8c8)
- Add a CVE-2023-5363 regression test covering related EVP parameter configuration and encryption behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a2fe10ca39f9a8e251b98bc03c3b1bbb6ad1496f)
- Add a QUIC README outlining QUIC build, API, and example entry points. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/514430c3747292edf864bf9d60b54dc3fed02685)
- Add a test for retries during application data sending, verifying that data is complete and not duplicated after recovery. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/dbbdb940d421daca4a65e765b5244bde6aed3f61)
- Add type casts to SSL API tests to fix type errors on certain compilers. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/74efc5477c8a78ca80187a1332e5b89ce5ed6c16)
- Add a link to the demos subdirectory in the README for easy example discovery. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5e3735b60082794e4c389990bb4c3f2aa121dd8b)
- Add an HTTP/3 demo entry to the example index. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a214e06640ce5d889a688ef4df0282a07a491ac3)
- Add a standalone README for guide examples explaining how to build and run them. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/056477860c0da50d6723e324214a3a9cfbae29ad)
- Add tests for uniform random number generation, checking range boundaries and interface behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2bdf45d875234a9b203a60e2143e4fe977ec5ff9)
- Add stream creation and stream data handling to the QUIC client fuzzer. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5d726f9392475205e7c8bc99e8f7603aa093d0ce)
- Add negative tests verifying that invalid states after IV length changes are rejected. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/de46fe6f3386f2a1c31fc124784825c97c9ca6e9)
- Add negative tests verifying that changing the key length without setting a new key fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/57fc9992584a7b9bede4b5eb40a1074f7e7e0df4)
- Add a unit regression test for priority queue issue #22644. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2f563dc3efa7ece9197aaf44cf099bf516ac7421)
- Add locks for multi-threaded access to `fake_now` to eliminate data races. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d025b228fe4c05d4307d245cc888d881e5555858)
- Fix the NonStop configuration so that the SPT thread model can run QUIC tests. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e3c38759608bb48d70ddfdaa1c88bcd86069952d)
- Add a link to the OpenSSL 3.2 manual page in the README. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1b83adc065130fcea913b4f7b1e13176d4aa1074)
- Add tests for the flow control consumed parameter, verifying that credit is correctly restored after application reads. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/50c56768e184f1c0559d1c88d09d5db001221f28)
- Extend the example to support IPv4 and IPv6 QUIC connections. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3b866985ba8a85b85034eb01d6ad286db678bb13)

### Engine Plugin Framework

- Add a common OSSL_STACK_OF_X509_free() helper function to simplify unified release of X509 stack objects. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/79b2a2f2eedb9d6b24a3f6748332328cf54568fb)
- Add a test for ticket key callback returning 0, verifying that applications can choose not to generate session tickets. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/79c8dcf3985a7b75eac8e53eb8652728af6c5d3d)
- Write AF_ALG engine errors to the OpenSSL error queue for easier diagnosis of Linux kernel crypto interface failures. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bd19999b396d03d39eab4a86c6402a970191c9e1)
- Add a regression test for AES-256-CTR engine crashes, covering failure scenarios when the engine is used alongside Provider/EVP contexts. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bd363ef32403d58a8b41553b5abd602b30073b10)

## Routine Changelog

### Bug Fixes

- Fix compilation errors of OpenSSL on Debian kFreeBSD amd64 and i386 kernel configurations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3a1fa0116a92235ba200228e4bb60d6a3a7f4113)
- Fix indentation of the tls13_hkdf_expand parameter list to conform to code formatting. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1b9e467887d7852d79270c73cb88383c50460b0a)
- Adjust the random number generator so that public and private DRBGs no longer call derivation functions to generate random output. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/505d44c623c2a883cf015f26a499842cea0161f0)
- Add tests for the DANE cross-certificate fix, verifying certificate chain selection behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/305c77aa8211beefe9c4081a8ffea4280c9765fc)
- Fix a double free in EVP_PKEY_CTX_dup() caused by freeing the same context twice. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/85407b77543a2d4330dbb40f6b8520ea0894a716)
- Fix a reference counting error in the DH dupctx path, ensuring that duplicated DH contexts correctly manage object lifetimes. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/21a0d9f3edda78d27d12cd7704de9e32976393ba)
- Fix several tests that fail on VMS platforms, making test expectations compatible with that platform's behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3dd74e21fa04c7d8d7793b25b9a4609625e314cd)
- Fix the FIPS module loading test to use file paths, locating module files in the build directory via bldtop_file. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c6ee5d5b42e27b407dfc1fc8845e08c5a75e2221)
- Fix dh_cmp_parameters() to correctly compare DH parameters and identify parameter differences. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cf1a231d44db81f8565ecae5498a4f1f6f0168c9)
- Fix errors in the descrip.mms template used for VMS builds. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2f9ded524c2c95ab4efcc12b14e098eb4613d2f5)
- Fix the information output of openssl speed so that performance test result descriptions display correctly. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9e1b6f3cdc9258b6759d00cd23819925c9e4c391)
- Fix descriptions and diagnostic messages for options such as -key and -in in the x509 and req commands, clarifying option meanings and improving error messages. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/611ef4f3737cc5812bdefe381403fdf1bacfba06)
- Fix the nc_email check for ASN.1 strings to correctly handle cases where the string contains NULL bytes in the middle. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/485d0790ac1a29a0d4e7391d804810d485890376)
- Fix uplink-related build file templates so that the generated configuration correctly preserves uplink settings. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6d56fcd86a75c6c3b061fc69bc2b3b100ebac24b)
- Fix the handling logic in test_cmp_http.t when the HTTP proxy string is empty, avoiding test failures due to empty proxy configuration. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1ed3249f253e4490a813279e2eb253c8e5cfaabb)
- Fix the parameter type of gf_serialize() so that the serialization function signature matches the actual data type passed in. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a7f58bdc1abe245dd09790e8f97d91df271578f4)
- Fix the path handling in mkpod2html.pl when calling pod2html, using absolute paths to locate input files. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/dc18f036f161e1e49e1d001046716c77d1699e70)
- Adjust related tests to match expected results after the default output format change of -nameopt. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7e399f03829aad161b52b9c433b8d349c5922739)
- Fix a potential double free when PRNG seed acquisition fails, avoiding double free on the error path of random number initialization. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/52dcc011191ad1a40fd52ae92ef009309deaca52)
- Fix the RAND error handling path that frees an incorrect pointer, avoiding freeing an improperly initialized address when seed setting fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/caf569a5b3271c2860732ee44509f3825a179fd5)
- Change the TLS RC4 suite strength check to be based on suite data configuration, avoiding inconsistency between hardcoded strength information and suite definitions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c3b5fa4ab7d19e35311a21fec3ebc0a333c352b6)
- Remove the unnecessary dependency on the NDEBUG macro in cmp_client_test.c so that tests are not affected by assertion compilation switches. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/574246ae02a206b49957b63b0d4f53992e855e13)
- Fix the return value of BIO_free() so that success and failure states are reported according to the interface contract. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d8f6c533cfcbcad350c9cfb2c112eb9f938ba83c)
- Fix the issue where BIO_ctrl() incorrectly generates an error queue record when receiving a NULL BIO parameter. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/398ae8231650c4bd8ddff0e5efd38233c23b1ca0)
- Fix the handling of the BIO_NOCLOSE flag in ssl_free() so that SSL respects the setting of not closing the underlying BIO when freeing it. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/dce910af3bb135bd6d7c5a4cc512043b3ad4acc1)
- Fix a leak in init_client() in apps/lib/s_socket.c where hostname memory was not freed. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2342d9b650ed3dafd65b7edadbe805e04a4966ba)
- Fix errors in the dynamic async engine (dasync) and restore its async cipher operation path. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/59cd0bc1364b5ea817af7f6d36df89c93610cdb5)
- Fix a memory leak in the AFALG engine by releasing algorithm context resources that are no longer used. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6f6a5e0c7c41b6b3639e51f435cd98bb3ae061bc)
- Fix a crash in s_socket.c that could occur when continuing to access via a NULL host or address pointer. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e8655e16cab9cd14ebfe9f2214c2f2aa39c67a26)
- Adjust the self-signed certificate check order in the x509 command to determine whether the certificate is self-signed before setting the issuer name. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/18e0c544b01ed61e7eab61a6cd187c2f4eaa78bd)
- Strengthen the result check of BIO_get_md_ctx() to avoid dereferencing pointers when the BIO or digest context is invalid. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/59a3e7b29574ff45f62e825f6e9923f45060f142)
- Return an error when duplicate names are found during attribute parsing, preventing later-defined attributes from silently overriding same-named entries. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8e61832ed7f59c15da003aa86aeaa4e5f44df711)
- Check the return value of EVP_PKEY_fromdata_init() to avoid using an uninitialized key context when initialization fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5e199c356d09aca3b625b5ea16966b36d24b0201)
- Check the return value of EVP_PKEY_fromdata() to avoid continuing to use an invalid result after key import fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d11cab47810715ba472070300b180944a1d93633)
- Fix a memory leak in ctrl_params_translate() where BN_CTX was not freed. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/922422119df1f6aabd2a15e6e4108d98b6143adf)
- Fix potential memory leaks in cmp_vfy.c and encoder_lib.c where BIOs were not freed on error paths. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/374d5cf2f6b8bdf87c04b5e293a7d291f2c23203)
- Adjust the symbol existence test to accept shared libraries with stripped debug symbols. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a85b4de6a6cbe03c46219d4b1c3b2828ca3fd51c)
- Fix a memory leak reported by CIFuzz by releasing resources left on the failure path. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/74b485848a608383d8d37c04480821ea7b613110)
- Fix another memory leak reported by CIFuzz, ensuring the corresponding object is freed on the error path. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/19b30f1c596a8df2a522f9d6dfc1c1782790fc78)
- Adjust the symbol existence test to ignore version information attached to library symbols before matching. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/73970cb91fdf8e7b4b434d479b875a47a0aa0dbc)
- Fix a potential resource leak in ssl_old_test.c by cleaning up objects not freed at the end of the test. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/34563be5368fb8e6ade7d06d8376522ba83cd6ac)
- Return an error when an invalid digest algorithm is specified with SM2, avoiding continuing with unsupported signing operations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d5d95daba59adc41ab60ea86acd513f255fca3c0)
- Fix a lock leak in evp_keymgmt_util_export_to_provider() by releasing the acquired lock on the export failure path. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fb0f65fff831d9294e34b6ef6f579c157db54b04)
- Fix undefined behavior in bss_mem.c where pointer arithmetic was performed on a NULL pointer. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a98b26588b683eb024ab81f3bb3549c43acd5188)
- Check the length when the application passes a buffer that is too small and prevent integer overflow to avoid writing out of bounds. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/43da9a14f0e73f42f28ae34219929b44df5d1a11)
- Fix SSKDF incorrectly reporting capacity for a buffer with insufficient MAC output length. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7be8ba546267787c1b0df8a4fddaf9cb29944cbb)
- Fix errors in the FIPS module signature self-test so that self-test failure and signature verification status are handled correctly. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/61adb6cf950b65a7bfce9a8d78a7744dfae9f978)
- Fix the test_CMAC_keygen test to correctly generate and check CMAC keys. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cff7d58eb4c8e0ef43e2fd0b12bc067bd3540e2c)
- Fix the sig_gen signature generation flow in ACVP tests to ensure test inputs and expected results are correct. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1b327433e52c8acd6db0a69bc772d4bd1800a109)
- Fix the PSK server callback in s_server to correctly handle PSK identity and key during DTLS handshake. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8b09a9c76d873f62c2507fa9628a9c96c1d66d5c)
- Fix a double free of memory BIO in OCSP_sendreq_bio() to avoid freeing the same BIO twice at the end of the request. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f99b34957f4173f68d6f19d0d9fac37d797b7e0c)
- Fix errors in the no-cmac build configuration so that builds with CMAC disabled complete correctly. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ef2fb64f9dfde1965cb0b8a5f8765c4f467c1604)
- Adjust X509_dup() to avoid copying the embedded EVP_PKEY object, preventing certificate copies from incorrectly sharing or duplicating key state. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e0c5184a56b6580127b39774f9e4e0f2caef696e)
- Fix an issue in cmp.c where a negative index was dereferenced, and use memcpy to safely copy the corresponding data. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/767db672c429aeb98a68b0e310dea15f1b48eb84)
- Fix test-rand incorrectly returning success when input data is insufficient, and allow obtaining data from the parent test context. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d4dfd983e32b32b633aaa9edec422cc30419c6f7)
- Fix a crash when encoding a public key that lacks a public key value; the error path now returns safely. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6187d9eac2738e873d23c0c91f9769333b1bb6af)
- Fix a leak of the Post-Handshake Authentication digest when saving the digest fails, avoiding leaking the digest context on the error path. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/963eb12dbd551df71d7eb054e095c1b85f4aaab9)
- Fix compilation warnings emitted by GCC 11.2.0 to avoid diagnostics during builds with that toolchain. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/37467b2752f75ce80437120f704452982b7c1998)
- Correct several code style violations that clearly do not conform to the code standards, unifying the related implementation style. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1287dabd0b23326be491125698dd982e4ae28887)
- Correct the return value description in the documentation and synchronously fix the expected error return values in the corresponding tests. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8b7d7789dc4ea0de11331cb4045bcb03ab0864fc)
- Correct the return value check of BN_bn2nativepad() to determine whether the padding conversion succeeded based on the actual interface return status. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/944fcfc69d16dfd20decdd9cd105436f0043dbe0)
- Fix build issues for Armv8 systems without AArch64 subtargets, allowing configurations such as ARM32 to compile correctly. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3841d0f6f02e1ad3a54beabf1d5395bd1c383254)
- Fix a memory leak in tls_parse_stoc_key_share() by releasing resources allocated during server key share parsing. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b3c34401c088dc247b8b54ea812e7cdde6caf361)
- Fix a memory leak in ssl_create_cipher_list() by releasing temporary resources during cipher suite list creation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3a069c1b0b4857b838186aeb55378195dfa50823)
- Fix a crash in fix_dh_rfc5114() caused by dereferencing a NULL pointer when ctx->p2 is NULL. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/09d91264c8ee1fdfcbe41f326a96a21cd85eb732)
- Adjust the test so that a negative return value from EVP_PKEY_fromdata_init() is also considered a failure, covering error return statuses. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/884400d78992d1da1573a3677876b06421b797eb)
- Fix an issue where a NULL pointer could be dereferenced when obtaining a digest; return safely when the digest context is invalid. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ab547fc005307ecf48451638e947cdabca147159)
- Correct the return value check of BN_bn2binpad() to avoid misinterpreting a conversion failure as success. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/098f2627c8d283a518a6e6e60e7893664c7510e0)
- Add return value checks for two issues reported by Coverity, handling paths where object creation or operations fail. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/73a815defe428e42ccc27fdc9d5be507f980278b)
- Fix resource leaks reported by Coverity, ensuring allocated objects are freed on the corresponding error paths. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1b4d9967a24154f1dc00f471eb843203ec7bb7d4)
- Fix a data race in default_DSO_meth initialization, ensuring consistent default DSO methods when multiple threads access concurrently. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e6a10b074e90f1ce3d8e9ae0ca740a835ff29bb9)
- Fix the logic for generating AKID via v2i_AUTHORITY_KEYID() in the x509 command, correctly constructing the authority key identifier extension. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9bf1061c44c81059102cd4749f6078b6ce71da9d)
- Fix the incorrect call to x509_name_canon, ensuring name canonicalization uses the correct function and parameters. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/09235289c377ff998964bb6b074bb2a3ad768fd2)
- Fix the DER encoding implementation for EC and SM2 output structures so that encoders serialize data correctly according to each structure definition. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2d49519210ed60ed16778b4b1097b4c6880541e5)
- Fix the incorrect call to X509_self_signed() to ensure self-signed status is determined via the correct interface. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/64c428c35053a101a452c42d5d0a9a8342493606)
- Fix the use of signature size sigsize in apps/speed.c to avoid allocating or reporting buffers with incorrect lengths during performance tests. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e7414634a59aa61c7917193a31382ced95d40eeb)
- Fix X509 extension parsing so that AKID and SKID handling conforms to the configuration settings for these two extensions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/adbd77f6d7cc4efb7b4bde483036fab8e48ce870)
- Make the x509 code use safe maths helper functions to detect integer overflow, avoiding overflow in certificate length calculations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/87fd67d997b236d1202546345d18384a968c9206)
- Correct the return value check when calling BN_check_prime(), handling prime detection results and error statuses separately. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/680827a15f12c3b37a6335fcb992555cf300730e)
- Fix a race in init_thread_stop() to ensure thread cleanup and Provider initialization state are synchronized. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3b9de0c9aa791bd9e6f0534ec091accbdf15292f)
- Fix the incorrect return value check in RAND_bytes_ex() to determine subsequent flow based on whether random number generation succeeded. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c9007bda79291179ed2df31b3dfd9f1311102847)
- Fix the incomplete return value check in EVP_Cipher() to correctly report cipher operation failures. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6d774732517f1d63b7999c5691fc0bf046023faf)
- Correct the EVP_DigestVerifyFinal() test function and its invocation to ensure the test actually verifies the final signature result. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e2e5e72d5aec4d8d633cc5e9930f762da7973ab6)
- Correct the return value check of EVP_PKEY_paramgen_init() to avoid continuing after parameter generation initialization fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6e0b05f3008a3f22105fd2bed9314b0bfa381f93)
- Correct the return value check of EVP_PKEY_keygen_init() to properly handle key generation initialization failures. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bf4ceeded1497c79e72fba4f9ff15febae58108d)
- Correct the return value check of BIO_read_filename() to handle failures based on the file BIO open status. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e3f0362407f6f40e413d6dcb35888514dbaed6f8)
- Fix the constant expression result issue reported by Coverity to avoid using fixed values in dynamic condition checks. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7cc5738a561933e38ad0e724f4df7b503c3c8e73)
- Adjust the verbosity of CMP client diagnostic output to avoid too little information for locating request failure causes. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/92df52119eb33ea980e8f02f9cdfe194ad6c04e1)
- Increase the verbosity of diagnostic information in the test_cmp_http test server to help locate HTTP CMP test failures. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a6838c8d52087f2b0494bbab8486e10944aff7f7)
- Fix build issues with perlasm/ppc-xlate.pl on macOS, adapting the PPC assembly translation script to that platform. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e67edf60f2e9be6e5f5465b52d01aa26bf715280)
- Fix the return value check of OBJ_obj2txt() to avoid continuing to use the result after object identifier text conversion fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2349d7ba57c9327290df6f7bc18b7f0c3976ca9e)
- Change the error code output in test_errstr.t to hexadecimal to match the actual representation format of error codes. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e7313323cc420e8dca2526578ae8a4a4d4d390be)
- Fix the issue where BIO_gets() did not fully check the return value, correctly distinguishing between read failure and valid read results. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7264068a15e7c4955efa25753430595a45caa16f)
- Fix the return value check of ossl_do_blob_header() to ensure that errors are reported to the caller when Blob header parsing fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/546b9f6b5cf6d0fde60aa37084eec1bb7d0fbc72)
- Fix the return value check of EVP_PKEY_decrypt() to avoid decryption failure being treated as success. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0650ac437b529274aca094c516a5a0127bbaf48c)
- Fix the return value check of TXT_DB_write() to correctly handle database write failures. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/aba9943fef8dcc8416ac9a219c97c616c1fd6344)
- Fix the return value check of asn1_item_embed_d2i() to safely exit when embedded ASN.1 decoding fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7f608e4b1d9473258445144ba66216fb0e63aebe)
- Fix the return value check of EVP_RAND_generate() to correctly handle random number generation operation failures. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a8f4cdd70c9d9ebe4553d7a72c67f73eaf0c169d)
- Fix the return value check of BIO_set_prefix() to report success or failure based on the actual result of setting the prefix. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ac6568ecc6050bc526adc6a7245835fd95d8dfed)
- Fix the return value check of BIO_set_indent() to report success or failure based on the actual result of setting the indent. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a9ed63f1d1d8993a8b30fc978ce09674f97f061d)
- Fix the return value check of SSL_export_keying_material() to ensure that the output is not used when exporting keying material fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/40649e36c4c0c9438f62e1bf2ccb983f6854c662)
- Fix the HTTP server port output and allow runtime dynamic adjustment of diagnostic verbosity level. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4599ea9fe31953c0c50738ed4b91ade76a693356)
- Avoid loading the same dynamic ENGINE repeatedly, preventing engine initialization and resource registration from being executed twice. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e2571e02d2b0cd83ed1c79d384fe941f27e603c0)
- Make OSSL_PARAM_BLD_push_BN() and OSSL_PARAM_BLD_push_BN_pad() return an error for negative numbers, clarifying that the parameter builder only accepts non-negative numbers. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/db65eabefe76e44818ff8bd19c68990e7dcc70d3)
- Fix CPU feature detection for ARMv7 and ARM64 on FreeBSD, correctly identifying hardware capabilities for both ARM targets. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c1dabe26e3e96cdce0ffc929e9677840ad089ba5)
- Make x509 return a non-zero exit code when IP, email, or hostname checks fail, enabling scripts to reliably determine verification errors. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9567fd3819398c6be70508dd6316046da0955b71)
- Make OSSL_PARAM_allocate_from_text() return an error when encountering unexpected negative numbers, avoiding silent conversion of illegal negative values. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8585b5bc62d0bf394ca6adf24f8590e9b9b18402)
- Check the return value of OPENSSL_strdup() to avoid illegal memory access after string allocation failure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/dc7e42c6a12637bae1660561d3f4cef039001475)
- Check the return value of CRYPTO_strdup() to avoid accessing unallocated memory after string duplication failure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b9648f31a4917b8594caebda3e6d8d313514fe24)
- Fix the issue where EVP_PKEY_CTX_get_rsa_pss_saltlen() did not set the output value, so callers can obtain the actual PSS salt length. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6f87463b62f9b2849510d74ff0fd6a62955ea947)
- Fix the diagnostic output and return code of parse_http_line1() on parsing failure, so HTTP status line errors can be correctly identified. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e2b7dc353b353efccd1d228f743baa7c2d2f9f49)
- Fix OSSL_HTTP_REQ_CTX_nbio() parsing of non-200 status responses, so non-successful HTTP responses also correctly return status information. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/38288f424faa0cf61bd705c497bb1a1657611da1)
- Fix the password query flow of the PVK encoder to obtain the encryption password through the correct password callback. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/baa88d9d170b95fd6f177b3e5f8d8818e024a55d)
- Fix the off-by-one description of the returned string length in the OBJ_obj2txt() documentation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/67890a738c0eb5e92c41189ba3c744fbc98a97ac)
- Fix the check for whether the port option exists in OSSL_HTTP_set1_request() and update the documentation accordingly. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/266383b44c4ebce5ddf551547e73ab6eec47805b)
- Fix the issue where set0_tmp_dh_pkey() incorrectly freed the EVP_PKEY on error, ensuring the borrowed key remains owned by the caller. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e819b5727312477f8c1f56bf928e611ad7e78315)
- Fix the Markdown links in SUPPORT.md so that links in the support page navigate correctly. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3410f1045af1913c89f5dc06ad4998a60e57fd90)
- Fix a memory leak in OSSL_CMP_MSG_read() when file reading fails due to unreleased resources. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d580c2790f9f304533a3eda2a9cf6b8eb22830c3)
- Fix the -rspin option of the cmp command so it works even when -reqin is not provided. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7ee0954a086ee3b4e0a8c6736600e3d6362485c0)
- Fix column alignment errors in the CSV file of the CMP HTTP test command. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5adda344c2268eced63151a62358ffaefbdfed50)
- Fix timeout handling in OSSL_HTTP_get() for redirect responses, avoiding failure to use the correct wait time after redirects. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f0d5a3b6ea1bbe4e5dac5b69d853c015db635621)
- Fix the behavior of cmp command options in builds with sockets disabled; network options like -server are no longer accepted in such builds. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/83b424c3f60a4401fa3e6e41ff7f08e85ee9df94)
- Remove unreachable dead code branches identified by Coverity, cleaning up invalid implementations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f0fc3c10d0617821a476b34aba1ee77d47a2a64a)
- Fix spelling errors of OpenSSL in the source code. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/44fde441937fc8db8ea6a7ac2e7c683ad9d5f8e0)
- Remove logical dead code identified by Coverity, avoiding retaining branches that are never executed. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/23effeb81fbcdc436b1e871e7fff34456d6bfbaf)
- Fix EVP_PKEY_eq() comparison for strict private keys, so non-exportable private keys can also be compared according to Provider rules. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f3ba62653815b2f7991103cdbea1ac155c8c916a)
- Adjust the OSSL_FUNC_keymgmt_match() implementations of each Provider to match the changed comparison semantics of EVP_PKEY_eq(). [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ee22a3741e3fc27c981e7f7e9bcb8d3342b0c65a)
- Fix boundary handling errors when parsing HTTP response headers in OSSL_HTTP_proxy_connect(). [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2490d10d5cca0163cad8045857248b175bdf83e7)
- Fix carry overflow errors in the MIPS 32-bit bn_sqr_comba4/8 implementation, ensuring correct squaring results. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/336923c0c8d705cb8af5216b29a205662db0d590)
- Fix the handling of program names with version information during VMS installation, making installed program names consistent with version configuration. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bc0ac16417b326abbe295cf359f47922d3b6b05c)
- Fix VMS installation configuration to read pointer_size from the correct target field instead of the wrong config field. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/59cf75435d45a678c81df246c1e8283dc60c4c39)
- Define the OSSL$MODULES logical name in VMS installation to ensure the Provider module directory can be correctly located. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a3a79ab3221a6484e89cd1321402f40395a05178)
- Unify the generation of shared library version file names in VMS installation, using the platform-provided shlib_version_as_filename(). [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/93b670abd104468db4478b79221c9c70613ba2f1)
- Fix the VMS installation cleanup logic to undo the same logical name definitions as during the installation phase. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0df8e71a6e32d0a993530b7f813603da3e7a6c4c)
- Check for the existence of Providers in the VMS installation IVP script to verify that the installation package contains the required modules. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0c5307ef4b1dea6dc2796cf08e84035e3d3a4510)
- Override the openssl logical name in the VMS descrip.mms template to ensure that build and installation invoke the correct program. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1b9fbae6a688a89efd5a26e16f6d648f7fa65b52)
- Fix libssl's handling of certificate verification errors to avoid misinterpreting verification failure status. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c1c1bb7c5e2baa109baec62d2af09d24caae5557)
- Fix the infinite certificate verification loop caused by the has_san_id condition, so name constraint checks can terminate normally. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6894e20b50c1204bfc990093b4e7ccd10f92865d)
- Fix the logic and documentation of mutually exclusive options -server, -use_mock_srv, -port, and -rspin in the CMP command. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a56bb5d64e7599140117f935eeeb34ba94c83aea)
- Fix a use-after-free in ossl_provider_add_to_store() where the Provider object was accessed after being freed. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/33df7cbe5e38feb0cf962386bcac061c3743ecf2)
- Fix the cleanup flow in context_init() error handling to avoid missing or double-releasing context resources on initialization failure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7ca3bf792a4a085e6f2426ad51a41fca4d0b1b8c)
- Fix the issue where dereferencing still occurred after a missing null pointer check. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/68b78dd7e40f57064b0f24728d8b544fe583599c)
- Fix a NULL pointer dereference to safely return on invalid object input. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f050745fe69a538952f3e12af3718d19ef2df2e2)
- Fix the include guard name of crypto/dsa.h to avoid incorrect or conflicting header protection macros. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7db69a35f9d2c7ac8029de11115b18a57d341bf5)
- Fix the post-release script so it does not generate format or version errors. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0d4c52320d245be80bd69346fdda4b12b4961eae)
- Fix the flow of the HTTP client cleaning up TLS BIOs via the bio_update_fn callback, ensuring the correct BIO is released on close. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cdaf072f90399efb9e8e19ee4f387d1425f12274)
- Fix the issue where Configure variable values overflowed into adjacent settings, keeping configuration variable parsing boundaries correct. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a595e3286ae9f033c56452967b3add2145f9085f)
- Fix the misuse of NULL checks for error objects or status judgments, checking return values under the correct conditions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ff7cdc15875293a330831a80d83edbafd25a9d36)
- Fix the handling of shared library variants in symbol existence tests so tests can identify exported symbols for different shlib_variants. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/805bdac5f37bb820658f70269941086bef6c085b)
- Fix compilation errors under no-asm configuration, allowing successful builds even when assembly optimizations are disabled. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a8251a32a0dc449fc39f44a1768e091fcc077227)
- Fix client proxy connection errors caused by the HTTPS proxy callback design issue, ensuring the proxy tunnel is established as expected. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/068549f8db6d792a88bb888118001c4582f79074)
- Fix check-format.pl diagnostics for constants on the left side of comparison or assignment expressions, avoiding incorrect report locations or content. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2e6afe1079c6993868c5d8a813605d16980e8e10)
- Fix a double free in app_http_tls_cb() when TLS is not used, which incorrectly freed the same object. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/97b8c859c64bc60fcf5bb27ed51489c81fde41b3)
- Fix compilation failures when -Wconditional-uninitialized is enabled, initializing variables in relevant conditional paths. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/abc4345a19430869b9a8925c6defc9e9ce977429)
- Make the passwd command release allocated resources before exiting on error, avoiding leaks on error paths. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ea4d16bc60dee53feb71997c1e78379eeb69b7ac)
- Fix EC_KEY conversion handling in i2d_PublicKey() and d2i_PublicKey() when the SM2 key is EVP_PKEY_SM2. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8582dccc4dd1f1667b0e91a098e2cc78c7146dd7)
- Fix typos in the source code. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e304aa87b35fac5ea97c405dd3c21549faa45e78)
- Fix the issue where OSSL_STORE generates extra errors when loading private keys, avoiding pollution of the error queue during normal loading. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/da198adb9c5626f31c52613fe2ae59a7066c3366)
- Correct copyright year issues and unify copyright information in source code and documentation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fd84b9c3e94be1771d1b34ad857081f7693318aa)
- Fix return value checks in five places for EVP_PKEY_CTX_ctrl() to correctly handle control operation failures. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7b1264baab7edd82fea8b27d9ddec048bafc0048)
- Make EVP_PKEY_fromdata() not return the newly allocated key object on import failure, preventing callers from misusing incomplete keys. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5b03b89f7f925384c2768874c95f1af7053fd16f)
- Fix the issue where OSSL_STORE_open_ex() treats file: as an unregistered scheme and adds it to the error queue. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7c64ca71c2ceeb1d47e8499bd351de7d0078ce37)
- Improve diagnostics for file access errors in load_key_certs_crls() so that the command line displays more understandable path error messages. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6e2499474cb96b28a51df1da25cc72f1cf342fad)
- Fix code formatting issues reported by check-format.pl in apps/cmp.c. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fd989c734dc3f9e15d700ff9ced15125a23d4359)
- Improve error reporting for configuration value and option parsing errors in v2i_AUTHORITY_KEYID(), indicating the specific invalid input. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6e98b7f153fcf9dfad1053fbb3a592166837c6fc)
- Fix several error exit paths to ensure correct return status and complete resource cleanup on failure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/10481d33844218694929a7bad57314411a33ab74)
- Fix multiple code formatting issues reported by check-format.pl in apps.c. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8cdb993d8b1ad9fd58fb5f41cc43df97014f00c9)
- Fix check-format.pl's diagnosis of constants on the left side of comparison or assignment expressions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/15ae69fa7bc0f367edded19bc48e6d9a5ce8d547)
- Fix check-format.pl's reporting of missing spaces before plus and minus signs, and allow 1e-6 notation in scientific notation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d45c0e1a5e89f01d83f6059c788524e901a11604)
- Fix check-format.pl's diagnosis of spaces before semicolons and allow spaces after closing parentheses. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c30bc4e2093f47a37736944da548653bc08d774d)
- Fix crypto/bio build failure in UEFI environments, adapting BIO code to UEFI headers and runtime. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/328bf5adf9e23da523d4195db309083aa02403c4)
- Fix resource leak in EVP_DigestInit_ex(), cleaning up the digest initialization failure path. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/357bccc8ba64ec8a5f587b04b5d6b6ca9e8dcbdc)
- Fix algorithm fetching logic for Decoder, Encoder, and Store loaders so that queries correctly match implementations by the specified algorithm. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cd1981a0dc165ab6af5e2945beaaa9efe4484cee)
- Fix pkeyutl's -kdflen regression so that the option correctly sets the KDF output length. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b82fd89d8bae1445c89ec90d1a6145fe3216d2d7)
- Add tests to check that the corresponding error is correctly thrown when parameter conversion fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/291c5b3e39f4c98e61cf7f65056fe49780d1f0ac)
- Fix the use of macros expanding macros in static initialization code, avoiding compiler inability to handle nested macro expressions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9c5d1451292566e546d5dd01c7f19950fa34391d)
- Remove expected error output from test_gendhparam to avoid treating normal test information as failure diagnostics. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3bfb7239daf3d6a89476e163dc925c641d356729)
- Fix evp_keymgmt_util_match(): when the first cross-export attempt fails, continue with a reverse attempt to export the other key to complete the comparison. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/37b850738cbab74413d41033b2a4df1d69e1fa4a)
- Fix memory allocation failure handling in X509_ALGOR_set0(), correctly returning an error and preserving object state on allocation failure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/04bc3c1277b8b20dc29f96933f7be592c0535aa8)
- Fix the KTLS receive offload path in the TLS encryption layer to ensure correct kernel TLS receive state configuration. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d73a7a3a71270aaadb4e4e678ae9bd3cef8b9cbd)
- Make EVP_PKEY_CTX_set_dh_nid() and EVP_PKEY_CTX_set_dhx_rfc5114() correctly return error codes on failure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f58bb2dd00c3004552c5c1e8d0f2c1390c004cf8)
- Fix build failure of bn_ppc.c under AIX XLC and XLCLANG compilers. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cfbb5fcf4424395a1a23751556ea12c56b80b57e)
- Adjust conditional checks in the speed command to avoid generating empty statements, fixing related code analysis issues. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a09a342ffb459d0913954111b7802815e9a3481a)
- Correct the queryable parameter list for DH key exchange in dh_exch.c, returning the parameters actually supported by the implementation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c1167f09d840b109ef1c1c1485e3de64be2fc625)
- Fix indentation formatting in related code. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2c9da416a608e2aaf19c16d920baddf2473c8392)
- Fix SM3SS1 instruction conversion error in sm3-armv8.pl, ensuring the assembler generator outputs the correct instruction sequence. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bca6cc53d4592ccbe78eeede3bc79f09d149603c)
- Fix duplicate BIO_FLAGS macro definitions, ensuring consistent definitions across header files. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e278f18563dd3dd67c00200ee30402f48023c6ef)
- Revert UEFI BIO build fix, undoing previous changes for UEFI. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/619c9bad41d041bab2ac6ba3933d526b48ceee2a)
- Fix C++ extern "C" wrapping in self_test.h so that the header can be correctly included by C++. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1bfd20f08c042072cae44a9eb81626cbfff81116)
- Fix incorrect malloc failure handling in PEM_write_bio_PKCS8PrivateKey(), ensuring allocation failure returns per interface rather than writing to invalid memory. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/59ccb72cd5cec3b4e312853621e12a68dacdbc7e)
- Fix DES EDE ECB implementation reporting incorrect IV length, setting the correct IV parameter according to ECB mode. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d450eb84c802b2f78971f905b251a0fb89ebb7d1)
- Fix the issue in lhash where 32-bit values are right-shifted by 32 bits, avoiding undefined behavior on 32-bit platforms. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2ce0a3d19005271e7e3c351b562d9da93e2d4c80)
- Fix endianness issues in params_api_test, ensuring multi-byte parameters are correctly compared according to platform endianness. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9927749ec2b8fc4b6146f0bd54cb6a44b8295974)
- Change the public key KDF context copy failure test to a success test, verifying that KDFs supporting copying can correctly copy the context. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/43332e405bea83a2d553e0519fdb04170879bc96)
- Fix indentation formatting in related code. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1751356267f64d5db8824cf4ff5b3496e15972da)
- Fix todata/fromdata conversion when EVP_PKEY selects only PUBLIC_KEY, ensuring import and export are consistent under the public key selection flag. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/944f822aadc88b2e25f7695366810c73a53a00c8)
- Correct copyright information in the source code. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9d987de3aabe54e65a55649a61953966f33b070b)
- Fix build issues under the DJGPP toolchain, compatible with the platform's compilation and linking settings. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b9b211fcb6b9068ef1d8729a4971fbe693fd2cde)
- Avoid direct access to the AES key length field, using interfaces to read key configuration to maintain structure encapsulation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/80ce874a093087b919e1c722427df30f81f5dad5)
- Fix typos in the source code. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/07c5465e9855cc485c4a84da8a4251a843bec258)
- Fix counter overflow so that when the count reaches the boundary, it is detected or wraps as expected. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/345c99b6654b8313c792d54f829943068911ddbd)
- Add compatibility handling for platforms lacking SIZE_MAX definition, fixing build failures on systems like z/OS. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/25a0a44dc6223e515f5e91e41798cccf09c5612b)
- Detect whether the OpenSSL runtime used by Engine is different from the current program, avoiding crashes due to cross-runtime objects. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9362a1b32b7330e24d3bca230b412557caea095b)
- Fix enginetest failure when deprecated API is disabled and --api=1.1.1 is specified. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/29af9fba64fd3e4e086808f2360501b463627ea2)
- Fix branch target markers in AArch64 CPU capability detection assembly and Keccak assembly, compatible with systems with BTI enabled. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3a23f01268ec47bf3423b849cc226be220745522)
- Update outdated source code comments to match the current implementation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1cc94e2fa7fd1d5c24ad4cc01f363ff9ba5a4f13)
- Change endianness detection in bn_lib.c to a binary condition check, avoiding reliance on possibly non-existent intermediate states. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/649999dc57419ddd9329f7062b048dee5ecd9306)
- Adjust conditional expressions to avoid irrelevant compiler diagnostics. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b84c6e86dd8ca88444207080808d1d598856041f)
