- Add explicit prefixes to random number test output to prevent test parsers from misinterpreting unrelated "ok" text as test results. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b089d546242bbc073aefb6f6471586e484118863)
- Fix assertions/conditions in sslapitest that cause test failures, making the test pass in expected scenarios. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7e1eda483ec9ead36c05066b45ecad618475544c)
- Rename the strlen parameter from strlength to a name that does not conflict with C++ reserved words, avoiding compilation failures in C++ projects. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/28e141c45d36757e052b72685fb874968f013d43)
- On AArch64 with Clang and BTI enabled, switch to swapcontext for async context switching, avoiding jump exceptions caused by missing BTI markers on setjmp/longjmp return addresses. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d2d2401aed7ff45f4c013201944e1218dce12da7)
- Fix potential null pointer dereference in BN_mod_exp2_mont for certain inputs, and add corresponding protection. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/43135a5d2274c24e97f50e16ce492c22eb717ab2)
- Adjust AArch64 AES-GCM assembly code to be compatible with older assemblers, fixing build failure of aes-gcm-armv8-unroll8_64.S. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2507903eb7c4e3ca4bc1a00074c16b409abc028e)
- Fix test failures when running with Test::Harness, so that related test results are no longer affected by the output conventions of that test framework. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fe537f89cd6b75c10e717a0eb159f33710729b67)
- Write a memory allocation error to the OpenSSL error queue when POSIX async fibre stack allocation fails, aiding callers in diagnosing the failure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/83c48d96ff24728d94e0890f320b0d1220d9cba3)
- When email address extension copy fails, free the constructed X509 email address stack and clear the pointer, avoiding memory leaks or leaving a half-finished state. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/175355923046921a689b500f7a72455f7095708f)
- Fix priority handling when reading default subject, public key, and extensions from CSR and reference certificate in CMP CRMF requests, avoiding default values overriding explicit CSR information. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c8c923454b52d64234c941553d81143918e502ea)
- When cleaning up EVP_MD_CTX, retain the fetched digest implementation to avoid repeated fetching and releasing causing reference count cache contention, improving digest path performance. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c0b7dac66edde45b8da80918f5b5b62d1e766a0c)
- Fix length boundary check in ChaCha20 PowerPC vector path, adjusting the critical value from 256 to 255 to ensure inputs exactly at the boundary take the correct processing path. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7e1f3ffcc5bc15fb9a12b9e3bb202f544c6ed5aa)
- Fix the context copy method of the scrypt Provider so that the copied KDF context retains the correct library context and can run independently. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e04c2c02e8e6b9ec71d93c26c14167ceb2165ce8)
- Fix build compatibility issue of the AES-GCM AVX-512 assembly generator in the MinGW-w64 environment. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/224ea84b4054de105447cde407fa3d39004a563d)
- Fix cached initialization and update of IV length in EVP encryption context to avoid continuing to use the old IV length when the context is reused. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b9a2f24e44f53c7c3a63a7f7b165e8267cbdda42)
- Fix typo in error handling code when EVP method retrieval fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ef9909f3c6471ba39be1e3d18a366044cbf30a19)
- Convert input length arithmetic in EVP encrypt/decrypt update to unsigned size_t before calculation to avoid signed integer overflow. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1832bb0f02e519a48f06a10467c7ce5f7f3feeeb)
- Fix potential infinite loop in BN_mod_sqrt for inputs that do not meet prerequisites such as non-prime modulus, and clarify the constraint that the modulus must be prime. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9eafb53614bf65797db25f467946e735e1b43dc9)
- Fix typo in CCM test vector title. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/930a7bd9128fd5e184c8a60153de5b8a16159b05)
- Unify Camellia-related declarations with actual function signatures, fixing declaration inconsistencies between header files and implementations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a12a71fafbe9b0ce90a51098fbf166d9da62b111)
- Suppress error queue messages generated by expected probing when dynamic engine checks OpenSSL 1.1.x runtime version, to avoid misleading users or polluting subsequent diagnostics. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bd5c91c82cdc4b6ffe4a2970f9512fc5ec7d2d06)
- Fix compilation warning in SSL session printing code on OpenBSD, and ensure the session information output path compiles on the platform. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9362638b080e328ccab43f89048bed27bcf2f11d)
- Initialize temporary point structure in NIST P-256 point multiplication path to avoid uninitialized fields participating in computation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/70cd9a51911e9a4e2f24e29ddd84fa9fcb778b63)
- Fix incorrect comment about round/register handling in AES-GCM AArch64 assembly generation script. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3b5b91992ca25bef8575dbd17d7d3a2ca9557e04)
- Initialize temporary values in elliptic curve computation to eliminate uninitialized reads reported by Coverity. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3e35d3a4808526b9586bb87d423d488cf1b18d95)
- Fix uninitialized reads in related algorithm paths, ensuring local variables are used only after successful assignment. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/09134f183f76539aa1294adfef10fcc694e90267)
- Release allocated resources in error branches pointed out by Coverity to fix resource leaks. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6889ebff01fa8cd7e5905f3f242edfed55fca443)
- Fix uninitialized pointer reads in sparse array related paths, ensuring pointers have definite values before use. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a0238b7ed87998c48b1c92bad7fa82dcbba507f9)
- Add overflow protection at length calculations pointed out by Coverity to avoid integer wraparound causing incorrect buffer sizes. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/81487b65b9eb8148471e729b8c1959521d62c69e)
- Fix documentation formatting and handle doc-nits check failure caused by NOTES-WINDOWS.md. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bd28a23eb120b4fdfd45d18a1f05cd7366ed8058)
- Fix incorrect traversal due to negative loop bounds, no longer entering invalid loops when input is negative. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0bcae9893b99666158dd8b35fb674e6188b0b5c3)
- Refactor storage and destruction logic of OSSL_LIB_CTX global state, removing dependency on CRYPTO_EX_DATA. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/927d0566ded0dff9d6c5abc8a40bb84068446b76)
- Fix integer overflow in parameter or length calculations to avoid size wraparound due to abnormal input. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/766a7d4676f08f815dd5070409e94954f4b64c6c)
- Fix out-of-bounds access in decoding path, ensuring input offset and length are constrained by actual buffer range. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d1592f21c0d4c2c94a8c6004cf7b5cad2dcb2637)
- OSSL_PARAM_get_utf8_string_ptr clears errors generated by type mismatch when trying different string parameter types, avoiding leaving probe failures in the error queue. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/327a720d5dd011b853acbdd0223933f6ecd22928)
- Fix broken link in coding-style.html, pointing to existing code style documentation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/31ff3635371b51c8180838ec228c164aec3774b6)
- Redefine X509_http_nbio macro to avoid indirect inclusion of ocsp.h, reducing circular dependencies between HTTP utility header files. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f593f32eede30ead69e0a16e47a564a664171283)
- Fix build rules on AIX when using -no-shared in Configure, ensuring required targets for static build are correctly generated. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b83c0a900f9303e0c9fd084829b791386d7c57ce)
- Check return value of bn_rshift_fixed_top to avoid continuing to use incomplete results after big number right shift failure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bc6bac8561ead83d6135f376ffcbbb0b657e64fe)
- SSH KDF writes Provider context pointer only after successful allocation when creating context, fixing null pointer access on memory exhaustion. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/148176ca323e3dfce5d5cdb5578c113c8d2440bb)
- Fix null pointer dereference in EVP-related code on allocation failure, returning via error path on failure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5f1424c6bdca8ddb9d5d88a78a1d738be19c4ea8)
- Fix option passing for disabling TLS 1.2 in tests, ensuring -no-tls1_2 takes effect as expected. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/40fb5a4ce3e90c9e8702aad0fcf43eb9f6edf419)
- endecode_test recognizes expected failure of non-FIPS EC keys as normal result, avoiding reporting compliance restrictions as test failures. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e8a4145968eea576788761f39c5e4cb68b7c4a42)
- Fix DTLS server to limit send length according to current maximum fragment value, and treat non-positive write results as send failure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e915c3f5381cd38ebdc1824c3ba9896ea7160103)
- Fix several typos in documentation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1ab8b7cd3bef5ae3bcb516a1c2f2fff4abd63c5b)
- Provider MACs such as CMAC and GMAC reset underlying algorithm context on reinitialization, avoiding retaining previous message state when reused. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c9ddc5af5199909d196ee80ccd7abcff2eb42a34)
- OCSP response construction generates internal error response and exits when creating a single response fails, avoiding continued dereference of null pointer. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4d50a5467b0a208c61d163239a3544bae06343ea)
- Check returned pointer when certificate store creation fails, fixing null pointer dereference. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3f075967f664aac12951a1d7aa3124d9235cd299)
- CMS decoding temporarily stores and clears non-fatal errors generated during parsing library context, avoiding residual error queue after successful decoding. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/45a3c592b94b66cab72e5bffbaf9d810c3fb29c0)
- Fix compilation issue when building AArch64 bit-sliced AES implementation with GCC 6.3, maintaining compatibility with older compilers. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5adddcd96255112ff04b350d661518302159e7e2)
- Reset element count to zero after OPENSSL_LH_flush clears hash table, avoiding statistics still reporting old entry count after flush. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e5da68183410c06f7b350a0721bc2bd6057e438e)
- Release objects no longer used after test in provider_test, fixing test memory leak. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/49d874e0b7514cb270e817103ff0e13d4689e1f0)
- Reuse existing algorithm context when digest context is reinitialized with the same digest algorithm, avoiding unnecessary release and reallocation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fe5c5cb85197aec7d68ab095b866ed22076850d0)
- DH parameter generation returns error when encountering invalid paramgen type, no longer triggering crash due to unrecognized type. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/359dad5178285d5471f2a57a5aa99c1f588dffcb)
- Fix OCSP_basic_verify verification of signer certificate chain, making verification use correct certificate and trust results. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/21f89f542d745adbf1131338929ae538e200d50d)
- Fix parameter setting and update path of RC4-MD5 Provider cipher, restoring correct handling of this combined cipher. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/33219939c782cf363b30e9e899b9997fb1ced440)
- Check return values flagged by Coverity, entering error handling path on operation failure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b11183be0cd3ad675248804922bb240fbbd448e4)
- Improve X509 lookup API to distinguish between "object not found" and internal/fatal errors, allowing callers to correctly decide whether to continue lookup. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0ce8271c20c95d21d9641c0ead76a86f818c45e9)
- Fix garbled echo in SSL echo example client/server, ensuring sent and received content is not incorrectly formatted. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3c0e8bc4a797d29b2152aebc6e687ddfa941160b)
- Fix use-after-free pointed out by Coverity, no longer accessing original pointer after resource release. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/588080cbf8e254ca2c033224146bc29fddea75a7)
- Fix another use-after-free path, ensuring no reading of freed object fields after cleanup. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/66cb4fcdc5039fe5b1476ed48a936137a307a58b)
- Eliminate third use-after-free issue, completing necessary state reads before end of object lifetime. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/71b7f34978c7332562300487af497559b67f600a)
- Fix multiple memory access errors reported by Coverity, ensuring array bounds and object validity are checked before access. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a381897470f5c6ac2f4e71f48d33d71cde7873dd)
- s_server reports error when connection initialization fails and no data read attempt is made, avoiding continuing service loop without output. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a6d52f178c4cb4665d0bf235001b5c9c1ff03da7)
- Initialize local values pointed out by Coverity, avoiding uninitialized variables being used in subsequent conditions or calculations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4e720f12fade8d433e5a0eb3ead9017193dac6e7)
- Fix buffer handling in CBC-MAC rotate-copy path on platforms with 32-byte cache lines, eliminating leaks and ensuring boundary data is correctly overwritten. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3b836385679504579ee1052ed4b4ef1d9f49fa13)
- Fix crash of ERR_load_strings when building with error string support disabled (no-err), shielding error string loading path according to compile configuration. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/11e85b8941cb6f728e37f15502f26e67231db6b6)
- Rewrite NIST big number copy logic to avoid strict aliasing violations, ensuring copy results comply with C language rules under optimized compilation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8712db5e4e0c508de10e887aebf639384dc20710)
- Fix build compatibility issues on Tandem platform and old POSIX systems, adding necessary header files and conditional compilation handling. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b98f989e0c741d7534a58ba3fb22f5af0f016ca4)
- Merge duplicate locale initialization code, unifying establishment and access of C locale for case comparison functions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/26ccb0e4e0b100423184636457cd6aab4cc779ab)
- Fix return values of BIO_get_ktls_send and BIO_get_ktls_recv to only return 0 or 1, consistent with API documentation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/524bac570702a79366b85ff1f66e07d3e002370c)
- Fix connection waiting and socket state handling when KTLS is used with BIO_new_connect. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/598bd7741568a1aae678e5472f18aae1ab991e8d)
- Check return values pointed out by Coverity, no longer continuing along success path on operation failure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6d5f636ce1ff6b57846e0e0fc82f7ed56aee2ac5)
- Add fixed-width integer type compatibility definitions for NonStop platform to support compilation on that system. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ec26144288fd6dce6dd76bd9e2b192b495033723)
- Name mapping lookup directly uses input string, avoiding extra OPENSSL_strndup buffer allocation for each lookup. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/dab5098eacb9e264c32a33332ba047f234a3de68)
- Optimize ASCII tolower conversion, directly handling common characters and avoiding extra access to character mapping table. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/286053fc8f78e34828a576830ef879c021640aee)
- Fix potential out-of-bounds access when openssl command help text width exceeds temporary buffer. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0d1a0ed63d1b4faa3711a69a19f7029947524cfa)
- Use controlled string appending and length checks to build command help text, avoiding buffer write out-of-bounds. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/aac6ae3774f341412bc45583ef9358df5b76a008)
- ASN.1 object initialization checks the method pointer when embeddable data is missing, fixing a crash in asn1_item_embed_new. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/557825acd622f98fc21423aba092e374db84f483)
- Fix a regression in the default key length of the Blowfish CFB/OFB Provider, restoring compatible behavior when no explicit key length is specified. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7a9e93dda58118c0fb1bade8fe915306b845325b)
- Adjust the loop termination condition to make zero-iteration and boundary exit conditions clearer, reducing the risk of misreading and boundary errors. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/36c269c3023f5eb626ec79777ed8b285ef939be2)
- Fix the condition for checking the return value of EVP_CIPHER_CTX_ctrl in the speed tool, so that control operation failures are no longer treated as success. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d649c51a5388912277dffb56d921eb720db54be1)
- Free the temporary private key object in ec_key_simple_oct2priv, fixing a memory leak in the EC private key octet string decoding path. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/22a96c6be41897d11a18455b2ab142422bc57f3f)
- Initialize fields used when constructing curve groups in EC_GROUP_new_from_ecparameters, eliminating undefined behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/97de6145851922a33f7afd9c308adfc1b2e5732b)
- Free temporary objects in X509_issuer_and_serial_hash, fixing a memory leak when computing certificate hashes. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b7e28c0bb1cdc07e36c7dc2467083236b931de31)
- Use a safe length pattern when calculating buffer lengths for GOST key exchange, avoiding key size calculation overflow or insufficient buffer. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2b5e89992e3ada1131beebb2a22722168b9389c2)
- Update the GOST engine to adapt to upstream interface changes, keeping the external engine compatible with the current OpenSSL version. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/828613c3e44ca1de6407a37d34de64c673ca61e9)
- Free objects allocated in the CRL issuer setting path, fixing a memory leak when setting the issuer list. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e9007e09792e3735d4973743634ff55d354fc7d8)
- Clean up code style in crl_set_issuers without changing CRL issuer setting behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cb0c36d124991e35a9e778056ec8fce23a14dad5)
- Fix return value checks in DTLS record processing, correctly distinguishing between successful processing, ignoring, and failure states. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/639e576023aa2492ca87e1e6503c40d2e8c9a24e)
- Fix the result check for EVP_PKEY_get_params so that only positive values indicate successful parameter retrieval. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7e5e91176b770a68bdaf73a5c647f1fc0d7f2900)
- Fix the return value check for EVP_CIPHER_CTX_set_key_length, avoiding treating non-success returns as success. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8d9fec1781751d2106d899c6076eeb3da6930bfe)
- Fix the return value check for EVP_CIPHER_CTX_rand_key, handling random key generation failure along the error path. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f15e3f3aa95df743f0da793da952f87370efb4ff)
- Fix the return value check for UI_method_set_ex_data, ensuring that failures in setting UI method extension data are reported. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1aef2c10f10e0685298008be596c80e148c71a51)
- Fix display misalignment in non-verbose test output, keeping test summary lines correctly aligned. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/128d1c3c0a12fe68175a460e06daf1e0d940f681)
- When HTTP trace is enabled, output the server response content after request failure, facilitating diagnosis of error status and response body. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e8fdb0603572bf051dad6abc56291cdf1313a905)
- Unify code formatting around the BLOCK_CIPHER_custom macro without changing cipher implementation logic. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1c5a4e3b5e05494876ebba9d8272d2cbca1e20a3)
- Fix the return value condition for EVP_PKEY_CTX_set_group_name, using failure return values to determine group name setting results. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/56876ae952b96b4a83266f6b2ec1393f599015d6)
- Fix return value checks for RSA-PSS salt length setting and reading APIs, ensuring error states are not misjudged as success. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7263a7fc3d0c0c17616c2e5309e0fd52ed654ecc)
- Fix the judgment of parameter setting results in evp_pkey_ctx_set_params_strict, correctly returning errors on failure in strict mode. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/00d5193b688019a85d1bd0196f2837a4476394bb)
- Uniformly fix return value conditions for multiple EVP_PKEY_CTX set/get functions, correctly handling 0, negative values, and success states. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2cba2e160d5b028e4a777e8038744a8bc4280629)
- Release held Decoder references when decoder instance creation fails, fixing reference leaks on error paths. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9ec9b968f93e4a8e7c90eb1e717f0d7cd4ab722d)
- Fix another resource leak on the Decoder instance error path, releasing instances and associated objects that were not successfully returned. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/da3193976380b8bd697a472025ff9f384cbca7af)
- Update iteration position and correctly release temporary Provider references when provider traversal callback is interrupted, fixing activated provider traversal leaks. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b4be10dfcd370960cecfda9773e1bfcc568a7390)
- Check the result of evp_pkey_get_legacy before accessing the internal representation of legacy EVP_PKEY, avoiding null pointer dereference. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b9a86d5dd8b5bd33be42390bcbb5121fe0ae71a1)
- Fix CTLOG_new_ex incorrectly writing the property query string into the name field, ensuring propq is stored in the correct member. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/163bf682fd93971d07e66e3da339c229b86dc849)
- Fix success checks for RAND_bytes related calls, explicitly returning errors when random byte generation fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c2f7614fb7b93fe3792068077ff01384f42f39bc)
- Fix the check condition for EVP_PKEY_check, only considering key parameters valid when a success status is returned. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/92d0d7ea9be40909ee79fb8861641a61eead2431)
- Fix the return value check for EVP_PKEY_pairwise_check, correctly rejecting when key pairwise consistency fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d016758706d0a7a104ff09db94448aeec1b38193)
- Fix return value handling for EVP_PKEY_public_check, avoiding misjudging validation failures as valid public keys. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bba14c6e28e9519b2d40fc5c551893996f2db246)
- Fix return value handling for EVP_PKEY_private_check, continuing only when the private key check succeeds. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/babc818c3f669214fa192229003953e3dead1926)
- Fix the return value check for UI_add_input_string, correctly aborting the interaction flow when input item creation fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5755c11fd6e50028946e6e17c835afcd56995699)
- Fix the result check for EVP_PKEY_param_check, no longer proceeding along the success path when parameter validation fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e85bef981c037a6ebc0ca39f61c11bd79ed89fb3)
- Fix the return value check for BIO_get_cipher_status, ensuring that final filter status failures are correctly reported. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/48b571fe771f283d547ca2a5999ce5dd9a5509d0)
- Fix strict certificate chain checking for TLS 1.3 clients, correctly comparing CA names in the server chain with the certificate issuer. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/89dd85430770d39cbfb15eb586c921958ca7687f)
- Upgrade the test SCT issuer key to RSA 2048 bits, meeting current security level requirements. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/479b9adb88b9050186c1e9fc94879906f378b14b)
- Fix an issue in a ternary expression that unintentionally assigned a valid pointer to NULL, preserving the object that should be returned by the conditional branch. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1a01e5c29dfaf09af3960b4c8e6ec0f8171eda80)
- Free temporary objects allocated during copying in evp_pkey_copy_downgraded, fixing a memory leak. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ae4d9573ac783dcf26279f461d42d0e261e978f7)
- Free temporary objects stored in the method store when algorithm method registration fails, fixing a memory leak in ossl_method_store_add. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fed8dbea27b7e01ee934951b25c6ffd40ad1d5c3)
- Clear the digest sub-context copy before copying the HMAC context, avoiding releasing digest state still held by the source object when copying fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cec1699f1f54ba8b87f055776dc77b48dd37d5fa)
- Free temporarily stored algorithm pointers when provider algorithm group collection fails, avoiding resource leaks on failure paths. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a7863f994955c45fb7ce29e30b81a6206994c3dd)
- Clear the digest object reference before copying the SM2 encryption context, avoiding use-after-free when dup fails and releases a shared digest. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/926c698c6f0a197e0322d4617db0ecd0d40f6e06)
- Fix candidate prime calculation and loop boundaries in RSA key generation, ensuring compliance with FIPS 186-4 parameter definitions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ad7e0fd550a9eb2946edf38003ebc6d5b988dac7)
- Explicitly report an error when no second prime candidate is found in RSA key generation, no longer using uninitialized candidate values. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d2399d8cd29f56e6614f0b3db4e7e563a745902a)
- Move local declarations in initialization functions within conditional compilation scope, eliminating 'defined but not used' compilation warnings or errors. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/979575c6ef10ab9b8d74d8c00852b2250eb78f29)
- Use separate locks for init_lock and initialization completion status, avoiding cross-reuse of the same lock for different synchronization purposes. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e9a806b2c265da3a4ca472acb4a4286d9c1b5c9d)
- Add regression tests for PPC64 fixed-length Montgomery multiplication, covering calculation errors previously introduced by this optimization. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/14f95126c098358c434d59835834f9f0be7ea498)
- Return failure when DH Provider parameter export allocation fails, no longer incorrectly reporting export success after OSSL_PARAM_BLD_to_param failure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/46c1c2d7fa9153da4eb5e1aefd7b0139dc507c00)
- Fix output size and failure status handling in the KDF key exchange derive path, avoiding reporting success or writing out of bounds when derivation fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0feb138fbeeec9ae09e63b212a0a6e345ed0dc30)
- Check object pointers in X509v3_asid_subset for boundary inputs such as empty address prefix sets, avoiding crashes when accessing empty address segments. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/01fc9b6bce82f0534d6673659a0e59a71f57ee82)
- Free temporary objects allocated in evp_pkey_export_to_provider on export failure paths, fixing key export memory leaks. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/115eb945acd9a27bf81c6c8923f43768f9e487a8)
- Fix the string parameter type in the OSSL_PARAM example, using OSSL_PARAM_UTF8_PTR consistent with pointer-type UTF-8 parameters. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/809526a06c1305d67a8f231ca15cd27ec800efce)
- Remove obsolete debug and build targets, cleaning up development build rules that are no longer used. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/909d590fe7a0935e7856ec618afd652ae03a9260)
- Avoid using SIZE_MAX directly on platforms that do not provide the macro, fixing portability issues in KDF key exchange code. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c6010d1a1020223274de39e3ce25643b33dac80d)
- Fix file operations in c_rehash so that file paths with spaces or special characters can be safely opened and processed. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ce60b13707add7e6b54c5817376234c4043506ed)
- Remove the issuer_name_hash= prefix when c_rehash outputs CRL hash filenames, adopting the standard hash naming format recognized by OpenSSL. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/87eee75010f7efaa617ff32163359bf81513a619)
- Integrate the priority queue source file and its generation/compilation rules into the OpenSSL build system. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4bb1fdf78c174e94c6e29fad7710c0c3619dee1e)
- Fix input range/parameters when calling bn_reduce_once_in_place in AVX-512 dual-path RSAZ modular exponentiation, ensuring correct result reduction. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4d8a88c134df634ba610ff8db1eb8478ac5fd345)
- Fix the result check for X509_REVOKED_add1_ext_i2d, correctly returning errors when adding revocation list extensions fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c540a82767954a616934ba6caa6ddc736502c574)
- Fix return value checks for X509_LOOKUP series lookup/set functions, correctly handling lookup initialization and certificate lookup failures. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e22ea36fa8296b402348da8f5ab5e258be8402cf)
- Fix conditional compilation issues in v3ext tests under no-rfc3779 configuration, making test code adapt to that build option. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b76efe61ea9710a8f69e1cb8caf1aeb2ba6f1ebe)
- The FIPS module no longer includes Encoder, Decoder, and Store internal headers, reducing dependencies on non-FIPS functionality and maintaining module boundaries. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b8fd15a8dc50020360862290ace7f34b6ef0e92d)
- Free objects allocated on the failure path of EC_GROUP_new_from_ecparameters, fixing memory leaks in EC parameter conversion. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/be50862e72d96e599f1111bbb69f41b5af651c97)
- Fix the failure path when importing data from the temporary memory in ossa_fromdata, and fix the import leak in RSA Provider. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/28adea95975c3ea53fc590efda35dee13efd4767)
- Fix the range comparison logic in range_should_be_prefix, so that it correctly returns whether the range is a prefix. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/30532e59f475e0066c030693e4d614311a9e0cae)
- Fix the repeated unlock issue in the same lock in the Provider core path, avoiding undefined thread synchronization behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/61f510600e2c7cdee6e61f8b7075fb0e939eb179)
- Use an explicit integer variable to receive optlen from getsockopt, avoiding reliance on mismatched union type layouts on different platforms. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8e949b35d396005d63f3a2c944c36a1c94e41019)
- Switch to using the FILETIME variable adapted for the Windows API to call SystemTimeToFileTime, avoiding union parameter aliasing and type issues. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8eca6864e080c9b8197fec81cd6f327be43bb14c)
- Release objects allocated during intermediate steps of CMP protection calculation, fixing the memory leak in cmp_calc_protection. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/74c929d00dce3a4755164859c600aabb3838a87b)
- Fix the error handling path identified by Coverity, ensuring consistent handling of related objects and return codes when operations fail. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f80cdee7c1eee93d13c7dcbeda32dfca3e1e4059)
- Handle negative return values reported by Coverity, avoiding the use of erroneous lengths or indices as valid values. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/be54ad88a67d2fba3b4fd51bef0fe7db0c01b99a)
- Handle another negative return status, ensuring failure codes do not flow into subsequent array access or allocation logic. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3ee2611677e7e9f90e270f3ee4f343c9d3d86835)
- Fix the condition check in the warning text for the cmp command's -newkeypass option, and extend warning prompts related to genm requests. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bbaabd16e9fd090ecdc9688f3364c3dbc56512d4)
- CONF_modules_unload now safely returns when called after OPENSSL_cleanup, no longer accessing destroyed module locks or global state. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d840f07bcdfc3910de5aa327a245866a67f94799)
- Check the return value of BN_one, and correctly report an error when initializing a large number to 1 fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7fe7cc57af3db1e497877f0329ba17609b2efc8b)
- Fix the AES-OCB encryption and decryption implementations in the x86 AES-NI path, ensuring correct OCB tag and plaintext recovery. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6ebf6d51596f51d23ccbc17930778d104a57d99c)
- Initialize uninitialized fields identified by Coverity, preventing them from being read by subsequent certificate or protocol state processing. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2e3e9b4887b5077b949cdee490ecc1526b2c5509)
- Return an error when the TLS usage state of the HTTP TLS callback does not match the requesting connection, avoiding a crash in app_http_tls_cb. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/96e13a1679872d879683346c1e09ca227f77efb0)
- Fix the BIO pointer mistakenly used in BIO_should_retry during HTTP non-blocking reads, ensuring retry decisions are based on the actual read BIO. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/059a4ad0999dd6dbd7340b5e4f7566812d51bb1e)
- Correct comments and documentation in the HTTP client regarding the purpose and lifecycle of the internal memory BIO. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7d5019c15af8f88443a7edddd4b150a7dafeda5d)
- Fix the typo in the openssl ocsp command help/option text where OCSP was misspelled as OSCP. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2837b19fcba4bf4ff2ecdc8435c650bf18c27552)
- Fix the performance regression reported on AArch64, restoring the performance of the relevant algorithm paths. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/65523758e546fcef0f930e5f8878ef51d174dbc8)
- Fix the feature flag passing during SHA, SHAKE, and Keccak assembly generation, ensuring the correct implementation is selected based on the target architecture. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a8b238f0e4c1198935e066ef19579ca694253286)
- Release temporary data when X509V3_add1_i2d deletes an extension using the delete flag, fixing a memory leak in that branch. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4798e0680b112993815098ca21d7d68ff31ebc6e)
- Fix the release order of APP_HTTP_TLS_INFO in http_cb_arg when cleaning up CMP_CTX in the cmp command, avoiding double-free or leaks. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8c094747d78bb8627e9ca5241fed0550a3de2fdb)
- Correct the HTTP Content-Length calculation so that the written body length is computed based on the actual buffer content. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/243465fd556837402bff52b7bf3d59420b68a02e)
- Release the encoded object created intermediately in EVP_PKEY_get1_encoded_public_key, fixing a memory leak when retrieving the encoded public key. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4e9a4997c540e64647d4e1708a1dbda51fb59a68)
- Revert the change to error reporting in the X509 verification chain construction, restoring the existing diagnostic behavior for chain construction failures. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1f00dc4f8c0ef0101368de2adf22495e5e295114)
- Predeclare all Provider core dispatch callback functions and fix internal function declarations, eliminating inconsistent declaration order. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9574842e90e29015daa2b071e965cec9aa885c17)
- Correct the HTTP Content-Length calculation again, covering buffer length boundary cases not handled by the previous fix. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8c65e1f719ecf7ec7ed3094bbd763f88708d26eb)
- Fix the CPU feature flag passing during EC assembly generation, ensuring EC optimization code is generated and selected based on the correct architecture capabilities. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/12186377cb987c4527d286e91e735e8261a45669)
- Use TEST_true to report boolean failures in OCSP API tests, showing the exact failure location and condition. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/180c8d7ae56378992b90ace9626d6df6ab1d4de8)
- Fix the s390x Keccak assembly implementation, ensuring correct permutation results for SHA-3/SHAKE. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/086d88a637ecf537af62260e16d4e0011dbb8d1b)
- Fix the code formatting in the CMS SignedData implementation, eliminating issues reported by format checks. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8fc120bda21b7ebe24db2283aa501ac0c396c026)
- Release the context allocated in PKCS12_pbe_crypt_ex on encryption/decryption failure paths, fixing a memory leak. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/af801ec89205aaf6ebf8522d510d0b1fc29e3233)
- Clean up the formatting and code style of the X.509 address extension implementation to satisfy check-format. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/30d398ad375bb4b15eae6497d67d54c03be2660d)
- Fix undefined behavior in signed shifts on AArch64, avoiding UBSAN errors and ensuring consistent sign extension results. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1efd8533e1ccc5c5e69795eb393a6b79b62e48e2)
- Fix the issue where check-format.pl incorrectly reported for(;; stmt) loops as formatting errors. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4e9fa07121abf3ebaaf7e0367bd9be3a8b273ebf)
- Improve check-format.pl's checking of blank lines before and after local variable declarations, correctly identifying missing or extra blank lines. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/23757b61d49ac3e46440dc34e56b83201106e440)
- Fix the certificate verify_callback in the openssl s_client and s_server applications, ensuring the verification callback failure status is correctly passed to the TLS handshake. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fad0f80eff188ef938fed614245a56ed56110deb)
- Check the input items and address prefix status in v2i_IPAddrBlocks, fixing a crash when processing IP address ranges. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b91ad3c69c27c35be4fd7f1e8811c33c31b02afd)
- Fix parameter handling for disabling DTLS 1.2 in tests, ensuring the -no-dtls1_2 configuration takes effect. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a6843e6ae8ae0551aae8555783f06dab7951f112)
- Fix the s390x GCM initialization process, ensuring the hardware-assisted counter and key state are correctly set. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/48e35b99bd0071207cfe39da22eb2502db5c09dc)
- Define a compatible implementation of VirtualLock for UWP builds, fixing link errors caused by the missing API declaration/implementation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ff1efe6e261598c3f71727b796767a8e716bcbe2)
- Fix the logic in CMP HTTP tests that adjusts the test plan based on the mock server's certstatus capability. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7a86cb69830bf0a8845cd17e606f612109c7564b)
- Reset the GCM record length counter on AAD updates, ensuring authentication data and subsequent ciphertext are each subject to the correct length limits. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3ebcb2fff56bda788ab1f363eb0023715018a4e5)
- Fix the quoting of the install_fips command in the Windows Makefile template, ensuring target parameters are parsed correctly. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/709651c9022e7be7e69cf8a2f6edf2c8722a6a1e)
- Fix encryption/decryption regressions introduced by the GCM mode refactoring, restoring the correct update behavior of the cipher context before the refactoring. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/186be8ed26f5561faf91d6da3ed14cd9cb6617dd)
- Add object checks on allocation or write failure paths in PEM_write, fixing a segfault when calling PEM_write. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/205957405d08ef199e6ab654e333a627bbca9ccc)
- Fix the judgment of SSL_pending and SSL_has_pending for received/pending data under DTLS, correctly reflecting the record layer state. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6d6b295ac39fcb0461f25fda69983d2dbb75f8f1)
- Fix byte order handling in AES-GCM-SIV on big-endian and little-endian platforms, ensuring RFC test vectors are consistent across architectures. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6f74677911de87f3271721073bd360806a93733f)
- Fix the default algorithm name in the openssl pkcs12 help information, avoiding a mismatch with actual behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/72a85c17aae602e881c917c3f6e93bd7f7260093)
- Avoid leaking record layer and BIO resources on the do_ssl3_write failure path, ensuring temporary objects are cleaned up when write operations end. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/771fef7793ae572be7567e408a07bfefe6a09ea0)
- Fix the return value of EVP_CIPHER_CTX_get_iv_length, making it retrieve the current IV length from the Provider cipher context. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e0e338c8c50c226efc92fe79c788c9cdc03fc01f)
- Fix the regression in EC_KEY_set_private_key, restoring the consistency of public and private key states after setting the private key. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b304b3e8f7397c3e949e3664e6ceaee5dc811b32)
- Fix the s390x GHASH assembly implementation, ensuring correct authentication hash calculation for GCM. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cd854f225bbef9561fad680e2628dfd55be1b141)
- Fix the AddressSanitizer error triggered by the Address BIO on invalid address input. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/278b0d8b674eba6f6e1ec51a18c3ccaf8db02701)
- Fix the AddressSanitizer report in the TCP Fast Open test, correctly releasing the sockets and buffers used by the test. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d272ef5372a16924a5804b74a76491b1bc8529b5)
- Fix the code style and several minor issues in timing_load_creds, keeping its test program compliant with project standards. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/45479dcee1672661e4f5b6d8b6c9a50453581e65)
- Make CRYPTO_mem_debug_push and CRYPTO_mem_debug_pop return 0 on success, conforming to their interface contract. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f868454257560c78570549f6a34d5918f03898a0)
- Fix the unrolled Montgomery multiplication implementation on POWER9, ensuring correct product and carry calculations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2f1112b22a826dc8854b41b60a422c987f8ddafb)
- Fix the skip condition for multiblock cipher tests, avoiding incorrect skipping of multiblock performance/functional tests when run conditions are met. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7c82a7a8f3a66f47f727d31691d6298d88ed158b)
- Release the temporary large number in BN_rand_range during random range calculation, fixing a memory leak in random large number generation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/70f589ae41928edda18470ba1c3df82af02a92b3)
- In the X509v3 extension cache, EXFLAG_NO_FINGERPRINT indicates a missing fingerprint flag rather than a cache error, so certificate extension parsing no longer fails because of it. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2c05607cd91fc5aab6d61f0324104d63a091d705)
- Check the key and parameter state when the EC Provider retrieves encoded public key parameters, fixing a crash in OSSL_PKEY_PARAM_ENCODED_PUBLIC_KEY queries. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b5db237def7e22ccea1a540ec777045b3ce4600e)
- Fix compilation issues with the imported recordmethod.h, making the record layer method interface header pass current compiler checks. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/11653dcd6ecbc7ff3c53f694474ece08ce4473aa)
- Distinguish fatal errors from recoverable errors when creating the record layer, allowing callers to decide whether to continue connection initialization based on the error type. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7c2939999f8e43d996d846867ba326b052f821d6)
- Fix compilation failures when compression is disabled, ensuring the no-comp configuration does not reference removed compression code. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/976b263d0a8581059c21fb34653df3375667f050)
- Remove the invocation of the record layer for the error state, and reduce the coupling between the record layer and the handshake state machine. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/651216dd54199c64bf9afd1256bbdde3990e1dfc)
- Update the platform macro for the AIX compiler front-end, and fix the conditional compilation for the related implementation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d4ee3456e98b1137a1ba013cf01f1052891dd3db)
- Fix the Montgomery multiplication calculation issue on POWER9, ensuring correct results for large-number multiplication. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/db302550d32c0a450a47ab17990dd1b66551186a)
- Add a QUIC receive packet parser that parses input data packets and dispatches received frames by frame type. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e921882d57201e14cc6a48765b2281065d6f5c65)
- Add EVP_PKEY_get1_encoded_public_key tests, covering success and failure paths of the public key encoding export interface. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ba9e3721febb073397248154a846f2088efd6409)
- Add tests for the verify application code signing purpose, verifying both matching and non-matching certificate purpose results. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/aeadd2981b214d5e2a8f578179c17b0dccc77042)
- Supplement the X509_gmtime_adj manual page, explaining how to adjust certificate validity times by relative seconds. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4d32f5332fa69ac949feec54c273fe63639ad891)
- Add the TLS record layer method skeleton, establishing an interface framework for replaceable record read and write implementations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5639ee79bdc905c1b7ed308fcd29e515e4f01a50)
- Move the record layer method implementation to a separate directory, organizing code by module responsibility. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6b5c7ef7713d913002f94068a3ef1f41b22eafdb)
- Split the record processing logic that varies by protocol version into different source files, reducing conditional branches within the record layer. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2b44565476d9d6d86f5af0ec736a7bf6f77a839e)
- Remove the dependency on the SSL_USE_EXPLICIT_IV macro in record layer methods, letting the record layer handle IV rules directly. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0a8a9f8f634306bbfaed8f924d71536f1ff50677)
- Remove the remaining dependencies of the record layer implementation on the SSL object, completing the independence of the record layer state. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/856f2aa7be6bb59bc72493845d92e31ef0523c79)
- Add missing data type declarations for FreeBSD builds, fixing platform compilation failures. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ed822619f5c051dba7b73c5e2eebe7c790351893)
- Initialize the ipi_spec_dst field in the Cygwin address structure, fixing build or runtime issues caused by its undefined value. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7f4c65749f7e9e687d5aa38e9b3fb548705511b9)
- Fix the datagram send/receive behavior of BIO_sendmmsg/BIO_recvmmsg on FreeBSD, adapting to the system call semantics of that platform. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/07687790a055a039bba93ee00ac970c9710f0669)
- Adjust declarations and formatting in bio_dgram_test to be compatible with warning rules of older Clang compilers. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/18274e1d6e10081fb7974e40f595e9a1d3224296)
- Remove direct calls to perror in libcrypto, using the OpenSSL error reporting mechanism to maintain consistent error context. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5121783b49327767c35ca39623734e586259c9dc)
- Fix the s390x ECDH/X25519 key derivation path, ensuring correct shared secret computation and output length. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3cca05cc194c0528865deea57d9e60ca3fb0e5d3)
- Fix compilation warnings in crypto/conf, avoiding GCC warnings on configuration module code. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8ae080bf851a25187b93803b8c6a93e82dd97437)
- Fix test conditions under OPENSSL_USE_IPV6=0 builds, avoiding references to disabled IPv6 address functionality. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ce41a53dc647184119876fee53afef66be6c7f4b)
- Restore the original struct name PROV_RC5_CTX, ensuring consistency between forward declarations and implementations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/53ef02baf80130a81d019e85c528fdc13af9db33)
- Update the VMS openssl.cnf example to match the latest configuration options and content generated by make update. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ee68d2b957923e2e4d1d76d7ad66e0e8b3674d71)
- QUIC implementation no longer depends on struct timeval, using the unified cross-platform OSSL_TIME time interface instead. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4fc04c71acf180dad0b4418d12b3ed31ba46179a)
- Fix whitespace formatting in source files to satisfy format checking rules. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0f4be8a14a2bcb8a92cf78d94d157152c0a03d88)
- Fix the AES-GCM optimization path on Power 8, ensuring correct encryption and authentication results on that processor. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9ab6b64ac856157a31a54c0d12207c2338bfa8e2)
- Fix QUIC handshake packet processing, correctly handling frames and key phases in Handshake packets. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/45e7ef5fe34b3f519f1454c47dc08aa4563e4247)
- Fix the OSSL_TIME fallback definition in e_os.h, ensuring platforms without support can still compile the unified time API. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/eb51673e522855400a11de4569a3612c98c7b685)
- Fix TDES test failures under older FIPS Provider environments, adapting test expectations according to Provider capabilities. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4d0249c2d1d0f81c211354d8a36738595936fad8)
- Fix default padding compatibility regression when interacting with OpenSSL 3.0.0 FIPS Provider. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9684335839fcdeac06d21b06628c4c37117b5478)
- Improve error handling in X509_STORE_CTX_set_default and fix code paths calling this API. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4fdc16af05d5e1e79ffebbae2b427f3a388227e3)
- Clear errors produced by parsing probes during CMS ContentInfo decoding, avoiding misleading error queue content after successful decoding. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/678b489a2ae8af289cef939a538235686b448c0e)
- Fix occasional failures in BIO_dgram_pair random tests, making test datagram communication results reproducible. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8e90a12ad82dec6d8b683eaa2e4feafa9796d377)
- Fix error return values of BIO_ctrl_pending and BIO_ctrl_wpending, returning values conforming to conventions when write/read wait length queries fail. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e9809f8a09147bc27f974caa908b04439c006625)
- Release async task resources allocated in asynctest, fixing test memory leaks. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c5d061290baa9466182b6d1a5b88aa9e5a4b2386)
- Add the synopsis of the OSSL_TRACE() function to the OSSL_trace_enabled manual page, fully presenting tracing-related interfaces. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/85b358b01a36757d07da118795a0ad13c9c2b4d7)
- Extract HTTP server logging code into separate log.c/log.h, and uniformly clean up request logging handling. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9ff519542387d32ab1c3a8b1f45a375e1712a383)
- Clean up EBCDIC character definitions, removing duplicate or inaccurate character constants. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9690b9737d46cc52cc93682a63b110f5513e7671)
- Standardize formatting of comments and option values in openssl.cnf, removing extra spaces before option values. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8bc703c2886c2104f1d472ab681bc7a8c081427a)
- Set the modified flag after updating X509_REQ_INFO member data, ensuring request re-encoding includes modifications. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d12b824ddaee502400c19bf8c32e1ada3111fc50)
- Restore the original struct name PROV_RC5_CTX, ensuring its definition matches forward declarations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1fcd84c7017416a3c9461914d7a943591ad87a82)
- libcrypto no longer depends on the system timeval, using OSSL_TIME for time handling to unify cross-platform behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e63f5fdcb2658961f29fe7bed7968c0dcf7328a7)
- Clean up whitespace formatting issues in code, keeping format checks passing. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2ba5bffa26c0c4677f48e730628c0b54c31c734c)
- Add hmacWithSM3 PRF support for password-based encryption, allowing SM3 to derive protection keys. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b85d53c1670e47273827bba508daff310c3263ab)
- Fix function name spelling, making symbol names consistent across declarations, implementations, and call sites. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3189e12733e676fbbc30b1b2d98952a6a9f78073)
- Fix expected values or parameters for triple DES tests under older FIPS Provider, maintaining compatibility with historical versions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/25d47cccf203c3b71171e78865e48ea061a039a8)
- Add documentation and tests for EVP_PBE_alg_add, explaining usage of registering PBE algorithms and their mapping tables. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0b3867634f74f6cb7b60b3a0adde396421207214)
- Add error reporting for generic stack operations and unify code style, facilitating diagnosis of stack allocation and insertion failures. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ed49476a16b8ff2688a53a2ba7e011e6911620f8)
- Explicitly add dependencies on CTLOG_FILE and TEST_CERTS_DIR to ssl_new test rules, ensuring required files are prepared first. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/894f2166ef2c16d8e4533e1c09e05ff31ea2f1d8)
- Fix KTLS support detection logic during Linux cross-compilation, determining based on the target environment rather than the running host's capabilities. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e077455e9e57ed4ee4676996b4a9aa11df6327a6)
- When the TLS 1.3 ticket key callback returns 0, do not send NewSessionTicket, respecting the callback's decision to reject tickets. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/928f15e71b0bccabb10cbdcbb9b2d4e85eeb5906)
- Remove the FIPS build skip condition for IV generation tests, increasing IV test coverage in FIPS builds. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/45ada6b92bc7e31772d95ab9dcb0e7d2a764cf20)
- At test runtime, detect the actual FIPS random number implementation used, then judge test results based on Provider capabilities. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c007f466aaebd8ef07111c8560e039d8bcb5fa7b)
- Fix the return status description and checking logic of OSSL_PROVIDER_set_default_search_path. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/19b6b5f4791e3531cd6d3aabc8706d590ade14b7)
- Add HPKE DHKEM Provider algorithms, supporting EC, X25519, and X448 key encapsulation/decapsulation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/af33b200da8040c78dbfd8405878190980727171)
- Add QUIC connection ID cache design document, explaining ID lookup, cache keys and values, and many-to-one mapping. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1567a821a4616f59748fa8982724f88e542867d6)
- Fix failure return values of BIO_ctrl_pending and BIO_ctrl_wpending, making wait byte count queries conform to BIO API conventions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c7340583097a80a4fe42bacea745b2bbaa6d16db)
- Release contexts created in asynchronous tests, fixing memory leaks in asynctest. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9929c81702381bff54f833d6fe0a3304f4e2b635)
- Migrate TLS write-side record state to a new independent structure, beginning to split write record responsibilities from the SSL connection object. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e1e93f7a07dfc7a8dddd4ddbb79d1d9bc9760d32)
- Fix the return value of tls_default_read_n, making read calls return according to actual read or failure status. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f78c51995e35889d39cb0bdadcbfa3e144bd8a29)
- Fix spelling errors near the name PKCS12_SAFEBAG_set0_attrs. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/af6379368f81025808689e843a5d86c6402a63a7)
- Fix occasional unaligned access failures in CI, using safe alignment before accessing cross-architecture sensitive data. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8511520842b744d1794ea794c032ce5f78cd874b)
- Fix parsing of LLVM and Apple LLVM version numbers, avoiding incorrect enabling of AVX-512 IFMA assembly implementations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/523e0577305bbcc732d22bcb063c6c8ca658874a)
- Fix compilation errors under SCTP configuration, adapting to the new record layer method interface. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/846975f367f75f3503b44c12e49d980dca181647)
- Avoid compiling ripemd Provider source files into FIPS shared sources, ensuring correct FIPS module boundaries and source file classification. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/155a82d1fe1c50d859081ff67f26633b9d7dada8)
- Fix file extension matching in c_rehash, accurately identifying input file types such as certificates and CRLs. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/706fc5f6ebd63e1fcd18d4764248206ab3c18a0a)
- Avoid implicitly refreshing the cached TBSCertificate after certificate field changes, keeping the signature/encoding cache rebuilt only on explicit updates. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/963e0bc43369a6dbe6644f709630f6c9f63dccf9)
- Refine error types for Provider loading and initialization, allowing callers to distinguish module loading failures from Provider initialization failures. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2d23ba14630551ee347acafcab81fa1a290c6504)
- Fix the make update generated file process, ensuring tracked files are updated by maintenance commands. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3c153d8722d52ac6faa0d98873060272e5f160ea)
- Fix tainted scalar usage reported by Coverity, validating input data before using it for control values or indices. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5e569f0a2e11a59cab7b6f525865232e7770e2f0)
- Initialize variables reported by Coverity to avoid reading unassigned states. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d06d5d6b68f39c7f75f1130f984efa78c291fb57)
- Remove unreachable code reported by Coverity, simplifying actual execution paths. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/dc45bfb4b452ba5a876ebf48791217b69d092ff9)
- Fix set-but-unused local variables in PEM paths, eliminating compiler warnings. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/71bc497dc321adeb08e7541556dea019c81c9a87)

- Use ERR_LIB_CRYPTO for parameter errors in OPENSSL_sk_set, ensuring errors are attributed to the general stack library. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3a09dfb4f9aace93d2c20d6d1b4968cc583884d6)
- Fix unreachable code reported by Coverity, removing invalid branches. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7ccccb26d6de39eced5b16ffce6040c9547bfe74)
- Correct the help text for openssl list -cipher-algorithms, accurately describing the cipher algorithm categories displayed by the option. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2eb75291c1357cdaf852e0da613edc14f3d5ae4f)
- Fix compilation failure of bio_comp_test, supplementing or correcting build dependencies for the compression BIO test. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bb2bbd53d49c510c2ae705d86e8fd2ed829cbd92)
- Fix record and key state handling in TLS 1.3 encryption tests, restoring expected test results. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/50bed93a7655dc6d990aa42e52b316a97e2dc820)

- Provide compatible definitions for Windows environments that do not define SHUT_RD/SHUT_WR, fixing QUIC socket code builds. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4ccb89bba76655d72285f94619f2f4014319d3d9)

- Fix tainted input propagation reported by Coverity, restricting external input from entering sensitive length or index calculations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/00e38edcfb95b556a59de96e0c18343828929c8f)

- Expand the PACKET_equal test buffer to comparison length plus one, avoiding possible out-of-bounds reads reported by strict compilers. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/91b7520e2385a513ad879dfa8fe8e45466315a27)

- Fix FIPS label script parameters and invocation after GitHub Actions upgrade of actions/github-script. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ada6f0533d3299833b27e623ff1bfe3134e8e466)

- CMAC key generation tests now use a cipher mode suitable for CMAC, no longer incorrectly using ECB as the underlying MAC algorithm. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a0783b83a3bd05a07ea64567995c7642621b4aa6)
- Fix set-but-unused variable warnings in DJGPP builds. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b9179ae5552ab59fa46bad5721125a84c76f8ab4)
- Clean up all record buffers when destroying the DTLS record layer object, fixing memory leaks in the destructor path. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/20c7febc860ae8e67f52912ee205d2e324e7beed)
- Fix spelling errors in test descriptions, making failure output accurately describe test behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cc910f1b316a6ea3d4df57e719adda1922393f8f)
- Fix return status checks in the speed command, avoiding treating failed results as performance test successes. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9dd009dd513276e602b6592bc337a8563a1a82a1)
- Correctly check the return value of EC_GROUP_check_named_curve, distinguishing non-named curves from check failures. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3b6154ccaf3e64bcdfda4859f2b98ef21b08c5b2)
- Validate the encoded length of the IPAddressFamily extension, rejecting inputs whose length does not match the address family structure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9351f675fab42abbc321f0994bff7e0b27cfbe57)
- Update openssl.txt content to align release/documentation descriptions with the current project state. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cab5b3a344199d54dd4432dbc6d4b361e10e11d1)
- Supplement header file inclusion handling for VMS builds, fixing missing declarations in specific VMS compilation units. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3c4e250eafd789bb8ff905cd2ec9dddc7e1656d2)
- Fix Coverity issues in X509v3 address extensions, validating range and address pointers before access. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/26cfa4cd85f6b26dd7a48c2ff06bfa4a2cea4764)
- Fix expected results in CMS CAdES negative tests, correctly failing invalid CAdES inputs. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/122ea851de77f761d7bc0ec5f3a7fc23e3a5d8be)
- Add the OSSL_NELEM macro header file for the QUIC TX Packetiser, fixing build failures. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/394f6f246af23876f3d7a0332eb194aaa5127643)
- Clean up formatting in CMP and CRMF source files, fixing whitespace and indentation issues reported by check-format. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/357bfe73453b018c7aee94cbb4f6eeca8b85695a)
- Fix the storage location of request states such as genp in CMP contexts, ensuring request states are read from the correct OSSL_CMP_CTX field. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/19ddcc4cbb43464493a4b82332a1ab96da823451)
- Fix handling of PKIStatusInfo where failInfo is unset or missing, avoiding misjudging legitimate default values as errors. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cba0e2afd6a222aa041e05f8455e83c9e959d05b)
- Clarify recipient selection and return semantics of the CMS_decrypt interface, and release temporary objects created during decryption to fix memory leaks. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/25dd78048b69c2a780ab1a5378b62447c77a5e75)
- Release the old decryption key before replacing it with a new one in CMS_decrypt_set1_password, avoiding leaks when configuring passwords multiple times. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/911045afda06bec038ccd15e9f849bff05b6f1ee)
- Stop adding misleading errors when recipient mismatch is not the cause of decryption failure, cleaning up the error queue output of CMS_decrypt_set1_*. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/60ea150b1f535e4f0c76e23af4130b3861fabf54)
- CMP gen_new only checks ITAV items matching the request type, fixing issues with reading incorrect extension information that caused generation request problems. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7e3034939b40ee15013bdba9ff6178de6bcc26d4)
- Clear genm_ITAVs when OSSL_CMP_CTX_reinit resets the context, avoiding reuse of previous general message information when reusing the context. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1c04866c671db4a6db0a1784399b351ea061bc16)
- Fix incorrect skipping of some evp_test cases under no-gost configuration, allowing tests that do not depend on GOST to still run. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d5e1fe9c04c7eb28e21070e3dfe0d2242504a9bc)
- Fix the cumulative index comment in P-521 felem_inv, ensuring the finite field inversion code comment matches the actual array offset. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/055d029610712a281aed0c23ddd3c8f4dbf40f80)
- Fix occasional assertion failures in attribute storage, correctly synchronizing or maintaining internal indices of newly created attribute entries. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/92a25e24e6ec9735dea9ec645502cb075a5f8d24)
- Fix Coverity reports in the HPKE implementation, checking input pointers, lengths, and allocation failure paths. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/450f96e965f0d5e89737755364df5933b5085639)
- Fix no-gost test configuration errors after rebase, restoring test filtering under GOST-disabled builds. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9ba4f489ecd30901603d66a8ec578cbca08fac06)
- Fix NonStop build issues, adding platform-specific fixed-width integer or system function compatibility handling. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d861bc03ee2ea9945f2a52f04548398ea0b92f94)
- Reuse already computed algorithm or length information, avoiding multiple repeated calls to the same query functions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d9ed3068df038811211b1f9c9f2f4ee2a6840aa3)
- Adjust HPKE test expectations in builds where ChaCha20 or Poly1305 are disabled, avoiding references to unavailable algorithms. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/36b4d7a69836fdf0ede1ea00879b26047bf93056)
- Fix parameter and return value checks in the rsautl command, preventing illegal options or RSA operation failures from being ignored. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4c3fadfe57b94f71fa83786726046b8833997c7c)
- Check the return value of EVP_PKEY_decrypt_init, aborting decryption if initializing the decryption context fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b794476df71441a6d30740ab9fadcc0f6d18d3d6)
- Check the results of BIO_set_write_buffer_size and BIO_set_read_buffer_size, returning an error if buffer configuration fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/25d02f333b9a5531fa88db294f69a8347f275858)
- Fixed AArch64 CPU capability detection on platforms lacking getauxval, using available fallback mechanisms to obtain features. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f97ddfc3059ff568919e92597b2691d5366fd34b)
- Fixed handling of BUILD_METADATA to ensure build metadata in configuration or version information is correctly preserved. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/83a5bd80708adc6726deac390e405a7b50dec540)
- CMP message validation confirms that the local protection algorithm type matches the actual protection type of the message, rejecting the message on mismatch. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fc93335760686ad7cf3633d457caf18b0ac83ea2)
- HPKE context restricts the range and timing of sender setting sequence numbers to avoid sequence number rollback or reuse of AEAD nonces. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cae72eefc3fbdd2f7a1a065f237bf3943619bca2)
- Fixed duplicate locking in X509 verification path to avoid deadlock caused by recursive acquisition of the same lock. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4d0340a6d2f327700a059f0b8f954d6160f8eef5)
- Fixed build rules when EC is disabled and KTLS is enabled simultaneously, avoiding incorrect references to EC headers or methods. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2dded44a4911250acb989a535d2bad0bcf0ccc78)
- Disabled false-positive stringop-overflow warnings in s390x and m68k cross-compilation CI, keeping other strict diagnostics unchanged. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8b63a305bf2db0e980cb76040fa66a17f781d6c7)
- Run memory leak checks only when UBSan is enabled, avoiding leak tests relying on invalid tools in non-UBSan builds. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1ca61aa56090356bbdbb16cf48916fbd9886c78d)
- Extended error code generation tool to support generating new error categories and more accurate error reason descriptions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2ee2b74cc7b78d2fd3c15dab08adb76fee6249f9)
- Fixed subsequent issues in deterministic ECDSA implementation, ensuring RFC 6979 nonce and signature results conform to the specification. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a5342007e7832cbd427fb62af24998f81d20c5f6)
- Fixed Armv8 SM4-CBC regression, restoring correct handling of CBC chaining and block processing in vectorized implementation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d89e0361d5ff5b32c24edac6c60c5ae38714e6c3)
- Return more specific error reasons for legacy signature algorithms rejected by security level, facilitating identification of the specific disabled algorithm. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/97b8db1af2f71059ecea986e4d12fc6a23699a74)
- Fixed RSA test selection when old FIPS Provider participates in merge CI, avoiding unsupported self-tests triggered by compatibility matrix. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/dc45d4c6faeb53bb68401141d899b9f857bbc51d)
- openssl storeutl now allows querying certificate objects by both serial number and issuer simultaneously, no longer misjudging combined conditions as invalid. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/abdf35158e4398deedcf160c28bd07c7080edf47)
- Fixed unused variable warning in CMP verification test, maintaining strict compilation pass. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/97446da7e05bd7164f5c36b68b8bef13a63e06a5)
- Fixed spelling and time difference calculation in custom timersub macro in timing_load_creds. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1cf2557063b142db3684b780c301f8ed609f1e84)
- Released resources pointed out by Coverity, fixing memory leaks in corresponding code paths. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a167e048a40151f9884014680c9a765ef79c3b44)
- Updated pyca-cryptography submodule to fix failures in CI dependency tests. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a8e7bc7c7445c0323ca9a0a9c96cf5ce5e2c82f1)
- Fixed logical conditions in modular exponentiation zero tests, correctly covering boundary results when base or exponent is zero. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/42061268ee8f9ae0555d522870740fc91b744f4f)
- Fixed BIO_f_cipher not fully propagating the underlying BIO's flush state during flush, ensuring encrypted data tail is written out. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e51dd6ee1bac6b54debea3f48c6f58b761229b73)
- SMIME_crlf_copy returns error on flush or BIO write failure, no longer ignoring final output flush failure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9e5bd8923bff3e4f0cbba05c7dadfe289c66eb6f)
- BIO_f_asn1 returns correct error codes on several write/state transition failure paths, enabling upper layers to identify ASN.1 encoding failures. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0f48050b5a8881870b8e25382f817b3a3dc14f16)
- CMS encrypted content initialization writes error and returns failure when algorithm OID cannot be obtained, avoiding output structures lacking algorithm identifiers. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bf3f8f2c0ea7bdfb007079aade8e01a06e79874f)
- Removed conditional compilation branches in trace category implementation, uniformly defining trace categories and simplifying platform builds. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/78bd646b2f6a18cf8515e05a5f3efadff03b3920)
- Fixed string initialization in init_info_strings that could trigger undefined behavior, ensuring initialization table entries are valid. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ee17dcc7ffbd6621f82838c75792f19aa97bd5d7)
- Fixed spelling errors in INSTALL.md. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0e4e03c8528ab54a5b125582afdf2cdadfb6c9bb)
- ssl3_mac returns failure when digest length is zero, avoiding division by zero length for MAC padding calculation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/624efd2ba6f1dabdcdecf17c77bd206c421efdaf)
- HKDF changes info buffer to allocate on demand and allows larger info input, avoiding fixed array limiting valid parameters. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e8115bd1654d5cd7718109679b2047ca573083a8)
- Fixed multiple spelling errors in documentation and comments, unifying terminology and spelling format. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a53d4f83fcfc3e12581da29f55ca5867d1e47ae0)
- Fixed CMS RSA-PSS saltlen test judgment under MinGW, verifying salt length according to actual Provider return format. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/55e2dd8c3162d7313d9408cb20fca8a4fe6e6f5a)
- Writes SSL_R alerts into error state, ensuring error string changes remain consistent in record/report paths. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a2a09af086e97da35225ec952f2ae75c833b19e7)
- Fixed SM4 test failure on big-endian ARM, adjusting tests or byte order handling to match platform implementation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/accd3bdd11bd4a69fdba42bbeead28945fe50e56)
- Reverted unnecessary unaligned access fixes, restoring architecture-related access optimizations that pass CI. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f83490fb9ce4dd1c09d4f94526fbcad14bd2fd85)
- Rewrote memory access in rsaz_exp_x2 to avoid undefined behavior under strict aliasing rules. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9506a2e274c643b94a2c265019ea9288f99a521a)
- Fixed random seed option macro name and description text, accurately explaining corresponding entropy source configuration. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9c3de015121fb4ebbecccfbbda9eed8d4a3cb2d5)
- Allowed replacing QUIC DEMUX read BIO, enabling connection multiplexer to switch underlying network BIO during runtime. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/964f0deb81f3025c11e451dc37f8e2f1c85548ed)
- Fixed QUIC RX handling of received packet references and releases after linked list restructuring. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/043a41ddeeaabc192c25aa0b46ccfc4546e4d3df)
- Fixed QUIC transport parameter encoding for zero-length parameters, correctly writing empty parameter values and length fields. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6946f1184aa4b0e42cc9c502115bf6c5dd72fa90)
- QUIC Stream Mapper adds CSM stream ID management and peer stream limit checks, rejecting creation exceeding peer stream upper limit. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cbe7f586ad42b7cf6d16b11a6d614798df0a5d29)
- Revised QUIC front-end API version string to match new implementation version. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6848e5eeeeae8ae28bfe7dcaa8d24673923a6ee8)
- Tidied QUIC source formatting, unifying indentation and code style. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d50e750e13f5a8f615da56ee73ddbd1a23007ebe)
- Cleaned up QUIC_CHANNEL and fixed a connection handling error, reducing duplicate state checks. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e28f512f045b91d4c52b8b9f2ea0800b24203a76)
- Reverted QUIC front-end API version string modification, restoring expected version identifier. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0651e0547490af29b33ce9fd55eb20b2f1499c51)
- Revised editorial errors in QUIC comments and documentation, without changing protocol behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/81b6b43c4a56e4158ee4059fc03c10f970423506)
