- QUIC RXDP returns different error messages for stream state errors and frame format errors, facilitating identification of receive failure causes. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2d2fd151d4e699da269e586713e785a758f45157)
- Fixed frame handling error when QUIC TXP sends STREAM data, and cleaned up duplicate send path logic. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/05f97354bb6fe29731a8a25a475a115a2c44720a)
- Tidied QUIC CSM code style, unifying state transitions and condition formatting. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/75b2920a219f9ec222e663ec5d2bb6101dc612f9)
- QUIC demux explicitly identifies and reports network receive errors, no longer treating socket errors as no-data processing. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/66eab5e08e3a5c7026a3468915ef2e42a43a1479)
- QUIC QTX explicitly handles UDP send errors, ensuring errors are propagated to upper layers instead of silently dropping packets. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0550829f53fe74f884e382ec0ec323342f77d181)
- QUIC_CHANNEL escalates underlying network errors to connection-fatal events, terminating unrecoverable transport states. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/df15e990ff2557fd43fe4d661c8e1988a3d0ffcc)
- CMP command still correctly writes obtained certificates and certificate lists when encountering non-existent certificates, not corrupting output files due to missing partial objects. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/60c3d732b7b634290e4ec5d7ca6fb9b0a37592bf)
- Fixed AArch64 SVE ChaCha20 lane/byte order handling on big-endian systems, ensuring output matches reference implementation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cd7a8e96bbdbedb05f7f13a8fb13fb5d9d0d17a6)
- Fixed SHAKE XOF output reading in s390x CPACF Keccak implementation, correctly handling variable-length digests. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/76aa4f3ac0d76e58f2111cbf87ae7f25c8766190)
- Fixed potential NULL pointer dereference, checking creation and lookup results before accessing objects. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/235ef96049dbe337a3c3c5d419dacbb5a81df1b3)
- Fixed s390x Ed448ph pre-hash signature implementation, ensuring message digest conforms to RFC 8032 signing process. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f225fbf9521395aff86e85883db9bcb083eab154)
- Fixed error code returned by i2r_ADMISSION_SYNTAX on parsing failure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/53b5d6c30f3b8eaf7a582da2265c0d1cfe14d54f)
- Fixed QUIC front-end SSL_get_error mapping for states like WANT_READ/WANT_WRITE. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e30c502ae930295b889cce7375a83a8c742c68b4)
- Fixed QUIC front-end SSL_read WANT_READ signal, correctly notifying caller to wait for network read when no application data is available. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/af8b52cffe303c41510d1228605f9fcff9af0ee3)
- Fixed QUIC TXP generation of CONNECTION_CLOSE frames, correctly setting close reason and connection-level frame content. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7f9d12495e3782fa384d9de3516478a490abc177)
- Fixed minor issues in QUIC test server, ensuring reliable test startup and echo results. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/091f532e0ef57cff71ab710c07eb5f6f9bb88b22)
- Fixed byte order swap in Padlock AES assembly, restoring correctness of AES-192 and AES-256 encryption results. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7331e7ef79fe4499d81cc92249e9c97e9ff9291a)
- Established independent build settings for different BSD distributions, avoiding OpenBSD inheriting Linux-specific linker parameters. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c3bd630df0c3630c66155fb8c4baf54810d24695)
- Fixed return value check of RAND_bytes_ex in FIPS 186-4 RSA prime generation, stopping key generation on random number acquisition failure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a2b01ae1c84ccc250d5d5cb5f2f8714573e3f11b)
- Fixed accumulation and comparison of total_timeout in CMP RR/GENM transactions, ensuring single requests and entire transactions share the correct total timeout deadline. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d7d1d0928af2f14e7e187fa8c78115d0d1aa28eb)
- Corrected error text for X509_V_ERR_INVALID_PURPOSE, and supplemented missing flag clearing description in X509_VERIFY_PARAM_clear_flags manual. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ec6cbda0f2e435ae0efaec308dc5569c75bb759b)
- Fix memory/index corruption when looking up CRLs in the hash directory, ensuring CRL hash links can be correctly retrieved. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3147785eb23bb27080a0b7accbbff46ac471e86c)
- Fix Windows build errors by adjusting platform conditions or system type declarations to be compatible with the Windows toolchain. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4a6e5a11c72a4f3ec082cc065b44906409ad8fae)
- Fix typos in source code and documentation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/91b968bc8e4125a1202e7955961f8e7dfcd17513)
- set_trace_data avoids freeing the caller's BIO twice on copy failure, fixing a double free in the OPENSSL_strdup failure path. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0fec2121c0c40d8b098896c9bdf629a48fbafa63)
- Fix the modified flag and return value of set/sign operations for X509, CRL, and revocation entries, ensuring the modification status accurately reflects changes in encoded data. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7e0013d9736db005695bdc7524295c3b52d711d3)
- The req command improves error reporting for duplicated() argument parsing and simplifies handling of duplicate parameters. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/66fc90f18c44cdac0126c35ffedb99ba7a8b9825)
- Complete the checks for EVP_CIPHER_asn1_to_param, handling missing ASN.1 algorithm parameters or conversion failures. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/114d99b46bfb212ffc510865df317ca2c1542623)
- Fix logical dead code identified by Coverity, removing unreachable judgments or branches. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/01a17b24f6649fc192ba6bb9ea34e28ce9678e6c)
- Fix handling when QUIC TXP sends STREAM FIN fragments, ensuring FIN is correctly sent together with the last data segment. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cf06f34727447fad04cbcbce2b8b3f269c1b9307)
- Adjust QUIC test server send/receive scheduling to eliminate timing uncertainties caused by different operating system network stacks. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c8e7f842b002f6c5081ff1519a9fe40d81cdeadd)
- Fix multiple state transition issues in QUIC FIN support, ensuring consistency among final offset, send completion, and close events. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1d40b151e252490e2187235d50228119c2b6f6d5)
- Fix the return type of compute_pqueue_growth so that priority queue capacity growth results use the correct size type. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6a9453572533e4a22e6f60fe8f6b7ef0823d9c1f)
- Fix error handling paths reported by Coverity, ensuring failure returns and releases created resources. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/00407fbf0b25d65f5e6d99defdb081432e810449)
- Fix another Coverity error path, adding return value checks and resource cleanup. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a4347a9a57dcb985283bba03dd3b16294b55945b)
- Add platform fallback handling for atexit crashes on NonStop process exit. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8bb55680e46c868b0aa09682c2bef954231841b5)
- Complete return value checks for X509V3_add1_i2d, correctly reporting errors when extended encoding or writing fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ecd445464a73bb3f125327a604dd13ad16303ebc)
- Adjust static library build options to avoid legacy.a and other libraries duplicating the same symbols in certain configurations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f6a6f7b6aa84dab44384780cb77050d15c5f575e)
- Release references held during destruction of Provider sub-contexts, fixing a potential memory leak in crypto/provider_child.c. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e788c772b12eea5ced4ce46619e13acf0e0eb6ba)
- Complete return value checks for EVP_CIPHER_param_to_asn1, stopping encoding when writing cipher parameters to ASN.1 fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e3663717fc16bd140f54ee7f1600bdced7f9ea66)
- storeutl and gendsa reject extra command-line arguments and add corresponding help descriptions to avoid silent ignoring of user input. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/323c47532ea7fc79d5e28a0fa58ea0cc4d5196b8)
- Fix symbol ordinal spelling or entry errors in Ordinals.pm introduced by a previous PR. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/77c8d6d703efd1d935b5c2603fd31f4b15b1214d)
- Fix build failure of SM4-XTS on Apple M1 Mac mini by adjusting architecture selection and assembly support conditions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cded5d05253df6eb29e025ab25d763805493479a)
- Fix GENERAL_NAME type confusion in nc_match_single, accessing and comparing the corresponding data structures based on the actual SAN type. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/748f478f814bc8e418542c68599ec7dbcbac97b2)
- Fix timing oracle in RSA private key decryption, reducing observable time differences between error padding and private key operation paths. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b1892d21f8f0435deb0250f24a97915dc641c807)
- PEM_read_bio_ex sets header/data output pointers to NULL after failure cleanup to avoid callers holding dangling pointers to freed memory. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ee6243f3947107d655f6dee96f63861561a5aaeb)
- Fix use-after-free caused by release order when BIO_new_NDEF fails, clarifying ownership of the returned BIO chain. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9cc85002a1138235bdc272b837d7eb32d6b7aa95)
- CMS -stream output checks failure status when BIO initialization fails, preventing reporting an unsuccessfully established stream as successful. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/625faca931957719c76fb16094cf5b6e0debe23f)
- Check parameter and public key pointers before FFC public key verification, returning a clear error without dereferencing when p is missing or input is empty. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bcec03c33cc00a7b5eb89ebeeee59e604570a86a)
- Reject importing DSA/DH keys lacking domain parameters, avoiding creation of parameterless key objects that cannot be verified or used. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9ac82e2e7225759c21e712cba6dfe8da22ef7e47)
- Fix type handling for x400Address in GENERAL_NAME_cmp, using ASN1_STRING_cmp to compare its ASN.1 string value. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7880536fe17c2b5450e279155bedd51771d28c9f)
- Add header file includes for VMS files, fixing type declarations or system API compilation issues on that platform. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ae08ed08e49b945d516294106fa185bb136669b2)
- Fix parity handling when randomly generating two-key 3DES keys, avoiding errors triggered during random key generation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/587e0407803af330c0b04238fcbce78521ce35d7)
- Check the return value of BIO_set_accept_name, correctly reporting errors when setting the server virtual host name fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a811b6305b1f98e8ec66b8a426d359150fea69b2)
- Release temporary resources created by s_server during initialization or connection failure paths, fixing application memory leaks. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8e2552b1eac4957214fed55457f64d7d5164ca37)
- Fix check-format.pl's recognition of the starting line of function bodies, accurately checking formatting inside functions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/33c7ad364fd757e312fc08b686e5a1f32a14f925)
- Fix check-format.pl's recognition of __cplusplus conditional compilation blocks, avoiding false positives in C++ header guards. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4381c02e9b2a22f2254ca30d73e84519a40513f3)
- Fix the formatter's statistics for whitespace and nesting issues, making check reports count actual violations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ee17148792943f7f6455213a3b9a86a4cc082c9a)
- CMS_decrypt_set1_* checks the content pointer when encountering content types not suitable for decryption, avoiding null pointer dereference. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/69b995c6fbc38163d69573803b7aa38ca64b074a)
- Complete return value checks for CMS/BIO_set_md, stopping verification or signing when digest BIO configuration fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/abf654645dee168b229f3fa6a365f6a8e4dd7c31)
- Check the return result of the default_check helper function, returning failure when the default Provider algorithm is unavailable. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/650f0474282330e3eb2a3df0eff5864bbdcf5845)
- Check the result of CMS_SharedInfo_encode, stopping KDF derivation on encoding failure without using incomplete shared information. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ba061818e9d76f332e8914dfe9168577b2378dde)
- Correctly handle return values and clean up copied state when BIO_dup_state fails, avoiding ignored copy errors. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/89601c72471a4b6bbb9e877f5c54f20eceba5f01)
- Check the return value of BIO_set_indent, not continuing formatted output when indentation setting fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/826374921a6b92293fd87655416eda8ef07301c8)
- test_get_libctx safely handles NULL Provider parameters, no longer crashing due to accessing Provider context. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/13cb5416f4dbbf50690fe129894e2856623af21c)
- Fix two incorrect return codes in CMP message validation update functions, mapping internal failures to correct 0/failure values. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7f7dafe98b10ef54593df175b901654a0f9890a7)
- Return accurate error reason codes when CMP transactionID or recipNonce does not match. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ed9c6f363ef2e9e5a7de6a1639e0518f86419c2d)
- Fix comments for OSSL_CMP_CTX_set1_recipient and set1_issuer, clarifying the exact meaning of recipient and issuer names. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8c29fa21a7983862f0bd4744523ffee61f17ca22)
- Correctly maintain recipNonce when subsequent requests in the same CMP server transaction fail, avoiding inconsistency between response retry values and the request chain. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/036a444fdc77b36e0bfcc8b765acf96036f5a0b3)
- Fix the return value of the AArch64 AES-GCM unroll8 kernel, uniformly reporting success status for encryption/decryption operations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4596c20b86871b2bb0f9a7f6b855c0b7f0d4fbf3)
- Fix regression in OSSL_CMP_certConf_cb's check for newly issued certificates, correctly confirming the certificate object to be confirmed. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6b58f498b3f5d8e4c9197c3c5228fb450e33aaaf)
- Fix KBKDF dup callback declaration to OSSL_FUNC_kdf_dupctx_fn, matching the Provider KDF method interface. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/344d3b326d573a0eeb5dcbffa643bc06f00023ed)
- Fix CMS test failures when DES is disabled in the build, adjusting tests according to build features. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c400a1fe477b44a5eacbad2be8d50f2eaa92925c)
- Fix temporary object leaks on error paths, releasing acquired memory and contexts on failure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c10ded8c2c862992c98b83909a679aa0bb448a55)
- Fix use-after-free, no longer accessing object fields or callback data after the object is freed. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c4a44e7b84c5371e6f1ac1e0a80d5fc737b2dc1c)
- Fix Coverity warnings in fuzz targets, initializing and checking variables for fuzz input handling. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1dbfd7fe24bcd50117bc57942b2046e483a3c5a5)
- Fix error branches in FIPS 186-4 RSA parameter derivation, returning correct status when prime or parameter checks fail. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/835b90a19cdb2901cdba8a26955ccaacf0d73062)
- BIO_s_dgram_pair no longer writes errors to the error queue when read operations are retryable, distinguishing temporary lack of data from real failures. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3f968ecf479ed6ab8a2b25bd1077300baf2287a7)
- QUIC receiver treats unknown frame types as protocol errors, no longer silently ignoring unknown extensions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ce3106baba7601bfaf1d1412221e18dec4878e18)
- Trigger a QUIC protocol error when the peer does not send transport parameters, refusing to continue establishing the connection. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/62d0da12e397811e26be5b5be8a1cfe54de5031e)
- BIO_recvmmsg propagates datagram endpoint errors when using BIO_s_dgram_pair, and correctly reports the number of processed messages. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/533390e46f8e2ea55d66d35fd54e724c2fb77571)
- Check the ASN.1 method pointer before calling evp_pkey_asn1_ctrl, fixing a potential null pointer dereference. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ab5a172f1b41b12133b95822d5bf004c322965cb)
- Fix double free when cleaning up request context in OSSL_HTTP_get, ensuring OSSL_HTTP_REQ_CTX is freed only once on error branches. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7fed5193d242938d9ac5a0c1cb32b22b33379a06)
- Avoid freeing CRL again when CMS_add1_crl fails because internal CMS_add0_crl has already taken ownership. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6f9e531003fd736e8e96d9a1a57f7763da9722b8)
- CMS_add0_cert ignores duplicate certificates when they already exist instead of reporting an error, keeping the set addition operation idempotent. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/65def9de8088ae39d8f251e0b57f1a0f204daa14)
- find-doc-nits outputs more specific error diagnostics when manual links lack section numbers. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9a2f78e14a67eeaadefc77d05f0778fc9684d26c)
- Added return value check for ASN1_item_i2d; on encoding failure, no longer uses ungenerated DER data. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5df5032ab02d7a17e07435de777d730bae190253)
- Added return value check for RSA_public_decrypt; on decryption failure, correctly reports error without reading uninitialized output. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8195e59986031f6f33e2569551d771904433fa04)
- Fixed potential infinite loop in nonce/candidate value calculation in DSA signatures, ensuring algorithm terminates and returns error on abnormal input. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3a4e09ab42654b3d223f0f8dd1a9c58b2902ddcc)
- Fixed potential infinite loop in ECDSA signatures, making bounded retries and failure exit when random nonce does not meet conditions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5f820bd7535b871fdfdc0303c3af23ba4be901f0)
- Fixed incorrect setting of digest property query in finite field parameter import, ensuring FFC algorithms use the specified property query. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3307338e26862070eaacad6ec7537a63a63b8a90)
- Fixed time difference calculation for TLS 1.3 PSK early_data age, avoiding incorrect acceptance or rejection of early data due to ticket age exceeding limits. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0513a38364a7a45c946fdd8f7d87b8a3ae01ffbb)
- Fixed error handling issues identified by Coverity, ensuring complete failure states and resource cleanup. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/dd573a2fc1e8806c67420a5d6df0de175745aaf8)
- Fixed type conversions to avoid compilation warnings or truncation due to platform integer width differences. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b1cd268c034268f4d37c665ee4b5148f9d8700bb)
- Cleaned up error queue entries in TLS early data rejection path, avoiding misleading errors from normal rejections. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/79abf0dff90d54840b8afa6270ea816ee2edd345)
- Added tests to verify that the error queue is empty when early data is rejected due to policy, distinguishing normal rejection from real failures. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/10836921e52ff9110c12b4b9f984e7c5ef1c89cc)
- Fixed command wrapping behavior of util/wrap.pl on OpenVMS, correctly handling platform command line format. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1939ee7f252ffebd91c29384db4133290489e026)
- Fixed OpenSSL::Util::fixup_cmd invocation of util/wrap.pl, avoiding loss of command arguments or duplicate wrapping. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5ec4f45ef56dfe7733b70413f8e78426f19fb433)
- Fixed type inconsistency between size_t and int in length calculations in cms_ec.c and rsa_sig.c. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/559e078d94f1213318105b03f4e88b848fc28314)
- Fixed erroneous diagnostics in speed measurement, no longer reporting unrelated operation failures during warm-up or boundary sample phases. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/07626ea9e5400bd857a58a4da06756748701e9ed)
- Fixed PKCS12_newpass support for changing passwords on PBES2-encrypted SafeBags, correctly re-encrypting key and certificate bags. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9191dfb0ef48f95002aecfa8e11d9db434b4093d)
- Fixed demo Makefile target names, ensuring corresponding examples can be built via make. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/67bfdfa17bc4ca8e2b819316299bb82748394c45)
- Released objects identified by Coverity, fixing resource leaks. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b36e677f8f563301207ea658e29b3a8f88c2951b)
- Corrected license/copyright disclaimer text, maintaining complete license declarations in source files. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c879f8ac56170a5cf929fab8067beb2a5902be2b)
- Fixed AES-GCM AVX-512 assembler version detection for Apple Clang, correctly identifying Apple LLVM version numbers. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2dbddfab08ed5464f9408f6f870c727c89835e5a)
- Fixed CPU feature flag variable name used by rsaz AVX-512 assembly script, controlling assembly implementation based on correct capabilities. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e5dd732749f524e2a0aaa67f8d514d34863dd89f)
- Fixed Configure test logic for -mips parameter in CFLAGS, avoiding accidental deletion or incorrect identification of MIPS compilation options. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8b399c5e51b4d010650cb344a67a696daf096a05)
- Fixed SSL_has_pending determination under QUIC connections, reflecting decrypted application data pending delivery. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/560470b5d97ea5f122d53d1b85e9f384f8ba9023)
- Fixed segmentation fault when calling SSL_shutdown on QUIC connections, correctly passing connection object required by shutdown wait predicate. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4e15b44864df0d3c6306a9bf354fea92147834df)
- Fixed socket event and timer handling in quic_reactor on Windows, ensuring reactor can run on Windows builds. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d293ebde01fc14dabbd64fd6e42dc837be7b1fad)
- Avoided destroying contexts still held by caller on error paths, fixing premature release due to error handling. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6ec3d3125f76aa9f11c133333f868c42b9b585c4)
- Tidied code and fixed a typo. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f42d6b7ae62a2b2914b144153af56096f9b4a6d5)
- Fixed compilation issues for CMP under Windows strict warning builds, adjusting platform types and function declarations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6f792f4d27b47213166e0fa9c9b10a3eab85b8f6)
- Fixed message recipient nonce update when cmp add handles -reqin, ensuring new requests built from input requests have correct nonce. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4b0c27d44514abb4ad2bb1153db96f106910fc04)
- Fixed CMP HTTP test server port and client/server configuration confusion, avoiding tests connecting to wrong port. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1738eb212bacb71d66db25e1a38ce2d420b72e35)
- Avoided duplicate definition of OPENSSL_armcap_P in multiple objects on 32-bit ARM builds, ensuring unique global variable for CPU capabilities. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/93370db1fc76ad37bd53cfbeb948d1ded43d3b2a)
- Fixed segmentation fault in EVP_PKEY_CTX_dup when context is not duplicable, safely handling returned null copy. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/864c70e43ea5f1d7fe20bfea457e53e79fd46b6e)
- Fixed lock acquisition order during QUIC handshake, avoiding deadlock from repeated locking in handshake callbacks. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4a530180e5b9921bc3d1b5228d9be96f2a0b4b07)
- Fixed QUIC idle timeout expiration determination, closing connection based on last network activity time. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4648eac53385c5e04bd4ec9dcefe04a74d4221c3)
- Fixed boundary conditions and state transition issues in Thread Assisted Mode. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/dbe7b51a8e3c0e20c3412fe4ff8309730a135255)
- Fixed concurrency race in QUIC test server, avoiding unsynchronized access to connection objects between test and server threads. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/99ed85bba9de5d9983d3796c18e62041d3ce6575)
- Fixed spelling and lock type in Thread Assisted Mode, uniformly using CRYPTO_MUTEX instead of mismatched read-write locks. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c4208a6a983278316c6615980f335f685c0be472)
- Fixed other state and build issues in Thread Assisted Mode, ensuring valid thread switch configuration combinations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9cf091a3c5f34277dca1cac979c7d632c7236d7b)
- Fixed QUIC test assertions to match new background thread processing order. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/54b86b7fa313789e7cc79317c692410c8f336660)
- Fixed memory leak in ECDSA_sign on intermediate computation failure path, releasing created digest and big number objects. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4befe81a99b89c52b749a87eece82c1cba4fab12)
- Fixed NULL pointer check order, avoiding checking wrong object or missing real nullable pointers. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6469043bbabc9728aed61d7708c32e2ae319be1d)
- Fixed compilation errors under clang-cl 16 and later, adjusting Windows built-in functions or assembly conditions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fae5a15573fb314d0a5bc06f3929fd5ec6ffe5a5)
- When new IV length or key length is same as current context value, no longer repeatedly calls Provider parameter setting, avoiding unnecessary state resets. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/eb52450f5151e8e78743ab05de21a344823316f5)
- Reverted previous RSA timing oracle fix, using implementation verified in subsequent fix. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4209ce68d8fe8b1506494efa03d378d05baf9ff8)
- Fixed ECX (X25519/X448) key generation path, ensuring Provider can generate valid Montgomery curve keys. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/40f4884990a1717755df366e2aa06d01a1affd63)
- Fixed typo in ssl_ciph.c. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e35a21334172997c40928933e1f44e3ba4d2317c)
- Fixed calculation of least common multiple in RSA multi-prime key checks, and added regression test to verify such key parameters can be correctly validated. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/efbff4de3e259cee71a4e1bbd86b30ebd86bbdae)
- Moved library context and property query parameters of HPKE context creation API to the end and made them optional, allowing old invocation style to continue working while allowing callers to specify provider lookup environment. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8b7b9aac444625195486efd10273694830c41398)
- Fixed `CertReqId` handling in CMP p10cr transactions, making request identifiers conform to RFC 4210 rules. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/25b18e629d5cab40f88b33fd9ecf0d69e08c7707)
- Changed declaration and documentation return type of `OSSL_CRMF_CERTTEMPLATE_get0_publicKey()` to match actual return value, avoiding callers using public key with wrong type. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/09f30b0c96e39e3a07f8e6854c5468332534c585)
- Fixed SM4-GCM test failure on Kunpeng-920, adjusting hardware implementation to be compatible with that processor's instruction behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/524c2cab6a64f408a5444188c2052b4d76b06775)
- Corrected typo in VMS build template, avoiding wrong names in template text and configuration instructions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/04e0abc8bb1c24534d16cc930b611ac1d03bc9bf)
- Fixed buffer over-read in ARMv8 AES-XTS decryption, avoiding access to bytes outside input range when processing trailing data. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/72dfe46550ee1f1bbfacd49f071419365bc23304)
- Check return value of `EVP_PKEY_get_int_param()` before continuing curve checks, avoiding use of undefined curve number when parameter reading fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4e5f3d691343a691ddae739c51f7ae71e9893c98)
- Only call `pthread_key_delete()` when pthread key has been successfully initialized, avoiding operation on invalid key during cleanup of failed initialization path. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/31295ca02c0a2d7209a33047c7f6dd1dabc12c93)
- Unified exit status constants in BIO example programs, ensuring demo clients return consistent and understandable status codes to shell on success and failure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/09ff84bd2752cac649f57cfbf95b49dbce1c69ee)
- Fixed build regression when POSIX I/O is disabled, avoiding certificate processing code referencing unavailable POSIX file interfaces. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3155b5a90e6ad9c7369d09e70e81686f4b321a73)
- Fixed `-rspin` handling in CMP: HTTP client initialization no longer fails when file name is insufficient, and added parameter documentation and regression test. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1f757df1f3de0c18cc22a4992d66e9a7b113f61d)
- Fixed data processing error in AES-XTS on AArch64 big-endian environment, and added tests covering that byte order scenario. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4df13d1054e143f1cbf13fa347491807289f87b7)
- Fixed lookup logic when stack is unsorted, allowing search to find matches even when sorting precondition is not met. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/eb0935fd21220708e4321374380db497e9c5ecdf)
- Fixed byte order error in MD5 assembly implementation on AArch64 big-endian environment, and adjusted build configuration to enable correct implementation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/004bd8f97d11bb7ac5f2de89f7060e03222b60fe)
- Adjusted RSA key generation to ensure generated primes satisfy `p >= q`, conforming to implementation's expectation of prime order. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/dc231eb598460aec239c7f597f560bca47d9f72a)
- Fixed compilation errors in Windows Cryptography test program under MSVC, enabling the test to be included in corresponding toolchain builds. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b5a635dc2113e1bc807ea358a670146c813df989)
- Fixed boundary handling in QUIC congestion control and its tests, ensuring acknowledgment and loss feedback update controller state per interface contract. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0f1c43c441acd23ba2c14c93f9ce059348a617b0)
- Fixed issue where QUIC channel did not pass callback arguments when invoking time callbacks, ensuring callbacks can access context provided at creation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/66ec534861cc278bfb074a8fa3fa1fe3385723f8)
- Fixed detail issues in QUIC ACK management and congestion control tests, keeping acknowledgment processing consistent with controller expectations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bb7f3701cecb49ca0faacb5e46d11fd07cf2ee02)
- Fix the conversion of legacy control commands generated by EC parameters to provider parameters, ensuring the curve NID is correctly passed to the new interface. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ac52fe5f5ae7a1d062f09adab7744e3a3b2ddbcf)
- Fix the bidirectional conversion between EVP_PKEY context parameters and legacy ctrl commands, avoiding incorrect mapping of parameter reads or sets. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1009940c14716ac03d5f161bdb4ae626ec6fe729)
- Fix the Padlock engine's handling logic, restoring correct behavior of the affected hardware acceleration path. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/849ed515c7838943eab42de5c29d6a1f91079a11)
- Adjust the input handling of the configuration file fuzzer to avoid specific configuration inputs causing the fuzzing to get stuck in a timeout path for a long time. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5f3adf396b06ee3b81938468995e69cff4ca64d1)
- Fix a use-after-free issue in QUIC where a stack object was referenced after being freed, avoiding use-after-free during connection cleanup. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ca9ef8ebf5908a6115990967df648d8f29e66f42)
- Fix a memory leak in `engine_cleanup_add_first()`, ensuring registered cleanup callbacks are released after engine cleanup. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8c63b14296f117b07781509ced529a8955d78fb9)
- Fix a typo in a Makefile variable, ensuring the variable name matches its reference. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e5a054b7fcafc98a1dbf4358da390dc6e7759de5)
- Fix the premature release of host and port strings in the CMP HTTP TLS info structure, avoiding TLS callbacks reading dangling pointers. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/20d4dc8898edc12806ead2100ac09b907662aff6)
- Correct a typo in a variable name, keeping the code identifier consistent with its purpose. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/060f370ebc21b4d334a71ca8b8ab54f22199f177)
- Fix TLS 1.3 handshake failure under the legacy ENGINE API, allowing legacy engine interfaces to still participate in TLS 1.3 key processing. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/be6497aa208948c960a28363bac98a429677bd9d)
- Fix `SSL_get_wbio()` possibly returning the read BIO in QUIC scenarios, making it return the BIO corresponding to the write direction. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0f06e7f5a6a7451a6cbf4eb0d86ebc9bbd95c55b)
- Correct the exponent value in the P-256 implementation comment, avoiding inconsistency between the comment and the actual elliptic curve constant. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/13069d0144096ef8cecc82fb7fcd1a1eed93d7a8)
- Return an error when the target buffer for EC public key parameters is too small, avoiding the provider reporting success after truncating public key data. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/91070877adb905f51eb4b19b730d42fc257bae13)
- Fix the file reference of `insta.ca.crt` in the example OpenSSL configuration, making the CMP example point to the correct CA certificate. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/14ca1b6f4694ad27b1163bcafda1683f4dd05a30)
- Fix the behavior of the CMP client returning an error response when a CSR is provided but no private key is provided, and document this input condition. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2d6585986f3b754750b25e7a296a08e7129a5320)
- Implement inbound stream rejection policy in the QUIC channel and application layer, so that new streams are accepted or rejected according to application configuration. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/995ff282103d444844a476ae6aba4a05936284fa)
- Fix lock handling in the QUIC_XSO stream object, avoiding concurrent access races, and update corresponding tests. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/13ac037d0148b6e461ca635bb1c627a4b759318a)
- Fix boundary errors in the QUIC stream manager, ensuring state consistency when streams end and queues are updated. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8b5278942be94b5764b93c0633ea4162685264ac)
- Fix the issue where the QUIC application layer did not recognize unidirectional streams created by the peer, so that inbound unidirectional streams enter the correct receive path. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/acc6fde0d44d22c7fa4578c967aee69c3fbcf350)
- Fix detail issues in the QUIC multi-stream implementation, keeping stream state changes consistent with interface behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/571aff4bfaf0407cadba2e304b60c0364684cee5)
- Fix list operation errors in the QUIC receive path, avoiding accessing or removing the wrong linked list node when processing the receive queue. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/93651dc245353ceda661b55332f0b163c4a3e8e9)
- Adjust QUIC multi-stream tests to accommodate the uncertainty of loopback interface selection on Apple platforms, avoiding tests depending on fixed local interface behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9715e3aacffece002f94725fb2105601111f6fa6)
- Fix minor issues in the QUIC multi-stream implementation and clean up code, ensuring tests and implementation follow the same stream lifecycle rules. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/22b1a96ff798cf73f4b573bff1d9f80236d3f102)
- Fix the judgment logic of `SSL_get_stream_type()` to return the correct type based on the actual direction of the QUIC stream object. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/59c5c016e53256e949225a2dd751b3450129cd72)
- Initialize QUIC availability state before use, avoiding stream processing paths reading uninitialized values. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/433d107a9b2b0250d6806ed6fdc147117637fed5)
- Fix QUIC multi-stream test failures on macOS, making tests adapt to the platform's runtime behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4f2d32d6b6b4b80ff5f55c5462b545516d252d11)
- Supplement and correct QUIC multi-stream related documentation, keeping API descriptions consistent with the implementation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5fc256cd6b991cf0fb5f7cb1da7be14c7e90653f)
- Rename the QUIC inbound stream rejection policy setting API, making the function name more accurately express the rejection policy it configures. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/83df44ae53c3c3bb1e79785af38ab52bb4f865cb)
- Fix QUIC reference acquisition logic, ensuring reference counting and lifecycle remain consistent when acquiring shared object references. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5da3e02c5eaac2bec9c14165d62874b1232213fe)
- Fix the handling of SM2 curve parameters in `EVP_PKEY_CTX_set_ec_paramgen_curve_nid()` in the ENGINE, so that legacy engine interfaces can correctly configure the SM2 key generation curve. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/43d5dac9d00ac486823d949f85ee3ad650b62af8)
- Fix stack data corruption during UI input reading, avoiding overwriting adjacent stack memory when reading user input. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a64c48cff88e032cf9513578493c4536df725a22)
- Fix test cases using `NEW_CONNECTION_ID` frames, making frame content and connection ID sequences conform to implementation requirements. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c301149ad43ee2c611e7b8d4f2826f524f3385aa)
- Fix the type of the PKCS#12 MAC salt length parameter, making the interface type consistent with the actual salt length value. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/26cf0767a71743de00cd20f90526052358d67d03)
- Rewrite potentially security-risk code patterns in HKDF, avoiding sensitive data handling methods that may be misjudged or exploited. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/56a51b5a1ecd54eadc80bed4bfe5044a340787c1)
- Fix the parameter validation logic of `EVP_MD_CTX_get_params()`, making it check based on the actual parameter type rather than incorrectly rejecting valid requests. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b501df3cefebcdaaeb7d6480b7a7b82d68927873)
- Change the QUIC `SSL_get_error()` error state to be maintained per stream, and organize the error reporting process, avoiding one stream's error contaminating other streams on the same connection. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/faa3a180efcf17c8fc7db354367d2b03d89f3042)
- Fix stability issues in QUIC multi-threaded tests on macOS, reducing occasional failures caused by platform thread scheduling differences. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7ba8f79a0ff5f085c9f1b4471496180c052360f5)
- Fix conditional compilation and execution issues of QUIC when thread support is disabled, making QUIC work in no-thread configurations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/629b408c12c56b2c9e3279de8658718e8dd658a2)
- Unlock the mutex before releasing the QUIC application layer object, fixing a lock release order issue found by the thread sanitizer. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/45b7c7e06e8d28ad9a7ea0f7662ec04f283f7c36)
- Fix trace output errors in `SSL_trace()`, avoiding incorrect interpretation of message content in diagnostic information. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b946a3eed5c40230955d5acc67884c3fd2fd6b18)
- When the CA configuration file does not provide certain optional items, no longer output misleading errors, allowing `openssl ca` to use a minimal configuration. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c8aec16383c7a9aec76b28e6eb95d36bef6f7e56)
- Fix unstable rehash results, ensuring consistent hash link results when repeatedly processing the same certificate directory. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/31c94b5e1159b5435b2354e6525355ec33683ecc)
- Adjust the QUIC `SSL_get_event_timeout()` API design to return the timeout for the next event that needs processing. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7ea497134733f8197f359fe3243ad24e97df0f1a)
- Fix details in the `SSL_handle_events()` documentation, clarifying the behavior of this function in handling events. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b626a0f1fdd306845e5ff7632329d32d5f9e2fba)
- Fix a potential use-after-free in the X.509 memory allocation failure path, ensuring error cleanup does not continue accessing freed objects. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/de53817ec386ea9e943d8f33716945dd9dbe1f31)
- Check the return value of policy layer node addition failures, avoiding continuing to build an incomplete X.509 policy tree after memory allocation or insertion failure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/95a8aa6dc0e283b1560dd3258d2e9115c02659b1)
- Use a read lock when `RAND_get_rand_method()` only reads state, reducing lock contention when multiple threads query random number methods simultaneously. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7f2c22c1b9ec46070aa588d7f4a5ad5fe4a60bf4)
- Remove outdated `ERR_clear_error()` comments in CMP HTTP code, avoiding comments inconsistent with current error handling logic. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3179995f114fca4cb9958116e353ad6b686b7ecd)
- Fix compatibility issues between ARM64 assembly code and GCC 4.9.4, enabling older compilers to build the corresponding assembly implementation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/09bd0d05a6ab9eb4965763c100edf9b86ae03d2b)
- Fix path handling for the `make tags` target in out-of-tree builds, allowing tag files to be generated in out-of-tree builds. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2a6a04406b2f4e81bf8fd67c3fa19e8b18912710)
- Ignore fetch failure errors when looking up legacy algorithms, avoiding misleading error stack information remaining after falling back to legacy implementations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/dc4ccc70245be870e2ef2e382d16234673bf28cf)
- Fix a memory leak caused by not releasing the content key when freeing encrypted CMS content. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7a1857483938b6b6eec5b8760c68c71a71296cd2)
- Avoid acquiring a write lock when traversing activated providers, reducing blocking of read-only traversal paths on concurrent provider operations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fc570b2605b8eb18c3903543aaf0234b1f698c8e)
- Fix failures in OS Zoo continuous integration, restoring normal test tasks on that platform. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ab77026cecb7fed31e8df99655da1d0f302c4ccc)
- Change to include the correct `poll.h` header file, fixing build issues caused by using the wrong system path. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c4ce0e3303d586bc4ec41f380c94ccb4fb84e2d5)
- Fix a potential memory leak in the `t == NULL` branch, ensuring failure paths release allocated objects. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a33842efa51ca3f021310e10f444afef1e779fee)
- Remove unused `i` variable assignments, cleaning up invalid writes reported by Coverity. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/82b81161de41fde034f0d19e2ccddf190e4d8baf)
- Remove unused `rctx` assignments, avoiding retaining random context state that will not be read. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c71b72acf24f1a3b4d9d07fe552fe1d5f76a2e35)
- Remove unused loop variable assignments, cleaning up invalid writes reported by Coverity. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/265920f2a78ff295264824b5d8294dd45173ae42)
- Remove unused `bags` assignments, avoiding retaining invalid state in the PKCS#12 processing path. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ef1ed411e1d526cdf6b87613d1b6021ab07d0f2e)
- Fix build errors on the Cygwin platform, enabling OpenSSL to configure and compile in the Cygwin environment. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9b9c42db3b7e7807e0d3311356fb0316af085171)
- Fix incorrect `ERR_raise()` calls, making error library and reason codes correctly correspond to actual failure situations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/959c150a1dcc4535c2d94ac6f3310566723911f1)
- Fix boundary handling when comparing two empty X509_NAME objects, making empty name comparison results stable and as expected. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ec59752835f616860cd9451d6cfcea16bfc3ad05)
- Fix compilation failures of the CMAC test program when DES or SM4 is disabled, allowing optional algorithm combinations to still build tests. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fbff5b57715471910ea99d28f03ba6a417b45135)
- Fix spelling errors in the code found by codespell. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/eb4129e12cdf7fe64b3ce352f539e3dbeb1b1321)
- Fix issues related to QUIC key updates, keeping status updates and test behavior consistent. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/692a3cab11932d2aaa7b1b628cacc513ba73a5e5)
- Fix function signatures in ARMv8 assembly source comments, making descriptions consistent with the actual interface. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6c0ecc2bce64cc86948a51f80f832b5e48a9ebea)
- Report the actual NID when an unknown algorithm error occurs, helping callers locate the specific algorithm identifier. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f52aec35260627c37f114352843dc0bc22311a17)
- Fixed TLS 1.2's `supported_groups` extension handling so that the group list provided by the client is correctly negotiated. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e609a4565f9ededc5c982175c297bb08058f767c)
- Avoid copying the entire structure during s390x capability detection initialization, reducing unnecessary data copies and preserving default capability settings. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/32d2b5fdd93ceee192abefb3fd0ce8f9a1b329c2)
- Fix the issue where `openssl speed -multi -evp` outputs incorrect algorithm names, making multi-process EVP benchmark results show the actual tested algorithms. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/33c09341bb081682535be0450ff6032df47ea141)
- Fix the QUIC multi-stream test script for group 18, making test commands and expected results consistent with the current implementation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0e1da9d7ec3c7dd2af49be0d9be3e0848c167608)
- Fix a typo in `s_client`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/55d3a6be6ba3af9781631e74833ea1dcbd4008e6)
- Fix memory issues in object allocation failure and reference counting management, avoiding leaks or incorrect release of shared objects on failure paths. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/97beb77f319f119957235233396627bb22283da0)
- Fix detail issues in the QUIC SSL behavior implementation, ensuring new API restrictions and state queries remain consistent. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d6e7ebba3370e06ea4dfae6381dfe0e1c21070e3)
- Fix the QUIC multi-stream test for group 19, making the test run stably with the current stream API and thread behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9289e59c9581d76f2c3e570d3d8eed6aea598bb8)
- Fix compilation issues when building with thread pools disabled, allowing configurations without thread pools to build correctly. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4890f1f75231aaf83e51c44342317fbad678b6c4)
- Work around relocation errors in m68k cross-compilation, enabling assembly and linking steps for the target platform to complete. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d001006e50e6ca591459f6b63d2365532578a36a)
- Fix TLS 1.3 HRR test failures when building with ECX disabled, making the test no longer depend on disabled key exchange algorithms. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c3832d79db0c8dc08935d86200d427f945deb92e)
- Fix random failures in QUIC multi-stream script 19, adjusting test synchronization to avoid relying on uncertain thread scheduling timing. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/76696a5413db0a93e374f9f0f55e5694f93ecc0e)
- Correctly report QUIC network errors to the application layer, preserving the actual socket error reason for connection and read/write failures. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5c3474ea563ed95bb7c86c08867139613655276b)
- Save and restore the error stack state during QUIC error handling, avoiding internal attempts to overwrite the caller's previous error information. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9c3ea4e1d7580fc061dfb754b620adb3439e683f)
- Continue driving QUIC event processing when `SSL_read()` fails in non-blocking mode, avoiding reporting temporary no-data as a final failure before events are processed. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/780b2527476a60f4a2bb791c2d4b1b72f6f0b423)
- Fix read/write timing issues in `test_quic_write_read()`, ensuring QUIC tests handle retries according to non-blocking API conventions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ff9728c6d5d23ebaa73cb729c8110c0582e66280)
- Fix compilation failures when building with SRTP disabled, allowing `no-srtp` configurations to complete the build. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9f3cd808b33767ae65e29461ce17a091049e7364)
- Handle `gf_mul` symbol conflicts, avoiding build failures caused by different assembly implementations defining symbols with the same name during linking. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/15e041b751c96ecf668a701d09a373d517610eae)
- Fix `SSL_trace()` assertions in QUIC API tests on big-endian platforms, making trace values compared according to platform byte order. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b6bf1cbf1d48bd02f3fae2fb0bf922100efd0be5)
- Fix issues with setting and reading RSA-OAEP labels in the legacy ENGINE path, ensuring labels round-trip correctly between old interfaces. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/64b1d2fb06c9a5233dcabfe130036ff95c3fdaae)
- Fix protocol consistency issues in QUIC packet processing, ensuring packet states and frame handling follow RFC 9000 rules. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0911cb4a072f55b5f982635faeaa7a992a14181f)
- Fix incorrect WPACKET usage in QUIC tests, ensuring frame construction buffers are initialized and finalized in the correct order. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7eebc3546fa5738a807722345006fe97fcad3013)
- Fix timing and state handling issues in QUIC multi-stream tests, making multi-stream read/write coverage stable. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/70cafc4479e7faf005068580d0354d5ab1c8c0d4)
- Fix random failures in QUIC multi-stream script 19, eliminating assertions that depend on occasional scheduling order. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/de9564bdd72142ab9353f84cc2f186ad559a8eed)
- Adjust details in QUIC implementation and tests, ensuring stream close events are processed in the current state machine order. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8aa6a436dce7c8139909d9a694c6ce2afea4e416)
- Fix QUIC multi-stream test failures on macOS, avoiding tests depending on platform-specific network loopback behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9ff3a99ea625c116833c950f51bff2554f6f7d1b)
- Supplement and fix minor issues in the QUIC implementation, making tests consistent with protocol state transitions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f36504cc393e10abd35a8b6a25d8965cddcacf98)
- Improve error name macros when applications load keys, certificates, and CRLs, making failure diagnostics point to the actual input object that failed. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4085ba874ee0f76eda412a8820a379f27664d763)
- Fix `ssl3_do_write()` handling of write retries, keeping buffer offsets and remaining data correct after retries. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/404fb9965ed0dc7752d80f72c93358dfb45125c8)
- Fix the issue where creating an EVP context fails when combining legacy SM2 keys with the EC ENGINE method, allowing SM2 keys generated by 1.1.1n to still be used through the legacy ENGINE path. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4c4fefa5c78a49b63113aec35a2bc8d6d9432436)
- Fix spelling errors in the code found by codespell. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a024ab984e540bff65d25407496c34b3567b55a7)
- Map all QUIC protocol errors uniformly to `SSL_R_QUIC_PROTOCOL_ERROR`, allowing callers to identify protocol violations through a consistent SSL error code. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2b8126d8a8ded94ce010234a37d059f8d3b71b1b)
- Fix test expectations affected by QUIC ACK and congestion control changes, ensuring regression cases verify the new packet accounting behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/709ef4093520a91f4d723aef29c857c6fd625cad)
- Limit the time spent checking oversized moduli in `DH_check()`, avoiding malicious parameters triggering excessively long computations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9e0094e2aa1b3428a12d5095132f133c078d3c3d)
- Remove unreachable code in `cms_main()`, avoiding retaining CMS command processing branches that are never executed. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8c34367e434c6b9555f21cc4fc77a18d6ef84a85)
- Fix unfreed memory in `openssl speed`, avoiding continuous resource accumulation when running benchmark tests. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cc7e2b20de02959c328f96e464e5fb8b256a00e0)
- Fix the execution logic of `openssl speed` for EdDSA measurements, enabling the benchmark tool to actually run and report EdDSA operations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0c85bcbaeabe3a695831bec44ab87964725a51a6)
- Fix build errors when configuring `OPENSSL_USE_IPV6=0`, allowing builds with IPv6 disabled to still compile. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9c8d04dbec03172d6ffe4eaa38ea4b1ac2741f26)
- Fix compatibility issues in Clang 6, 7, and 8 strict compilation modes, keeping builds with older Clang versions passing. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6cac1ce47128f5095b1f0b99f304589db034c305)
- Reject inserting duplicate attributes into `STACK_OF(X509_ATTRIBUTE)`, avoiding duplicate entries in the signed attribute set. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7551264186f176ca5801aa84d60c7b91d8fba31f)
- Fix error handling in pipeline tests, making tests report correct results when encryption or decryption fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/06a0d40322e96dbba816b35f82226871f635ec5a)
- Fix unreachable and redundant code in the X.509 certificate path lookup by file, ensuring correct branch returns when lookup fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ae29622f39f7deb0599624cc7a771bfc05f1353f)
- Provide a usable definition of `SIZE_MAX` for HP-UX, fixing the issue where the constant is not found during compilation on that platform. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7f14656e1cc002a09b2d6148302a1fc71a30f7cd)
- Update the GOST ENGINE dependency version, fixing its test failures and restoring related engine tests. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b1fdeaca8c1f19e546a1648b871472ec3091bd36)
- Fix QUIC STREAM FIN frame generation errors, ensuring FIN and corresponding stream data are sent with correct offsets and order. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/553122cd7cefaad68826f37455c7644dc6cd72b9)
- Output the corresponding script number when QUIC multi-stream tests fail, making it easier to locate the specific failing case. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0786483adffec3ce0f2e77133388b7f119df4975)
- Automatically drain unfinished QUIC streams and fix related state handling, ensuring no incomplete stream resources remain when the connection terminates. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/63fac76c2485c7c675fcbd5bc719c969c76ecc01)
- Fix a regression in `X509_VERIFY_PARAM_add0_policy()`, ensuring newly added policy nodes correctly transfer ownership and remain in validation parameters. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e3d897d3fa3b48bb835fab0665a435469beea7ae)
- Fix operand usage of the `vshuf.b` instruction in LoongArch assembly, avoiding incorrect vector byte rearrangement results. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/780ce3849f9efc5404d94464e0eeff966bebbbf1)
- Add an optional include line suppression parameter to the code formatting helper tool, allowing callers to control formatting output content. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fafb7d30038678bffba739eaa933b926d2ee194f)
- Fix the conflict between the sentinel value for certificate request IDs in CMP and the ASN.1 integer parsing error code, avoiding misinterpreting invalid parsing results as valid request IDs. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2c8d9f19e351a84d4329fbe2f68a4a8a49cad3ef)
- Fix CI build failures caused by incompatible changes in the QUIC branch, making the merged interface and implementation match again. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1e7cc86b7516bb035b91c23a38f2d9e6323d33c9)
- Unify type and legacy interface names, avoiding ambiguity in calls or documentation due to mixed old and new names. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6861f5a703ebd6547bbe598d7fe4c7de9912e384)
- Allow test initialization functions to fail without forcing help information to be printed, enabling tests to correctly report initialization errors without unrelated help output. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/badf3c162d2b67635beee3fc948db32f13d274af)
- No longer output fake empty data when exporting an empty selection set, avoiding misrepresenting no actual selected content as a valid encoding result. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1ae4678cebaa13604c0f31bdf2c64cd28bdaf287)
- Correctly map QUIC TLS errors to QUIC protocol errors, so applications receive error notifications consistent with protocol layer semantics. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/80bcc4f1aeb67f0a05dbff04372e0b9563d4a779)
- Update missing transport parameter tests, verifying that connection failures produce the correct protocol error code. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/457678d68238433b321805eb158a2e15d9331195)
- Fix a typo in the QUIC channel. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7c793cd343cd1fad50091f8eb264e5ce7ddcc6e9)
- Add consistency tests verifying that QUIC CRYPTO frames with incorrect offsets or lengths are rejected. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/27c2f62f96287d7bbe2aade5fc3e3c86e88c4496)
- Improve error reporting in the QUIC channel, providing callers with more specific handshake and transport failure reasons. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/741170bef340b31a32a94a4ea86cc0d7744c01b2)
- Fix a detail issue in QUIC code. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/828c9c6690dc2791cee7873cf6793db187b558bb)
- Reorganize QUIC TLS error handling, ensuring TLS errors are converted and propagated as correct QUIC connection errors. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7a2bb2101be4f4dfd9f437ebe1d7fd5dbc14b894)
- Change TODO comments in QUIC failure tests to ordinary comments, removing markers that no longer represent pending items. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/40e28577dd81d1b3e775240c4815ebce5d56f868)
- Fix the handling of `0:` labels in the ARM assembly conversion script to prevent numeric labels from being incorrectly converted. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9607f5ccf285ac9988a86f95c5ad9f92b556a843)
- Fix the ChaCha assembly implementation on 32-bit HP-UX Itanium platform so that stream cipher results are correct on that architecture. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6d38ccedb25f31dfab232e2669415fd4db18b20e)
- When releasing CMS content info objects, no longer report errors for non-enveloped-data content, avoiding normal cleanup triggering unrelated error stack entries. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/13342efbb9e16ec8f97b1ac5ab4aa2b3b3490596)
- Fix `OPENSSL_init_crypto()` initialization deadlock when loading encrypted strings, avoiding initialization threads waiting on each other. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6b1a1275b3f3f8af0b4e0603d529a7bb2da4402a)
- Fix the issue of using a freed `TXPIM PKT` object in QUIC send packet processing, avoiding use-after-free in the send scheduling path. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6a2b70e21b0ae4a6d1550a86833f3996b78b6755)
- Fix the regression in QUIC integer set after switching to list implementation, restoring correct set insertion and lookup results. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/dc5e5c51e2ffa8e6f472eeb13efea3b3e113a6d1)
- Fix the QUIC unidirectional stream test so that the test runs to completion instead of ending early after partial assertions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/839f6ac32f844164dcb965f4ee429188f0bf141f)
- Fix memory leak in event queue by releasing processed event objects. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/77a66117ab0c5bdd885d13dc302ace7010aca826)
- Fix memory leak in QUIC congestion control test, ensuring controller resources are released at test end. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3887546dcf98f1369d5a03eae9772a7deb0c9b89)
- Fix null pointer dereference in QUIC TLS path, ensuring safe error return on initialization failure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4d6ca885998f7c397d8224290a32a27967a97c0b)
- Fix null pointer dereference in QUIC integer set, ensuring safe error return for empty set operations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8761efb2ccc96f81201af279ec66e8ceeee9c7a3)
- Fix the issue of closing file descriptors twice in QUIC tests, avoiding double close during test cleanup. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/410a90f598a77546b847ba28b4bd8559ac57ab31)
- Fix minor issues in QUIC implementation to keep protocol state and test results consistent. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f2609004df4d91a365338e11d04ff67589f2d3e3)
- Fix QUIC API test missing wait for socket readability, avoiding premature read before data arrives. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a5df3fc093c8ef17152e6c645be6fa9a77c56679)
- Clear written digest pointer when key level setting fails, avoiding partial initialization state on error path. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9d005bafacbaf9e8ac8c2e1bf90b124c4254022b)
- Fix conditional compilation issue when both DTLS and TLS are disabled, allowing combined configuration to build correctly. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fb32f6ea42e6916ff88cc44cf5de6e63ba596aca)
- Fix compilation issue when both DSA and error reporting are disabled, allowing configuration option combinations to be handled properly. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4efd84fdd648279367683b280c9d9feb2ba54e9e)
- Fix use-after-free of object in `quic_tls.c`, avoiding use-after-free in QUIC TLS path. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/643f542a89bee93e043d0899b2a1ca700d1cc418)
- Fix memory leak in `OSSL_DECODER_CTX_new_for_pkey()` error path, ensuring temporary resources are released when context creation fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3d254b31344e82b8f10fda8bab196757a377eb63)
- Make decoder use the property query string passed by the caller, ensuring provider algorithm selection follows the specified property filter. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/39ed7636e0d8a90512e7ccb811cd0bfcb7a79650)
- Fix memory leak in error path of copying OSSL_DECODER_CTX, ensuring temporary context is released when copy fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e16c0103083af676af8c5564bb21585d4574f992)
- Fix memory leak of allocated object not released in `tls_new_record_layer()`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0577dbad0709f1b3717297420069c6160245e74d)
- Change TODO markers in QUIC new record layer to future work notes, clarifying unimplemented follow-up work. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/79cdbe893da0c613db97356d05c0b088e885707f)
- Fix spelling errors in QUIC trace output. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/604a607222933aebba0b5fbb09e9839d4e37faad)
- Fix resource leak in `ossl_encode_ctx_setup_for_pkey()`, ensuring created objects are released when encoding context initialization fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8ef63b6ff8301a0139c00df6c40173a63fd2db01)
- Remove TODO marker for QUIC TLS tick error reporting, indicating error propagation on that call path is handled as designed. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/68b9a32aa397030d9e49ee1ae84ca1ce6b58efd3)
- Improve QUIC error handling details, unifying reporting behavior for protocol errors and local operation failures. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/96014840b69b3ec2f82e230a27cc5c1fa3bfb1bc)
- Switch TLS handshake keys early when not sending early data, avoiding use of keys prepared only for early data during handshake. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/84a149254f977f502dd2314169812fc6eae8c309)
- Process type fields in fixed byte order in `SSL_trace()`, avoiding different trace results on different endian platforms. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1cc8c53b0fc06d148a9f62e5d2d5bcd859f948cf)
- Fix format string used when throwing error in `quic_tls.c`, avoiding mismatch between error log and parameter types. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5ad3cc19282e2f98189467cd6aeae8d0389b999b)
- Adjust default value of `ess_cert_id_alg` so timestamp signatures use compatible certificate identifier digest algorithm by default. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/10536b7f5b07aab3dc9631e94a56258155a1d942)
- Fix condition check for QUIC stream creation backpressure, making backpressure scenarios return non-I/O error instead of incorrectly triggering network retry handling. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7a5f58b2cf0d7b2fa0451603a88c3976c657dae9)
- Fix error merge result in QUIC multi-stream client example, restoring compilable demo code that correctly creates multiple streams. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/dac42bdce1ed19e646c2adf04b27fc92a9d0e374)
- Update Cloudflare Quiche dependency version to fix build issues caused by it. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1cfdbdd0d454b9b3882e364addfab920af3a29d5)
- Fix connect-or-fail helper function in QUIC multi-stream test, correctly distinguishing connection failure and success states. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a1d2a9d12d269ba551b1d2d3bc825aedad8984c9)
- Fix minor issues during a rebase, keeping code and tests consistent after rebase. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bd3b026faab3b5ee5aa6b52ba6eb4080bc144b28)
- Fix fixed-size handling in datagram pair and memory datagram BIO, avoiding buffer length mismatch with datagram boundaries. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b56b034e9afc980c846a61dbf581da3c46e67952)
- Fix spelling of deprecated macro in documentation comments, ensuring macro name matches code definition. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4d5cfb229be8a52cdf961d9b7c5f6c1c0fa3b4f7)
- Dynamically determine whether errors are I/O errors in QUIC application layer, to correctly distinguish retryable read/write states from fatal errors. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a954f761feb9ace245ea425d3b746ec6602580f3)
- Fix stack space allocation in PowerPC secp384r1 assembly implementation, avoiding temporary data overwriting other stack contents. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/50f8b936b00dc18ce1f622a7a6aa46daf03da48b)
- Provide clearer error message when QUIC handshake does not negotiate ALPN, indicating ALPN is required for QUIC connections. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/881e3299dcadd65cc4a2843ba47abc6548ced8f4)
- QUIC connection processes and reports only the first protocol error, avoiding subsequent errors overriding the original cause of connection failure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/549d0a700be311d9a65560cb9eed3f725546b5ed)
- Improve QUIC application layer error reporting so callers get more specific reasons for connection and stream operation failures. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2e1760118b5ba316cdf0b144a21fb4c21f796c71)
- Fix minor issues in QUIC BIO and channel implementation, keeping address mode and datagram query behavior consistent. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3760747ff452fcb3e29190e670073253c5b47d49)
- Fix boundary issues in QUIC application layer implementation, ensuring connection mode and stream operations execute correctly according to state. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8d7f034622c0235d06f4d6526f71dcab2f71b0c6)
- Fix interface and implementation conflicts after rebase, restoring QUIC API consistency. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7b1ca59995a0d0ad933b5d475face79b8ec99828)
- Fix minor issues in QUIC interface implementation, keeping error state and polling results consistent. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/016a80dcf441189ac6d84533f1951506116a3b98)
- Fix QUIC I/O error state management, correctly setting last error state after successful operation completion. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/72ca0b88fc8cd97a20528d2f92e145e181194a98)
- Add multi-stream test comparing `SSL_want()` and `SSL_get_error()` states, verifying consistent interpretation of retry reasons. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9ff816106c2b2ccbffe5c4e3619a840547088674)
- Avoid overwriting non-volatile XMM registers, following calling convention and fixing corruption of caller register state in related assembly functions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7b8e27bc2e02238986d89ef0ece067ec1b48e165)
- Handle non-I/O type retry errors in QUIC, allowing API to distinguish internal retry conditions from network read/write waits. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3a0012cb52bef4df54bd46946d7ff783c24b4305)
- Move ALIGN32/ALIGN64 macros to common internal header and fix their definition compatibility under clang-cl. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/12d08fe3a50f28fe80ff591e05d7f8253148afb4)
- Change default salt length of PBES2 KDF to 16 bytes, increasing salt length for newly encoded private keys and aligning with current recommendations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3859a027259b5b571eaf5e8cf4c0704611950c2c)
- Fix compilation error of `quicserver` when SSL trace is disabled, allowing test server to build with no trace feature configuration. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b12c07cfba9651ae80b7020ffe8e634f47581389)
- Trigger assertion when mutex lock acquisition fails, avoiding caller continuing to modify protected data without holding lock. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/17a0e930d2607e1d571c82912d5e1fa3393b2053)
- Fix newlines and redundant text in key, certificate, and CRL loading error messages, making command-line diagnostics more accurate and readable. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/81d037b8adb0232c8a4d4654f79c883dafb102bc)
- Fix issue where CMP command cannot reset `-csr` and `-serial` option values, allowing subsequent parameters to override previous settings. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/374945a9aa545d4d6f015de0b48cbed6a90258e0)
- Fix internal memory leak in `OPENSSL_MALLOC_FAILURES` fault injection path, ensuring resources are released when simulated allocation failure occurs. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e2cf38d5751d6b48c8625b622c3765d0a39958d7)
- Adjust CMP HTTP tests to work in both IPv4 and IPv6 environments, avoiding tests depending on fixed IP version availability. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/769e47ecddd1ca4292ffa792f45998629447d743)
- Correctly check error return value of `cms_sd_asn1_ctrl()`, avoiding CMS control operation failure being mistaken for success. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/00a413e2483257a17239cef5dde52df14926284c)
- Fix clearing and setting of OSSL_STORE error flags in provider path, ensuring successful reads do not clear errors and failures do not lose error state. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/17dd9a2c6262c00800301fddd9441a9c590a630e)
- Fix memory leak in `SRP_VBASE_new()` failure path, ensuring partially created SRP verification database is released. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/68e95f7840d0d8ac4e5e03381cf9d305578dd1c7)
- Fix corrupted output of `openssl req` command, avoiding request content and progress/diagnostic information overwriting each other. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d2873946dfaff5537ea3d1adf3890e33a3f276ff)
- Fix issue where CMP HTTP test error path unexpectedly terminates Perl process, allowing test failures to be reported normally. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c499cbc3239e3ac93fa5acf85cec7ea7df116518)
- Fix build failure when system provides only `recvmmsg()` without `sendmmsg()`, detecting batch send/receive function availability separately. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5d96106c43d5b4e2d97406e5d3934323ae5bd1b4)
- Make QUIC multi-stream tests support multiple test concurrency, avoiding mutual interference caused by shared resources and fixed identifiers. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/84f371a130dbe7a46595fbabd274f152a0e6385f)
- Fix a potential memory leak in `rsa_pub_encode()`, ensuring temporary resources are released when RSA public key encoding fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/285eb1688f05ad477fefc681bf05d0afedc46d40)
- Fix the lookup logic when handling QUIC SSL objects in `SSL_CIPHER_find()`, so that QUIC cipher IDs map to the correct cipher suites. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/547ea58821644bdc9089b2dcb163286d789f732a)
- Return an error when the cipher context cannot be copied or cloned, avoiding silent loss of the requested algorithm state copy. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/39d857bb610d25b3de4e414264246ec41753c446)
- Fix the `dupctx` implementation of AES-GCM-SIV, ensuring that keys, counters, and algorithm state are correctly preserved after copying. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c32c3f2653e6c6ac42e09a83a2f51f8667827a04)
- Fix a memory leak in `prepare_rsa_params()`, ensuring temporary parameter objects are released when RSA parameter preparation fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/46def829afa4d8bed8f53d484bdf842d65f0e176)
- Fix spelling errors in the code found by codespell. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/23def9d37156cc3b2c00fb45ec3b8e271a5d4563)
- Ensure that QUIC-TLS errors generated during the QUIC channel startup phase are passed to the API caller. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/982dae89d8d19fcb9cc2c3b8ba74afef352ecc41)
- Fix datagram BIO test failures on NonStop platforms, so that platform-specific return values are handled as expected. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/572f290c9c2d892d5f891c6b8dcebf4e1ac65aed)
- Further fix the test behavior of `BIO_s_dgram_mem()` on NonStop, ensuring the memory datagram BIO test adapts to that platform. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/58165d8da493d4271b8a026ef4056ecaeefd3916)
- Fix memory leaks in legacy SSL tests, releasing connections and temporary objects created by the tests. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/21f0b80cd4b32ba80843b812b01a6056daf14093)
- Adjust the wait time for QUIC test server tests, so that tests complete on slower machines without being misjudged as timeouts. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/556f33837af8691f9b03c716e47d6d06186a6752)
- Fix an off-by-one error in the QUIC multi-stream test, so that connection and stream operations use the correct state. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e501e8b606a2398d9b860eb10344113e9d1d375b)
- Fix build issues for SHA-3 when assembly is disabled on ARM64, ensuring the pure C implementation compiles correctly. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/46b43c9f98771139735656e541c8f4c8018c2667)
- Fix error handling during ENGINE cleanup, so that cleanup failure status is not ignored or incorrectly overwritten. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/00f2efccf5b9671a7af2b12571068258e9c255a5)
- Fix the failure to create PKCS#12 files when certificates contain auxiliary data, so that certificates with auxiliary attributes such as trust/reject (X.509) can be packaged. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/388a8e731445d190a46ec27b2ff5b4bf334d526b)
