- Fix regressions in EVP tests in the provider compatibility CI, so that tests use compatible algorithm selections and configurations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bbb6d620f6014274cb00a8186225447a2a114543)
- Fix the logic for re-pointing key data in multiple ciphers, ensuring that key pointers still point to valid storage after context copy or update. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0ca5cf989101cae6ffeaef3518e99839fbccb9ba)
- Fix multiple memory leaks in OpenSSL command-line applications, ensuring that loaded resources are released on command failure and normal exit. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8c040c086ca11a519975c58961a5dc933aa6524a)
- Fix error handling in `CRYPTO_get_ex_new_index()`, so that index allocation failures are correctly reported and the error state is preserved. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d4f22a915ac50570015a23ad794032c4fb9496cb)
- Fix error handling in lhash shrink operations, avoiding continued processing as success after hash table shrink failure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5fbfd641aeebdf4b29a0749e13a79a1e59502878)
- Adjust CMS tests in builds without DSA support, so that tests skip DSA-dependent cases without failing entirely. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/dee2129802d9694d72e8745aec2d8bd4577ee4ee)
- Fix signing with legacy application method keys when ENGINE configuration is disabled, keeping the legacy key signing path usable. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1acc3e8cc3c69187b55cc557c1bc03278ab38063)
- Fix code indentation to conform to project formatting. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6935101354e1ebcb43aa8afb158603f94c0f9bc6)
- Make FIPS self-tests not depend on the real random number generator, avoiding self-test results being affected by system entropy state. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fffa78c2fd01accd97c9229018d4c380f7a20335)
- Fix a memory leak when the FIPS provider is used across threads, ensuring thread-local provider references are correctly released. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/be203ea3d3a60a881993d1f7552084996d34ba0a)
- Fix compilation issues when SSL trace configuration is disabled. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7f5b29c4bf909964f1a73d80af1474f0e4a95624)
- Exclude SM2 ARMv8 Perl assembly scripts from the FIPS module source manifest, avoiding the build manifest containing non-FIPS source components. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b07a0b1ac272c4a75c5115e10f274e2d7a477658)
- Fix timeout scenarios in the QUIC multi-stream script 13, stabilizing the timing of waits and test server exits. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ad4af6dfca8344516bb658b1745a530635af9433)
- Fix the arguments and paths for lcov in the Coveralls workflow, so that coverage reports are generated correctly. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/07829409b6fb40ce4f5c4ec633180280909c732b)
- Fix indentation formatting in the EVP extra test file. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b49cafd86b295aa5e177d6c1368b06a1202ec2b3)
- Fix the behavior of `BN_gcd()` when shifting negative numbers, avoiding undefined results from negative shifts. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0f7a3b0caa33a87c900536dc1c02fa553d2193cc)
- Fix the Coverity warning path for reading uninitialized data, ensuring variables have defined values before use. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/31fc8a83bc9aa435ae40c3eff713ced441eaa011)
- Rename variables to avoid using `sun`, a name that may conflict with platform macros. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7ae31586a77c09d45838fff73b589b2958fbd18b)
- Continue running subsequent steps when CPU information collection fails in Windows CI, avoiding non-critical diagnostic commands blocking tests. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4ace824852f385002facf077c5be2815b0780032)
- Fix address construction and comparison in BIO_ADDR tests, so that assertions conform to current address copy API behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/581c87b088105db0bddaf80a572b45a23b74e929)
- Avoid division by zero in KMAC bytepad length calculation, preventing undefined calculations triggered by abnormal input. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/91895e39b10033178e662fc7427a09d7562cf8e1)
- Add the `OSSL_CMP_OPT_USE_TLS` option to the CMP HTTP execution process, ensuring the CMP client enables TLS connections as configured. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ac0677bd2394c04632f7ad526879a866b6ed149f)
- Improve malloc failure tests, extending verification of resource cleanup and error returns under fault injection. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3df5736cf303d2c69654ba1c295a9772b738608e)
- Fix bracket and separator errors in configuration, ensuring related fields are parsed as expected. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/008ca01e506d85acf0cc06ea8f219a883328344c)
- Fix the default inbound stream dequeue logic in QUIC, ensuring the default stream in the accept queue is popped only once. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cd138c33d82cc889fe6a16d18806fbe939279d25)
- Remove the duplicate key schedule pointer saved in the GCM context, avoiding maintaining redundant state. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/143ca66cf00c88950d689a8aa0c89888052669f4)
- Fix the incorrect linear search judgment under the EBCDIC character set. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a47fc4ed401da4e2d84e035cc4add566e85b03d0)
- Fix Markdown formatting in examples, so that documentation passes format checks. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9f54da4136063aa1c2aba8a80c15363ab9517997)
- Remove duplicate error output in application code, avoiding the same failure being printed twice. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1d768852e938ea1b4c6076df0c5a1e59f9027f8c)
- Reject inbound tokens exceeding the QUIC transport parameter length limit, preventing reception of oversized Initial tokens. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/461d41174b33e365677d21bf176d6959b15c2468)
- Fix a use-after-free in QUIC received packet handling, avoiding access to packet objects after release. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1f8a8c1de90ebdb4f3c9dbbf3d1329e3f025e946)
- Release created objects when QUIC record layer construction fails, fixing leaks on error paths. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d8a4451fa76c83ba08b42b38848ba9705fbe71a2)
- Improve the error message for random pool overflow, indicating insufficient random pool capacity. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0a8faac3c7cc2e88f46a8bdce5bd039dc22abdec)
- Fix handling when seeding from weak entropy sources, avoiding treating insufficient entropy errors as strong random seeds. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7998e7dc07d8f1f516af32887f2490c03cd8c594)
- Fix the issue where `memset()` only uses the lowest byte of the parameter, causing incorrect zeroing values. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0bf18140f491024232beca4e139c8feecfe207e9)
- Fix a memory leak in `openssl speed`, ensuring benchmark algorithm contexts are released. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4e09305ee092dea14b7d4feb3fac2889b41428f3)
- Fix the `s_client -quic -debug` combination, making the QUIC debug output option available. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1a91fda1839bc745e8359f82d19a5ef44ca36f7e)
- Output connection close reasons when multi-stream tests fail, helping to locate protocol or application errors. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/687326ce0ac56c405029cfedd435b2e6625a22e3)
- Avoid using `gets` in prototype parameters, preventing conflicts with standard library symbols. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2e471a740b621481b3f3236f82fdd677414900a1)
- Revert the mistakenly modified fuzz checker workflow, restoring the original check configuration. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/dc1cc3e4836e4135c1bf0b5bdd14ff86ff62acd6)
- Return 0 on error, so that callers can identify failure via the return value. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e63c1a1eab6fac262f0d107e058ff02aa1fef065)
- Explicitly report an error when the BN output aliases the modulus, avoiding results overwriting the modulus that is still needed. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/29c0d8156629a988db5a4af30704736579f7c313)
- Fix the issue of non-deterministic generation of AES-GCM AVX-512 assembly, ensuring consistent code generation for the same input. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7aff0b284bce2eb94bc1676118a50715330e22ad)
- Fix a potential null pointer dereference in legacy SSL tests. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a595e90032a246276c441cf7276a9cf8811a2aa4)
- Fix the error in printing request information in `X509_REQ_print_ex()`, so that subjects and extensions are output according to options. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e17656948663adb14b9030aeae70171da5588179)
- Fix handling after `EVP_PKEY_get_size()` failure in CMS/PKCS#7, avoiding use of invalid buffer lengths. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7eab00ec470693bd44c9de7ab5c06fe691aa3077)
- Release the label OCTET STRING on the OAEP error path, fixing a memory leak. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f77057815be474528ad0e798e08bc9b36a7d4a4d)
- Make s_client correctly accept data that exactly fills the `BUFSIZZ` buffer. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b4a33ba9aaf8022589dd15261f41d35729277a68)
- Fix the calculation of the QUIC send queue drain state, correctly identifying when pending packets are exhausted. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/18a431b6f17204ed2790f5a4fec265ffa5ca061d)
- Fix random failures and synchronization timing of the multi-stream script 38 on Windows. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6366192d56d22f44992aa891634085865b12d418)
- Fixed memory leak in the built-in compression loading function. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/21f1c2d9d513010f3585d5215b373ac4bef67c29)
- Fixed the judgment of the "all confirmed" status in QUIC send flow. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d6b7545b60e72a11894a9fc043325256495473cf)
- Fixed duplicate address binding handling in quicserver. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/28932ab1acc4372fbb4f0050fa7748f1fa079d0d)
- Fixed resource leak in the error path of `ossl_quic_new()`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b45d053fcd124854ea5bb7a24bea6a67d31489b0)
- Fixed issue with uninitialized variables in TLS path reading. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/96e58e32ffd7deaf5184d5e502b476554d39216b)
- Corrected parameter and return value usage in the uniform random number interface example. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/173dca8ea144d9fde41bd129a6a7890fbe3157d1)
- Invalidate previously set IV when IV length is modified, avoiding reuse of old configuration. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6941eb4d86661488faa3b5c07fc80cfc84ed8934)
- cipher update/final now returns error when key is not set, instead of using uninitialized key. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e7cb2117c9ccc0d531d521e45ae780be669e4ffc)
- Fixed error path that leaked keyslot when QUIC key setting failed. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/478d14cc3fde378fe722374bc1023c1e0cf00bec)
- Fixed dereference of null channel pointer during `ossl_quic_new()` cleanup. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e2c6a1d9a0df7a468f73a7ba62a33a860daa9bb6)
- Distinguished DH and FFC validation flags to avoid bit conflicts in check results. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6061fd58a30f181c956e5ba5a06932f99d2c9a18)
- Fixed memory leak in `custom_ext_add()` failure path. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9f9dc855adf7e9a65b4a0de7fa1ea19d0a55094f)
- Fixed printf format specifier and argument type mismatch in rehash. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2e40770aa55cde037fd4ef63e1a3de09cd5ca124)
- Fixed heap reordering error after priority queue deletion. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f5f4bc30f303a229d29dea148bc2d289f6fe04e3)
- Fixed encoding logic for implicit length STREAM frames when PATH_RESPONSE is included. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ab3b83636e02a12ee678eac8efc1515abe54b687)
- Fixed potential memory leak of SSL temporary PSK structure not being freed. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0fdf6e0a1b16c9f359c63fd94aaa7648dc8ef24c)
- Removed check requiring install prefix to be absolute path during cross-compilation, supporting target path configuration. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/50a536afe234f1ad8f77ccc961d74e5bdc0fbc21)
- Corrected variable name spelling. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/667f974ba7107b9c2a68fdac55b0f13bc84592fe)
- Fixed potential memory leak in `dane_tlsa_add()`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7f943d40bda4539d63da34ecfbbc8556f2603fb3)
- Fixed potential memory leak in `PKCS7_add_attrib_smimecap()`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f06d4082394d4fd6d9ba96a441bbb1c6ef524020)
- Fixed memory leak in `CMS_sign_receipt()` error path. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/757d6491ebebc541f54c0aa8043b8e5b31a58a7e)
- Fixed use-after-free when cleaning up `custom_exts_free()` extensions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/54e178640dee143742a11842469591dc315d5b5f)
- Fixed potential leak in `PKCS7_add0_attrib_signing_time()`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1ad7f4bf979fcb5fb50013b0c9106e26c6ed2fac)

### Refactoring

- Moved warning for overly long section names in cmp command to a position where it actually takes effect during parsing of related names. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/39a8d4e13219580c8c89a234d6db5d261408cadb)
- Removed unnecessary -certout parameter from test_cmp_http.t, simplifying HTTP CMP test invocation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/75cce8ddee8c108039d0329c4f84466aad0f9c3c)
- Removed settings overriding OPENSSL_ia32cap in multiple test scripts, avoiding forced changes to CPU capability detection results. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/39ed07454d8df794a36e2b6788043842a22b0909)
- Fixed cleanup logic during BIO creation, ensuring allocated internal resources are freed on creation failure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f11c01a666e9d5b97e859cbc74586802549dee00)
- Removed unused sctx variable, cleaning up invalid code. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f541419c792600f6ebe476168587d2a1436d87a3)
- Removed redundant RAND_get0_private() call, avoiding repeated retrieval of the same private random number generator instance. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a87c3247ca641f2593391bf44d47e3dccc7f8d73)
- Removed duplicate test cases, avoiding redundant execution of identical scenarios. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/10cf46c4ef93e22f999b7b6d2c3aadc4db965e5a)
- Removed useless strlen() call in x509 command, cleaning up unnecessary string length checks. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a18cdd28077be05ec88538be84a761469f3f20c4)
- Removed unused flag_fallback field and ossl_provider_set_fallback() function in Provider, cleaning up redundant fallback state. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/90c311315c15a4fea895fd317d9c8fe801ba04a0)
- Removed redundant isinited state variable in child_prov_globals, simplifying child Provider initialization state management. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/464c2b988ea149badabaf958a96fdc480df89dc7)
- Refactored configuration loading process, splitting Provider activation logic into separate functions, separating config parsing from activation actions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/07ba69483a7d8005a53284cbde55b9dac8c5c554)
- Renamed MIN() macro to avoid conflicts with same-named macros in other components. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f4f77c2d9756cee12875397276799a93f057d412)
- Cleaned up redundant code in o_names_init(), simplifying object name initialization. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c50bf14450f3cd242f2211ca7e500191053d8050)
- Removed duplicate ERR_raise() calls, avoiding the same error being pushed multiple times into the error queue. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ecf60b9e27c041e7c95669b52a399fc2f20fd0fe)
- Refactored test_fromdata() in evp_extra_test.c, splitting and simplifying the key import test flow. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5fbe15fd3b7c90a0cfb9f00be16225d8ed18b0dd)
- Cleaned up CMP enrollment test cases involving non-existent directories, removing invalid test scenarios. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7e5be5c3267dc90a77d243d900448c3a62c0b1c9)
- Simplified CMP request/response read/write flow, removing handling of req parameter that cannot be NULL in read_write_req_resp(). [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/61fa00a4d03f6808389bc1847937f72d184f0627)
- Removed redundant macro undefinitions in bn_asm.c, cleaning up redundant preprocessor directives in assembly source files. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/17cca0e85e83eac23069ddc5c5ebab6d7dd13ee1)
- Moved variable declarations related to RSA exponentiation to before code statements, compatible with build environments that do not support mixed declarations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2437832be1d0e11e6a601c19a18d7247aff22f0e)
- Removed duplicate parts from multiple getter/setter definitions in cmp_ctx.c, simplifying CMP context accessor implementation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/08dfbe0798f57ac9e9793fdfcaff54cfdf6b3359)
- Simplified constant initialization of server_args struct in http_test.c, compatible with GCC 4.8.x. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a497a90213b50c499f2a385e63e1fa6e13ef283a)
- Renamed getter/setter for OSSL_CMP_CTX trustedStore, making function names accurately reflect trusted certificate store usage. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6be83cc655af819be0e3f2701c726a2550357953)
- Refactored gen_init initialization logic in RSA Provider, organizing key generation parameter setting flow. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/22778abad905536fa6c93cdc6fffc8c736dfee79)
- Renamed enc_flag to enc_name in PKCS12 application, improving readability of encryption option variable meaning. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/870871e5df4f47611c38e81d3f50e38cbf362082)
- Cleaned up trailing whitespace in demo files, unifying example source code format. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b461aff257e57b8ba8e72667078fdf6d5047bc91)
- Removed empty statements in e_dasync, cleaning up invalid code. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/14951ef01f9b54d804baf2fabdf0a715c630827b)
- Refactored big number binary import logic, merging BN_bin2bn() and BN_lebin2bn() into a unified implementation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c2cab43574dbb65094d6caf4dc1bf691e826a4fc)
- Refactored initialization flow of bn2binpad() to be consistent with input preparation of bin2bn(). [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4e26fe508bf18732983212ca4749eabb1f02e142)
- Refactored bin2bn() to process input from least significant data block to most significant data block. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c30de601850f367e4c16ad91c0168a2e0dc647c0)
- Adjusted location of ERR mark management functions, placing ERR_set_mark(), ERR_pop_to_mark(), and ERR_clear_last_mark() into appropriate modules. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fbe88706a4f93f9e1940a07062d77c81b7fdf04d)
- Made make clean also delete the built FIPS Provider shared object. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d94c2f1b98318cea4416c4dcd5e5f878de8d458f)
- Moved e_os.h into include/internal, organizing internal platform interface header file location. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d5f9166bacfb3757dfd6117310ad54ab749b11f9)
- Organized KTLS record length checks, unifying validation logic for maximum TLS record length. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8fff986d52606e1a33f9404504535e2e2aee3e8b)
- Reorganized dependencies between configuration files and generated build files, ensuring configuration changes correctly trigger build rule updates. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/468d15179d6b0d0c2f5674bcbef66743925f2133)
- Removed no longer supported statistics tracking in LHASH, retaining only still valid statistics such as item count and node allocation, with other fields in output fixed to zero for compatibility. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/77d7b6eebb411fdb2c3d1390ac779300757aa9dc)
- Removed __ARMEL__ check in AArch64 assembly, unifying code path selection based on architecture/target features. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2bd5cde57e748d6ff21ed26cb448835f86b86ea1)
- Removed unused runonce and onfree internal functions in OSSL_LIB_CTX, simplifying library context implementation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ed7c64fc540c5808efe4092465af1147c76555a1)
- Moved cleanup call of case comparison module to end of OpenSSL_cleanup, ensuring locale state remains valid during dependent cleanup steps. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1d64b068ca74b68394c96fd2e3020235d32928f2)
- Removed duplicate header includes, avoiding redundant preprocessing and keeping source files tidy. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e257d3e76ffb848b7607b04057257323dc51c3b4)
- Refactored pre/post condition handling for algorithm method construction, clarifying control flow for cache hits, Provider traversal, and method registration. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/10937d5867039afbf869c8514245ed7599b61307)
- Added ability to remove registered algorithm methods per Provider, clearing corresponding cache entries after Provider unload or change. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2e4d0677ea858c619a33235265dbee19520a9d35)
- Improved algorithm cleanup flow in OSSL_METHOD_STORE, ensuring related cache and reference resources are handled when removing algorithms. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/03454ba2a234197c961920f1bac37cc9f4cf3f54)
- Removed internal function to get Provider method by numeric ID, unifying algorithm lookup via name and generic retrieval flow. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/16ff70a58cfb5c40197e6a940cf4666226f31b79)
- Clean up declarations and include relationships in public headers, making them suitable for C++20 header unit compilation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/eab9dbbdd1f102dc1a26549a77fcc5c167385cd5)
- Remove no longer needed VxWorks compatibility code, simplifying platform conditional branches. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a6680123643bc3289ecbcbd6bce844a814c1510a)
- Move OPENSSL_strcasecmp and related implementations into o_str.c, centralizing cross-platform string comparison functions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/71c17c36d913a82742c7d4ecd91ad047906cdae0)
- Remove ossl_namemap_add_name_n and simplify batch name addition logic, reducing duplicate lookups and copy paths. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b00cf0e790661636e1df1026554f712cc513592d)
- Change the suffix of 32-bit x86 assembly source files from .s to .S, enabling the build system to run C preprocessing on them. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9968c77539d6f7c5b1dcf0162fd4b57c144318c7)
- Remove the forced dependency of configuration.h on regular build targets, avoiding unnecessary rebuild failures when the configuration header is not generated. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ef8040bce02758de86fc55412ee4ac9102f9ffab)
- Move the undefinition of types.h macros required for Windows cryptographic API compatibility, avoiding conflicts with wincrypt.h types and macros. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3c58d447497b37f7b4f458aaa2956a7e226c6d65)
- Remove the early exit in BN_consttime_swap when the two parameters are the same, keeping the call path executing fixed-length swap logic. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a644cb7c1c19c78e2ca393c8ca36989e7ca61715)
- Remove the duplicate saltlen upper bound check in the DER writer, retaining unified parameter range validation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/05e51bc79bac45e194dd6f0bf73c99ed5ca06272)
- Remove the duplicate BIO_get_ktls_send query in do_ssl3_write, reducing redundant state reads. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ad2f4cdcb1875b16e0f5581ab1ee0bae572c68e6)
- Use ASN1_STRING_set0 more in libcrypto refactoring, unifying ownership transfer of ASN.1 string data. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/33847508d5605d8dbe868d7694a4eff79d785404)
- Change asn1_string_to_time_t to internal symbol ossl_asn1_string_to_time_t, and update references accordingly to avoid exporting internal APIs. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6097eb215266a825c9eedfab8f9c8482567ad4ab)
- Remove unused 8-bit GCM implementation code, streamlining the GCM multiplication implementation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a8b5128fd724bc23f7454d64e401d15129634a01)
- Remove unused 1-bit GCM implementation code, retaining only the actually used multiplication path. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7b6e19fc4e6cc1a7000f71789ef50636dacdbb85)
- Remove unused 4-bit GCM code, reducing maintenance burden and eliminating ineffective implementation branches. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7da952bcc54604141ea8ed40ec5ed1fd2f74cc25)
- Organize the calls and definitions of GHASH macros, reducing duplication and ambiguity in macro expansion. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/95201ef45711220455e8abf1cc6b334393384af2)
- Clean up the GCM_MUL implementation and delete the no longer used GCM_FUNCREF_4BIT branch. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d50e0934e5b1537db0ea43986464b8f8f8b4e9fd)
- Remove the no-ssl, no-tls, no-tls1_3, and no-dtls-method options from INSTALL.md as they are no longer provided. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5f18dc7facc9bd477173ae97a1bd84f21758da58)
- Split TLS connection state from the SSL object into SSL_CONNECTION, reducing coupling between core protocol state and the public SSL handle. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/38b051a1fedc79ebf24a96de2e9a326ad3665baf)
- Rename the timing program to timing_load_creds and include it in the build and test system, clarifying its purpose of measuring credential loading time. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c02036e1ad759fca228a2201f1c4752670ad59bd)
- Move record layer method implementations to a separate subdirectory, organizing new record layer code by responsibility. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4840c2a5e6c412a09dbb1c3c76f3117e5721bb76)
- Split record processing code for different protocol versions into separate source files, reducing conditional branches in a single record layer file. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/50023e9b7e2253c27e1a731c8bace64224aae0b8)
- Move ktls.c into the record layer directory, centralizing integration code for KTLS and record processing. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5b24990ba4b81ea576aac6c8711f7e9420bbee50)
- Remove duplicate KTLS handling code from non-KTLS-specific files, avoiding dual implementation in the new record layer and old paths. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1853d20a008a85d327f4faa9e07be40a85549f8e)
- Remove some direct dependencies of the record layer on the SSL object, using the record layer's own state and passed parameters instead. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9cd9e0978b237ebb6cc4110532d95903b1c6bf5c)
- Remove the SSL_USE_EXPLICIT_IV macro dependency from record layer methods, letting the record layer handle protocol-specific IV rules. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/88d616805cab4fd052bcff890627668a8f4bae33)
- The record layer no longer reads fragment length limits from the SSL object, using explicitly passed or record layer configuration to limit record fragment size. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ffbd6e67874475e025e942e0ee9f51badfea42b5)
- Move TLS sequence number state into OSSL_RECORD_LAYER, with the record layer independently maintaining read and write record sequence numbers. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0755722c28309a52f29573221e411a2b37175e37)
- Move TLS early data counting from the SSL object into the record layer, with the record layer tracking 0-RTT data volume and limits. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9dd90232d537f0ccd457fe1e23f4cbe83917c70a)
- Merge read and write encryption context representations, removing the duplicate separation of enc_read_ctx and enc_write_ctx. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6366bdd9be281984d675865ed5467bcf523640c5)
- Remove the last few references of the record layer to the SSL object, completing the independence of record layer state. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8124ab56d4e8985151c5a0c4dca6af128fa89f2c)
- Add regression tests for BIO_s_mem datagram mode, verifying that memory BIOs save and read data by message boundaries. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/eddb067e2ce82bc2ea104b3ab5286fe334c0525d)
- Supplement documentation for BIO_s_mem datagram mode, explaining message boundaries and buffering behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/222cf410d5e7bdd58dd50a0a3f1f0805707808ef)
- CMS_final propagates the failure status of CMS_dataFinal, preventing successful CMS output when data digest finalization fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bfc0f10d0640fddbe63c0828389247691ab617f0)
- Fix quoting in the install_fips command in the Windows Makefile, ensuring arguments are passed to the installation script without incorrect splitting. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/81c9ebd9099e7aac92a8c855a9ae1a30bad1d9cc)
- External oqs-provider tests now use an available liboqs working copy, ensuring interoperability tests can run after CI builds. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4564b47d7546a2225e1565715030981387b8e393)
- Correct the compression option description, noting that the default value is uncompressed. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/499b2c4654a28838924b60cab754fffa7b9f5609)
- Add encoding and decoding functions for QUIC frame fields, implementing on-the-wire format conversion for frame types and variable-length integers. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8bbf7ef63f95e0ef99e235eab777878d134ed302)
- Fix DTLS's judgment of SSL_pending and SSL_has_pending, enabling correct identification of pending read data in the record layer buffer. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2f90f85cc018c55d2b73c691f192909e402d1416)
- Add TLS handshake tests, verifying that protocol processing completes correctly even when the first application data record is exchanged with the Finished record. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cffafb5f57da07d90b23d0bc215371078d1ecbef)
- Reject negative IV lengths, avoiding the use of invalid parameters in cipher context caching and buffer operations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c77d4556732e2e41e975211498406c777136fbaa)
- Fix a null pointer dereference identified by Coverity, validating pointer validity before accessing objects. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1b285ac13726f443d1d737a1e2389ba6e17ba98f)
- Fix byte order handling in AES-GCM-SIV on big-endian architectures, ensuring consistent computation results across platforms. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3de7695928478bce22dcf6bf87883688d895dc43)
- Fix resource release on error paths in do_ssl3_write, avoiding leaks of BIO or record buffers when record writing fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/19d00444488c0a5861911ac8ba6b71c5c1f6c19a)
- Clarify that EVP_CIPHER_get_iv_length returns a negative value on error, and callers should distinguish between errors and valid lengths. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d3192c2643e4de2e2c36e107b7759f845a6e2bff)
- Fix state regression after EC_KEY_set_private_key sets the private key, ensuring the private key setting is correctly saved. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9007412c1e1fd4bb9298901dae36064cd279c02a)
- Clarify the documentation semantics of EC_KEY_set_private_key when receiving a NULL private key. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/df60982574338309856d4f746a2b641c108b1276)
- Update deprecation notes for DH_new and related APIs, indicating the recommended EVP_PKEY interface. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f6aab7b1e1410cf28ec45410aa4ee54f40baf13d)
- Change the documentation parameter name for signature output from sigret to sig, consistent with the API prototype. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9b7fb65e1520f398344ea8b7f3b4b097ae2617d7)
- Test whether EVP_DigestSign correctly returns the signature length, avoiding callers getting an uninitialized length. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/226ed5fb390f8cfc8b80cea79f57ae7837bc9b96)
- Fix s390x GHASH assembly computation, ensuring correct AES-GCM authentication hash results. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7f7b0be8e3d452ecf5154203c5669f72683fde3f)
- Adjust the timing of attribute cache refresh, reducing the impact of algorithm query cache hit order on Provider discovery. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1704961cf085a64b0e104bd0c9cb81188f061698)
- Organize the formatting of timing_load_creds test code and fix other minor issues. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e6b1c22b41b5feaffe7fe2bb24996fb6763586af)
- Supplement EC key parameter documentation, explaining curves, encoded public keys, and parameter import/export meanings. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/aaabe58072924c24c862a0660cdfe78de63099c2)
- Pass SSL protocol options to the record layer, ensuring the new implementation adheres to connection configuration. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3d623896eb50f5b15d3ef8f53b9f1e5c7546695a)
- Remove redundant KTLS branches from non-KTLS-specific code, avoiding duplicate management of kernel offloading in old and new record paths. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1957148384c72ea7bc33a5c415d8f84526ed6480)
- Add tests for read_ahead buffer spanning key switches, ensuring records before and after key updates are processed with the correct keys. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/948c656c66a3846337a0262197766c80ec7c9e59)
- Rename the multi variable in OCSP and related commands to n_responders, clarifying it represents the number of responders. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5e87fddc971210ebb6df3fe77eeb858cd0bc4dea)
- Refactor HTTP server logging implementation, extracting logging functionality into log.c/log.h and unifying invocation methods. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8a2ec00d7f4bc34ca9111561699ec5ac03a3923e)
- Clean up EBCDIC character set definitions, removing duplicate or no longer used character mapping constants. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/44e47328178328198018c23e6918884af5e8ce4b)
- Organize the format of options and values in openssl.cnf, separating comments from option values and removing extra leading spaces. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fd24de9f93049b05a54c48da5316f42882489230)
- Move OSSL_TIME to libcrypto, making it available for SSL, QUIC, and other library modules. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/02d0f87a8ba143eaeaee3334a2f63543b10148a9)
- libcrypto no longer depends on struct timeval, using OSSL_TIME for time values to improve platform portability. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5d1bb4fc47582b06dd224a788bdfaaced60e72a0)
- Remove legacy OBJ_create calls in openssl x509, using the existing object identifier registration mechanism. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/51024f75591d00a52dd867906a763b4e2107e288)
- Move CSR automatic format loading logic from cmp.c to apps.c, making it shared among multiple command-line applications. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/200d844782956b4c6db9bdd92a53113d9c2dc3c7)
- Remove the FIPS conditional skip in SM2 tests, running related tests in FIPS-compatible builds as well. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/919adfcf6683d82f876060b6cf9f57e875d547b2)
- Remove the FIPS condition skip for the IV generation test, and expand the test coverage under the FIPS configuration. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3fd255acb7b65a30afd1b23e17db2163fb9ffd8d)
- Delete unnecessary macro definitions to avoid duplicate or invalid conditional compilation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1cef04091ab12bda0e1c6a5874e9d113c44bbdd8)
- Move alert checking to the record layer's message dispatch logic, ensuring protocol state machine decisions rather than record layer alert handling. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3eaead7166ef5aff027e571a9be0def6581ef20c)
- Move TLS write buffer allocation, expansion, and release into the write-side record layer, centrally managing record caching. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/151f313e53c1515f2730b3b36e3fc966e1a8010b)
- Migrate the numwpipes state to the write record layer, which maintains the number of pipeline writes. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e7694c69b5fed37f5cdf72b70f507c7188db7e3d)
- Move record version determination out of the record layer, and have the protocol layer provide version information based on connection negotiation state. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1d3676778c280ef05044c4c9e696a4f8096530ea)
- Remove the no longer used empty_fragment_done state field. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/91141aa1b0ff9d92323e2545bb9f6f0d1e2a8844)
- Migrate the logic for whether to send empty fragments to the record layer, centrally managing record write policy. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b9e4e78342df6575b358def3d951227e9c6cebda)
- Remove the record layer's reference to the SSL_CONNECTION_TREAT_AS_TLS13 macro, avoiding direct dependency on SSL state macros. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f2892e21619a2c59e957f7f9121f24713bcad3e9)
- Fully migrate the record padding callback into the record layer, which uniformly calculates and applies padding length. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5f95eb77e780cc0b90a7da6cc4f79c7bb153ca64)
- Fully migrate the record block_padding capability check to the record layer, which is responsible for padding support and record layout. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/eb7d6c2a9b3b9d1582e3e1b65c9d431cf3209207)
- Remove scattered references to SSL_CONNECTION in the record layer, continuing to reduce inter-layer state coupling. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5361a5a9664046aefcd1a72858826bcb4c93ad9f)
- Fix the explicit null pointer dereference identified by Coverity, adding validity checks before using objects. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c6186792b98e93cf2d5d2a9fb85e4aeab31db890)
- The HTTP application server logs request and response content when tracing is enabled, helping troubleshoot client-server interaction. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4fed6ee1ce4a3374d7223654db13132144275c05)
- Initialize the ipi_spec_dst field for Cygwin to avoid network address handling exceptions caused by undefined fields. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d5ab48a192d45ec51355ef2a186125961331eb9b)
- openssl list adds an option to list all available algorithms, allowing viewing the complete algorithm set provided by Providers. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3c9ffd0273b7e1e1e425be03c5e8a58ef07c4625)
- Fix tests under IPv6-disabled configurations so that IPv6-specific assertions are not compiled or run when OPENSSL_USE_IPV6=0. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9643ddb13af88c153c150c91ae538ff04808577e)
- Fix the default padding incompatibility issue when using the legacy FIPS Provider, restoring 3.0.0 interaction behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f817a7439eaa705429cf699dd0485e665b0ffc49)
- Remove legacy OBJ_create registration calls in openssl x509, relying on existing object identifier definitions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/82d28c6b3cbd8074faaa34cc2ce57dacc580792f)
- Move the CSR automatic format detection function to common application helper code for reuse by cmp and other commands. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5139dec255d0e2f991083cba9d9c62dbe6637046)
- Replace direct access to SSL_CONNECTION's wbio with the record layer bio field, isolating output BIO state. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bfda3aeec5986d9374e1ceb33b823a2d82bd29ca)
- Migrate TLS write buffer allocation, capacity management, and release to the write-side record layer. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2f6e24eb5bd6a3ea4c5e18ff003acc4e812b527f)
- Migrate the pending write pipe count numwpipes into the write record layer, uniformly maintaining parallel write state. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4bf610bdce3b0e474c5ce7db5be77e152f3649b6)
- Remove the no longer needed empty_fragment_done state variable. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f93c0f546423eab65be3bc50a8cdfc3d5eb6b2e1)
- Clean up remaining SSL_CONNECTION references in the record layer, continuing to separate connection state from record transmission state. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2582de25902510cdb934c5ff59845fc26a7f2e28)
- Remove KTLS-specific logic from TLS write buffer initialization, delegating kernel offload preparation to record layer methods. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5bc226ab3217525584a553ff1729567219d00e78)
- Remove the const qualifier from the PKCS12_SAFEBAG_set0_attrs parameter, making the function declaration match its implementation that transfers/modifies attribute ownership. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9eaf07ffe39e76aca2dfb8e22b8060c75fcbd8e0)
- Remove create_empty_fragment calls from the DTLS write path, uniformly using the record layer's empty fragment handling strategy. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/22d6e8547f11dae2e4c026be93331e9acfe9b940)
- Migrate dtls_write_records into the record layer, so DTLS write records and TLS write records share the layered interface. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fc938db6cc46c6b59ab9da39f3c5b9c9a97ad33a)
- Remove redundant record type assignments in the DTLS write path, avoiding subsequent encapsulation overwriting or misuse of type state. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3abc0d3e8504f730117e3b68dfb1aab81c5e51fe)
- Move sequence number increment to the post-encryption processing step, ensuring only successfully constructed records advance the counter. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/421386e392151c267ac7d3de6a2dd23c0ab62aed)
- Delete the old dtls_write_records implementation; DTLS write records are now replaced by the new shared record layer method. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/43dfa5a9319f67cd652fdc3a8711fc53859cd11e)
- Remove the RECLAYER TODO comment no longer needed after DTLS migration. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/faa3e66c27a5e88f048f3ed30cfca297eda13eb6)
- Delete old record buffer management code, unifying buffer allocation, reuse, and release through the record layer object. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e158ada6a74e5903354fdd5a6f56a32bbbba69fd)
- Remove duplicate logic in the record sending path, simplifying common TLS/DTLS record processing. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b92fc4ae189fb0d5b0a2f34bc28e59cd7e1eed5a)
- Delay releasing the BIO held by the SSL connection, ensuring other cleanup steps can still use the BIO reference and avoid premature invalidation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cd6e89b6b6ebe204cc442da9b563213bd67eb27f)
- Remove the internal thread destruction helper ossl_crypto_thread_native_terminate, unifying exit handling through thread object lifecycle code. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4f32754f79d697e3af78d821296fd02fbba6e186)
- Remove the no longer used ossl_rand_pool_add_additional_data function, simplifying the random pool entropy input path. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/da7db83cc44d2c8761e9074caf8befd443ea8be8)
- Remove unused callbacks in record layer methods, simplifying the Record Method interface. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fba0206da7c0cc68854bb63a6ee9b96a74f4ed7a)
- Move DES tests out of the FIPS self-test/validation scope, separating non-approved algorithm tests from the FIPS test suite. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c511953a0828e126b80a9ea8cee12d001d685ba8)
- Remove the specific FIPS conditional branch for the 3DES test, allowing tests to run based on actual Provider algorithm availability. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/464c1011b02936850fc779739013dba52650840a)
- Remove DES/3DES algorithm implementation registration from the FIPS Provider, avoiding these non-approved algorithms being provided as FIPS implementations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fc0bb3411bd0c6ca264f610303933d0bf4f4682c)
- Remove the Data::Dumper dependency from the OID generation Perl script, simplifying the generation tool runtime environment. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/57d2bccdb2112cc09de1bec585da878161b1364f)
- Remove unused header files from the pkcs7 source file. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2a5c0d93cfe65b5fbb9bd91ec62371256eb26e12)
- Revert the change that moved DES tests out of the FIPS test suite, restoring the old test organization. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c69cf38ec4b592a488f0c8d3042ecc345787ffc9)
- Revert the 3DES test condition adjustment, restoring the previous version's FIPS test judgment. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ccc860a77e542bee24f64e44f7bcea5706068866)
- Remove remaining enc_read_ctx/enc_write_ctx and read/write hash references in the record layer, unifying encryption state in the record layer. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f471f60a8adcbb72314be974f6bc320943786b96)
- Remove compression/decompression context fields from SSL_CONNECTION, with related compression state now maintained by the record layer. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6d814fd6074b5f293abc3f19a190d3e34c426b6a)
- Remove TLS read/write static IV fields from SSL_CONNECTION, consolidating IV state into the record layer. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b83eac48ed44afecd0d392c2fa055d345578078d)
- Remove read/write MAC secret fields from SSL_CONNECTION; the record layer encryption object uniformly holds the required key material. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1e42708e175f1453bd12f4632fbc0c61bade4e81)
- Move tls_pad.c into the record layer methods directory, organizing TLS padding implementation by protocol method. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c89c21f8f59d4973b7ccda6d6aaa2756636e2121)
- Move record layer public and internal declarations to corresponding dedicated header files, clarifying interface visibility scope. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/23c57f001d997b939f9b7c76ffbf9e81a16b0141)
- Rename SSL3_BUFFER to TLS_BUFFER, making the name applicable to multiple TLS/DTLS versions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e9189cc4af045523f91b2d9265add1ab1326fcdf)
- Rename SSL3_RECORD to TLS_RL_RECORD, indicating it is an internal data structure of the record layer. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/22094d11a780f7485f0929ccfac806e0d02f82a9)
- Rename generic linked list internal fields to names that do not expose implementation details, facilitating internal list structure evolution. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ccdcb08d05725673a3c416f221905fb362dcf1a6)
- Rename the X509 purpose internal require_ca parameter to non_leaf, accurately expressing that it restricts non-leaf certificates. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8a2f9a7cc8ab588d23fb96afd696f9da2c61c2c7)
- Organize S/MIME test CA configuration, using default SKID/AKID generation to simplify configuration and correct descriptions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a275afc527d05b5187b457bdbcd0e1dcb18839f1)
- Directly include internal/nelem.h in source files that need the nelem macro, avoiding dependence on indirect header order. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f2a6f83862be3e20260b708288a8f7d0928e9018)
- Remove redundant assignments in P-521 felem_mul_ref, avoiding dead code and compiler warnings. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3d4dfeb28a5cb944b8300b4cf807e19ab97d04f5)
- Refactor HPKE AEAD encryption/decryption helper functions, explicitly passing OSSL_HPKE_CTX to read algorithm and key state. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/910221454bfcabf1917fa65462f4cae48be5a624)
- Remove trailing spaces in INSTALL.md, fixing formatting check issues. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/98663afce7a909be1518921a9995540308a52462)
- Remove the unused ALIGN64 macro, simplifying RSAZ dual-path modular exponentiation assembly helper definitions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4b65d79d7132d6e46bfb385a76082f6502ef617b)
- Delete unused macros in RC2 and RC5 internal header files. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bb4a32ba24ab186aba0b45150934d9eae68d78d5)
- Remove duplicate function declarations, avoiding repeated exposure of the same symbol in header files. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7d7a8d416529c4d560fbd5ca73bb3b24383a419c)
- Refactor the DCID consistency check in QUIC RX, ensuring invalid connection ID checks do not access invalid packet header data. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0f7b5cc9f3d487641dd5f4003e0be88fb2111e98)
- Remove TODO comments in the QUIC receive demuxer that cannot be converted into checks or implementations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/27003aa6ebcb9f3a03c253dbd26fc152e1481fab)
- Remove receive demuxer tests from the QUIC record layer test suite to avoid duplicate execution of the same component tests. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6a8001986265ce9ce91469b6fa735cf95dd7b4bf)
- Integrate the QUIC_SSL_CTX control into the public SSL_CTX ctrl and remove the no longer needed helper functions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8a1a6d6d9e9a6bc091f0dc21503da214e2614209)
- Simplify SSL_get_tick_timeout, removing duplicate calculations and invalid return handling. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a1660c9422c8fef9e7c74d1dedd249106d4be18b)
- Remove small redundant implementations in QUIC code to simplify control flow. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9f0ade7c470b0ee9be3c25d38bbec7d05ca4237e)
- Simplify the QUIC BIO poll descriptor custom interface, reducing the event fields that callers need to maintain. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/692df8d34401c95ee446e26033711030e471e7e7)
- Rename the want_net_read/want_net_write flags in the QUIC API to clearer read/write intent names. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b639475a9433c827675b8154ea9e0ce361403c76)
- Remove unused macros in CAST and DES internal header files. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2fb5fa468613a3472d148deaf0991fa7e78c83a6)
- Refactor QUIC demux linked list operations, centralizing connection ID entry insertion, deletion, and reference management. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/64222fc0274a88a6f42d5600c4bfdf57eeb40155)
- Mark recordmethod.h as an OpenSSL internal header to avoid exposing the not-yet-stable record layer method interface as a public API. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ca20f61fd7cc424f1fd52d6471cf8d801229acf2)
- Remove unused function callbacks in the record layer method, simplifying the SSL/TLS custom record layer interface. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e5103dfc1200c2f4a450f8b4ff234ad84342d4b6)
- Remove the QUIC Dummy Handshake implementation and interface that have been replaced by the real handshake layer. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c28f1a8bb9ccfecb76bcf3b7987e2a526b427bca)
- Remove the redundant user_ssl field in QUIC internal connections, simplifying the mapping between wrapper SSL and underlying connections. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e95d6e1eec2f080713aa91c12e411cea4cffee65)
- Rename 90-test_traceapi.t to 90-test_trace_api.t to keep naming consistent with other test files. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b02997c571a7bc3321fc33fb5208f7888d66b380)
- Refactor the probe result reporting in the QUIC ACK Manager so that callers can use probe data for accounting and in-flight packet management. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e2212b20bcf96c62c17a5e124c3bd61a98b8fcfd)
- Unify the OSSL_QUIC_FAULT naming in the test framework to QTEST_FAULT, clarifying that this is a test-only fault interface. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c12e11133625569f5b92a2a78486ecb70cd23df7)
- Remove AES-SIV algorithm registration from the FIPS Provider to avoid providing an unapproved mode as a FIPS implementation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c3f985cfd6fb4b8ab1765403d65fed3d006b2163)
- Remove the no longer used RSA-PSS parameter configuration helper functions, simplifying the RSA-PSS ASN.1 parameter generation logic. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1735531c8ba7542e5fb2fe2f0becddb595955ace)
- Delete duplicated code segments and unify calls to existing public helper functions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6b08b786ccba8fb84759a487cca439566a8678c2)
- Fix a segmentation fault when the parent RNG cleans up the global default random number context, ensuring correct child/parent context destruction order. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6d45fd47f4849c8dc55b8dd5fa1e1b8a158774a0)
- Organize RISC-V extension detection macros, unifying ISA feature bit test expressions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/86c69fe84118f0dca656d9bfc1131052e2a8e9b8)
- Move reusable AES RISC-V Perl assembly helper functions into a shared Perl module to avoid code duplication across multiple assembler scripts. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c8a641c39f524051900feac7175ca7300f151f85)
- Simplify RISC-V GCM multiplication/accumulation steps, removing unnecessary intermediate values and duplicate calculations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b24684369b76df8b226fe9aa95fca2bccfc6a175)
- Remove the leftover ossl_crypto_mem_barrier declaration and calls in the threading implementation, cleaning up the obsolete memory barrier wrapper. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/41c3c71382f31a5a913bd09a74295d101d837055)
- Refactor fipsinstall command option parsing, unifying option definitions, conflict checks, and argument handling. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/83ccf81b1dd8886d54c570354ef8c532af4c514f)
- Remove the no longer recommended DSA-512 performance item from the speed test. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7c966ab6b332d3666870856edb122d67cb09ead5)
- Move the QUIC_CHANNEL lock to a controllable position during connection initialization/destruction to avoid exposing lock state when construction is incomplete. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4847599b54ca3fffe0da21cdccaefd74015d9d67)
- Refactor lock acquisition in thread helper mode to a non-failing synchronization path, avoiding state corruption from lock creation/acquire errors. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/20f457436d0240f07835e098a6508668da9b02a4)
- Adjust build file layout to clarify source files and conditional dependencies for the QUIC thread helper component. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6c091ca968f4c62744a5161d54f2c2dd89a76993)
- Organize duplicate logic in the finite field cryptography backend, unifying parameter and public key checking code. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a76ccb9d0ddc24f6551afbc220b41fb3c4e64c6a)
- Remove the outdated FIPS Provider cross-version check script, replacing it with a new independent verification action. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3254f7b66695f09d16f27a0b057446415ff81921)
- Delete the cross-comparison step between the current build and the OpenSSL 3.0.0 FIPS Provider, avoiding reliance on deprecated versions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5303608523e40f4328f56755a775f9b5dc0da321)
- Move fuzzing corpus out of the main repository to reduce source repository size and maintain test data separately. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0f735011962830ceaa9a7ab0b9d91129d9ba011d)
- Remove unused type-punning unions in the NIST big number implementation, reducing invalid internal state without changing computation logic. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f659f7a1c70709caa1727bb0b7f836d170d35bb5)
- Remove unused `PTR_SIZE_INT` internal definitions, cleaning up obsolete platform type aliases in big number code. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/dcfeb617477dd957f69e713cbc61fd4dca0f2db4)
- Organize comments for CRMF certificate template accessors, clarifying the purpose of each `OSSL_CRMF_CERTTEMPLATE` function. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8b6bbcaa7bd2f0b44d7d0d867acc6002ba09a6fd)
- Organize the internal implementation of the Argon2 derivation function, reducing duplicate steps while keeping its derivation result and parameter semantics unchanged. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a901b31e99442f087051ae7efdcbc9ad6e6a5b33)
- Refactor DTLS connection state handling, consolidating scattered logic into a common path, preserving protocol behavior and improving maintainability. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/24a322544373f7acda05e19f64a6c3120d459d5b)
- Move the test-only dummy congestion controller from the production QUIC build target to the test build, avoiding compiling test implementations into the library. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b633cf876435f094e4944f30be4dbbc70115f230)
- Remove unused parameters in the speed tool's message printing function, cleaning up the internal interface without changing benchmark output. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a8eb81ccd2d3daeb92c0842a02dc688eae298250)
- Delete unused macros in `common.h`, cleaning up invalid definitions in public header files. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6aeb42eca97227c8235af0986d1525ee4a916504)
- Change the QUIC application layer interface to accept an SSL pointer, unifying the public call entry and avoiding exposing internal QUIC connection pointers. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/072328dddb8371b865bd18caca9a77698e883c80)
- Remove the dedicated branch for stream 0 in the QUIC channel, allowing channel logic to handle streams in the connection using a unified stream model. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c6c0432cca9c46bde56a9480796762f25d2a18a7)
- Refactor stream state and operations in the QUIC application layer into the `QUIC_XSO` object, unifying management of connection objects and their stream objects. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cb5c208bf2e39bf2367b051136c599cff1fc3683)
- Organize the handling logic for QUIC send stream and receive stream states, reducing error paths caused by interleaved handling of the two stream states. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e8b9f63235e82403b7e144ff9a1a3985d44f1c4e)
- Clean up the use of WPACKET in `quic_txp_test` to avoid missing proper initialization or finalization when constructing transport parameters in tests. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/dbca844cb3e74b19acf46eb9a2222d30802ad642)
- Delete an unused variable to eliminate a compilation warning reported by Clang 16. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/abbbc06a94cafa7e212bfe7dcbf818ac33d986d3)
- Rename `msg_callback_s` to `msg_callback_ssl`, clarifying that this structure holds SSL message callback state. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c2786c8ea732592f708e588f0f5849716914a313)
- Rename `SSL_tick` and timeout query functions in the QUIC implementation to new names to match the updated public interface. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6084e04b25378a4590798a034633e90791cf74a3)
- Update event handling API names in the QUIC manual pages to the new interface names. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/041d48c9636478563b5dcd936c1fe816d1628732)
- Update QUIC event-driven related options and calls in `s_client` to the renamed API. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a35e38a2128163209db76eb9135e29b1bbe54c9e)
- Organize the handling of `EVP_PKEY_decrypt()` output length in the TLS server, correctly checking and passing buffer length based on decryption results. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fdef95716dbcc6127d05f8cfc90f389a84acaf9b)
- Add shared decryption helper functions for CMS, PKCS#7, and CRMF, unifying output buffer length handling when calling `EVP_PKEY_decrypt()`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/36b91a198ae027c054ef128a35a268bc3c307f00)
- Refactor the DRBG implementation, moving lock management into each DRBG instance so that each random number generator is responsible for protecting its own state. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/189ad3ab2028babd39241015fc3975e8334c87eb)
- Remove meaningless warnings during PKCS#12 import to avoid misleading prompts for normal input. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4c56539cb338f1583289f93379ee254b45b66568)
- Refactor input format parameter parsing in applications, unifying format option handling and reducing duplicate parsing logic. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ca857d7332d042142ced23b37fdd1d52dbf152b9)
- Delete unused variable assignments pointed out by Coverity, avoiding state writes that do not affect subsequent logic. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4bcbf8d4445e763857563d9ca016972e095c622d)
- Remove assignments to unused variables, eliminating invalid state writes and satisfying static analysis checks. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0c6c3782870663dd190009103f22263f9a99c82e)
- Delete unused `wvalue` assignments, avoiding leaving unread intermediate values in the decryption processing path. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f9a4e2b663ab97de718e016b29644d0c2bd9b7c3)
- Remove unused `wvalue` assignments, reducing invalid write operations in PKCS#12 processing. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ade969e27b71a57e4d44ebada093929cc8f4193c)
- Change the QUIC send path state output to an extensible structure, allowing additional state fields without changing the fixed layout interface. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a3a51d6ec38a8c2fd88e7c64c2f21632e55cbbdf)
- Remove key update handling from the QUIC send path, delegating send key update responsibility to the channel layer for unified management. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b65b0d4ebe67ba9d53b96887b54ca9a0f5bf523e)
- Remove the restriction that certificate cross-signing can only use self-signed certificates, allowing cross-signing of other trusted certificates. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7f4cc3bc34e2fc1acf2abf1f2d791855c446c611)
- Delete no longer used code in the QUIC server, cleaning up leftover server logic after multi-stream implementation adjustments. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3f7b67fb21e31b4262bb0c0dd83c01c221f3278c)
- Simplify the logic in the QUIC API that blocks ordinary SSL interfaces, centralizing restrictions on calls not applicable to QUIC. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9562842b336c885b79385f2f6d65d0b2ff22a826)
- Refactor command-line option handling, unify option parsing and validation flow, and reduce duplicate parameter processing across subcommands. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/db2f98c4ebb17a60307f70c330834beffb8f1253)
- Remove unused internal functions and clean up internal interfaces that are no longer called. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/926601dc0feab2def91ad5a98213436779864459)
- Move the Keccak rotation table into the read-only data segment to avoid repeatedly constructing constant tables at runtime. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2fd82c228363cfd16c5047a348e7c853defa42eb)
- Remove duplicate configuration values to avoid the same parameter appearing repeatedly in the list. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f1b7243cda208d8bd74f75c95990f8205e977ae4)
- Refactor QUIC stream manager and stream object states into RFC-defined sending and receiving stream states, unifying handling of close and termination transitions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2f018d14f06d54c9528ac41d40e95d7638371e50)
- Rename the CMP function for obtaining CA certificates to `OSSL_CMP_get{,1}_caCerts` and update the certificate request API documentation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ec5a9cd11b92e75f097dbaa41c512f29cf8625e7)
- Remove unreachable code in `SSL_use_certificate_file()` to keep the certificate file loading path consistent with the context version. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/33ef5fc2c2bf0b0587bfcba28f61329abd83be70)
- Remove duplicate conditional checks, simplifying the execution path without changing input validation results. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/85b89a8c6da76c241b2f62cf2944b5cf35dfcc24)
- Move the tracking responsibility for QUIC maximum ACK delay from the state manager to the ACK manager, so that ACK timing and acknowledgment state are maintained by the same component. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/79a80f8b58bed854eb30a22e1643869dcc29e005)
- Organize the handling of maximum ACK delay in the QUIC ACK manager and track values for sending and receiving directions separately. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fd6c1476a09b4766ce6c435d50ad16acebcd46df)
- Centralize ARM CPU capability detection into `armcap.c`, uniformly maintaining hardware feature identification logic. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/08e6eb216c9d65d502dc136a40e1c0adaefab759)
- Refactor QUIC packet padding handling to correctly pad packets such as Initial based on packet space and post-encryption length. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/faebafda9fa232dc84fee2bbf16d478425703490)
- Update QUIC sending path tests to verify the refactored padding rules and packet length calculations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9441624ee91705bf9f7a35ba06ec336a9d63868e)
- Move QUIC sending-related deadline management into the sending path component, centrally scheduling probing, retransmission, and sending wake-up timing. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c206f2aa62ace93ce06a940e6992ffb4f3316bb5)
- Rename the QUIC stream count flow control mode to a name that better reflects its actual function, avoiding confusion with ordinary data flow control. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1051b4a0b9e307e51fdf491e6824e6610007824d)
- Move the QUIC error code to string conversion function into a separate source file, centrally maintaining error text mapping. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/398922463fd2fb0df52443932ca3e140554e5334)
- Remove redundant internal decoder header files to avoid maintaining duplicate declarations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/98d81174d3763053b32b8cfd7225acd0a111d456)
- Remove explicit setting of `PTHREAD_MUTEX_NORMAL` to avoid overriding platform default mutex attributes and fix related compatibility issues. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e4d808652b0a1a19cfe615a6659e65ead0245108)
- Remove redundant checks for non-empty store context in CMP general message handling, simplifying branches already guaranteed by calling conventions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2b2eedfdd614e4c5e2104acf44da0bcdd5b90ade)
- Remove unused variable `single` in TLS minimum/maximum version calculation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d561fe5a0aabb4d0a9400b5086441bb4f4b4dca4)
- Rename QUIC `SSL_set_initial_peer_addr()` to `SSL_set1_initial_peer_addr()`, using the function name to indicate that the call copies the address parameter. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ce7a9e23fb1ea249e08c3dfa9c9f701a701f2719)
- Remove redundant parentheses in the secp384r1 implementation, cleaning up expressions without changing elliptic curve operation results. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/670e73d9084465384b11ef24802ca4a313e1d2f4)
- Remove redundant elliptic curve point multiplication from `ossl_ec_key_public_check()` to avoid repeated verification of the same public key point. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3961991593f788b3efb2a27563d358c7c58f854c)
- Move the pollable capability flag into the QUIC reactor, letting the event loop component uniformly determine whether the underlying object can wait for I/O. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0b8b75e242e95db034e8026f462a799c0dafaefc)
- Organize QUIC channel management of poll descriptors to avoid repeatedly acquiring, caching, or releasing underlying polling resources. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/be96180aa65fbf620eaf3ca8965a814b04e99130)
- Remove duplicate words in documentation and code. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ad31628cfef5893b2198077752302a7d9b58135c)
- Remove unused AppVeyor configuration file, cleaning up CI settings that are no longer run. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8ac32e1e1b1a786366333acf897d332339610e6b)
- Remove `_Static_assert` usage and switch to a project-compatible assertion mechanism to support compilers that do not accept that syntax. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fc785a554cc37dfa94710b28ced45b03006f0300)
- Improve Coveralls coverage mapping and remove OpenSSL 1.1.1 data that is no longer covered. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/798d69c8670283bdea5c39b03ff573d0c6a6b9fc)
- Remove duplicate definitions of `trace_data_stack` in application code to avoid declaring the same trace stack state multiple times. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/46ea5486f34ff8c2fed67674da2a363bbd66691b)
- Clean up duplicate handling logic in test helper BIO, reusing existing datagram BIO operations uniformly. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c29b13a7356432a0f177dd838afbec963f1d6212)
- Remove redundant inclusion of sparse array header files to avoid introducing unrelated internal definitions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/442d08f215c48896f59e9c09a14773058f9e56bf)
- Remove duplicate free calls for NULL pointers, cleaning up invalid resource cleanup code. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c37184f502eb7341e3095ef358a9ebd21facbc46)
- Adjust the inclusion order of `e_os2.h` in the QUIC test library to ensure symbol definitions remain consistent across platforms. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b07107e31149bf870bc1ae17e59444859fe4e23a)
- Exclude `test` directory files from coverage reports to avoid counting test code in product coverage. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/febe8cf4dee9939ee3e5523b6f14d9dc1ec74153)
- Remove redundant checks in RSA parameter conversion, allowing valid keys with missing CRT parameters to continue conversion. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4ad3a44ba45a4026170336161228d435f6784564)
- Rename examples so their names accurately reflect HTTP/3/QUIC usage. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/17b8f405a212a01f9258eb7edcfb687a90cc8d28)
- Clean up redundant code in the QUIC client fuzzer, simplifying input handling. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f7620555189edb94cc5840814c0ab1864041c148)
- Remove invalid print flag settings in `XN_FLAG_COMPAT` mode. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6c6e9d4a18954f9aa4e7e153a4430cc935c18a90)
- Remove unreachable code in the transport extension cleanup path. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1ee0a9d8d3f351813e9db1d528c569c4c8ac3eac)
- Release the old record layer when clearing the DTLS sent buffer, keeping resource lifecycle consistent. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2bb83945bb99c98b1a67c5ba6307ad8b0dde5370)

### Tests

- Adjust test environment selection logic so tests always call the same Perl interpreter resolved from PATH. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/473664aafdff1f60db99929bdd43c2a9b26d14cd)
- Adjust test cases in endecoder_test.c to match encoder and decoder behavior changes. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/602bfb8b98125f6745cd40dbc5fce9614ae5e418)
- Fix out-of-memory test cases in EVP tests to ensure tests check for Out of memory errors as expected. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1e7479e8a4f33b1afa7d62b07c682f6987e6a515)
- Update copyright years in test files to the current project year. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a8d9bd8114510d3a1708da3922f07e7f707674bc)
- Adjust Provider tests to no longer rely on global EVP_CIPHER and EVP_MD objects, instead using algorithm instances obtained from the Provider. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e59bfbaa2dbd680f77e1121e382502bd522a466c)
- Increase code coverage for the x509 command, adding tests for previously uncovered execution paths. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/415e6ac80405e13b20b083315747e431274fbd33)
- Set the strength of the TLS CCM8 suite to 64 bits and update tests to verify the new rating. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e07102220afe4059bc45aa3d7073b7678329e26e)
- Fix potential interference between FIPS and KTLS combinations, adjusting related test handling to avoid conflicts. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a5d8a2f8f10b83e5afb297698fe72cee77b1837f)
- Make cipher suite string parsing support standard names, allowing callers to specify algorithms using registered standard suite names. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d1b26ddbf6a9165c71884eff228300e3d83be1b1)
- Replace the AES-128-CBC-HMAC-SHA1 test cipher implementation in e_ossltest.c, updating the algorithm used by the internal test Provider. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/64da15c40d15aac58e211fd25d00e9ae84d0379b)
- Update the gost-engine submodule to a newer version to keep it compatible with OpenSSL's new default name settings. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/518ce65d93692ecd4c004b96b47d58da8e5922ea)
- Extend custom certificate extension tests to cover encoding and handling of newly added extensions in certificate requests. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0db3a9904fa00569905be130854a31dab7b8f49d)
- Add short buffer tests to verify that cipher and derivation interfaces fail safely when input capacity is insufficient. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/15ff7d7c2569a1aceaf6e85b61aee62422628fc9)
- Allow core_obj_add_sigid() to use an empty digest or unspecified digest, accommodating signature identifiers that do not require a bound digest algorithm. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4f716249643fe97a2bdf59a11cc10e1bef8103e9)
- Fix X509_PUBKEY_dup() handling of EVP_PKEY, no longer just incrementing the reference count, to ensure the copied object has proper independence. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7e35458b511f042d9a37d49227b01096c444e575)
- Add tests to verify that after importing a key via EVP_PKEY_fromdata(), the key can still be used for subsequent encryption or signing operations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fd19fc4c2726b08282b8db15f9bace2f04712498)
- Check for NULL before using pointers in ssl_old_test.c to avoid crashes when object creation fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8c590a219fe30b97cfde2efdd8ea94c03a90a8c6)
- Ensure cached algorithm fetch results are saved per Provider, avoiding mixing cache entries from different Providers. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/dc010ca6ec01d313a84c3c4b040232655a1772ad)
- Add tests to verify that non-exportable keys obtain signing implementations from the correct Provider. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/051228353a9842eede597294603cc06a55e3a22c)
- Check return values of BN_new() and BN_dup() to avoid continuing to use null objects after big number allocation or copy failures. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d99004fe5de934120765d342586f08d22131b8ed)
- Change weak DES key and parity checks to constant-time execution, reducing the risk of timing information leakage from key-related branches. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8db9d07508e201d95e40f8006ede3a76494bbef3)
- Allow do_sigver_init() to reinitialize the operation context on an existing signing or verification operation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ae6b68b761b9c5f30897747487ea943ccfab53ba)
- Only deactivate child Providers when truly necessary, avoiding unnecessary Provider deactivation operations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c59fc87b338880893286934f02c446854f5baabf)
- Extend test_multi_load() to cover combinations of loading and unloading multiple Providers. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/293e251e6f0367a9aa0d3d46037b19d1a6c91b20)
- Add 1536/2048 bit double-precision exponentiation optimization for Intel Ice Lake, improving performance of corresponding large-number modular exponentiation operations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f87b4c4ea67393c9269663ed40a7ea3463cc59d3)
- Make d2i_PublicKey() able to use EC parameters from the passed public key to complete decoding, supporting elliptic curve public keys where parameters are provided with the key. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/615a9b8798e6ec58f1b2e1ec08a0f6b3c8cb7f60)
- Upgrade Windows CI image from windows-2016 to windows-2022. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c87a4dd7a728288da943cb4e2e51150df5dfd1b8)
