- Add RSA key PVK format conversion tests, verifying PVK encoding and decoding flows. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a44eb8421d0e84c069a5fa55ced796878e6b0966)
- Skip symbol existence tests on Windows, avoiding test inapplicability due to stripping or export rules on that platform. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d09f4501e47e0b969caec5a3059af52d227e961a)
- Pin Windows CI to windows-2019, avoiding build environment uncertainty caused by windows-latest updates. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c37ebbd6f97d23b291c49c4ae2b94c27d732de30)
- Extend CMP command tests, verifying that -reqin_new_tid can regenerate transaction IDs for input requests. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/20b0579cbfd1986d00ad8eb2167bc865519f23cd)
- Fix resource release order in cmp_vfy_test.c tests, releasing allocated objects before returning. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/869b7dd00046951efb06dbb13c052ff9d7c87113)
- Fix get_ecdsa_sig_rs_bytes() not releasing the object returned by d2i_ECDSA_SIG(), avoiding memory leaks in the ECDSA signature parsing path. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ec9135a62320c861ab17f7179ebe470686360c64)
- Improve empty digest signature algorithm registration tests, covering the case where digest is empty when adding sigalg. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2080da84a49b0c52fc8c6e6caef5d373235bd3e4)
- Add tests verifying that the buffer size passed to pem_password_cb() is PEM_BUFSIZE. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c7debe811123951a60cdfe73716184ca8fdd79d2)
- Uniformly update inconsistent copyright years in auto-generated files to 2022. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0088ef48c3e7d9c68e5b3c75cb077da601d22f37)
- Add TLSfuzzer submodule configuration, pinning the fuzzing tool version used by CI. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cccbb4fa60ca890a0ce6757fcba5669208fffa46)
- Add initial TLS Fuzzer test infrastructure, providing a framework for running TLSfuzzer cases against OpenSSL handshake protocols. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/db87f89b7393eea395b82050c7fc4e1869ef112e)
- Run TLSfuzzer tests in CI, continuously checking compatibility between TLS protocol implementation and fuzzing test cases. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e66c41725f03dae2b295df048312fe6d28729e98)
- Add EC parameter import tests, verifying that incorrect elliptic curve names are rejected. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d4d8f163db1d32c98d8f956e6966263a7a22fac1)
- Statically link the legacy Provider into endecode_test, ensuring the test can access legacy algorithms when Provider dynamic modules are unavailable. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7ee992a5d931ab5ad9df00d2d8e47e1b7a72d7ac)
- Run tests only when the TLSfuzzer environment is correctly configured, avoiding CI failures due to missing test dependencies. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e5fb4b1469f317aa92768cdf804dfa29b72cb8f3)
- Add tests for fetching encoders, decoders, and store loaders with query strings, verifying that attribute queries participate in Provider selection. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f5e97b3702916e69873746108ac7c100a31d2241)
- Add lock protection for TSAN operations as needed in thread tests, accommodating different TSAN detection capabilities. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3d4d5305c292f5db62b4abf732f6682b2ada6f44)
- Allow PEM_write_bio_PKCS8PrivateKey_nid() to use an empty passphrase, supporting PKCS#8 output without passphrase encryption. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1d28ada1c39997c10fe5392f4235bbd2bc44b40f)
- Add test: verify FIPS Provider on 3.0 branch using master build. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3fdf4b9365900889b54734a348012eae38dedce5)
- Skip evp_test cases when the required r parameter is unsupported, avoiding invalid failures. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bbbd1210b43d7a7aff60ccc3c92561beaf6b2bb3)
- Add test: verify FIPS Provider on master branch using 3.0 build. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0c47b8a879c6cd2d553831f930af5ee9df291eca)
- Apply correct Apache 2.0 license to test code. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7585073892af9cffd28b7b5872c2b102b99af807)
- Check whether the library context created by OSSL_LIB_CTX_new succeeds before using it in subsequent test operations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8d215738a05350baa583c47a2c52371d9cff3197)
- Perturb malloc return addresses in tests to expose implicit assumptions about allocation addresses or memory layout. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c1b364cee39029e7c37ea97804d22c248a40d582)
- Add configurable fibre stack allocation and deallocation functions for POSIX async contexts, and provide query interfaces, allowing embedders to manage async stack memory. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f6f56f4776727e18d4dd5490e3b507bae068013a)
- Make ossltest engine tests not depend on platform-specific temporary files or environment assumptions, improving test portability across systems. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/abdb2278d2b65ae87bee3121be83322e4219b396)
- Deprecate the negative return value retry convention for certificate verification callbacks, instead using SSL_set_retry_verify() to explicitly set retry status, and complete its documentation generation dependencies. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/dfb39f73132edf56daaad189e6791d1bdb57c4db)
- Add return value checks for tests and application helper functions that call BIO_new, ensuring BIO allocation failures do not lead to null pointer usage. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/edba19760fa682ed095ca26ba89ba95530003bfe)
- openssl rsa -check now accepts both regular RSA and RSA-PSS keys, avoiding misreporting valid RSA-PSS private keys as non-RSA. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/388d6f4506892a47e69d28232c4b7ebd43706478)
- Cross-compilation targets do not run tests that depend on the AF_ALG kernel interface, avoiding misjudging target platform test failures on the build host. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fecae608a9ad366a1bc740ad94628520cdf38d25)
- Correct documentation describing that TLS 1.3 session ticket lifetime hint can exceed one week, explaining the protocol's upper limit on this hint value and actual ticket validity rules. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0089cc7f9d42f6e39872161199fb8b6a99da2492)
- Add regression test for duplicate HelloRetryRequest, verifying that clients reject a second HRR per protocol. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/db44b55aaa42141921217183667800425227b658)
- openssl cms adds -digest parameter, allowing CMS signing of precomputed hexadecimal digests. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/07342bad1bf850657e1a1f21188ee9a8a75e3a19)
- FIPS installation tests fall back to config(FIPSKEY) when FIPSKEY is not set in configuration, accommodating different configuration sources. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ffc22e038e92d5dd956362fa48babd03cff202d1)
- SSL_CONF_cmd allows loading DH parameters at any position in the configuration command sequence, and continues attempting to decode supported parameter formats. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b2b8d1883a3b7e64006b0b4ada0cbcf3eb6dba1a)
- EVP MAC tests recompute tags after context reinitialization, covering MAC context reuse behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e58ba181de6b0dfad0dc371f8d962c82138a906e)
- Uniformly switch internal case-insensitive comparison calls to OPENSSL_strncasecmp, ensuring comparison behavior is provided by OpenSSL's cross-platform implementation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fba140c73541c03e22b4fdb219a05d129bf0406d)
- Add test for fetching EC public keys using Turkish locale, covering the impact of locale-specific case rules on identifier lookup. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c29cf39449f78008e39af8f83760f2464815248b)
- Add test that calling Final on an uninitialized SipHash context should fail, covering illegal finalization states. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4b694f29ea78ab8a94e67c89d4d81df18c5e3bf1)
- Add test for EVP_PKEY_CTX_new_from_name without explicitly calling OpenSSL initialization, verifying that the API can perform required initialization itself. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2d96bfd957149e491feba55a3d04afb26b2668b5)
- Improve locale tests, covering algorithm name lookup results in more language environments. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/93983e555531a8d9bf70d12e4cfdb5ce2f337e3b)
- Add tests for invalid OCSP responses and the -no_cert_checks option, covering response handling behavior when certificate checks are skipped. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6ee1f4f40b5100ef2744866a727bb4b9ef8ea39e)
- Update copyright years of files involved in this modification to the current year. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fecb3aae22aeda493f348739ebf7943071ecdbe1)
- Include e_os.h before including standard string.h, ensuring platform macros correctly affect system header declarations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cf91a2b3c196ee4d7be93ab9f8fc8e097128ad68)
- Disable corresponding command-line tests when TCP Fast Open is not enabled in the build, avoiding tests depending on non-existent features. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/be4b2ed2fbae0efe8d4984d1bfefe5a8e3a45d02)
- Add tests for default key lengths of Blowfish cipher modes, preventing CFB/OFB defaults from deviating again. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/091e60c42c5d2a194936da7f4de3ce82527b27a3)
- Add QUIC wire format support code, providing variable-length integer encoding/decoding for QUIC packet fields. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/416d0a638c1635a182e57fe80c7c065dd76818c0)
- Check whether the UnsafeLegacyServerConnect configuration item exists, and add renegotiation-related test coverage. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/abe90105ba0908d5a2f500997f2bf2fceb263acd)
- Adjust commands and dependency settings for running SSL tests individually, making it easier for developers to run only specified test files. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/eec204f4b19f86e726aa09c5c919a57bdf2ee1d0)
- Add thread synchronization for configuration loading state of OSSL_LIB_CTX_load_config, avoiding races when multiple threads concurrently load/update configuration. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ef7a9b44f04ef18b652cb47cd9eb3826301cca9e)
- Update expired SCT issuer certificates used in tests, restoring certificate transparency test validity. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/770aea88c3888cc5cb3ebc94ffcef706c68bc1d2)
- Add first runnable empty QUIC protocol test, verifying that QUIC SSL objects and method frameworks can be called by test programs. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e44795bd5db081260ef05c7be6fd17c080ed9437)
- Update several other certificates that are about to expire and affect tests, keeping certificate validation tests repeatable within validity. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5d219937d067a761fb871483369a6020c60a3cb8)
- Add positive test for key checking with unknown private key parameters, verifying that the check API does not incorrectly reject acceptable Provider private keys. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/08e0aad293f1c283dccf7e9065ec28af5e143304)
- PKEY KDF tests first query actual derived output length, then allocate buffer, to verify failure handling for oversized output sizes. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f68283c18eaf015e7500e59a6adf3dbb3ee74f59)
- Constant-time modular exponentiation always ends with normal Montgomery reduction regardless of input size, unifying result reduction and avoiding returning non-canonical values. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0ae365e1f80648f4c52aa3ac9bbc279b6192b23e)
- Test recipe setup parameter handling for paths with spaces uses safe argument splitting, fixing test failures when working directory or source paths contain spaces. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e3ba938b584275839dda404d846abdb06a88693f)
- set_dateopt returns success status when date options are successfully set, making the -dateopt parameter of openssl commands take effect properly. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/55b7fa2609e1fe354517a745b78182323bce24ed)
- Add tests for decoding EC explicit parameters and then importing/exporting, verifying that the decoded-from-explicit attribute is not lost. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f7346cab45a7d2090009e96b304e4196038697f4)
- Adjust bitwise boolean expression writing in tests, eliminating Clang diagnostics for misuse of bitwise operators. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6162a2402d6b47c597c271bfb6a67d64bf183383)
- Add more test coverage for optional build switches, expanding build and test scope for different configuration combinations in CI. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c267588fd400593c090ebb24643c2be5158bfbcc)
- Add AES-OCB test vectors, verifying encryption/decryption results for multiple key, nonce, AAD, and plaintext sets. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2f19ab18a29cf9c82cdd68bc8c7e5be5061b19be)
- When a digest or cipher algorithm is disabled at build time, evp_test skips MAC tests that depend on them to avoid false failure reports. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c8a016cac44d5402df3106f46c9725aa1b480e40)
- ocspapitest checks the return value of functions that may allocate memory, and safely terminates with an error report on allocation failure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ea809510f69e5aebc2ab95aa7530e01060e8a960)
- dh_to_text prints the private key length when it is set on the DH object, preserving short private key information when outputting parameter files. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ff54094cb9e1e5033f6e3e72717e741cf24f5c29)
- Tests short private key generation for known DH primes, verifying the behavior of the specified private-value length. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2266d1cad008ef03cb0791397b1cca9aaa6a4428)
- Copies the private key length when duplicating finite field cryptographic parameters, preventing DH parameter copies from losing short private key configuration. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5f311b10ab3dd6417a3247c62b4ec072751459db)
- Fixes an explicit null pointer dereference path by checking potentially null references before access. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d768f853bb05b5a49a2aeb5b5702776834e68d06)
- External OQS Provider tests now use an available liboqs working copy to ensure interoperability tests can run after CI builds. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7e32ca79e33546682a5a75bb41b9d6dcd732787a)
- Adds encoding and decoding functions for QUIC frame fields, reading and writing frame types and variable-length fields according to the QUIC wire format. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/dffafaf48174497a724d546c3483d2493fc9b64c)
- Adds handshake tests to verify that the TLS state machine still handles correctly when the peer's first application data record and Finished record exchange order. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4000827fdbf3f6d70949186fdd2bc57638500885)
- Releases evictable entries in the session cache before adding a new session, avoiding the inability to accommodate new sessions when the cache reaches its limit. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4842a27b902660b672d72d2ed23e941461ca481c)
- Adds EVP_DigestSign tests to confirm that the first signing call correctly fills in the output signature length. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fc5888ccb60f33b366972299f30b976c4dc12162)
- Defines OSSL_TIME as a structure type, providing a clear representation for the SSL subsystem to carry time values and time units uniformly. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d13c8b7725437490be8c1a2b438936af10f808d0)
- Updates SHAKE documentation to explain the default output length and how to set variable-length digests. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b7cf9dd2393de8e90a15e83466d9b8b781b18385)
- Builds UBSan configuration with -DPEDANTIC to expand strict compilation coverage for undefined behavior detection builds. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/17b94de3df327e6619e52529e345a340d4a0a100)
- Adds code signing purpose validation tests for the CMS application, ensuring CMS signer certificates are validated for code signing purposes. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/19914fec9bac08ca7c7917eddc1b7d1dba67e4a7)
- Migrates ssl3_get_record calls to tls_read_record, allowing the SSL state machine to read TLS data through the new record layer. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4030869d24309bfb5292e7bec41cd2b3012ba99d)
- Passes SSL options to the record layer, ensuring that protocol-specific features and record configuration continue to take effect in the new implementation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/79eebb08434e31aede316d934b53e4096c131b8f)
- Updates the oqs-provider submodule pointer so that external post-quantum Provider tests use a version containing the required fixes. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ac837d4d2eb542a9119bd8ed0ade7242887c531b)
- Adds EC_KEY_set_private_key behavior tests to verify the key object state after setting the private key. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/279754d4199f6e80e17b3e08fa261fbfd3e646c5)
- Adds CODE-OF-CONDUCT.md, publishing the OpenSSL community collaboration code of conduct. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fa4e92a70a5f363fbbee192c0ecab697e3aa1248)
- Switches TLS session timeout calculation to OSSL_TIME, unifying time acquisition and timeout comparison. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/200d9521a0d406a7d02778d1c6c5a5230caeecf5)
- Fixes compilation errors after importing recordmethod.h, making the record layer interface header compile. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/555dd9390ba56f1c400d3f067a2dfe7b00fbf7d3)
- Adds DTLSv1_listen tests to confirm that listening handshake behavior remains normal after record layer changes. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/723844d3762c05727e8f6f21d0a1098e23302ebd)
- Moves ktls.c into the record layer directory, centralizing record layer and KTLS implementations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ec279ac21105a85d9f11eed984eb64405811425d)
- Fixes explicit null pointer dereferences identified by Coverity, checking before using certificate or record objects. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fbeb4866f4250a4a23e7afb884a0aa0456d152f8)
- Updates oqsprovider/liboqs submodule to v0.7.2, using a compatible post-quantum algorithm Provider version. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0b664228a8b217751a225e383a47936fab5c1d64)
- Makes unit tests select expected results based on the FIPS Provider version, accommodating algorithm behavior differences across Provider versions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/eaac0584db6e7452fdb627502527fb0678bb9a93)
- evp_test supports selecting test vectors conditionally based on the FIPS Provider version, avoiding false failures due to output differences between old and new versions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/54a7bbedf43a1ade98c8f47eb8896d75f3db0165)
- Skips tests related to explicit EC curve parameters when using the 3.0.0 FIPS Provider, as that version does not support the corresponding functionality. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e1289d90d0069ea1c3ea8ae80bfc3916077ec24e)
- Explicitly declares CTLOG_FILE and TEST_CERTS_DIR dependencies for ssl_new tests, ensuring complete test environment preparation steps. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8b3ccf1cd590c3f9f63d4665106fa4defbffa51f)
- Adds tests for session ticket callback returning 0, covering the behavior of applications rejecting ticket generation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3b7a3241c225b152ba8519f540bcac5b680312c2)
- Detects at test runtime whether the FIPS random number generator is used, determining test expectations based on actual runtime capabilities. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c91f972c9fba61c5db761a49e13df4dadcba068a)
- Fixes null pointer dereferences reported by Coverity, ensuring no further access after object creation failure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/82d46d14462491681f25d016508715e85c1dc4d1)
- Adds BIO_s_dgram_pair, providing paired in-memory datagram BIOs for testing and local send/receive. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b88ce46ee88c4128f72694e42160622844971d04)
- Removes perror output from within the library, using the OpenSSL error queue to preserve calling context and unify diagnostic format. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ecc920b3277311e859282b6d400ba8566d7ea8c1)
- Fixes the s390x ECDH shared secret derivation process, ensuring correct elliptic curve key agreement output. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e869c867c1c405de3b6538586f17b67937556a4b)
- Adds TLS 1.3 record padding API tests, verifying the padding length set by the client and the record output. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/508e087c4c9e0f6548816e0044022b257f179585)
- Proposes the introduction of the OSSL_CRYPTO_ALLOC attribute to annotate the return value attributes of allocation functions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ac44deaf00ad24fd18b9d74de4a23d98a2b75c8d)
- Switches QUIC time handling to OSSL_TIME, removing platform dependency on timeval. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2de00f4f1e20d3dd4cb8e3165f30146c1294f6d4)
- Places OSSL_TIME in the public internal area of libcrypto, allowing multiple libraries to share the time type. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0747f94b5f7b7f07f21384507ba1adaea6f99e88)
- Adds a design note for the QUIC TX Packetiser, describing how frame data is combined into QUIC packets to be sent. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8d703a045f553eeebc1a2267a6a66c580b48031f)
- Restores EVP_PKEY_eq compatibility for comparing keys from the 3.0.0 FIPS Provider. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8c7d847e2e6ac6bfded210c19fd8461254bb2be3)
- Improves error handling in X509_STORE_CTX_set_default and fixes callers ignoring failure results. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a1de5eb88479515535e5de090ded800455c3d4a7)
- Changes Provider constructor results to an enumeration, clearly distinguishing method creation success, failure, and skip. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/830225901365b7076eaa5afc580529394e2a137f)
- Extends DTLS dropped record tests to cover more message types and retransmission and state recovery after packet loss. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e5a7536eaeaacd18d1aea59edeb295fb4eb2dfca)
- Fixes occasional failures in BIO_dgram_pair randomization tests, stabilizing datagram endpoint read/write timing. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/681c461910b1b72af263ec735bac1310b2fadcd0)
- Unifies the compression build macro to OPENSSL_NO_ZLIB, making the zlib option follow OpenSSL's feature disable naming convention. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/59d21298df9176b64b41cc8583c7024f7f5895d4)
- Extends the COMP_METHOD interface to support certificate compression methods and compression type information. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7e3cacac943d298348d97c8f7f980ca0916378c5)
- Adds the RIPEMD-160 algorithm to the default Provider, allowing the default algorithm set to obtain this digest. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ecd831469919215b0a45693b00ec0fd7d42d5d61)
- Makes pyca tests link against the OpenSSL built in the current workspace rather than other versions of the library on the system. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/097752da99d9c27702e9e9d51609efedd3a4d0cf)
- Serializes concurrent thread join operations to avoid state races when multiple threads wait on the same thread simultaneously. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4e43bc06f7673597a99f61325543449e72070c8c)
- Replaces test_thread_noreturn with new thread testing tools, covering thread exit and join behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6ca4bd2e4c92531e74acba3e1ff08e6fbb664b20)
- Adjusts test expectations on platforms where the EGD random source is insufficient, avoiding treating entropy requests that EGD cannot satisfy as random number failures. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/04d07ffbed483660c96d3b197df28ab3b1420637)
- FIPS self-tests use RSA CRT parameters for RSA operations, covering the correctness of private key CRT parameters in self-tests. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c7424fe68c65aa2187a8e4028d7dea742b95d81a)
- Checks whether read/write buffers are actually allocated or freed, fixing duplicate allocation/deallocation or incorrect status reporting. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ee05588dabeac7b9d034bf16dad122a93d1688a4)
- Corrects Dependabot configuration documentation, clarifying that the configuration file is not a GitHub Actions workflow. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/950968982a0c75d7c380f9d5a81e7d71b953af5a)
- Skips legacy DES-based tests in FIPS mode, avoiding including non-approved algorithm behavior in FIPS expectations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5db2b4a292b4576185287a9e01e4ba4098b4aa66)
- Updates FIPS installation tests to cover current self-test classification and installation verification behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6e38ac39bbf2bc899485c5f710a2ced6238b1ea1)
- Adjusts DSA/EC pairwise consistency tests according to FIPS 140-3 IG 10.3.A, and supplements corresponding specification condition descriptions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5b234be4c44f5b178bc69da3d610ae1b70441873)
- Verifies after FIPS self-tests that the RNG has been restored to its pre-self-test state, avoiding leftover test random number generator state. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/33290c534750f031cbf384f0ad8c05555a16f726)
- Fixes potential null pointer references in test code, checking allocation results before reading structure members. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/15c8df81083f31dd35aedbe2d58ec702d0c0dc65)
- Updates GitHub Actions dependency versions, adopting Dependabot-suggested workflow versions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4ff66347f0ab3c054c5622dd862f36c731c889ed)
- Confirms that EVP_PKEY contains a private key before EdDSA signing, returning an error if the private key is missing without calling the signing function. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f5a10d5cc19215ab22be55b4a2ee1e41bd38fb14)
- Adds hash command alias signature tests, verifying that command forms like openssl sha256 select the correct digest algorithm. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e9e6827445528caf1d9d6647953fbe67a0c78716)
- Adds the QUIC CFQ (send queue) module, managing pending frames and the send queue by priority. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c282da8bc77500cb40ec63754b5230b4bc883242)
- Implement QUIC Packetization/Packet-in-flight management interface, organize packet sending and in-flight status. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d77aea591650cd3bfe7c25cbb6955011bb21b416)
- Add QUIC Frame-in-Flight data flow design description, describe how frames are associated with sent packets and confirmation lifecycle. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0ede517cfa73fd3566d2ecd32215b4b12dd1d3b5)
- Revert skipping FIPS mode DES testing, restore original test coverage. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/75fcf1062817421d8c5850ad0d52a913a2e6499a)
- Limit BN_mod_exp_mont_consttime modulus bit length and internal table size to prevent integer overflow or excessive allocation from extremely large inputs. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4378e3cd2a4d73a97a2349efaa143059d8ed05e8)
- Change Punycode decoder output writing to WPACKET management, unify boundary checks and remove custom range operations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/905ba924398f474e647de70345b4ae4089fedba7)
- CMAC parameter setting rejects non-CBC cipher modes, ensuring the algorithm works per CMAC specification. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/94976a1e8d9b127999df14c2e0c38e918c2badda)
- Update Ed25519 certificate validity in tests to avoid expiration affecting continuous integration. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/42f917432999b34ad8618ae03a5f199738a2b5ba)
- Re-issue test root CA certificate and extend its validity to ensure certificate chain tests remain valid for years. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/43086b1bd48958ce95fadba8459ad88675da4fdf)
- Explicitly cast format parameter related values to matching types, fixing printf format type warnings. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1555c86e5f7e3c46b4f696ed665c2f988976b81f)
- Add QUIC receive stream management module, responsible for caching and reassembling receive stream data by offset. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bbf902c34a90435bacea8a551ac39a559c8df6b2)
- Integrate QUIC receive stream data delivery into flow control module, limiting received bytes by connection/stream window. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e77396f6f508f604b69f795e624896c427fe8b06)
- Add SSL_shutdown test under asynchronous write conditions, verifying state handling when closing connection before pending data completes. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cbf965b4f3ba8567624767239aebe4d04c62558a)
- Remove explicit duplicate checks for legacy ENGINE options, unify handling of legacy implementation availability via opt_legacy_okay. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2fea56832780248af2aba2e4433ece2d18428515)
- Use openssl/e_os2.h to obtain OpenSSL-defined base types instead of directly depending on stdint.h. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7bc5ce4a79c61ab7238b188f9af48f41ff1392f9)
- Add DH parameter check test with non-null libctx, verifying check operation uses the library context of the DH object. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5e38e0acf4e1681ae32fa1b164adbc08719bd613)
- Skip timing_load_creds test on VMS platform because it does not support its timing or process operations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/81929ac49aa583b2347348953d8399ad775c6fd1)
- Use OPENSSL_SYS_* platform macros instead of system-specific macros to improve cross-platform consistency of timing_load_creds. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/83c1220ad137bb4b651478444c3666c66ec9d640)
- Split thread pool test into standalone test/threadpool_test for independent execution and reporting by component. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c48c32807f2d945a9672c48b59bff4083885a5bc)
- Implement QUIC send packet assembler and stream mapping, splitting pending STREAM frames and mapping them to QUIC packets. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a73078b79fc6f229b95312dcb20e4f61120a108c)
- Implement RFC 9180 HPKE, providing public interfaces and algorithm support for encapsulation keys, AEAD encryption/decryption, and mode negotiation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ad062480f7490197b174edad8625ce40d74f6e68)
- Honor explicit OSSL_PKEY_PARAM_EC_POINT_CONVERSION_FORMAT setting, and default to exporting uncompressed EC points when not set. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a16e86683e8d76c4b9268d757c584b5c971db728)
- Improve version parsing and failure handling in FIPS version check script, making it robustly distinguish Provider versions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/abff8bd842b802c09b981b7552bd92ef1d0ced64)
- Add RFC 6979 deterministic ECDSA known-answer tests, using EVP test format to verify signature results. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5375fd8e948234e8b8a10ded94badf6f59b53608)
- Remove extra printf used for debugging in evp_test to avoid extra output polluting test results. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/12c20c5486b6440a9b667c93f130a8fdea029b81)
- Change "a RSA" to "an RSA" in documentation to conform to English grammar. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a63fa5f711f1f97e623348656b42717d6904ee3e)
- RSA-PSS signature queries the actual salt length used by the Provider, ensuring signature encoding reflects Provider-generated parameters. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5a3bbe1712435d577bbc5ec046906979e8471d8b)
- Limit RSA-PSS salt length to within digest length, rejecting configurations exceeding algorithm constraints. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6c73ca4a2f4ea71f4a880670624e7b2fdb6f32da)
- Update actions/setup-python to 4.3.1, fixing GitHub Actions Python environment version. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7176c1af1077e1740f9d5e0dfc1028cf8a422792)
- Fuzz testing CI uses common include directory, compatible with header installation locations of different fuzzers. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/aef6b82882750594b7f8e50ad734d9fe1d70f049)
- run-checker CI changes no-shared to no-modules, testing no-module builds without mistakenly deleting shared library configuration. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d5696547e46e9ea85fcb7581b9d49c58b7c24eeb)
- Revert run-checker change replacing no-shared with no-modules, restoring original shared library check configuration. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b71b9534c423eaae79378556337e466a7dec8e35)
- Skip RSA implicit rejection tests that depend on synthetic plaintext under legacy FIPS Provider, avoiding tests using historical Providers lacking this behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ddecbef6e389d263b728b7fa30fd3d9ce13feddb)
- Allow OBJ_create to create object mappings with short/long names when OID string is empty, while still rejecting invalid calls with all three parameters empty. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b79da97cf8751d7b196a87cc8bced0bb3334a0d3)
- Mark timing_load_creds as requiring POSIX.1-2001 interfaces like getrusage, and restrict builds on platforms not meeting requirements. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/adf289b5b67ecb414ab709a2c25b0c6f0d463d31)
- Update actions/setup-python to 4.4.0, following CI dependency versions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/43a9e682d80d0abe4ffd0c76d18c43cf059a2bcc)
- PKCS#8 tests ignore extra CR characters in text comparison, compatible with Windows newline differences. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a78a7917aef5134cc6efd77e7dbf7d18bbaec528)
- Add OSSL_PARAM_BLD zero-value BIGNUM test, covering encoding behavior of zero numeric parameters. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b49cf273883c8d3f47542941fe5cc6cb51aec9c9)
- SSKDF returns SIZE_MAX when querying KDF output length via KMAC, indicating output length depends on KMAC configurable length. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e8add4d379075a6daef2591edd830297d469b9f4)
- RSA_generate_multi_prime_key returns error when public exponent e is NULL, no longer crashes due to null exponent dereference. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7efc653c43851dcbc3ec043baded029c7d31ab9f)
- Initial encryption level packet discard is handled by upper layer, no longer automatically discarded by QUIC receive layer, to correctly control connection state. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/203b0d00e00f524f786f5e911f889cc96e32402d)
- Prohibit QUIC TXP from sending STREAM frames before handshake completion, avoiding application stream data being sent earlier than allowed handshake state. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cda88bafe7532083a1e7c5bc08a9971735724c10)
- Temporarily disable unstable QUIC front-end API tests, re-enable after interface implementation is complete. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b940f943a245ae5a5ea6f62417a21fe05933e973)
- QUIC demux supports path MTU that changes over time or is auto-discovered, limiting UDP packet size based on current network path. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d7668ff21328c03f137d665b37f228e7c1f7a32a)
- Revert restriction on BN_mod_exp_mont_consttime modulus size, restoring original legal large number input range. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/92d306b32b63dd502531a89fb96c4172be0ddb49)
- Limit modulus size for bn_mul_mont and BN_mod_exp_mont_consttime, preventing table size overflow or abnormal memory consumption from excessively large inputs. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/30667f5c306dbc11ac0e6fddc7d26fd984d546ab)
- Update actions/setup-python to 4.5.0. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c4edfa220e6d3705a0c6299463c83e61fd5f9d2c)
- Add QUIC test server, implementing listening, handshake, and basic connection handling. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/51a168b804be963e320c5515656301d25ea48322)
- Add QUIC test server echo test, verifying server can receive and echo client stream data. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f42781ad16598ed0d2d23ce8c67a6bea3d7fe0f0)
- bn2bin rejects negative length parameters, avoiding conversion of negative numbers to large buffer lengths or triggering out-of-bounds writes. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c9466f38e0191aa86e0bd49267b0c4ef33e3a3d2)
- Allow reading OSSL_SIGNATURE_PARAM_NONCE_TYPE, enabling callers to query the nonce type used by the signature Provider. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1d857945324810f43a302c9d062c617207093387)
- Cross-compilation CI allows setting target CPU capabilities, configuring runtime capability flags according to target instruction set. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/af0a4c46846323fc33f713b5ccd758a526c19ed0)
- FIPS test documentation states that default property query must include FIPS property to verify algorithms come from a valid FIPS Provider. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d8523bf16205399363604c8fc90256791ad8c019)
- req generates X.509 v3 certificates by default, only generates v1 when -x509v1 is specified; X509_sign ensures version is v3 when extensions are present. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/342e3652c791bdb06e08abcc169b4456c83ccd00)
- Extend record layer constructor parameters, allowing custom methods to receive their private state and initialize record layer objects. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3f9175c7a46b13a3528d9b5776030a78eb1f9454)
- QUIC test server tests end-to-end stream end on both read and write sides, verifying EOS signal propagation and shutdown behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c0f694039a863a7f8999695e30fd93de23c9ae43)
- Clarify purpose of QUIC ACK probe types, distinguishing retransmission probes and PTO probes. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8ca3baa9bdf972b963a70769780db67ebcbdf779)
- Allow QUIC TXP to generate PTO/ACK probe packets, sending probe traffic per ACK Manager requests. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fee8f48e35f7009065227ae76c50672632ea5c40)
- Organize interfaces and state handling in QUIC probe support, ensuring unified probe packet parameters. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2477e99f1055194902dc4864124316ea57ac3efa)
- ChaCha20-Poly1305 no longer accepts truncated IV, requiring standard full-length nonce, avoiding incompatibility from different implementations with short nonces. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a01152370676e7e11fb461cff8628eb50fa41b81)
- Add another BIO_set_md return value omission check test, covering digest BIO configuration failure for other signing/verification entry points. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1bb5ce7a3327335690a9f5c8a0b64a0b491a4bf5)
- Adjust post-handshake fuzz test state machine so fuzz input can reach post-handshake message processing code. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2b9e2afc382490592078cdb69d06f54f0fefd4c6)
- Unify FIPS module source file classification, organizing build boundaries for approved algorithms and non-FIPS implementations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/25624c9087d5422c3bb93cd987a066cb7c883a16)
- Expand QUIC header file comments, explaining state and call constraints of key interfaces. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/da81f1e563c80a1d4ab82e545f3f5ba6e715267e)
- Do not mark test server as connected before handshake confirmation, avoiding mistaking TLS handshake completion for QUIC handshake confirmed. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ce8f20b6ae8f95493d86ed2f521ad2c371974f45)
- Extend packet corruption test to truncate entire datagrams, verifying error handling for short packet inputs. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6a9ab9bc6879b11110183704ca6364bafe794764)
- Added tests to verify that QUIC connections only advertise the expected supported TLS 1.3 cipher suites. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0c9646ec373e7f3f9b07f218a348ecb82219eaa7)
- Add test to verify static library symbol conflicts are detected. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1817dcaf556df559a32eed14d0947ff961be7b4f)
- Added initial pluggable TLS signature algorithm loading, enabling TLS 1.3 to use non-built-in signature algorithms provided by Providers. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ee58915cfd9d0ad67f52d43cc1a2ce549049d248)
- Ensure session ticket time is non-zero when issuing tickets for early data, providing a valid basis for age calculation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2be5065b0a5a4bfab7424fd8de07b62441be6468)
- FIPS Provider tests no longer run AES-SIV cases, as this mode is not part of the FIPS Provider supported set. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a5ad015898718a99ed6cb3e1d096434e112c2862)
- Update Coveralls GitHub Action to 1.2.3 to maintain coverage CI workflow dependencies. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d0a3b9d1eb1fc510ec3447b44803bbf5520a0c47)
- Update Coveralls GitHub Action to 1.2.4, following up on coverage workflow dependency fixes. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c4cb151b4d19f5f2eb302baafac65e36e4408381)
- Pass supported branch information to Coveralls so coverage reports can distinguish supported maintenance branches. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e8ca529feb0d062ab9f869ac25a37cc4c6b8b329)
- Improve interval caching, reading, and final size management for QUIC receive streams, reducing complexity of out-of-order data handling. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2113ea584cdfd59892bbeb7acd78d8b1a825a156)
- Add randomized test for adding empty final frames to QUIC receive streams, verifying FIN-only stream and empty stream completion behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7fa216095a7ebcfa8fbf9f6143b0fb336e77c964)
- Skip datagram tests that depend on binding when BIO_bind fails, preventing false positives due to environment permissions or port issues. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/12c4e67675e691d7556a526aa062effff05a6532)
- Increase PBKDF2 FIPS self-test salt length to at least 16 bytes, meeting stronger test data requirements. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/451cb23c41c90d5a02902b3a77551aa9ee1c6956)
- Run decoder regression with Punycode fuzzing corpus in routine tests, expanding malformed input boundary coverage. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/931369429564b5a9bb09711de8e885fef546a0ac)
- Allow x509 and other applications to accept a private key when a public key is needed, extracting the corresponding public key from the private key to complete the operation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0e89b396197f75993c8d64c07b4af6aa2d97e2af)
- Disable exponential growth cases in policy tree scale tests based on build/test conditions, avoiding excessive resource consumption by the tests themselves. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f5935fcf8e4bc2191ac4a32e5b7ec32817642f1e)
- OBJ_nid2obj returns an undefined ASN.1 object instead of NULL when receiving NID_undef, unifying object lookup results for invalid NIDs. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/908ba3ed9adbb3df90f7684a3111ca916a45202d)
- Ensure the cmp command uses the last -reqin parameter, avoiding reading old files when repeated. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f1e144f277fd98a0fde73b884aae541fdc73d063)
- Symbol existence tests print specific symbol names when duplicate exported symbols are found, facilitating identification of static library conflicts. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e7a4aac3a56cddf3b75a8fd6d20eee8c612e4b82)
- Add EC EVP test reading x/y affine coordinates, verifying legacy key to Provider parameter conversion results. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1ffb6e19eeee95784456831da329cbccaa59fbcf)
- Add certificatePolicies extension to test certificates, covering policy tree and certificate policy verification paths. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a4e726428608e352283d745cb0716248d29ecf26)
- Implement RFC 7250 Raw Public Keys support, allowing TLS peers to send and verify raw public keys instead of X.509 certificates. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3c95ef22df55cb2d9dc64ce1f3be6e5a8ee63206)
- Add tests for the -drbg_allow_truncated_digests option, verifying FIPS configuration allow/deny behavior for truncated digests. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/78bcbc1ea440feac3e9a3292dba4b055b81ca29e)
- QUIC TXP reports to the caller whether ACK-eliciting packets were sent, for congestion control to update in-flight state. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/134b79c0568457415bdceba03cb355fd746166fc)
- Extend QUIC tserver tests to cover handshake, sending, timeout, and shutdown in Thread Assisted Mode. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3b1ab5a3a0a10798ea9a1547b6cb50182edaeb5b)
- openssl speed adds Provider-based KEM and SIG algorithm tests, measuring post-quantum/custom encapsulation and signature implementation performance. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4557e280086d9e300c56183b8ad0671857530dc5)
- DSA_sign checks that p, q, g and other parameters are non-negative before generating signatures, rejecting illegal large number parameters. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9559ad0e8d433a2a212b63cc848fa2ac82a9b048)
- Update Coveralls GitHub Action from 1.2.4 to 2.1.0. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/37ae8549f03be5ec6478c8f8028668ef477d457d)
- CI checks out submodules to ensure regression fuzzing such as Punycode can obtain corpus files. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1ac0464d4c9cac7294b8fe739600ffbf8b8c7195)
- Declare the data field returned by QUIC get_record as const, preventing TLS consumers from modifying internal receive stream buffers. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2eb91b0ec325924ae4b7dc596617a6fff71d7ae6)
- Unload test provider at the end of CMP tests, avoiding residual provider state affecting subsequent cases. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8835940db58229fc467cdea1eebf3f064352a086)
- Add tests for `fipsinstall -pedantic`, verifying strict mode accepts or rejects installation configuration as expected. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c8093347f736c7991350d26048b680d0e64974a0)
- Update Coveralls GitHub Action from 2.1.0 to 2.1.2, so coverage workflow uses a newer action version. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0e1b7c816dcfa4655f443ae5dd8eea4fb42f3493)
- Replace `{ 0, NULL }` terminators in OSSL_DISPATCH arrays with `OSSL_DISPATCH_END`, aligning end markers with new interface conventions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1e6bd31e58dba0bb5d7f21cf1fe1e0d9e4ee3c30)
- Update actions/setup-python in CI workflow from 4.5.0 to 4.6.0. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3ac96c8f715672ff77025d48b5773f5de4f84215)
- Fix FIPS configuration file path used in SSL API tests, ensuring tests load the `fipsmodule.cnf` generated this time. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bc5d9502c5f3c726e42ef72263a4076fd48300d6)
- Update EVP random number test data to comply with FIPS strict policy requirements for random number configuration. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cf3d5c2fbaf734731b1ccbd3a84e21eeb6d0f30d)
- Update TLS pseudo-random function test data to make TLS KDF tests comply with FIPS strict mode restrictions on digests and key parameters. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e07999369a13a29243f34cbd5d24281783984299)
- Enable strict policy in FIPS module configuration generated during test preparation, ensuring subsequent tests actually cover `-pedantic` mode. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c04e78f0c69201226430fed14c291c281da47f2d)
- Update Dependabot configuration so the dependency update bot uses correct branch scope and contributor agreement label settings. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f9730779eb4f7896f54627c8364af3f30904fe2b)
- Update fuzzing corpus with new input samples to expand coverage of protocol and parsing paths. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/91d39be797fb248edfe9da9678d05c937b1f88af)
- Refactor QUIC congestion control abstraction interface, defining unified state update and send permission interaction for congestion controllers. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/90699176b07469e0b6b688ed88bc3f1deb5ccc26)
- Add tests for QUIC congestion control, verifying NewReno window updates and loss response behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ab11c165f6d9ba1b98c85d4c9d1a906de0fcd13c)
- Supplement and adjust QUIC congestion control test assertions, covering boundary scenarios in controller state changes. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/422368aebd908d6966639597aab786ee12eb62f1)
- Use OSSL_PARAM to represent QUIC congestion controller parameters, making parameter passing follow OpenSSL provider parameter interface conventions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/878df9be67df14c90ef584e5762a8c1f5c8f9749)
- Add X.509 test with CA file path containing dots, ensuring path parsing does not mistake dots in filenames for extension boundaries. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/42a6a25ba4ddb40333e92e6e2fc57625d9567090)
- Extend SSL context minimum/maximum protocol version tests to cover new protocol version boundary combinations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f612673049b93387eb7f93c207aca821496da861)
- Update fuzzing corpus submodule to include new test input samples. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3868807d2fe5a72aa897ce5f7f7ba7e9cc3c09cb)
- Correct spelling error of the word DTLS. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2913b5c09fcc4e5d493589ded2c22a3116127ed0)
- Clear ownership flags of the new session on the original object when copying SSL sessions, avoiding double release of shared resources. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9fdf9a44bbe3827fe653165a07281ccae8ab0947)
- Synchronously update underlying stream state after QUIC unidirectional stream object loss, preventing flow control layer from using invalidated send stream state. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5d27e7e9ae7135f8ba92498e3c4e7f9b77f2f8e3)
- Fix stream creation and lifecycle handling in QUIC failure tests, making tests conform to multi-stream API usage. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9caf981237c3e655c18ebef7193153238f2855db)
- Generate QUIC `MAX_STREAMS` frames based on receiving-side flow control state, advertising to the peer the number of streams this end allows it to create. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a6b6ea17376572e3c0227b98f21dedc48215aa9a)
- Enforce `MAX_STREAMS` limits in QUIC receive path and stream manager, rejecting peer streams exceeding the negotiated stream count limit. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/90cecc406f58b229ffa9c8e8473eaa6924c4a5d5)
- Remove redundant copy of termination reason when QUIC connection terminates, as the reason no longer changes after termination. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/723cbe8a73fe3644bb4d8f20d475e57f44955b54)
- Add tests for QUIC multi-stream support, covering stream creation, acceptance, and multi-stream send/receive behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ed835673ae5d99cac39d0bef6677597a68d1e248)
- Add test verifying that `SSL_free()` correctly maintains shared references when multiple QUIC SSL objects are released in arbitrary order. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/97f30fd5d84d3409a4720226f61e94b6442fb3c9)
- Remove `SSL_attach_stream()` and `SSL_detach_stream()` from public API, avoiding applications depending on unstable internal stream attachment mechanisms. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/de521629c1f296a6eb50a84ab2d2b200fc766bc6)
- Make QUIC stream manager explicitly aware that the current endpoint is a server, to handle stream numbering and stream limits according to client or server rules. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5915a900af86db8625caa77a02fd50cf9a3b3e1b)
- Use random ports for QUIC test server, avoiding test failures due to fixed port conflicts. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1a0de4c1eea1f32a3e1113add26625d49b3854d8)
- Make testutil text output functions thread-safe, avoiding data races when parallel tests write output. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2fa90442984349b41401e008df26ee707b6c851d)
- Resolve function name conflicts in DJGPP environment, enabling compilation of related source files under that toolchain. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6dea91f56dcbcb0979dd36790664808ad960faf9)
- Fix handling when tracing QUIC stream frames and encrypted frames, ensuring these frames are not incorrectly decoded or omitted. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b09e246aba584cd17d1d027f735f238b1b7f082c)
- Add internal functions for setting message callback and callback argument, centralizing callback configuration and keeping both consistent. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5cf99b4040eb1ef63b3254090d16299cad690b1e)
- Update actions/setup-python in CI from 4.6.0 to 4.6.1. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a1c8edcfc907a84d2595bc52ea7a43f4b33c7339)
- Add `SSL_get_handshake_rtt()` API, enabling both client and server to read handshake round-trip time, facilitating application measurement of connection handshake duration. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cee0628e0d53be82bd644ce258c3d3e90e64eced)
- Update the fuzz corpus submodule to a newer dataset, expanding input coverage for parsing tests. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/26baecb28ce461696966dac9ac889629db0b3b96)
- Add `no-http` build option to disable HTTP client functionality at build time. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3ca28c9e81fae36b0b44dc39beecd2b5a7561975)
- Convert characters to `unsigned char` before calling `isdigit()` to avoid undefined behavior triggered by negative `char` values. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8229874476cc2955e6947cf6d3fee09e13b8c160)
- Update Coveralls GitHub Action from 2.1.2 to 2.2.0. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ac083de6513324a5ea9aecbaeccd17ed32716b8e)
- Restrict the FIPS provider version used in KDF tests to avoid test results being affected by incompatible provider versions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/45fefe172aa630686561e5a3cfcb25262db23edc)
- Update the CI test version list to include OpenSSL 3.1.1 in compatibility testing. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/67fc06a776b6c1767caae2893e2782301a1936b2)
- Update the CMAC cipher algorithm list and test cases to align the supported algorithm list and expected results with the implementation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fd54fadba6bc138fb35a82c033c540f7e97322c8)
- Convert characters to `unsigned char` before calling `isspace()` to avoid undefined behavior caused by negative `char` values. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8a2e74d0536c91585fbe789e0ab7b06cab0289c2)
- Check the parsing result of `sscanf()` to avoid using data that was not successfully read when input fields are insufficient. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a3fcafb34994c4864d8dc92a88f9d8e354230d12)
- Update CMAC test data to match the current algorithm list and implementation output. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e8dc77f85f251752258203cf9cbfa077fd8b3173)
- Update the test/integration version of liboqsprovider to a newer release. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f3f3f86a14dac76f3079fb50cabd14fdab418bb0)
- Fix the implementation of the QUIC application-layer coverage time callback so that custom callbacks are indeed used for subsequent timing operations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e3e9794aa49f61e5b034608488034daa01125c85)
- Add QUIC key update tests to verify that connections can still correctly transmit data after rotating sending and receiving keys. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/693b23e3d07813985de510a00b1db58070439a51)
- Allow QUIC applications to actively trigger sending key updates, providing an interface for callers that need to control key rotation timing. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2525109f90cf3a91a909621266ec6854a83805e2)
- Add `no-apps` build option to completely disable the OpenSSL command-line application at build time. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ff88545e02ab48a52952350c52013cf765455dd3)
- Only set the wake-up deadline when QUIC congestion control still allows sending data, avoiding repeated wake-ups when there is no sending quota. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b49d9de0e66a5fe7570652186e3bb8c4a4d9f556)
- Migrate reference counting operations in test code to the structured atomic counting interface, ensuring tests use the same lifecycle mechanism as the production implementation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/59a967030ec1cccf6eb4031a4531c8f555a393ec)
- Change the TLS 1.3 HelloRetryRequest invalid group test to use P-521 instead of X25519, avoiding tests relying on inapplicable group negotiation prerequisites. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5c3c8369f3b42ce4b816606bb9bbad00c664a416)
- Reorganize the runchecker CI tasks, adjusting the grouping and execution configuration of check jobs. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6497ad58588492901838654a36445ad90497ef61)
- Make FFDHE group tests run only when DH is enabled, avoiding failures in build configurations with DH disabled due to inapplicable tests. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e4ab9d5b5cb5a6d792c313ff4d5724612351818d)
- Prohibit calling SRTP-related interfaces on QUIC TLS connections because QUIC does not use DTLS-SRTP negotiation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f082205bcfc8e361e53bb2f39f46b46097ec784a)
- Prohibit using non-QUIC cipher suites on QUIC, avoiding negotiation of ciphers incompatible with QUIC TLS constraints. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/09d56d20a2db3170b97ec98dcde9862ee7e00e78)
- Restrict SSL options that can be set on QUIC connections, rejecting option combinations that would break QUIC protocol or TLS behavior constraints. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f0d9757cafef98a346088b9f7fa988964e301c67)
- Prohibit QUIC connections from calling interfaces that depend on TLS readahead, avoiding applying inapplicable record-layer read-ahead behavior to datagram transport. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d0638fd5f0296ea84ff6fc314e9bfea8b5f06392)
- Prohibit QUIC connections from using TLS early data interfaces, avoiding calls to the 0-RTT data path not supported by the current QUIC API. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/82a2becab332c35b53c31d3f8a743fba66bef869)
- Implement dedicated buffer management for QUIC SSL objects, properly connecting read/write APIs with QUIC stream buffers. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fe33e2c8c1a99b82509e1119235dd106118c3f84)
- Implement HelloRetryRequest handling for QUIC SSL handshake, enabling QUIC to complete the TLS 1.3 group retry flow. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a1c56bbe79bcafb25880ce1deb7b75e2c6f5e0ce)
- Allow QUIC SSL objects to bind a datagram BIO via `SSL_set_fd()`, enabling common file descriptor initialization methods for QUIC. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5e6015af4df7c4b3ef2e6c3c2f3657bafde88805)
- Prohibit QUIC from using TLS pipeline-related operations because the datagram and stream model of QUIC does not support that record-layer pipelining mechanism. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/38c0ff1f404a25bc6711a2055efd92a20820ec38)
- Restrict protocol version settings for QUIC connections, avoiding versions that applications configure but are unsupported or do not meet QUIC requirements. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0eecf8418a7bdff1b19c319b4c5973ce8d53b92e)
- Implement QUIC handling of `SSL_set_quiet_shutdown()` so applications can set quiet shutdown behavior according to interface conventions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f66f0d3ce1667c04d08f158565320237a59593f6)
- Prohibit copying QUIC SSL objects, avoiding `SSL_dup()` producing copies that cannot correctly share the underlying connection and stream state. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/764817c4aa1b7f9aa188cab0d3b2033e08025c73)
- Prohibit calling `SSL_clear()` on QUIC SSL objects, avoiding resetting generic TLS state and breaking QUIC connection state. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5f69db396c61165b25c38a7506d608200561f228)
- Refine details of QUIC SSL behavior implementation to match the latest API constraints and connection state rules. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6e5550a1045bb03afb40dac29f82cbc7158dbfc3)
- Output `uint64_t` in `%llx` format to avoid truncating values on platforms with narrower `long` bit widths. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0cea6df239fc5c5c5902b4c660305bf953f03eb1)
- Skip QUIC SSL trace tests in configurations with related features disabled, avoiding tests calling capabilities not built. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1be2ee683c3cbd8690728a05e05b778d5f7674b8)
- Add an injectable `fake_now` clock to the QUIC test library, allowing tests to deterministically verify timeouts and timed events. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f9fcc7c727f0589fcd1f28ef09c8e10deee4f229)
- Skip SSL trace tests when ECX is disabled, avoiding tests using unavailable X25519/X448 paths. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/47ef3b9fc03c92f6a387927f1b8b0fe1014eb646)
- Do not build QUIC SSL trace tests when DH is disabled, avoiding tests depending on finite-field key exchange not compiled into the build. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/61cc84d9f9d8ad3f918d5bd908096d39b72c3969)
- Add tests for `OSSL_ERR_STATE_save()` and recovery operations, verifying that error stack state remains consistent before and after temporary operations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ff0de1637b5e25719a976c85f969598086d80358)
- Modify the issue feedback template to guide users to submit usage issues to GitHub Discussions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bd38c6b61be589e97495742a33d4e38dfbd63bb0)
- Update actions/setup-python in CI from 4.6.1 to 4.7.0. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/dbe36351dc3fcb5bd3582075b40d34e0b103b15c)
- Update Coveralls GitHub Action from 2.2.0 to 2.2.1. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bdff3258310a30e216b9c5620fd2f4eaf4b90438)
- Only save relevant pointers when using a static buffer WPACKET in QUIC packet headers, avoiding pointers pointing to freed or non-static buffers. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/69aef722645a6b0b2708ca3f08dde1599e2998a4)
- Add multi-threaded concurrent PEM file loading tests, verifying that shared decoding and file loading paths are safe across threads. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/29f25a10e505d7b5cabadf457a1be7a5c75a8b80)
- Release buffers no longer needed in QUIC streams and correctly calculate the final size of `RESET_STREAM` based on sent data. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/28d0e35cd675c67355d91caf65aa180df49f9db4)
- Enforce the shortest legal encoding for QUIC frame types, rejecting non-minimal-length variable-length integer representations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6c1d0e28650164d782909abfea92ba834d0babd5)
- Allow the QUIC wire codec to read and write reserved header bits, enabling conformance tests to construct these bits and have the protocol layer perform validation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5b9452e03797e623681d64a0dae6ed1e2cc99f27)
- Add QUIC frame handling conformance tests covering RFC frame formats, state restrictions, and error inputs. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e26dc8e3d54a414ba9dc85f54e13112617e32556)
- Fix QUIC minimal frame encoding tests to accurately verify that variable-length integers use the shortest encoding. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/49a38dee0d65c6fb7ddcf9dbf76e9afa0ed3c776)
- Adjust minor issues in the QUIC implementation to keep stream state handling consistent with test expectations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/96b7df60b3e54641c6046fea31c7a5cb535c2eeb)
- Continue fixing details in QUIC multi-stream state handling to avoid concurrent stream operations entering inconsistent states. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1d547f8fc4fa2bc16fca935703ec90cfdf3fefd4)
- Treat the maximum ACK delay as zero in Initial and Handshake packet spaces per RFC 9000 Section 13.2.1. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/76908b45823f958f29b6bdf11efab6eac47f61ca)
- Add packet size boundary tests for the QUIC sending path, covering minimum, padded, and maximum packet length handling. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d49c6ca7b95902655e200b34876d5ad965428722)
- Allow disabling the QUIC channel event clock advancement during tests, enabling precise control of timing behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/03b3859501b69d48c8710b6a0754842c7166a7c1)
- Add QUIC shutdown flush tests, verifying that stream data that can be sent is flushed during connection closure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cd5e438065a8de960b2581db9ba5a18e406d187e)
- Extend QUIC write/read tests to cover session resumption, verifying that connections can still send and receive stream data normally after resumption handshake. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cf355bd6e5564694e589d3f96e8bde192519649c)
- When automatic configuration loading is disabled, PBE tests explicitly load the required provider, ensuring algorithm tests still have dependencies in that configuration. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/52ea255d9d560513f69c3f7f3f21513a693c865c)
- Skip provider tests that depend on configuration files when the configuration is not loaded, avoiding false positives when the test environment lacks configuration. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a9dde749504065e6e66b63cc12c25381465ec721)
- Skip tests that depend on automatically loading FIPS configuration when FIPS functionality is disabled, avoiding false failures in non-FIPS builds due to missing module configuration. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9a255aa2bda07286d5516a77b269178fc03779b4)
- Send the corresponding `PATH_RESPONSE` when receiving a QUIC `PATH_CHALLENGE` frame, completing the path validation challenge response. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7eb330ff7aa5580d7d97f2d183606c2d6bbbb449)
- Allow QUIC `PATH_RESPONSE` to be sent unreliably, avoiding unnecessary inclusion of path validation responses in the reliable retransmission queue. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/371c29582aa683ab10d58ec448aef1bded208076)
- Do not process QUIC 1-RTT packets before handshake completion, avoiding application data entering the connection before handshake state is established. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2a6f1f2f6e321abe6deb2ce89084ece4aa50b3de)
- Add tests verifying that the QUIC receiving path rejects processing 1-RTT packets before handshake completion. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/869ab3e70708c391ac49fe0fc4f671781c381e3d)
- Add QUIC CRYPTO flow control tests, ensuring that handshake data is correctly limited when reaching the quota limit. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ab6c63456f30a849e3046532c582b4eaff7716d6)
- Add QUIC PADDING fault injection tests, verifying padding frame exception handling and connection state updates. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/97684a1517ec07300cb87cebe107fa7a709a04ba)
- Verify that QUIC PING triggers ACK generation, ensuring probe packets are acknowledged according to the protocol. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/17340e87855fb785a986f09208af4279f74a201f)
- Added QUIC ACK frame generation test to verify that the ACK range and delay are correct after receiving an acknowledgeable frame. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ed0d6ba4589acc4da38c4197910aed9705eba5e1)
- Verified that QUIC ignores the `PATH_RESPONSE` frame when it is not applicable. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a1aff2c63f84ddfdb656302653a29aa5035d7275)
- Verified that QUIC ignores BLOCKED frames with unreasonable content and does not incorrectly adjust flow control state based on them. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/477944b67b26287e1eee0d315f52c5761c71ef84)
- Added QUIC CRYPTO stream malformed data and excessive buffering tests to verify that incorrect offsets are rejected and buffers are limited. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/de56eebd6ac0805172d74c5d5679ca7425ccc631)
- Added `NEW_CONNECTION_ID` frame tests covering connection ID updates and their sequence handling. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ed75eb32f3712b80a47ad783d0082c66164c732f)
- Added tests to verify that duplicate `HANDSHAKE_DONE` and non-minimal encodings are handled correctly per the protocol. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d56b81ac9f02dd55ecf3281d16fdb156897b4d8d)
- Added QUIC connection close reason tests to verify that close error codes and text are correctly propagated. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d49a1634f4efd21807c5c785d2b0d6ef8683e91d)
- Added QUIC `RESET_STREAM` and `STOP_SENDING` tests covering stream reset and stop sending behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d63b8cbb1be215bab4bea34e4a17c7fd13f8da49)
- Added QUIC STREAM, `MAX_DATA`, and `MAX_STREAM_DATA` tests to verify stream data and connection-level/stream-level flow control limits. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1623bf374d4ee3119629c33938fcd075bd79e6a8)
- Ensured that stream names in QUIC tests are non-empty to avoid empty string dereference paths identified by static analysis. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/dbf247ad1dd0fc4c7b365593a9a5c69fc94a3732)
- Fixed static analysis issues in QUIC flow control tests to make test inputs and error path checks safe. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/451055d2882eb81c8d620eb7736a4c1236e30935)
- Only run QUIC record tests that require ChaCha when Poly1305 is enabled, to avoid build or test failures when the algorithm is disabled. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4cc16b0557875829041547dc63c7600b1ca57a14)
- Made the HTTP test server accept IPv6 client connections, expanding local network test coverage for IPv6. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/830b6a13f9aecd42da61b79c93f236575cc58793)
- Handled stateless reset in the QUIC receive path so endpoints recognize stateless reset and terminate the corresponding connection per the protocol. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cdd916313a89def99493e00b49958ced894ca209)
- In legacy SSL tests, check the consistency of the length and data returned by `SSL_get0_alpn_selected()`, and report an error when the length and pointer do not match. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8b940b69457f0dd43496c16afaa01f510f0d8a19)
- Added a test where a thread blocked on `SSL_read_ex()` is woken by a QUIC FIN, ensuring that stream end can unblock waiting. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0fa6612ed69411ddbfca9e7a2e28e263b7b6d346)
- Updated the SSL trace reference documentation to include trace items that the current message callback can output. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b7278eea441cb70debfbbba350026e58ad41cb83)
- Made both client and server continuously run during QUIC test connections, avoiding deadlock caused by one side not being driven during handshake waiting. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/608a95f4969202083eccd4c1c7e91dec021ea79b)
- Made QUIC test client and server share a pseudo-clock to ensure synchronized timeout advancement and reproducible test results. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/617cab094f0f0d4e71f8b9da5663be8ab06cba92)
- Made the QUIC send packet generation function always report whether packets were sent, avoiding callers scheduling the next send based on incomplete state. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/64fd69911e04cec45f65b396a4e91d6caa4fdc9a)
- Allowed RSA control parameter conversion logic to also handle RSA-PSS keys, so legacy RSA controls can be used for PSS type keys. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e2c2cca4b2fd1ad946d93507e9ca4f9ea910a114)
- Continue sending datagrams as long as QUIC has pending data, avoiding stagnation after a single send call processes only one datagram. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/aa433014bb36bfff0af17c0eb9d25b6fb2d7d068)
- Added a test to verify that QUIC sends multiple datagrams at once when appropriate, covering batch send scheduling behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8c5284ff194f444877ae25012d3d07ee46e46219)
- Adjusted CI trigger rules so that failing tests can run on pull requests, catching regressions early. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/86051eb2bb86e3a89e69abfb6419409aa701bcf7)
- Always use `uint8_t` to represent TLS record types, avoiding differences in wire format handling due to enums or platform integer widths. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/eb1eaa9af4c241baea00cb16557f41811ed9e097)
- Corrected the canonical address for IPv6 localhost to `[::1]`, avoiding the use of invalid address `[::]` for local connections. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7a12e7af0fccb51b0a569a1b27de5cd877c966b1)
- Adjusted OS Zoo CI trigger rules so that pull requests can also run that platform's tests. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/467e5c1fb7efee2541b8ce7e5bce39f1b4614079)
- Skip the CMP HTTP IPv6 address test when IPv6 is not enabled on the system, avoiding tests that depend on unavailable network stack features. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/56562c472aa0cdc27670c04dbb7008b099029641)
- Reverted the OS Zoo pull request CI change, restoring the original trigger strategy. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/597ff76e2886df55c0f56ea31ddd4b15f9606429)
- Reverted the CI change that ran failing tests on pull requests, restoring the original test trigger configuration. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/975f372a6f7ae20e0c4c55a930a6844f2585ee6d)
- Added multi-stream tests to verify stream creation backpressure, ensuring that the API blocks or returns the expected waiting state after reaching the stream limit. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/14551f1effa80a773e029d4ae6cb7657eef74bc2)
- Extended the QUIC client test timeout to avoid premature termination of valid connection tests in slow CI environments. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0f9caad5b95e901b87fe45cf85c9582071ca0b23)
- Added QUIC version negotiation tests to verify that endpoints exchange negotiation packets per the protocol after receiving an unsupported version. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/69169cd9faf68e6d8fb83895233c184543151168)
- Added QUIC multi-stream tests covering `WAIT_PEER`, verifying completion behavior when the local end waits for the peer to close. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3bc38ba0712283bbbd57994af0259791dc42e704)
- Added `OSSL_PROVIDER_load_ex()` tests to verify that provider loading and algorithm lookup are correct when a specified context and attributes are used. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4f3e3d9d3cb9632a8263cfe27ff11f342bf93351)
- Extended the X.509 fuzzer to perform verification on the parsed certificate chain, covering certificate path building and validation logic. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/399c2da08ab9c6a382f8e9950742a022e847fec0)
- Changed the QUIC connection close information query API to use a flags field, allowing callers to select which close information to read. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7d9e447ab812df34bba581c5918721cc704fdacb)
- Run encoder/decoder tests with non-FIPS algorithms also supported by OpenSSL 3.0.0, keeping compatibility tests valid for older versions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/75ac8f04b78e9e1283b0402a791728ba2728e159)
- Updated GitHub Actions checkout from 2 to 4, upgrading the action used to check out code in CI. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d4231af60a8d04196b3b873c2fa8638daff36173)
- Added a test to verify OSSL_PARAM_BLD support for empty BIGNUM, ensuring that such parameters can be correctly constructed. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a535e5b73fc374dbbef54d2629728e9602ecf6be)
- Updated the fuzz corpus to the latest commit, incorporating newly generated parser input samples. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/72a0688552176b658943972b282f2716dff614a0)
- Improved CMP protection calculation to correctly handle Edwards curves, ensuring that Ed25519/Ed448 signature algorithms can be used for message protection. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e664ef78b92532bf94c7976b181d88c4abf83074)
