- Updated the FIPS compatibility test matrix, updating the OpenSSL 3.1.1 correspondence to 3.1.2. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/09d73d7ba13b868db96476d265c8d99616ca7809)
- Run all QUIC multi-stream scripts in both blocking and non-blocking modes, expanding stream API test mode coverage. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5881dd2c080c10ab9f9ca38a2db64deaa79f853a)
- Fixed the synchronization method of the QUIC multi-stream script 20 to avoid races between client and server due to different wait orders. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/99d6b9f9e2fbf272160a07bed5f4ab7ce2b3e20e)
- Adjusted the busy-wait strategy in QUIC multi-stream tests to reduce unnecessary CPU consumption while waiting for events and stabilize test timing. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/769c9b1a99b4bb7878a7b4d031d928376de1b8c3)
- Updated copyright year declarations to keep year information in affected source files accurate. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/da1c088f599af3755aaeed1c447a39621ef12e1f)
- Changed the CPU capability object in ChaCha tests to use the unified `OPENSSL_CPUID_OBJ` name, matching the common internal CPU detection interface. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e6b6b18af3e85a6b5f0d8ea1070f7070557d6357)
- Update Coveralls GitHub Action from 2.2.1 to 2.2.3. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ebce766bb82b472eb3b796c5ee5ee2c19beb71f5)
- Fixed pointer declaration format in documentation, changing `foo * bar` to `foo *bar`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bbaeadb068c3289c7df3b7bea0049f70a648ba00)
- Added a test to verify that the client correctly handles the handshake result when the server does not return ALPN. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d012319145b1c95ecb9ada29f4f03a3b30cf0f41)
- Added a test to verify that QUIC handshake fails as expected when the client does not provide ALPN, because QUIC requires application protocol negotiation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/122d4e20cd2e88daa64d8bfcd7b8a6e39a0260e4)
- Use the correct OpenSSL 3.1 build version for the FIPS provider to avoid compatibility tests loading the wrong version module. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8f51b2279eda1e0cffb3400c2e5b5c3771f62ea7)
- Added OSSL_STORE delete API tests to verify object deletion success and failure paths. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b8aca10d8efac1611cfcb739202c34da39f7e3d0)
- Updated OS Zoo CI configuration to restore and maintain automated test tasks for that platform. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e900942587a18cdd6e3b064d6b21c9ce36a7b640)
- Added SSL tests covering read/write poll descriptor and network read/write requirement query interfaces, verifying that returned status matches actual I/O requirements. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7e1b0dc1ef3cf0a7b02af0a09ab8aa5608134990)
- Documented the purpose of the `OPENSSL_TEST_RAND_SEED` environment variable, explaining how to set reproducible test randomness. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7251b2eb14427341630881cf10e4be3fa8661e8c)
- Added noisy datagram BIO tests to simulate loss, delay, or reordering conditions in datagram transmission. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/43b94c7fe4a427ad95f7401dd24f42d2ae094dfb)
- Extended noisy datagram tests to send more data, increasing coverage of packet loss during sustained transmission. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/19d79bb2ba45729a49cbac9f98bd916190be0b4b)
- Used a pseudo-clock instead of real time in noisy datagram tests to eliminate test duration and timing result uncertainty. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5d3933eef0d937a4845a439d5fbfa76738592fc0)
- Extended noisy datagram tests so that packet content can also be perturbed, covering network faults such as bit corruption in addition to packet loss. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b1584a85d07fdf1cfaa7423392fba439f7b6b0ac)
- Ensured that datagrams sent from client to server also pass through the noisy BIO, covering transmission faults in both directions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0a2369fd446e27f59f0025d8d885c07a107df615)
- Added a reference to the socket BIO before handing it to the test server, preventing the server from accessing the BIO after the caller releases it. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f13f9b716e8b148b97dbe49e823b9dc3f235de1f)
- In the QUIC cleanup path, release all nodes of the BIO chain like TLS does, avoiding leaking subsequent BIOs by only releasing the chain head. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/18fd0ea04d6bd37809a4e9a669c49cf9bc146bfb)
- Clarified the terminology for network perturbations such as loss and corruption in the noisy datagram BIO, avoiding referring to all faults uniformly as packet loss. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/523c5a06c590b7f2950043a6b8308c3f3e49cb51)
- Added the `-outpubkey` option to `genpkey` to write the corresponding public key to a separate file when generating a private key. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6c03fa21ed4bbc9fd6d3013fdf9f4646d231f831)
- Remove the temporary implementation of `BIO_ADDR_copy()` in the test tool and test the real public function instead. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d058ae6e0397faaa60c18c6ae3aecaff64dca47b)
- Emit a compatibility warning during provider cross-version checks, alerting to the risk of provider and libcrypto version mismatch. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/54e60d2a05f86e947dface08e5c20b831be17bf8)
- Fix the FIPS version check in the EVP cipher `dupctx` test to avoid treating incompatible versions as context copy failures. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1a18596149a325a679f8244bac52b6796dfcc48e)
- Update copyright years in affected files. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/556009c596e0242689df4c26dd7fccdb7f0e1add)
- Add QUIC client certificate authentication test to verify client certificate request and authentication flow during handshake. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9f6eb62221358fe84b3d70e63378ae651bbc9705)
- Update actions/setup-python from 4.7.0 to 4.7.1. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f7e7bbcd7850b96d02dd0f4dd49b3365b320776c)
- Remove Coveralls no-shared and unsafe fuzz build configurations to prevent inapplicable jobs from polluting coverage results. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4a1bdb0b7a3d04c1f18a27a10a1aab354eef608b)
- Reduce noise generation frequency after detecting QUIC PING to prevent fault injection from continuously interfering with probe packets used for connection liveness confirmation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/21d2041da02c67218c94cef6792d8b84d810710b)
- Add test to verify that application-defined legacy key methods can complete signing, preventing regression in legacy key compatibility. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/860e36d0dd72f4aa4791e88aa185cb42065a30c4)
- Update actions-download-file from 1.3.0 to 1.4.0. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/456e6ca5d73972cdb4228e6c5ec9acdf19237308)
- Make the property list to string function preserve and correctly parse quoted attribute values, avoiding punctuation inside quotes being treated as separators. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fb20e66c6b2651067f50bab8cf098c71e2caed4b)
- Apply backoff processing to the first noise-affected packet sent from client to server, ensuring loss recovery scenarios enter the expected retry flow. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8f67c6bb7cab70bbcc231ee3e18d140a2857ebdb)
- Remove repeated waiting in each round of the test server idle state check loop to reduce test time. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2e62b07a41cca299f7abb69c892053b99ec762b2)
- Use real time for test server timeouts to avoid mismatch between fake clock and system wait API clock base. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/79997a919f6cf3823d04fa9b34adaaa5aadd871a)
- Fix the working directory of `opensslwrap.sh` in the provider compatibility workflow to ensure scripts run from the correct directory. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/29890415487b04b965e47aee21f00a7f6a2e7268)
- Remove the restriction on specific FIPS provider versions for ECDSA/SHA-3 verification tests, allowing tests to run across compatible versions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d0bf0106a5d25f4b9f13ff4e7187c4babe7694e1)
- When the `-server` parameter is an HTTPS URL, automatically treat it as enabling `-tls_used`, and improve option conflict validation and documentation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4a9299ac5090dc7997bd1f2cbc56e5e11c6277ff)
- Replace `and` with `&&` in random number tests so conditional expressions evaluate using C logical operators. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/715242b1ca2b7267a70fb13c3544a84b947a6e81)
- Even if setting the DH public key check result code fails, continue processing according to the verification result of `DH_check_pub_key()`, not misinterpreting auxiliary result assignment failure as invalid public key. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/eaee1765a49c6a8ba728e3e2d18bb67bff8aaa55)
- Split trace statements in trace API tests so each trace output can be independently verified. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0496d2dc35b6165873ce4fe4ffbef2458bdd8a0f)
- Allow RSA-OAEP label to be empty to maintain compatibility with legacy calls. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/21b98da9d80c561b6273b0c51c259196d6740e70)
- Correctly check object identifier conversion results in CMS encryption to avoid using invalid algorithm identifiers. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bd160912dcc5e39bcdc925d9aa6538f20e37ad16)
- Change Java trusted key usage to PKCS#12 command-line option so users can control whether to write this extension. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/21f7a09ca256eee0ccc9a8fc498e8427469ab506)
- Add QUIC post-handshake session ticket test covering the path of receiving NewSessionTicket after connection establishment. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/055f3dd140f124df6f2d8f3f910f00928224b04f)
- Skip QUIC packet data encryption/decryption during fuzzing to reduce unnecessary computation and increase input throughput. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5415383d2c7e8ee8147eb01361f3f952ceec3761)
- Improve QUIC client fuzz harness, expanding connection input and error path coverage. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/acee7d68e1037d18f34d03bcd70af6b1b6e48299)
- Do not immediately fail on invalid DCID during fuzzing so subsequent transport parameter parsing can still be covered. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b62ac1abfcac4091cdf8e5e4194c9e3bcc6d382d)
- Ignore CertificateVerify signature errors during fuzzing so malformed handshakes continue to reach subsequent state machine paths. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/702bb16b9f38c4b17879b0d22bd08ea495c578e2)
- Remove inapplicable fuzz security flags in some builds to avoid affecting regular fuzz targets. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d8bf5ea19d388028004f06f2ffcd40dbd80c1845)
- Make the QUIC client fuzzer advance controllable time to cover timeout and retransmission logic. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9252efdb8d1b21ef05aedef2cc40eee46dd72b96)
- Adjust fuzz CI jobs so each fuzz target runs in applicable configurations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/27d8827052ae3c43316582424e9311aea0255bde)
- Skip QUIC client fuzzing when QUIC is disabled to avoid CI failure due to missing targets. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f2c2857accd5650cbcb0767019abd75925b51a3e)
- When the openssl command-line program is not built, the daily checker no longer attempts to run it. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bde541104908421a46268a88c211c02f71343765)
- Preserve original order when requeueing delayed URXEs to avoid changes in QUIC receive event order. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/86e11b1e78ad6937ef32d64ca34013306c9abc28)
- Increase the initial timeout limit of the QUIC test server to reduce false positives in slow environments. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d73299136ef85d83b221364201619e7342523ad7)
- Output connection close reason in QUIC multi-stream tests to help locate failure sources. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8c110311fd5e7f04e2c152b17d44acfd4a279ea6)
- Add QUIC large client and server certificate chain tests covering handshake buffering and certificate transmission. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3860ef2ae69ad9187acc17e0d1c78261dbc63125)
- Advance QUIC TLS tick for each received packet so the handshake promptly consumes newly arrived data. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/82b7a0eee90e3280bd0e2dd4a9812b3873a7f462)
- Fix QUIC ACK timeout calculation using correct acknowledgment deadline and time base. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4d100bb76ad43da75660fa8661d258eaa78fb1c3)
- Make BLAKE2b use `OSSL_DIGEST_PARAM_SIZE` to set output length instead of inapplicable `XOFLEN`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6a0ae393dd554eb718e5148696e8f437d4faae5b)
- Update copyright years. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2699ffe5fa686c35e1ea1c176817a6bbc2dbcd86)
- Update fuzz corpus to cover new parsing paths. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/caa16b949d6230acc4abde10f002db0b78e1d178)
- Make `evp_test` support the third parameter of digest tests. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f205958d9f9a2ead9edc088b0d3f060ee7c5b8c4)
- Ensure test-injected QUIC frames match packet types. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6aa921f27bba5c55acd4ea83d4f221b6c876c59d)
- Allow fault tests to output deleted TLS extensions to cover missing parameter handling. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0561c3cfe6c0623a9e7c8a0e062bdbb78b94ccf9)
- Add QUIC transport parameter tests covering missing, malformed, duplicate, and invalid values. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bcff823ca7a9a35772b2ac84f2ae291ca8b0ba27)
- Make the QUIC client fuzzer call `SSL_write()` to cover application data sending. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8dd7ee8665a2e9f5bab570998426d3eedbdc6128)
- Fix condition variable notification order in multi-stream tests to avoid lost wakeups. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b4cf49cb634d283abffaaec6db682af9eeae3261)
- Limit BLAKE2 variable output length to within algorithm-supported range. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/aa95fb14003121d0b6c86e564c31cb95424d4bed)
- Adjust CI job scheduling to improve build and test execution order. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/43919068a406de60b6c172eefc171a8ec965b902)
- Upgrade GitHub Script Action to 7. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9a7a076565f8feaae532d35646a0f8171c03c4a5)
- Update copyright years. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8bee92a80174b233a7f4071c57bf14c55c7983aa)

### Performance

- Add performance benchmarks for OSSL_PARAM_allocate_from_text() with different bit-width integer inputs, measuring overhead after text conversion extends to arbitrary-length integers. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b556713a6f2884eadc7f56428bc82a844e9a49e0)
- Add AES-GCM stitched implementation for PPC64LE POWER9 and newer architectures to improve segmented encryption throughput. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/44a563dde1584cd9284e80b6e45ee5019be8d36c)
- Add ChaCha20 assembly implementation using 8-way parallel processing for ppc64le to improve large block data throughput on that platform. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f596bbe4da779b56eea34d96168b557d78e1149a)
- Optimize Decoder's algorithm parsing and name mapping process, caching input type identifiers and reducing repeated string parsing overhead. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/247554458435eaab175cdc9d36878158b9eb6f6e)
- Optimize case-insensitive string hash calculation to reduce repeated character conversion overhead during hash table lookups. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a4e21d18d5b7cb4fef66c10f13b1b3b55945439f)
- Revert unproven performance optimization in PKCS7_verify, restoring original verification path to avoid behavior or compatibility issues. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/30adf6d209002fab688aa76e313ac077e4b2f88c)
- Add SVE2-based ChaCha20 optimization path for AArch64, leveraging SVE2 instructions to improve vector encryption throughput. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bcb52bcc9f9c36a85d037976676fd5ca52f307cd)
- Optimize s390x KMAC implementation using architecture instructions to improve Keccak-based MAC performance. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/affc070aabc930aeaba50f0dd6b3e0b7a2ddc399)
- Optimize SM4 round key generation to reduce computational overhead in key expansion steps. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/704e8090b4a789f52af07de9a3ebbe11db8e19f8)
- Optimize Encoder's process of collecting multiple encoder candidates, reducing per-layer lookup and object creation overhead. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c3b46409559c18f103ebb2221c6f8af3cd7db00d)
- Optimize FIPS RSA key generation performance, reducing repeated operations in candidate prime testing. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/dd1d7bcb69994d81662e709b0ad838880b943870)
- Further optimize FIPS RSA key generation to reduce overhead of generating primes that meet standards. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d2f6e66d2837bff1f5f7636bb2118e3a45c9df61)
- Optimize EVP_PKCS82PKEY_ex to reduce unnecessary allocations and attribute queries when converting PKCS#8 private keys to EVP_PKEY. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/52ce351a674bf459c836ffd01afb09917889f047)
- Simplify and speed up S/MIME test certificate generation script, reusing CA configuration's default SKID/AKID settings. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b7cc2d2f295efc342cd46e7e59f93e0ac0472dc0)
- Optimize functions such as d2i_AutoPrivateKey to reduce parsing overhead during repeated private key encoding formats. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/dba97d4c7142621fb279ef2074cd5c0a04eca7d3)
- Optimize the data processing path of `CMAC_Update()` to improve performance when updating CMAC tags in blocks. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/dc19f2f6223db0578be826d03ba8012cca076d28)
- Use `__builtin_expect` to optimize common branches of `EVP_EncryptUpdate()` under GCC and Clang, reducing branch overhead in the encryption update path. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ed6dfd1e3694b3438249f3d0117bc314afa6b240)
- Optimize the SM2 arithmetic implementation on AArch64 to improve the efficiency of SM2 key and signature operations on this architecture. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6399d7856c75abde9ed23782d10960013de03810)
- Optimize the cipher reference counting path by removing unnecessary EVP_CIPHER increment and decrement reference operations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8ed76c62b5d3214e807e684c06efd69c6471c800)
- Use `vpermxor` to optimize the six-way unrolled implementation, improving vectorized data processing performance. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3d3a7ecd1ae5ab08d22041f7b3b035c34f12fa02)

### Security

- Document the adjustment of the CCM8 suite security level and its reasons. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/56ffcce492ffc6f36b2f0d9431e23febe054dd04)
- Raise the OpenSSL default security level to 2, tightening default TLS and certificate algorithm policies. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b3a33dac8880b88038083b64d234506659921436)
- Update documentation to state that the default security level is now 2 and its impact on connections and algorithm selection. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a4c4090c21058a75e8bf1ffcc469b6d9755c55ce)
- Add a note that security level 1 also prohibits certificates signed with SHA-1 and MD5, clarifying certificate signature algorithm restrictions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e2e3f84fa5e96eb97b3cde46a213867fa79f235c)
- Restrict SSL 3, TLS 1.0/1.1, and DTLS 1.0 to be used only at security level 0; security level 1 and above reject these old protocols. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7bf2e4d7f0c7ae19b7a8c416910886a7171e9820)
- Return an internal error when certificate chain security checks encounter an empty chain head certificate, avoiding null pointer crashes. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/dc0ef292f7df4ce0c49c64b47726a6768f9ac044)
- Fix the output length upper bound check in the Punycode decoder to prevent writes beyond the caller's buffer, fixing CVE-2022-3602. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3b421ebc64c7b52f1b9feb3812bdc7781c784332)
- Fix the copy length calculation when inserting characters in the Punycode decoder to avoid out-of-bounds writes caused by malformed domain names, fixing CVE-2022-3786. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/680e65b94c916af259bfdc2e25f1ab6e0c7a97d6)
- Fix CVE-2022-4304 RSA decryption timing side channel to replace the previous implementation and avoid new timing differences. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f06ef1657a3d4322153b26231a7afa3d55724e52)
- Adjust FIPS DSA security strength checks so the legacy verification path accepts or rejects keys according to the correct strength. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/71cf587ea21c1422640847e358019a51806d2811)
- Reject inputs with a modulus of zero in `BN_RECP_CTX_set()` and return failure immediately, avoiding invalid states in subsequent modular reduction operations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/43596b306b1fe06da3b1a99e07c0cf235898010d)

### Documentation

- Update parameter names in the EVP_PKEY_gettable_params manual to align documentation with the interface definition. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6f2f59944826b5b7e033af438f5831493d0362c9)
- Improve OpenSSL documentation to clarify usage instructions for related commands and interfaces. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b9f96f30eea550650a8d9f8000cea940c6ee8150)
- Fix errors introduced by commit 9067cf6 in the documentation, restoring consistency between descriptions and implementation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/48b3ad05680ba3e3668bcb6491bf4447033464ed)
- Fix errors introduced by commit 6882652 in the documentation, updating inaccurate interface descriptions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6d55d27b2da7a84c9f4b872060be979b5f64af2c)
- Update the openssl passwd manual page, removing duplicate descriptions and retaining the unique parameter introduction. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/116799ff6a8fc803ec4685fc432c7329d0511e23)
- Fix the SSH KDF example code so the example constructs and calls the key derivation function according to the interface requirements. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/81280137a1f33685d7d7fc531ea8fbac38e9a4b7)
- Fix several errors in OpenSSL documentation to prevent manual content from conflicting with interface behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5ecf10a0d2fb1c858b25afd5e48eafe6ef76edd4)
- Fix the HTML documentation state transition diagram in life_cycle-kdf.pod; the synchronized manual page, kdf.dot, and lifecycle table itself do not need modification. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e82fc27bcd34f246e1acd42a61e8ba62907e1d19)
- Update the openssl-x509 manual to state that -signkey is an alias for the -key option. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cc0d1b03a94b71dd9d8ee9aa11ee22fdc3659821)
- Fix the documentation for SSL_set_num_tickets() to accurately describe the ticket number setting behavior of this interface. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4603b782e6dbed493d2f38db111abc05df66fb99)
- Add a note to SSL_get_session() for server-side TLS 1.3 scenarios, clarifying the behavior of this interface when returning sessions on the server side. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9e51f877930dbd4216438a5da3c9612bf4d0a918)
- Note in the fipsinstall documentation that FIPS self-test callbacks must not be used, avoiding callers configuring unsupported callbacks. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8d257d0dc6ed9d5aeb8366de6be0af01538557ea)
- Update the -nameopt manual to state that its default value has been adjusted to UTF-8 name output. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6923d261b819cdd5d9e0a72337da6d6a92cef2a2)
- Clean up trailing whitespace in documentation lines to unify the text format of manual files. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/57cd10dd1ee9659b94cfa8a8e74c5a151632975e)
- Remove redundant commas in manual example code to fix example syntax. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c249f48778473ebca46a44282b0b0ff40b8665b8)
- Fix the include directive syntax in documentation so included files can be parsed correctly. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/50aba7ffde860dbc5a8d1eb3f5d9f49c58ebc2ce)
- Update documentation to state that OBJ object creation functions are now thread-safe. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/06394a6cc7c784b46bc6d1e65a1ff39637093934)
- Fix the EVP_PKEY_keygen_init() manual: this function does not have a parameter named pkey. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ce0502ce1246046f78dc1e2b26a2790eceedd8b1)
- Fix variable name mismatches in example code to ensure examples reference declared variables. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/29a84567fb859ee6ae7245115b0c347994b03012)
- Add the PVK KDF to the algorithm list of the legacy Provider so the legacy provider can provide this algorithm. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4667b0f0731a8bb8d6236ea18b8c0591016f53d7)
- Add the PVK KDF documentation to the build manifest to ensure the corresponding manual is generated and installed during the build. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4eb27149f066d567393771986f01267707982a7e)
- Document that these KDFs require the legacy Provider to be available, clarifying the prerequisite for algorithm loading. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5fae7b432e3e2e14916bdcd1ad0fe594a27628c6)
- Fix several errors in OpenSSL documentation to ensure accurate textual descriptions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9efdf4ad6b130aa4e206a8fd612539925c0b1e45)
- Update the manual to reference the IANA TLS Cipher Suites Registry as the authoritative list of TLS suite numbers and names. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0865200fe59e7b18fbef07077897e09ab39741dc)
- Fix spelling errors in the crypto(7) manual. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ce2892940902124b4a807c27a7df458f5049189f)
- Update the BIO_f_ssl manual to clarify the object type that should be passed for SSL BIO parameters. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/34901b0c39ed8fe7ddb81de4ad9fc0a7b2c45a0d)
- Fix the section title in the random number generator man7 manual. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0c75a7387d940468a530ee7470e0afce30f5a227)
- Document that Provider attribute names must be unique. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/78de5a94d8e2b0a27ae026de29c195e944a49c6d)
- Document that OPENSSL_CORE_CTX should not be cast to OSSL_LIB_CTX to avoid confusion between different context types. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2b80a7490d5008fa40417b804ea16e8fee13d93d)
- Fix the documentation for the signature newctx callback so the function prototype matches the actual interface. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5fdc95e443b4d62a3d1f7094ae6d6ae4682b77e0)
- Fix function signature errors in documentation so published interface prototypes match actual declarations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/10343fa52731c6a66a761b578d2aa37a364083c8)
- Make the -reqexts option of the req command an alias for -extensions, unifying certificate request extension options. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/251e941283f554f0dc4b315e3a8fb82ef5b71982)
- Clarify the label parameter type of EVP_PKEY_CTX_set0_rsa_oaep_label() in the RSA OAEP label setting documentation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3d63315366f673328b41750a6e1708d3d6cf11a0)
- Clarify the behavior of EVP_PKEY_get_utf8_string_param() and EVP_PKEY_get_octet_string_param() when the output buffer is NULL. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cde5a12d5e83ba0d7c5b2dd7bafa4d2abe2d45bf)
- Fix several incorrect descriptions of function return values in documentation to ensure accurate success and failure semantics. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f1d077f1108b1bc2334350a4d53a46e29e082910)
- Fix documentation errors mainly caused by BIO_ctrl() return values, unifying the description of control interface return value meanings. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5001287c0dcd8ca4ffc564b360f86df79bba40c1)
- Clarify in the max_identity_len documentation whether the length includes the trailing NUL byte, avoiding callers miscalculating PSK identity buffer capacity. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/25ead551aa31feae91cab91b648a2ca16bc7a8dc)
- State in the OSSL_HTTP_REQ_CTX documentation that the returned BIO must not be freed by the caller, clarifying its ownership. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4258845e4eb86854f723c0160c051a727ec08a56)
- State in the OSSL_HTTP_transfer documentation that the returned BIO must be freed by the caller, clarifying the resource ownership responsibility of this interface. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a26c089ba3637a3283cc0e76ea9529e90d18ba0d)
- Fix several function signature documentation errors so parameter and return types in the manual match actual declarations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7b2bde500dfdad6628b15c5faa641d0f6602110e)
- Note in the migration guide that ERR_GET_FUNC() and function error codes have been removed, helping upgraded applications adjust error handling code. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b387274d0fb3097d3a252d397226b86b8f98f30d)
- Fix several function signature documentation errors to ensure manual prototypes match code declarations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1e4cef5fde59e28f10ec555801349d06471d1da0)
- Change "NULL terminated" to "NUL terminated" in documentation to accurately describe the string termination byte. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/089df6f135b7cef4e7d0e7b7acecb1d90f5ef3ed)
- Allow EVP_PKEY operations to use a compatible fallback path when the current Provider does not support them, maintaining the usability of existing key operations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/839ffdd11cd48d329a1d89565d62e0be082f9d08)
- Clarify the documentation for the byname family of functions, explaining the names used when looking up algorithms by name and the lookup behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/971dbab4ad20193c27e8c3865e92e8f487b89334)
- Update the documentation for BN_bn2lebinpad() and BN_bn2nativepad() to explain the byte order and behavior of these two padding conversion functions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/aedc5a819ee3f5267a7ec5c795b97481a1c63dc6)
- Fix the EVP_PKEY_fromdata example code to ensure example parameters match the actual key import interface. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4ce64ed79d301939c7f2844a9e5e5fdd2033605f)
- Add documentation for internal integer overflow helper functions, explaining their interfaces and usage conditions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cccfc668286cc1010911c28ea1e76be7e1eac3e7)
- Document the behavior of repeated DigestSign/DigestVerify calls, explaining the results of repeated invocations of the same digest operation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3641f04fb06e9679a67da113bab65e5f1bb5e9ba)
- Stop registering callbacks in the child libctx when it no longer needs to receive child Provider callbacks. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cad22202a32a94059e351d9819e6c9ed5c66605a)
- Use a write lock in ossl_provider_find() to protect lookup-related state, avoiding data races between concurrent lookups and updates. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4aced11785f2e54875ad56f30c05bdee02b6e4e2)
- Clarify PEM documentation, correcting the description of PEM format and related read/write interfaces. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e48fe798409eb22f067f635c62b9a72e18cd4347)
- Document that OSSL_PARAM_set/get/construct_BN() currently only support non-negative numbers, clarifying the range limits of the big number parameter interface. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b33fb68a3230b8fc87f6663212ac3ffae0b361c5)
- Clarify and correct the EVP_CTRL_AEAD_SET_TAG documentation, explaining the timing and behavior of setting AEAD tag parameters. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3607b8ad8ee1980a079e985333a196e0c79f8f00)
- Correct erroneous macro names in the documentation to align descriptions with actual definitions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/01fde90eec721b64bc0e1c01cd94a9fd431adcc6)
- Remove non-existent callback items from the documentation to avoid describing unimplemented interfaces in the manual. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6d770c5ba36d43f495b232392cfaa8fa460f17af)
- Add explanation of the OpenSSL 3.0 engine working method, detailing the relationship between Engine and Provider architectures. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/29a27cb2c5c1757831f42117871f8c59058343a9)
- Add the previously omitted ok parameter description in the OSSL_HTTP_close() documentation, clarifying its impact on the close state. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4ee464cf8e0b8dc39970306bfbb49a6e06863e1c)
- Clarify the BIO connection and disconnection callback behavior in the OSSL_HTTP_transfer documentation, explaining the resource operations handled by the callbacks. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2080134ee98a6b23f7456c17901e7b06e4a42ed5)
- Clarify deprecation warnings in the documentation, explaining deprecated interfaces and alternatives. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3dbf82438004b31258627f324841476c4f586c19)
- Complete the documentation for OSSL_HTTP_open() regarding server and proxy parameter check conditions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/59b6b5a94f5a5f756aa323d1fb061697ca9eadf8)
- Clarify the server parameter of OSSL_HTTP_open() and how it is passed to BIO_new_connect() to establish a connection. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/119f8145c3bde29aae5d5b18c44d1663df975ef5)
- Clarify the purpose of the subject/req parameters in X509V3_set_ctx(), explaining how to construct SKID based on the public key hash. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/15ac84e603678140ba32832c288e5f1745a258f8)
- Clarify the purpose of the subject/req parameters in X509V3_set_ctx(), explaining how to construct SAN email addresses from the subject DN. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/317acac5cc0a2cb31bc4b91353c2b752a3989d8a)
- Correct the documentation for tlsext_ticket_key, making the description of the ticket key callback parameters accurate. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b0be101326f369f0dd547556d2f3eb3ef5ed0e33)
- Correct erroneous details about the random number output range in the BN_rand() manual. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/391ce6d980c4f8f474ed28d1ea83849cc9a00100)
- Add explanation of selector bits in provider-keymgmt(7), explaining the meaning of different key part selection flags. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e67254e4c3d82b1b8f5102bc4a0e7914f0b87ef0)
- Add documentation for RNDR and RNDRRS, explaining the ARM hardware random number algorithm and its Provider usage. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e8b597f33143410fb50bdeba8722c249524bc0b9)
- Add possible error codes returned by stack push operations in the documentation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/40586e462df76adf163e0d31879dc2d5fa90e109)
- Improve diagnostics when detecting TLS option combinations in the CMP command, explaining which options should not be used together. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ad1a1d715dcab875dafd6e792b8eb65eb84d6b9f)
- Clarify the password semantics in the pem_password_cb() documentation, explaining callback buffer and terminator requirements. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5b5342e04ff24d5138c054c1677c32729d47e938)
- Improve X509V3_set_ctx() documentation, clarifying the purpose of context parameters during extension generation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b6144bb8c1be63935ae09e1992c04fbe6e0f88a8)
- Note in the X509_cmp manual that X509_NAME_cmp() parameters can be NULL. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/acef3b2f84b22c7cdb3cbc02fc8fc7b76cbb6ea7)
- Clarify the documentation for integer parameter getters, explaining output parameter types and value ranges. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/254217a4a0c9e64869495447a0e6bdc2323d4cd1)

- Add the SSL_CTX_get_ssl_method manual page, explaining the interface to obtain the current protocol method of SSL_CTX. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e12bee78d4e64da2176dd9a7ec19ec680dd3bebf)

- Fix a spelling error in the X509v3_get_ext_by_NID manual page. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f5f288bdba3d2ca6f2ad4b39225b42f06ef28638)
- Add public APIs OPENSSL_strcasecmp and OPENSSL_strncasecmp, providing case-insensitive string comparison unaffected by platform differences. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4b2bd2722b8294a6b27c9e1fcf7d76f7d9de9b44)
- Add cross-references to related manuals in the pem_password_cb manual page for easy lookup of password callback companion interfaces. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7510aee28a3262cde442230c06daffa1e7609fd6)

- When generating CRMF proof-of-possession EdDSA signatures, do not pass a digest algorithm, conforming to the direct signing interface requirements of Ed25519/Ed448. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/de56f726e163e99128ff93a04d74a8461f5a724b)
- Remove the Provider operation bit cleanup helper function and its calls, simplifying Provider operation registration and query state management. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/20b6d85ab2b9cfa4cd29d2422d69c3e3f4db0a41)
- Split the method store refresh into an operation that only clears the query cache, avoiding accidental deletion of registered algorithm methods when clearing query results. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/60640d79ca7ea0980dc09c71fe6a297b5f8588a2)
