- Improve the deprecation documentation for DH_new and other interfaces, clarifying the reasons for deprecation and the EVP/Provider APIs to migrate to. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/65b41ab31917e3bb25920c4b27017786db668850)
- Change the parameter name in the signature API documentation from sigret to sig, consistent with the function declaration and output buffer usage. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2bd8190aace8109a06aff495a3e20c863ef48653)
- Fix the OPENSSL_MALLOC_FD documentation to explain the correct way to set the memory debugging output file descriptor. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2c35d61790603530d11f52eda9132ff8640f9852)
- Add a QUIC receive-side depacketizer that parses received packets and dispatches frames to corresponding processing paths. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d7fed97e37a4a66e8c7d53015684790951f6e943)
- In the QUIC architecture diagram, uniformly rename "RX Frame Handler" to "RX Depacketizer", corresponding to the receive-side depacketization module's responsibility. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/42a0817d5ff7771ff1d9c8cc9b89553b6ddf1126)
- Supplement EC key parameter documentation, explaining the relationships among encoded public keys, curve names, and other parameters. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/58135cb3c020805354ecc869aca040934d1299c8)
- Fix AddressSanitizer issues in the TCP Fast Open test by cleaning up test sockets and input data. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e0c4e43e40390e44614d14817e34b47e1c17d630)
- Add a standalone timing tool to measure elapsed time during program execution. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/63b94b3fc7feb12713fa46d386e10d109f074d28)
- ecparam now supports Provider property query parameters to specify attributes for selecting EC parameter algorithm implementations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a148f864a86ba5f6999825dad9a98280bd054cbc)
- Add a property query test for the pkey application, verifying that command-line parameters can select Provider algorithms matching the properties. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4c10099086dd75d583317056fb5ce0f88825bf0c)
- Add a code signing purpose test for the CMS application, ensuring that CMS signer certificates can be validated according to code signing rules. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7af110f9f5fb9b039cc09b63768a0b989a7bf5ad)
- Migrate the ssl3_get_record reading logic to tls_read_record, making the protocol state machine use the new record reading entry point. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d9aca2dd9b56dbfa6a0566cc3ad8b7c713ac61b2)
- Implement KTLS receive support in the new record reading layer, allowing kernel offload and OpenSSL record reading to share a unified interface. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d4ffdc42cb579a1ea6e3c95601c5845866028ebb)
- Distinguish between fatal and recoverable errors when creating the record layer, allowing callers to handle the two types of initialization failures differently. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fc2be2d07acc0cfe954320c2491b8c5461cbef09)
- Support transferring unprocessed record data from one epoch to the next, for use in DTLS/QUIC phase transitions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cae1d08f2c967cba960163075bda39f33d41c156)
- Add a preliminary design for the QUIC congestion control API, defining the interface boundaries for congestion feedback and control algorithms. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/891b639377af8be10f5e41616db6afabceae7cef)
- The HTTP application server now logs requests and responses when trace is enabled, facilitating troubleshooting of server interactions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8aff29f020752c96cc2ab7d111d9c33aaf55c671)
- Add a design document for the QUIC TX Packetiser, describing how pending frames are assembled into packets. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fabce8090c3ba49527d434a4621c660eedad2aaa)
- Fix spelling errors in function names to align implementation symbols with public interface naming. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/edb271ee5d68f31260da37e9ad5ed2f9f4dd7274)
- Update the openssl command documentation to state that standard input content should also be read in binary format. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/054189bf7a9e68a7374744e7eaea344ba1784e68)
- Clarify the return value semantics of OSSL_PROVIDER_set_default_search_path and fix caller checks. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/257cade411ef9217305c5db47f40e5dacdb99c71)
- In the QUIC overview, add a description of the mapping cache from multiple connection IDs to connections. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4efc969852cdb7883d240e423e887a57504dcd36)
- Add an EC key encoding example demonstrating how to encode and output an EC private key using the EVP Encoder. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9be2693438756b5f1b789f1b8db76c3b987063dc)
- Use __STDC_VERSION__ to determine the C standard version, avoiding reliance on the non-standard __STRICT_ANSI__ compiler macro. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9710d72b95f4fc218ed613f42dc90ad0d263b14f)
- Add a CI test matrix for older versions of the FIPS Provider to check that compatibility code remains effective in historical versions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/47cd0e5b1f98fb88d6d8337f7ec0e16bb83cea32)
- Correctly identify retransmitted ClientHello messages to avoid duplicate handshake messages causing connection state reset or misjudgment. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8377f26c2e6dadd9a1728805cd895fa94669f2d0)
- Clarify that the maximum valid return value of BIO_ctrl_pending/wpending is SIZE_MAX, preventing callers from truncating lengths with narrow types. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d7f3a2cc8691c062ef5bdeef28b66f80c8f7d5c3)
- Fix spelling errors in the design directory README. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5d32acf0f53c85e5da787035e47f775221486c64)
- Fix the OFB and OCB documentation for the FIPS Provider, describing the actually supported parameters and mode behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7d3600a6c906c5f4cf81db95aec7b58b9d018c49)
- Update RIPEMD-160 documentation to state that this algorithm is provided by the default Provider. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fdc5043d58900663b493147298e64f11353b35fe)
- Document in the manual which OPENSSL_sk functions tolerate out-of-bounds pop, shift, or value access by returning a benign result. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0778364f8ec1f9702e62e6298aaa212ec40b6111)
- Fix copy-paste errors in the documentation to ensure that the described functions and parameters correspond to the correct interfaces. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5b9480fc1e814bf8fa2dce0dbbede147f04d477c)
- Fix parameter names in the RSA private key example so that the example conforms to the RSA private key encoding API. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c8c678e7d91ca2cea41c6c574cf7656a9404646f)
- Supplement TLS pipelining documentation, describing support conditions, buffer management, and application invocation methods. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3929345ee49f6b3a04f446c9589bb338f12e71ac)
- Fix documentation for several i2d encoding APIs, clarifying that their return value is the encoded length or a failure flag. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/943051d0f9ce8dcb38707774a5757a5dc436704f)
- Add QUIC Flight-in-Flight Manager design document, describing the state of in-flight packets, acknowledgments, and retransmission associations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/66a6659a244f9c1da301a675c6013db4db71d39e)
- Fix spelling errors in the COMP_expand_block algorithm documentation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/aa197f36a8868654418cb9b3a85ae56b8ecf7da5)
- Update QUIC Stream Receive Buffers design document, recording implemented buffering, reassembly, and read behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6f3072212c2d56cae598bc1d180b2673b3df9be0)
- Update keymgmt export helper function documentation, explaining how the selection bits choose public key, private key, and other parameters. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/504427eb5f32108dd64ff7858012863fe47b369b)
- Fix explanatory errors in the documentation to align the relevant interface descriptions with actual behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/96796ab9baedee8b7de8d9ffc9e19dc0d8f8327a)
- Update the record layer design based on implementation experience, correcting models and responsibilities that are inconsistent with the final interface. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/48cc4e0c2046624c28d431ac51cdfce1a6e6a597)
- Improve comments on code signing and timestamp certificate verification purposes, explaining the differences in checks on leaf certificates and chains for the two purposes. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3fa6dbd1be0791210853b0367b8483d4e6291e4f)
- Add QUIC and TLS integration design document, explaining how TLS handshake state serves QUIC key derivation and encryption. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/88113f5dc6828694820d39612c3a760e386a0aa5)
- Fix version numbers in multiple HISTORY sections, avoiding incorrectly labeling OpenSSL 3.0 interfaces as other versions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f5e602b5500cc736fe774b114dc66180341a5ce7)
- Fix OSSL_CMP_ITAV_set0 manual formatting and update examples, clarifying ITAV object ownership transfer rules. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/33a73e33dce1e62613d67471ba8b68afe01166c0)
- Fix spelling errors in the openssl x509 manual. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0b7ad5d928f9ee749cfc670ad08067a961217fea)
- Fix spelling errors in the EVP_EncryptInit manual page. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0dbd3a81e46dd7ea9f7832307fdd0b2ac207a5bf)
- Remove 3DES algorithm registration from the FIPS Provider, so it is no longer listed as an included algorithm. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4a7231df5ca9f3d8759dc8c22cb8e9f27b312024)
- Fix the parent Provider parameter passing for EVP_RAND_CTX_new in documentation and examples, matching the actual constructor signature. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0a7528cc7c3b80e00e3093f5d32525574138a33e)
- Clarify the EVP_PKEY_decrypt manual page regarding buffer length queries and actual decryption calls. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8f48a978e5cce483eadfd0df20e34480f205511c)
- Fix function names, parameters, or return value descriptions in the EVP_SignInit manual. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/328dc33365f8bd9c097bc95c3632467f690502a4)
- Add QUIC connection state machine design document, defining connection establishment, handshake, closure, and error state transitions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/269ad8d571e68513175fdc66227943786353cfa8)
- Move OpenSSL core type descriptions from the general manual to a separate manual page for easy lookup by type. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/801e54d65ca5d87f3b003477f26597541b95b55b)
- Change some type names displayed in bold to cross-references to the corresponding type manual pages for easy navigation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/318a9dfa5f7bd1237d2697ad950697d241b8b49f)
- Fix the introduction version record for the RSA implicit rejection feature, ensuring the version noted in CHANGES/HISTORY is accurate. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8548d3bfc9fbbbe86027c685c24df393daa88674)
- Update HMAC() manual to describe parameter requirements for key, digest algorithm, and output label. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d1ebd99397e18de367918c201c25c389f1f07005)
- Refine descriptions of parameters, return values, and object ownership in multiple API manuals to avoid misuse by readers. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/dd1f28427b375931fda45180619c8f5971cd6bca)
- Move deprecated ECDSA_* function descriptions to a separate manual page, organizing documentation for deprecated interfaces separately. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c99209264de98da94937b073a42219bada9ff7f5)
- Add manuals for EVP_PKEY_CTX_get0_pkey and get0_peerkey, describing interfaces for reading operation keys and peer keys. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3be76745e55eab9ea976f7a23e6c8ecd3bb8136c)
- Update HISTORY records for EC_GROUP APIs, indicating the introduction versions of each curve group function. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8d927e55b751ba1af6c08cd4e37d565a43c56157)
- Fix function behavior or return value descriptions in the d2i_X509 manual. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5adca946c3b6f779eb593bff6dbefe0a85238d84)
- Add an API for QUIC congestion control to query the time until the next available send quota, allowing the sender to wait until budget recovery before scheduling. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/97c5c52d6c2c5d13db0cc59b3dbf4d75c40ec3ba)
- Update QUIC API documentation, supplementing current implementation capabilities, call sequences, and limitations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2fff884c86a7a0497c10a73da74b006d5e6e9039)
- The QUIC front-end API prohibits enabling EPW during AON (application-only network) mode, avoiding switching to an unsupported write state. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/dfc227bd245c356aea11dfdec9fe0f3d66bca16e)
- Fix QUIC API parameter and function descriptions in the documentation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b8a132d68f665b4c3fb6dcb8f3e8c625bbfb57c4)
- Remove inappropriate __owur markers from the QUIC front-end API manual, avoiding misinterpretation of non-boolean interfaces as requiring return value checks. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/19142ef1242939f9bacbf0255582a9c4a5f16c9c)
- Explain that when QUIC blocking mode is enabled, the underlying BIO blocking is automatically disabled, clarifying that read/write calls may return retry status. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e6b653535f4a0513a1a3710e095411cd45723080)
- Explain that OpenSSL error codes are not guaranteed to be stable across versions, reminding applications not to rely on error code values as a long-term interface. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ae61bd34e676036f22e44e8184d1ba649a990619)
- Supplement X509_STORE_CTX_get_current_cert documentation, explaining that this interface returns the certificate currently being verified. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ed77c14d91ec008d4d78d7293f37bd4aa60071f0)
- Fix the EBNF rule for unquoted values in attribute string syntax, accurately restricting acceptable characters. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9866200bbe8eb65628f3f3e6eb290c48efb5a8ac)
- Fix the return type and success/failure meaning in the BIO_do_connect manual page. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2bde260ac0e30da39f4453e972c461cd9d1342e0)
- CMP documentation clarifies that message timeout and total timeout limit the time for a single interaction and the entire transaction, respectively. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5acd4007a0646ef1f9d0015ce438b891d1b24a62)
- MD5 manual recommends using SHA-2 or SHA-3 digest families instead of MD5, advising readers to avoid using outdated algorithms in new applications. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/61222b95ff20f6a7bb20668e43b657561efdb922)
- FIPS Provider documentation states that the EdDSA algorithm has not yet been validated and should not be considered a FIPS-validated algorithm. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8353b2dfacd723db5ba8b833b95e68e9600d1cf5)
- The HTTP client outputs the response body via OSSL_TRACE_STRING in traces, limiting and safely presenting the message body. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/35b76bc818ebeb9d36bed22ea0a7b4f03204619a)
- Clarify the behavior changes of enc -S for salt input and output in OpenSSL 3.0, updating compatibility notes. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a4aa977d3a8049d5386dc583e16c17727c712eaa)
- RSA public key import documentation points out that e (public exponent) is a required parameter; without it, a valid RSA public key cannot be constructed. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6e3b1c81736b1829584e3f40c2d00040fe1aa881)
- Update QUIC FIN documentation, fixing explanations of EOS, FIN, and stream end conditions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ff6e3a26f92208df8b4257760c72cae5c0e0269c)
- Synchronize EC_POINT export manual, stating that OpenSSL 3.0 will follow the OSSL_PKEY_PARAM_EC_POINT_CONVERSION_FORMAT setting. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f66c1272f92bed6bc8aa17f6a8956d9e2e5b7798)
- Add fault injector design document, defining mechanisms to trigger allocation, I/O, and algorithm errors in tests. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/55ff8fb4ed4d48cb819ff5ae5d74cc08256e7ed1)
- Fix a spelling error in the BIO_read manual. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0414899887b98f973067f286ac126d8b529873e3)
- Remove EdDSA from the list of non-FIPS algorithm documentation, reflecting its approval status in the current FIPS Provider. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/92c0e33e375b9f3ba191484fff84a5aba41f1f6e)
- Improve CMP applications and manuals regarding trusted/untrusted certificates and server certificate srvCert. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/260878f7aab7b077f4ef9496e3541ec8c19c9d1c)
- Fix spelling errors in the config manual. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d54e91d3944304bca2bd4c74af52ccffc49c6126)
- Supplement OSSL_DECODER_from_data manual, explaining its input pointer advancement and return value semantics. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7e5505107aacdf58a4d0c00da90af4b7407c8d65)
- Update documentation to clarify that EdDSA is currently not a FIPS-approved algorithm. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fdd4716dd61e3e8fce77c04987e9dc5df7be7d9d)
- List the RAND algorithm names and purposes provided by the default and FIPS Providers. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7066c57dce88994778daa10ba2c81490fc5cf0c7)
- FIPS Provider documentation clarifies that attribute queries must include fips=yes to ensure the use of validated algorithm implementations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0a81220a01e888c3ee4ab18dfdcab6472d9e214c)
- Update AES manual to state that the FIPS Provider does not provide AES-SIV, avoiding documentation implying that this Provider supports this mode. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/977950bf385a3b12a09f03444bf7a213eacbba14)
- Fix spelling errors in the default Provider example code. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/08a11ba20461ce14b0a6b9c9e374fbea91fbd8cf)
- Supplement provider-keymgmt manual, explaining how to query parameters for EVP_PKEY_get_default_digest_name and get_default_digest_nid. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ac57336cd258e0432ffa485615d11c7c7ecfe81a)
- Explain that prediction resistance increases the performance overhead of random number generation, helping callers weigh security attributes against speed. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d90bd3468a9a8d2af6b821be50c1034e21d782ca)
- Supplement documentation for obsolete SSL_OP_NETSCAPE_*_BUG options, explaining compatibility behavior and their legacy status. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fe52208c560fb4d16fc40cfe395032544b82271e)
- When context copying fails, signature or digest operations no longer fail immediately; instead, the current context is used to continue the call. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0fc00fc0e3867fc5f95fab1046ad7d2a85db06f8)
- Fixed the function return type in the documentation to align the manual with the public header declarations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/04af51c276e7d785a194eb9ed199abf250c5b3b6)
- Limited the single sleep duration of OSSL_sleep to avoid excessively long millisecond parameters causing threads to sleep for days or years after conversion. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bea92b8c3d61960a2d06f8d342ef01d30a2fa195)
- Documented the default iteration count for PBKDF2 in the enc manual, noting that it can be adjusted via parameters. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6678b0868b7660177f8b5af299894e2e99330a21)
- Updated the EVP_PKEY_get_id documentation to clarify the return value of algorithm type identifiers and the behavior for provider keys. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a2a543e0e3ec277d136772b4b0e0bb3d1181d337)
- Improved the help text for the -reqin/-reqout/-rspin/-rspout options in the CMP application, distinguishing between request and response file formats. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/77aa00697623bab31b312451855c36789204ed60)
- Fixed the X509_VERIFY_PARAM_add0_policy manual to state that this API transfers ownership of the policy object. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5ab3f71a33cb0140fc29ae9244cd4f8331c2f3a5)
- Documented the restrictions on digest algorithms used by DRBG in the FIPS documentation, listing the allowed HMAC/Hash DRBG digests. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e14fc22c90ce5a9e6d66d8658fc6bb37f95019da)
- Fixed a typo in the EVP_EncryptInit manual. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/027226eb229c41d7066366a8b9ef8241da7500bd)
- Updated the SSL version query API documentation to describe the protocol version behavior returned by QUIC SSL objects. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/418c6c520764491262018c45481a20ef10cd3bca)
- Updated the return value types and success/failure semantics in the PEM_write_TYPE and PEM_write_bio_TYPE manuals. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/dfb8e185134df90fd3f21fb6ec625e7c295fdcea)
- Fixed the description of structure fields in the manual to avoid misleading documentation about the meaning of data members. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b2023d5dfc957cf5a3cfca16961f97e79842b941)
- Corrected the relationship between paired API functions to ensure that documentation names and function prototypes correspond one-to-one. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/51b941ac290864103d00a3d6a3018372b58b01f4)
- Fixed broken links in the crypto manual pages so that algorithm and provider documentation references navigate correctly. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d79b6104ae947b8749623d3152c309f398387a54)
- Added command-line documentation for `fipsinstall -pedantic`, explaining how this option affects FIPS configuration validation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d30fec6ff438f73f4e255b0b9c6af3ea57ec122a)
- Clarified in the EVP_KDF documentation that `info` parameters are concatenated, preventing callers from assuming repeated parameters overwrite each other. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fc374a087e7fc5a5bd243ea42ce9879e8432b20e)
- Added a QUIC I/O architecture design document, explaining the responsibilities and data flow among connections, channels, and underlying reads/writes. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/aed7082419465d1c74a0b96cbdc9ae938deaff06)
- Further revised the QUIC I/O design document to clarify the interactions between I/O layers. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c48cc764ed57e49456d5b90a7d885e8af196df78)
- Added a QUIC glossary to unify the meanings of terms related to connections, channels, and transport in design documents. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0af6523ead9764d7899067b87b9525cc9fc5e19f)
- Clarified in the OCSP command documentation that certificate input format options can be flexibly combined with different input formats, avoiding misinterpretation as fixed format restrictions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9748e6127634e26483ff796d6572a303b1d514b7)
- Made the `-CAfile` parameter of the application accept DER-encoded certificate files as well, and added documentation for the verification option. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/57c0205b4df7d612a0333415dfc0a845c22e7458)
- Updated the `SSL_rstate_string*()` documentation so that the listed strings match the record layer states actually returned by the implementation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/476e06eb1635a4cc9419da090d23fde9fed5a693)
- Clarified the relationship between `ASN1_OCTET_STRING_new()` and the generic ASN.1 string constructor, noting that it creates an OCTET STRING type object. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/26f0150fce64dd878b77eddc4504fd441cbdef87)
- Added documentation for the `-no_*` protocol options of the `openssl` command, explaining which SSL/TLS versions each option disables. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/06565f36e78e6155834875ad544bb48838a812e4)
- Updated the generic stack lookup API documentation to state that the lookup function no longer reorders or modifies the passed stack. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1c35e39ac0e5845c5bc7ca7f802ee832ca95388d)
- Updated the QUIC congestion control design document to reflect the new controller abstraction, parameter passing, and NewReno integration. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1c44ed7bd3546d12fc95d8624e292412e357f789)
- Fixed broken links in the provider asymmetric encryption manual pages so that parameter and algorithm references are accessible. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/010333be5362a07508888124c83efac35b28760f)
- Updated the QUIC minimum/maximum protocol version API documentation to describe the applicable behavior of these limits on QUIC methods. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e89f9ef424548f0c9153f8bbb4a0beb1208e357e)
- Added documentation for `SSL_SESSION_dup()`, explaining the relationship between the copied object and the original session in terms of resource ownership and lifecycle. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/25bab273ccc9b517cc4c1783950e3f95421cb570)
- Fix spelling errors in the code found by codespell. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9a271795f84eb5402ce1ecfbcfd21392ad1560d0)
- Updated the QUIC multi-stream implementation so that connections can manage and schedule multiple streams according to the new stream object model. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/71e55512631332085f10fd3b02eb351383b230da)
- Updated the QUIC multi-stream feature documentation to reflect the current stream API and multi-stream connection behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/496b8162b6b7048a05809f5e85825ed02dff8a7c)
- Fixed the description in the QUIC multi-stream API documentation to align interface behavior with the current implementation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/47238a3d3f87c8381f5c9e013e6fc7c4e0d34557)
- Further corrected the documentation of the QUIC multi-stream interface to ensure accurate descriptions of default stream conversion and acceptance policies. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5e125829c01afcc21daad07ac19cf57d1a5ca579)
- Adjusted the interface design of the QUIC connection close information API to allow callers to query the connection close reason text. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bb9b8a333ffaf998e60016819ee1e8c2da58f0fd)
- Fixed erroneous descriptions in the QUIC documentation to align with current multi-stream interface behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2b5a546ce1960883febc51f5d2a71a8b7c1b3ab9)
- Added a QUIC manual page introducing QUIC protocol support and its interface in OpenSSL. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f89c2a992690dcc6f3f27706a8a97cb2b7d670ee)
- Updated the QUIC manual page to describe the usage of multi-stream connections and related stream APIs. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4b4e246ccb6e32de9aaf228c8f9ee415ede4b612)
- Added a preliminary design for QUIC error handling, describing protocol errors, transport errors, and their reporting paths to API callers. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/95d3c148ca3818a8773f293e9a886a3ec4185353)
- Updated the message callback documentation to describe callback parameters and the supported range of protocol messages. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bfcf1356f9fdc6ad939f73f2d4e505bd519c33d2)
- Clarified in the provider parameter documentation how string data is returned, explaining how buffers and lengths should be provided and read. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/eab96453bb145cde8e447f420a4ba099a5fa7004)
- Renamed the QUIC event-driven APIs `SSL_tick` and `SSL_get_tick_timeout` to more accurately express their purpose of handling events and querying timeouts. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f8503ede86115b67e1552cf332d53fe43f0cb5bd)
- Updated the `SSL_get_event_timeout()` documentation to explain the meaning of the returned timeout and when callers should drive event processing again. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a28ac613dc35465096026f349370cc2ec973835f)
- Updated the QUIC documentation to state that the DTLSv1 event API can be used as a compatibility call in QUIC. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/06fb6b97174ec8031de9b35203e35dee8f716365)
- No longer acquire a write lock when releasing EVP_PKEY, as the cleanup path does not need to modify shared key state, reducing unnecessary lock contention. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/36424806d699233b9a90a3a97fff3011828e2548)
- Updated the version information in the FIPS provider documentation to match the current provider version status. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/73f59aa8ebee4231ef8d4072b474974c571efb96)
- Revised the descriptions of `-subject`, `-issuer`, `-keep_alive`, and `-untrusted` in the CMP documentation, clarifying the impact of each option on requests and connections. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/168d93a21d512028572777ea5bc96994f2df6c36)
- Fixed the erroneous documentation that marked `openssl-genrsa` as deprecated since OpenSSL 3.0. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4ad2dd43d0959b850c06c5a681d34aeb78d7c4b9)
- Documented that OpenSSL's ChaCha20 output implementation does not conform to the standard definition, preventing users from misusing it in standard-compliant scenarios. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c69756e7a0133b67df50525e89206c9cc4a7d2b8)
- Improved `asn1parse`'s adherence to RFC 7462 encoding rules, making certificate and PKCS#7 input parsing more standards-compliant. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/34df960a75aeb85b97e5ac70465275c2057ee1a3)
- Split the blocking TLS client guide into two pages to make basic usage and subsequent detailed explanations clearer. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2df71d618505056f86cf4e033d9fdfcde0e6574a)
- Fixed detail issues in the documentation and examples to ensure the blocking TLS client example builds and runs correctly according to the guide. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/edd5b9d708d03ce1bdc1cbfc026ccc9183d586ad)
- When the default digest query returns success but the name is `UNDEF`, make `openssl ca` handle the result correctly instead of treating it as a valid digest name. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/af99d55078582fb2ac35787043d56e0c10b1fe97)
- Added abbreviations for send key update, receive key update, and key update to the QUIC glossary. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5633a323df5a533e003163b9f556a476b9f9df45)
- Added a QUIC API overview design document, outlining the responsibilities and relationships among connections, stream objects, and their SSL APIs. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5c0356a240d095ca7084d2ec2436a664f3c05156)
- Expanded the description of connection close and shutdown in the QUIC API design, clarifying the closing phase and application-layer behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9532c517591c7e4cfa43dfdd1bff76e5ce1593cd)
- Revised details in the QUIC API design to align interface descriptions with current stream object behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0a3fb1fb05ee55c2bb477071b376c3a180eb474b)
- Further organized the QUIC API design description, supplementing the interface semantics for application-layer stream operations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/aef249612759a12683c472a1032629ad90f8fd4a)
- Proposed a QUIC multi-stream API design, enabling applications to create, accept, and manage multiple streams on a single QUIC connection. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e4c2988dc5f70f15a9cd88e8fa047325c1e41cd2)
- Updated miscellaneous notes in the QUIC API design, correcting descriptions of interfaces and object relationships. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/64aa8eaf125a33c573d47067849e3cfe89d23070)
- Added a design outline for fast parameter lookup, describing a scheme to accelerate provider parameter name lookup via indexing. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/15821a48e558d595895fc8cf1c9c038d7c455550)
- Fixed the documentation of the `retain_fallbacks` parameter in `OSSL_PROVIDER_try_load()` to accurately describe the retention behavior of the fallback provider. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/063cdca61adfd4d418affe850f8bebbf528d05d9)
- Added documentation explaining the cleanup effect of `SSL_OP_CLEANSE_PLAINTEXT` on QUIC sent stream data. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/42926ca7f237126331a46cad159e6d31e2eafcc8)
- Updated the reference counting documentation to describe the interface and usage after the structured atomic reference counting refactoring. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/52c362b3fe5ab9b1c44ec560820b242eb3df0e3b)
- Prohibited the use of the NPN extension in QUIC TLS to avoid negotiating legacy application protocol mechanisms unsupported by QUIC. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/68dbff4c040e6f1b65f84b649185aa466c4fba24)
- Prohibited client authentication after the TLS handshake for QUIC connections, as QUIC does not support this post-handshake flow. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7163617f3310a2d8579388866a156df62b78bd69)
- Constrain SSL method settings for QUIC SSL contexts and connections to prevent switching QUIC objects to incompatible TLS/DTLS methods. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3ea30e76d788a4f1982785c0f29853cf1211d37e)
- Revise the CMP documentation on PBM-based and MAC-based protection to clarify the differences between the two message protection mechanisms. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/89ed128d7a871b627693bfc1a89703d1a12cb402)
- Expand the EVP_KDF documentation on the purpose and combination of `salt` and `info` parameters, clarifying their role in key derivation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/61c8146aa36b84afd9d83c87c9a01138979ffd60)
- Improve QUIC connection ID handling so endpoints can maintain connection ID sequences and manage their use and retirement per the protocol. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5be15438fc0bcb81fdf22dee6c7801ca3089fb74)
- Fix the EVP_Cipher API documentation on the AAD parameter and return value, clarifying authenticated data handling and call result semantics. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/51a7066e2092b062a502e29166adfcc297803058)
- Fix a typo in a function name. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/38c70a161cc6f96682bd77c8a935c5767355438c)
- Complete several QUIC pending interfaces and behaviors to align public call paths with expected QUIC support. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/44cb36d04adb737be1aee32908232003deeb67dd)
- Integrate the crypto manual page content into the OpenSSL guide, making it easier for users to find cryptographic library concepts in a unified guide. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d5b7d0a6a2c7263c4bc22a9403e05bc80d324f9a)
- Integrate the SSL manual page content into the OpenSSL guide, providing a unified introduction to the TLS library and its interfaces. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2c2ba73b7aa64859f1a6e68ccae781c19a1ef29f)
- Update documentation build files to include new guide pages and content in the build output. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ec34c887d1fea79001490e65342201a78514667e)
- Keep the legacy interpretation of the path parameter in HTTP request line and request setup APIs to ensure existing calling code remains compatible. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/45c02183c65f0e1abf59909c2900764606334664)
- Add memory BIO documentation explaining buffer growth, read-only/write-only modes, and data access behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a25715be77ff43cd816a2011fff50b68204e9c0e)
- Update the QUIC `SSL_accept_stream()` manual page to explain the behavior and return conditions for accepting inbound streams. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1875642c8954638077ca82a77b759b448c28e2d4)
- Update the QUIC `SSL_shutdown()` manual page to clarify the phases and completion conditions when closing a connection. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/51b2a670c8f8499dc4f89efbd711e5b5276791b2)
- Fix the default value description for the `ess_cert_id_alg` option in the `openssl ts` documentation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5ffad4bad9bd701cc3d14c96304484884ace0831)
- Fix references to non-existent constants in the documentation to avoid relying on API identifiers that cannot be looked up. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/de4661b23776dde80fe5832eea34c63d5e15a6e4)
- Update the blocking QUIC client tutorial on shutdown, clarifying the waiting and completion semantics of blocking close calls. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b7f3d5d67d17aa1a384811014e79b461ce0e23ca)
- Update internal links in the guide to avoid using `crypto(7)` references that are not suitable for general user navigation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/306101e5d919476a4f68ae8e6f73993d8ebe269e)
- Clarify the behavior of `SSL_accept_stream()` and `SSL_new_stream()` when a default stream exists, to prevent callers from mistakenly thinking they operate on the default stream. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a855ee857645614483976fb1be1b3907a2fe94d8)
- Distinguish stream-level errors from connection-level errors in examples and tutorials, explaining which scope applications should handle different errors in. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/02e36ed3525a2f0fda1b21e948ec5f522cf9379c)
- Add documentation for `WAIT_PEER`, explaining the trigger conditions and return semantics of this waiting mode. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7ff1696e5b4d090839b0baa92c5333d482240fe0)
- Further clarify the waiting and stream close state descriptions in the `WAIT_PEER` documentation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/84adf0764bf21e6be9f0a7b3c7a6b0448541bf0f)
- Add `OSSL_PROVIDER_load_ex()` to allow specifying a library context and attribute query conditions when loading a provider. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9d2f7e1f611f03e65f25adf08b76e08821b315da)
- Add a design document for runtime parameter activation, explaining how to select and enable parameter sets in providers and library contexts. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9f5102bffc8bb3a9b02a0a5e3c1de4326622fe04)
- Fix defects in the memory-based and libuv-based examples in the QUIC demo driver model so this run mode correctly handles buffering and events. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b1cb0675e5c76c6dd78863e6857b5456718da7b5)
- Allow setting the target host and port of the QUIC demo from the command line, making it easier to run the same example against different test servers. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/30302c66bd47220f6fa65f32bc510d3e679ec3d9)
- Update the Makefile for the QUIC demo driver model examples so new examples can be compiled via the documentation build target. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/be4c3446a475a8449aa00e550de9de887ec44a70)
- Add planned QUIC adaptation changes to the blocking connection example, demonstrating how to call QUIC APIs with a blocking application structure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/37f12107ee98670bae4b967110370a2bdb171c16)
- Revise the blocking connection example to handle additional adaptation issues found during implementation, ensuring ALPN, connection, and resource cleanup flows are correct. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b96e5cc60b6c9b4985eab829fd7b1161481da428)
- Add planned QUIC changes to the non-blocking connection example, demonstrating event-driven connection and retry handling. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e6ad003d73b3021790f486ded07fe038a6d30335)
- Revise the non-blocking connection example to handle BIO and handshake differences found when adapting QUIC, making the event loop driver runnable. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f3792076597a8e9003f61333e5e9a84818f12529)
- Add planned QUIC adaptation to the file descriptor blocking example, enabling applications to establish connections via UDP sockets and synchronous APIs. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/43f4b8a80ead05900a3a23196c3c4bbb3ed045b1)
- Add actual adaptation changes to the file descriptor blocking example, configuring the ALPN required for QUIC and correctly handling connection cleanup. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/24e583619c6f4bb1e7659b6b4f06cea920710688)
- Add planned QUIC changes to the file descriptor non-blocking example, demonstrating poll-driven QUIC I/O and event deadline handling. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b3e71dbf6863343cfabdbe2ba0443fcb69343874)
- Revise the file descriptor non-blocking example to adapt to QUIC's retry and event advancement behavior, improving the underlying socket waiting flow. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/92db6d628016baa146bfce8b645c13f64ad4bf68)
- Add planned QUIC adaptation to the memory datagram non-blocking example, demonstrating driving connections via datagram BIO pairs. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/70dc50c2659f70c47f20e45939b0b43fe9436610)
- Add implementation details to the memory datagram non-blocking example, explaining that QUIC output is datagram frames and the read buffer must be large enough. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/47eceab67aef371c00504354939f7b5aff211c60)
- Add planned QUIC changes to the libuv-based memory datagram example, demonstrating coordinated driving of UDP and timer events. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5e73999803a90126386c78eb68164192ca0d76ce)
- Revise the actual implementation of the libuv QUIC example, improving clock conversion and UDP send completion handling. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/74d588ca1581924e51e9bc72d26d372cdcd269f2)
- Add planned QUIC adaptation to the non-blocking connection thread helper example, demonstrating call patterns driven by background thread I/O. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1ed2d7929a3f3fe86242116b0bc1366d36c3782d)
- Add actual adaptation to the thread helper QUIC example, configuring ALPN and waiting for network events via poll descriptors. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c276217e4bc9db08f0741882af837355f50c18ab)
- Publish the QUIC demo-driven design conclusion report, summarizing API requirements revealed by blocking, non-blocking, file descriptor, memory BIO, and thread helper examples. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/277880e754c5a19cc456165560344204373a6b40)
- Update QUIC documentation and implementation details to align example usage with existing interface behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/abeb41b42fa3cdca99d3f3fef48ea6ee04023d68)
- Add a QUIC stream origin query API so callers can distinguish locally created streams from peer-initiated streams. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d2e9e12b23fe331b71abe8c201f2610266090dde)
- Add documentation for `OSSL_get_thread_support_flags()`, explaining how to query capability flags for thread support in the library. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cc50b44e555d71a10cdc45588d1b57523fb95ee4)
- Allow third-party BIO poll implementations to use integers instead of pointers for poll descriptors, increasing the portability of the poll descriptor interface. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2619d10ace8ee8e56425771bac48aa12769421f2)
- Emphasize the interface contracts of `BIO_sendmmsg()` and `BIO_recvmmsg()`, explaining batch send/receive return values and partial processing semantics. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cf269150630aa2854589b250f9aa61186a6b74ce)
- Update the QUIC API table to accurately indicate which SSL APIs are supported, restricted, or not applicable on QUIC connections. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0c125b611d55b2d3a79a977614431745f149afbe)
- State in the documentation that QUIC does not support `SSL_set_shutdown()` because connection close state is managed by the QUIC protocol layer. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/728741f5880d538444754ae18557342d552b56ae)
- Update the QUIC API overview document to include current interface support status and differences from ordinary SSL behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ca5b030306b8b4c98afca5dca216bc59c24e6aca)
- Explain the differences of `SSL_want()` on QUIC versus ordinary TLS, preventing applications from interpreting QUIC return values based on TLS record layer read/write states. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e24f784b0c3be754b2114902ca3d8ec8e67711dc)
- Fix a typo in the `openssl-pkeyutl` manual page. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/59d87f6e7eb41018a2a17fef2198d3fcf04e63f4)
- Only output progress dots for `openssl req` when `-verbose` is specified, avoiding irrelevant progress information in ordinary command output. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c1673a60e40f6dcd110d1a4ff3e11a3297ada2da)
- Expand the non-blocking programming guide on performing other work during handshake gaps, showing how to schedule application tasks while waiting for network events. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/38c3c1dbefa8b8333e78e0d9d38fac7c4359f826)
- Return a null pointer when creating a BIO for `quicserver` fails, preventing subsequent code from dereferencing an uncreated BIO. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/11b7d46fa7e2684e0ad0f12a7806163dba99983d)
- Add notes in the manual page about using PKCS12KDF in FIPS mode, clarifying algorithm availability and provider configuration requirements. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4ee8c1fb51687ea811fc2abf87e173c70d018bc2)
- Fix documentation and deprecation markers: `d2i_ECPKParameters()` and `i2d_ECPKParameters()` are not deprecated and should continue to be supported interfaces. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2508629765403375b3075300a0951705ec26fe27)
- Add documentation for the OSSL_STORE delete API, explaining interface parameters and deletion behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ed4a71d18d939f557b430c4369d118b55c1c0b6c)
- Update documentation for poll descriptor and network read/write requirement APIs, explaining how to use these query interfaces in TLS, DTLS, and QUIC. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b509d0bd25ca457764aaa5e7dcb797dc6a17a98f)
- Refine the `OSSL_PROVIDER_load_ex()` design document based on review feedback, clarifying the roles of context and attribute parameters. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/54fbb9e416524e09d6125ffc551cefba83306992)
- Rename the `OSSL_PROVIDER_load_ex()` design document so the title matches the final provider loading interface naming. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0988de278c2f861e47d63cd284992befa686e4a8)
- Invoke the verification callback when CMP certificate acceptability checks find an expired certificate, giving applications the opportunity to decide acceptance based on custom policy. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/080bd08fd32608b4f2edfa4b1e87e199b08a8835)
- Use the configured seed source to seed the FIPS DRBG as configured, ensuring random number initialization follows the requested entropy source selection. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4cde7585ce8eb53682256ba79e6af1949498fbfe)
- Fix Markdown table alignment and formatting issues so the documentation passes markdownlint checks. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5995dc37197dd85baa749325ba23f5aa8ccbe1f6)
- Fix the `SSL_CTX_set_info_callback()` manual page to accurately describe callback parameters and their invocation timing. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/92986c0be56bc8bf9c414dab59d4f1e128f1e843)
- Updated documentation to explain that `lh_stats` API has been deprecated in OpenSSL 3.1. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6a92159d01116495e5e642e55fe0f6e4c821696e)

- Corrected documentation for `PKCS5_PBKDF2_HMAC()` parameters and derivation process to accurately describe API usage. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/82496b8663f20ff12f02adbe46a060a94b0cbfc5)

- Removed unnecessary EVP header file requirements from the PKCS#8 BIO decoding page to avoid misleading callers into adding irrelevant includes. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/831602922f19a8f39d0c0fae425b81e9ab402c69)

- Emphasized in `DH_check()` documentation that callers must check return values to avoid ignoring parameter validation failure flags. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f7b80136a3df4396b19ebb86d4814d8cefe6d6db)

- Updated documentation ahead of time to reflect interface changes in PR #22480, keeping documentation consistent with planned changes. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/df226ab548bc404ef22a0cfdd32010909512c03b)
- Fixed spelling of "certificates" in documentation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/017fc90a1c3cc02b272c7adc8d1e9ffd7344b2b0)
- Clarified the relationship between `EVP_PKEY_get_size()` and provider key management size parameters. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d9aa640d12861eba845e7a5b5013495ad65ea126)
- Updated QUIC README to reflect current API and support status. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a9044628c8cddfdf0686215b2c61dcbea61f95a4)
- Updated QUIC DDD README to reflect current examples and reports. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/97750ca0b2c2fe7fd94e82ae841fec92f7173a35)
- Updated QUIC DDD example code and Makefile to match current API. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0d4a8667e25406b39785c6d4a25b34a825eb798a)
- Updated guide tutorials to match example parameter and behavior changes. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6198e73f7479ba46373357dea43b722dbc9f7449)
- Fixed KMAC output length description in documentation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b4081e6ca4a855d09c93dd62658ab808f1273dc6)
- Used correct SSKDF parameter name to pass shared secret input. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/076d4750c8af8bfcfc93f35aaae8ee6fb729b95b)
- Updated OpenSSL logo resources. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bcb488d3a5f7e24667c739c5fe5fa4ac6d10ca87)
- Updated provider documentation to align with current interface behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fb61722e9ddf5e96fd9af2b4b6f0b08bb453f9d1)
- Explained variable output length behavior of KECCAK-KMAC XOF. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/09adcb0bd5349e3a1128deb95400f1375a74e5b2)
- Fixed MAC `block-size` documentation to distinguish block size and tag length. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5647960dd5126a7fa25f9bb518ed8d7e031c4827)

### Build / CI

- Unified optional parameter notation in documentation, changing [[ options ]] to [option...]. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a4ffb33ea8b7bcf04b8181dafce7ac512081d0ab)
- Enhanced srctop, bldtop, data, and result helper functions in the test framework to check and report command results. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2fe2279d1f3bbfa934e432d4f2c3a7e6a6b0f958)
- Fixed call parameters for OpenSSL::Ordinals::set_version() to pass only the short version number to match its interface convention. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/435981cbadad2c58c35bacd30ca5d8b4c9bea72f)
- Made OpenSSL::ParseC recognize OPENSSL_EXPORT and OPENSSL_EXTERN macros to correctly parse export declarations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7a2ad00f3ecffa6be350e9e72992d4ec003f54ae)
- Explicitly set build target to 64-bit for macOS 10.15 and later versions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b926548b362531e8a64e7482c081611fab7183a8)
- When configuring afalgeng, no longer attempt to auto-infer unreliable configuration details; instead use explicit configuration options. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9e1a54f4a187195fc417ad0f90e84d208d478968)
- Markdown lint now allows code-style content in fenced code blocks to avoid misinterpreting markers in examples as formatting violations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fb2cd91f27d0cc0bbf1883dd188788016165a458)
- Markdown lint no longer enforces a single space after list markers, allowing existing list indentation formats. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2046f80bc84deff5f3b8e3f8ffe54528bc684658)
- Configuration phase now directly generates configuration.h and re-runs configuration generation steps when that file or build files are missing. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2522889620446f1e56338367d1b6b028ea952bb4)
- FIPS module checksum dependency tracking now includes configuration.h, correctly updating module checksums when configuration headers change. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4d02d500aac80c136e3d6582b908e0fab77bbf42)
- Improved diagnostic wording in check-format.pl, changing "no" to "missing" and refining some error messages. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/63263147a3ad55cb26d7cfcdfca474ce324a15ac)
- Stopped automatically trimming Configure-generated configuration content, preserving explicit configuration results to avoid unintentional removal of build features. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/54a84f02998b72fa63cd5309d915e43cfd85a06b)
- Made the record layer directly recognize Encrypt-then-MAC mode, executing MAC and encryption-related processing in EtM protocol order. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7f2f0ac7bfdd676cd919dd94b971874eade41830)
- Renamed RX Frame Handler to RX Depacketizer in the QUIC overview diagram to unify component names. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bf16ee4f95c31a66e76056c691f25a0d2b4a39c4)
- Rejected SSL2_VERSION identifier in SSLv3 record headers to prevent illegal record version combinations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ed9876cc39ca7a86b35dbaba8a106eee6f2bc8e6)
- Updated oqs-provider submodule to a newer commit to obtain fixes needed for post-quantum Provider tests. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6549041704a4827af5ccc01b0552afb6fb7a442b)
- Removed further references to SSL objects in the record layer, with the record layer maintaining necessary state itself. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/521f07eb08cc267001ecb4be67d46ea79dbb62b1)
- Made the record layer directly track Encrypt-then-MAC mode and process records in EtM order. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3e139f3d85396cab0bac5d263472b3223a51b76a)
- Grouped kill arguments with parentheses to ensure the wrapper re-emits the same signal when the child process receives a signal. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ef6d6e452dc57ef4a55d7a6ec0693be650009bb5)
- Cleaned up errors generated by internal probing during CMS ContentInfo decoding to avoid residual error queue entries after successful decoding. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6e2f15ca93f0a7d0a91dfdba5e7010a4cf14b052)
- ParseC.pm now handles DOS CRLF line endings when reading source files to avoid carriage returns at line ends interfering with declaration parsing. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c507db9678f50482df5f6c58e42572fe6fe3007c)
- Adjusted symbol list ordering in util/other.syms to maintain a consistent order for auxiliary exported symbol lists. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9dabb55da3463f651582db8ac9030afb5a718bd8)
- Added an empty handshake layer for the QUIC prototype to validate connection and transport components before full TLS integration. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f71ae05a4d22d52780fc7cfc7e60710b74fd3dd7)
- Updated QUIC generated files and indexes to include new QUIC API/source files under make update management. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/44019a69c4ae305f1f753f836c4d06ac63a8c6f3)
- Build rules now use the resolved configdata build_file field to avoid picking the wrong generated file name from target configuration. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/aa2d7e0ee15d1b7015479c38f370a25ceec690fc)
- QUIC SSL objects now support querying TLS protocol version information, making SSL_version and SSL_get_version return correct versions on QUIC connections. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/50769b15ea76123406b5ccebe85b2402e64e9fc6)
- Implemented `SSL_accept_stream()` and accept queue length query APIs for QUIC, allowing applications to accept queued inbound streams and know queue status. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cb68ce9fa7e2312afd8e5346a799d32024b67d02)
- Added `util/ctags.sh` to generate code labels after macro expansion, facilitating code browsing tools to index symbols in macro definitions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/859521e57970027c2ec763928753a1e5f843cf69)
- Updated OpenVMS build configuration to keep platform configuration in sync with current source code and toolchain requirements. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d6175dcca746f0996db18ab2b6b37a4152097afe)
- Added workarounds for specific build issues to avoid build or test failures in affected environments. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b4a123100720c959b018529f15818fd5fde8a323)
- Allowed man7 pages without a DESCRIPTION section to pass documentation checks, accommodating page structures that do not require that section. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a2b6865be563810fad17e2d925304fde8d7eaf30)
- Simplified string construction in the Perl parameter name module to reduce hard-to-understand magic value handling. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3691f1e5567d6b61ed917adf48b40ae95ac5cb17)
- Fixed field names used when generating `fipsmodule.cnf` to match the FIPS module metadata structure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/89111cbc3238dcdf47e921af38d6b12c4b32d326)
- Updated QUIC-related generated files and auxiliary artifacts to keep generated content in sync with source file changes. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5a75ef37e94902c5221889604f2eda480fc18977)
- Automatically disabled QUIC when TLS 1.3 is disabled in the build, because QUIC's TLS handshake depends on TLS 1.3. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0e94bba0df257d47fb9623b9b95ef2b64402fde5)
- Installed module configuration in a FIPS-compliant manner by default, so the default installation process generates configuration files usable in FIPS mode. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/dc6f3b9b8d6e54ea7d8669a158fd73b451862c7d)
- Made `no-module` only disable module loading without disabling DSO support, preserving other dynamic shared object functionality. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3d2f96e2c867fa3e79a453639304b70ba0508076)
- Correctly tracked read position in the QUIC test server request buffer to avoid reprocessing or skipping request data on multiple reads. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/747b51f48338e3b7e53d0b7a87002edefc7e8439)
- Let the QUIC test server output errors to the standard error stream via BIO, keeping OpenSSL I/O and error output paths consistent. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4dec9285d3c833c26cfc589f31d896e93a36f498)
- Extend `quicserver` to handle multiple QUIC streams for client examples and multi-stream tests. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f6225f4f692ab45c0e891f83a7782a7dcd211204)
- Remove unused `ssl3_comp_st` structure definitions, cleaning up unsupported compression state types. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2de153dc5a2565bd922b9a15955f2abca02b215e)
- Update the missing encryption algorithm documentation list to synchronize `missingcrypto.txt` with the current documentation state. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/84364b9dc693a30fa55c22e684b45978a5bcc77b)
- Update generated files for the OSSL_STORE delete API to include new interface declarations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1aac593600f9656170dd144ca1219fdcd8ee8322)
- Adjust markdownlint configuration to adapt documentation format checks to current Markdown style. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/acf95129bce6ea9c42558d1cd5ac7b6b5841577b)
- Update generated files to synchronize build content. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0f8c67080075597745f13467e1e841fc4b904251)
- Stop the test server when QUIC handshake or request reception fails, avoiding continued operation after errors. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bb8ecea8ed22862184b431f5185c23f0f6cfd9c2)

### Maintenance

- Adjust the release branch name in dev/release.sh to use the branch name determined by project voting. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8e706c8ae5d6abf69b1b0aa0c4ab3517607522d0)
- Adjust DANE certificate priority so that the issuer certificate specified by the TLSA record takes precedence over the certificate sent by the peer. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/661de442e4231a9b0411dc8562f9e465d1d7fabc)
- Fix type compatibility handling for the VMS x86_64 cross-compiler to avoid type mismatches during compilation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9150ca6017f8654e671bd7d7d1b494ce1ce3fa0d)
- Make the DECODER match the name of the first decoded structure with the user-specified structure name, avoiding returning data of the wrong type. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/73dd5d67c506cfeb9bf6183f0c19832c7d3f174d)
- Adjust the object type and data structure specification for the PEM-to-DER decoder to keep type information consistent in decoding requests. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/98408852c167d895a662dcda824fd5170cad3f7d)
- Set input structure types for certificates and CRLs in the OSSL_STORE file: scheme so that file loading uses the correct decoding target. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/821b3956ec698927281a5b29c55cd87eb7b2793d)
- Strengthen boundary checks in the BIO write path to avoid improper buffer handling in bio_write under boundary conditions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a04b06573e2b3c6a5c703a60bd95354c6c6e91dc)
- Make Socket connection information output the target address it is connecting to, for easy identification of the actual connection endpoint. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/994fa5f9861df94c07699cb118ad5c5470a868b2)
- Make install_fips create the OPENSSLDIR directory if it does not exist, avoiding FIPS installation failure due to a missing target directory. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/85efdaab4d068f7de354b0a18f70f1737941dc7f)
- Replace tabs with spaces in two Perl scripts shipped with the release package to unify indentation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ea0d79db9be9066de350c44c160bd8b17f2be666)
- Adjust the sharedlib_simple() configuration logic on Unix platforms to recognize shared library build settings for different platform variants. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bfbb62c3b0a8f8d223f84ebf7507594cee99f135)
- Set the default value of -nameopt to utf8 so that OpenSSL commands output name fields in UTF-8 format by default. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/86cfd132ffc4f6198cc640a29c293850c0a59914)
- Remove unnecessary explicit inclusion of synchapi.h to avoid build dependencies on unused Windows headers. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/eeb612021e220de734e1ff08499f42bb962c3916)
- Lower the security strength rating of the TLS CCM_8 suite to reflect the security limitations of its short IV. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1a473d1cc67e04ae9fea517b36dc332143250cf5)
- Update iOS build configuration, adjusting platform build settings in 15-ios.conf. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/aa58071e4b8b245db1564f476731c978738e7e98)
- Add thread safety protection to OBJ_ series object operations to avoid races during concurrent creation or querying of objects. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/397065c621e733fff80dedb28252120ec143693e)
- Fix the README file name reference in SSL documentation so that links point to the correct file. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8b6a7da304d4fdd0de38ddd6037d8a02491e3e4e)
- Adjust callers of the PVK derivation function to use the new PVK KDF implementation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1ffac6ca174d25a61f2e1e70dd0fd1eb7eaacbf5)
- Fix source code comments in ssl3_read_bytes() to match the current read behavior of the function. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/105af0ad923a665ca5fee296b52dbf34b524a2aa)
- Fully initialize cipher and digest application handles to avoid uninitialized fields affecting subsequent algorithm operations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a8cc0efe0d8fdd7bfa1d40b3c008d7d6ddf970db)
- Free bindhost and bindport configuration strings to avoid leaks of listening address configuration during cleanup. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0ce0c455862ed29bd7f2acdbddbe8d0b1783c1c9)
- Ensure EVP_CIPHER_CTX_copy() works with the dasync engine, correctly copying asynchronous engine contexts. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a0cbc2d222743fc4ffd276b97bd5f8aeacf01122)
- Fix pkey_set_type() handling of ENGINE references to correctly manage engine references when setting key types. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f7d6868d0d48fedd5d9daad0c3e0cbcaef423ff3)
- Adjust ENGINE reference management in provider_util.c to avoid references being incorrectly retained or released prematurely. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/86c15ba87488f88e6191f098ff154f79ce91847b)
- Enforce output buffer length checks in EVP_MAC_final() to avoid final MAC writes exceeding the caller-provided capacity. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b97f4dd73b4711eebf731ae0efa6e9b77c7f3304)
- Update the pyca-cryptography submodule to incorporate its upstream fixes. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7cce994d3e57345ba729388b9321d9bf8b661b4f)
- Add range checks for the -multi parameter of openssl speed, rejecting invalid numbers of parallel tasks. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7220085f22cf6c49933ea8287eb15db57f7ab0db)
- Adjust the fetch order for EVP public key operations, first selecting methods based on the key's provider to ensure operations match the key's provider. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5246183e7a9f9fb1819d50ab40e2fecc68235e0d)
- Check for the existence of key objects in all operations using EVP_PKEY to avoid null keys entering the algorithm execution flow. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/433e13455ede1a39d415b690b8a564b4f36b8dee)
- Fix obj2bin() in the Windows Makefile template so that resource files also participate in the conversion from object files to binary files. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/01451721afebabd0b7bdcd4cb3a183c9b590d266)
- Adjust the prompt text for secure renegotiation in s_client and s_server to accurately describe the negotiation state in command output. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/af5e63e1e3300f784f302a5d3309bf673cc08894)
- Make DES_set_key() always set the key and return the same check status as DES_set_key_checked(). [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6450ea27ffdc22194f27e90796ce5538af2d81e2)
- Clean up logical dead code identified by Coverity, removing condition branches that can never be executed. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/182cc644b3a3690bddfecba925486fefa421d6ec)
- Fix negative parameter issues identified by Coverity, checking non-negative constraints before parameters enter related operations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fe4125382301201e42a3251544cda429bba0c9d7)
- Change KTLS algorithm identification to use EVP_CIPHER_is_a() to determine cipher types by standard names, avoiding reliance on internal NID values. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ff3e4508bde0d7f7ab211ca9f027bef820ba1d70)
- Adjust the PPC64 Keccak assembly implementation to load input data in 8 byte blocks in little-endian mode. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8f9842fd03945d9484dcc9e5fab37dce7caa5f50)
- Increase the growth ratio for stack memory reallocation to reduce repeated allocation overhead during continuous expansion. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8347bfa04fc62dcf684b8a43905709fa18f6a3b1)
- Make all signature algorithms in the Provider support possible operation reinitialization, unifying the reuse behavior of the signature interface. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3ffd23e9529d725903bc97fd45489a77b831876f)
- Avoid writing to global ossl_property_true and ossl_property_false objects to prevent modification of shared immutable property values. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6de9214a5062e9d015c84cbbab681184e16fccaa)
- Remove unnecessary ossl_provider_find() calls in ossl_provider_new() to avoid repeated Provider lookups during creation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/dc6d9ede6241e6858f8fa78435d6c8eb9cf85aa1)
- Continue the Provider deactivation process even when there is no store, avoiding early exit due to missing store and leaving state behind. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e39bd6215123f375ddcfe92fa2b2550294da0b73)
- Fix the activation flow of OSSL_PROVIDER_try_load() to ensure that the Provider being tried is activated as expected. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1e8ed3e596162d7490b26fb12e58af5208f52402)
- Hold flag_lock when calling child Provider callbacks to protect Provider flag state accessed during callbacks. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/addbd7c9d784e1cb630d43487b0572e867bfc86d)
- Reset read/write state before calling ASYNC_start_job() to avoid asynchronous tasks inheriting state values from previous operations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/07f620e3acf0dd76a3a03ada9911c544aa483aa7)
- Reject creating ECX keys with input of insufficient length to avoid incomplete private keys entering key objects. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/50938aec35fd57fb3bec707ead2eee381fcfaf04)
- Extract policy and certificate extension handling in CA.pl into separate configurations for reuse and maintenance by command scripts. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3066cf2614d22182ae0dafd4557a96ab6b698d4f)
- Make the CMP server log the received request type before checking request details, to aid in diagnosing parsing failures. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ae8ff109c1d80399a6a1c9f50aa37381bc3a1c5f)
- Make ERR error reason formatting ignore flags bits when falling back to decimal reason code output, correctly preserving flag semantics. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9350aaa41db8fcb0b55dadbd5fbe807ef5288557)
- Restore consistency between ERR_str_reasons in err.c and error reason definitions in err.h to avoid mismatches between reason codes and text mappings. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3ae55288387a3ff9cf9b1cba2da22bd1aafbc66e)
- Set the key before setting parameters during RSA signature initialization to ensure rsa_signverify_init() uses the determined key state. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/eaae5d69eb5a8cd9c054b23cc388397cbb4ffb98)
- Allow EVP_MD_CTX_copy_ex() to copy digest contexts that have not been initialized, supporting the call order of copying first and then configuring the digest. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9ece8323ea2230092227bf20e5d93012d15d92e9)
- Allow OSSL_PARAM_allocate_from_text() to perform sign extension for signed integers, correctly preserving the bit-width semantics of negative numbers. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/946bc0e3ec19ca019fcfa95f93c37f34e12fe0bd)
- Check the result of OPENSSL_strdup() in app_rand.c, safely handling random seed file paths on allocation failure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3e0441520b9a349dc50662919ea18f03dfc0d624)
- Clean up the doc/html directory during build, avoiding the clean operation deleting generated HTML documentation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bc6d9c9395a74a31b4e0c4a8cd729197adbf6a46)
- Exclude disabled cipher suites from the TLS 1.3 cipher suite list, respecting the disabled settings in the build configuration. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6cb814de6f276106eea39dbb813b9134b1b72041)
- Fix failure return values of do_X509_REQ_verify() and do_X509_verify() so that verification failures correctly return -1. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bc42cf51c8b2a22282bb3cdf6303e230dc7b7873)
- Prohibit the TLS 1.2 GOST cipher suite from using the Encrypt-then-MAC extension, avoiding enabling incompatible combinations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d724da69389196cdb9ef8db036656882fbc5a6ab)
- After clearing and resetting the buffer, always set the KDF output length to 0 to avoid retaining the old length when reused. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d2217c88df6e65c756013417e5ee4f470dd12470)
- Fixed the issue where the PVK KDF output length was not reset after clearing the buffer, avoiding reporting the previously derived length. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a632bfaa4ee3339749f7a6a07ab4d0abee4eaaef)
- Check the return value of BN_dup() in rsa_lib.c to avoid accessing a null object after failing to duplicate a large number. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9d1a27051dcd4e7a621df54a073587c6c4486476)
- Check the result of X509_get0_pubkey() in s_cb.c, and safely handle certificate callbacks when the public key is unavailable. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5fae09f3d8da7c182c6cfb6a295dcfd15ae828ae)
- Make the PVK decoder prompt for the PVK passphrase instead of incorrectly prompting for the PEM passphrase. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/28257d60577932e66934096d0ee8a5dfaca1191e)
- Make key_to_type_specific_pem_bio_cb() use the passphrase callback provided in the parameters, reading the key passphrase according to the caller's configuration. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c22b6592135bfba95a315e438ac7bfc6db461407)
- Improve diagnostic messages for OpenSSL commands when arguments are missing or extra, and when unknown ciphers or digests are specified. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d9f073575fdb07b486cd1b38974cd177687ccc1e)
- Improve diagnostic messages when CMP transaction IDs do not match, helping to locate failures in associating transactions between server and client. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a3ea35c2936acbe6a53b1d52d2d7addbfb6bbd5a)
- Check the return value of BIO_new() in t_x509.c and cmp_vfy.c to avoid dereferencing null pointers after BIO allocation failure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/318e97997a514b16ca497cedb49730bc75764a05)
- Correct the file path output by s_server to return the actual file name used. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2e3b82926a8cdae5a1bfbf3ac47a6012c270391b)
- Export OSSL_provider_init() as OPENSSL_EXPORT to ensure the initialization entry point is visible to external providers. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d977a26ed8ca5066d4d72a6d73f1669c8619f4a1)
- Adjust the recipNone check to be consistent with the improved CMP transactionID check logic. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e46997111af3a11632df411b01d62fd39cc3faaf)
- Clear the buffer before writing to it in bn2binpad, ensuring padding bytes have deterministic values. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/858d5ac16d256db24f78b8c84e723b7d34c8b1ea)
- Fixed a memory leak in EC_POINT_hex2point() where the point object pt was not freed. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/dd2fcc1f7c44c5fb5aa2d33aecdc699c7018ce01)
- Fixed a use-after-free state when combining HMAC and digest context copying, avoiding access to freed objects after copying. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ad2fcee1632d3f21a37e8e108d4c0dcf9099686d)
- Fixed the issue where the storage object created by X509_STORE_new() was not freed, cleaning up certificate store resources. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c81eed84e4e9025e933778f5e8326b1e4435e094)
- Added compatibility handling for HTTPS clients to bypass the HTTP line reading issue caused by SSL BIO not supporting the gets method. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/606c79e29bbc26c27c3b85cc52fe7d72051184de)
- Handle the case where namemap lookup returns NULL when querying non-legacy ciphers or digests, avoiding continued use of null results. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7a85dd46e0b2f67b341c777509f0126e3252938d)
- Still send the SNI extension when using s_client through a proxy, ensuring the TLS server can select a certificate based on the target hostname. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ea24196ef224d3aa3aaecb8000004bb7a0a100a2)
- Use a stack structure to efficiently convert attribute indices to strings, reducing repeated traversal in property name lookups. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2e3c59356f847a76a90f9f837d4983428df6eb19)
- Fixed the case where the UI method always appends a NUL terminator, avoiding double-counting of the passphrase buffer length. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ef65bbb96352650bf9ce4ff46c60c71d9f138d08)
