- Fixed the handling of the trailing NUL in try_pkcs12(), correctly distinguishing between the passphrase content length and the terminator. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1dfef929e43ebfa3a7f1108317f75747f92effb6)
- Clear the stack buffer after using the passphrase in try_pkcs12() to avoid leaving plaintext passphrases. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/da7db7ae6d7d1929893a58e41335c88e472fc364)
- Fixed the path in close_console() where the lock was not released when held, ensuring the lock is always released after closing the console. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5bea0e2ee9bda4d9be6e88c79f2c1b411bb65351)
- Check the return value of ossl_bio_new_from_core_bio(), and do not continue using a null object when core BIO wrapping fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/352a0bcaab8eda18cce786d2871e8d4ec6f9cbfe)
- Validate the trace category in OSSL_trace_end(), rejecting invalid category values. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ee8a61e158c42c327c3303101083422b9a7cc504)
- Enable USE_SWAPCONTEXT on IA64 platforms, using the context switching implementation supported by that platform. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d26b3766a0a35668ee62b839a62acbdcd9ff2a98)
- Check the return value of EVP_MD_fetch() in ECDH and DH exchange implementations, and safely report errors when digest fetching fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/04e3ab64d58bb43efc4392d12c607bb4b5a2c562)
- Correctly free resources allocated via CRYPTO_malloc(), avoiding memory leaks during object cleanup. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1b87116a0c43b8b4e1ad88b851d5bcf27c1a5f64)
- Fixed the issue in ssl3_record.c where the digest context created by EVP_MD_CTX_new() was not freed. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/949e4f79d202d43519d373b2af6b1a4948bf1a74)
- Change the alert protocol identifier to a public protocol constant, unifying alert types used across components. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/81b741f68984b2620166d0d6271fbd946bab9e7f)
- Remove unused parameters in get_construct_message_f, simplifying the construct message callback interface. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e1c122711edc3b9d64e506a51c3c0482569b7498)
- Stop using __ARMEL__ and __ARMEB__ macros to determine byte order in AArch64 assembly, avoiding reliance on macros not applicable to that architecture. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/40c24d74deaad8a0ad7566a68ea5ea757bc3ccef)
- Make EVP_PKEY_derive_set_peer_ex() import the peer key into the matching keymgmt implementation, ensuring derivation operations use the correct provider. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/64a8f6008acce93d0bf184559c63e66c0cc0e23d)
- Ensure that digest implementations created by EVP_MD_meth_new() follow the legacy path, compatible with old-style custom digest methods. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d9ad5b16b32172df6f7d02cfb1c339cc85d0db01)
- Fixed the issue in EVP_DigestSignFinal() where *siglen was read when sigret is NULL, avoiding access to uninitialized output in the length query path. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a4e01187d3648d9ce99507097400902cf21f9b55)
- Remove unused callback variables, cleaning up redundant declarations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/64a644530e023d3064db9027b0977d33b1d2ad9a)
- Improve the detection capability of ThreadSanitizer lock detection, making it easier for concurrent code to confirm whether explicit lock protection is needed. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e22cbe5e67461470590e6fb8858c95285fcdea0e)
- Make the core namemap use the updated TSAN lock detection capability, correctly annotating concurrent access to the name mapping table. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d8ed9e4a9079b55a84bdbbc3172d36aa3be8bed7)
- Make the object module use the updated TSAN lock detection capability, avoiding missing thread safety checks in the object registration path. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e6b8f359e79cdbe09033d02eaad7ecb4e24adb73)
- Do not generate memory usage statistics when ThreadSanitizer support is not available, avoiding misleading analysis from statistics lacking necessary synchronization. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5c41cee225094e6298799b709278b0431643fb1f)
- Lock the lhash statistics retrieval operation when TSAN is not available, ensuring thread safety when reading statistics data. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/43f132778b138870120d965f2fb61aa7411b78b2)
- Clear md_data only when necessary, avoiding unconditional reset of digest private data that could affect reusable contexts. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8086b267fb3395c53cd5fc29eea68ba4826b333d)
- Clear all temporary data in the s390x assembly implementation to avoid residual sensitive intermediate values. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/79c7acc59bb98c2b8451b048ed1dd8cc517df76e)
- Fixed the issue pointed out by Coverity where dereferencing still occurred after a null check. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8c870f6bed241ec80c67453e60592461f0d8f2b8)
- Added SM3 acceleration implementation using AArch64 SM3 hardware instructions, improving digest computation performance on that architecture. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/71396cd048072b69559b46d98cfebfd4474cd712)
- Reduce memory consumption of the property module when OPENSSL_SMALL_FOOTPRINT is enabled, lowering static data overhead on small devices. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/57645339ab645fe5abffe14fc005b5402ce03b84)
- Check the name parameter before calling ossl_ffc_name_to_dh_named_group(), avoiding passing NULL. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3b53f88c008d288e86d2bbdc0c4e2d16c29fcee8)
- Change double semicolon statement separators to single semicolons in ssl source code, removing redundant empty statements. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1e3317278e4890e812a032b39c7c9dc43ca01458)
- Change double semicolon statement separators to single semicolons in the ca application, cleaning up invalid statements. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e52698f9e33d77419dca827774e5d0bc1815100d)
- Change double semicolons to single semicolons in other related source code, uniformly removing redundant empty statements. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d715dbd8e566e7827ce8b2e9b6687c2bcd8a89a0)
- Optimize SM4 implementation using ARM hardware instructions, improving SM4 encryption performance on ARM platforms. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/15b7175f558bf9eb057ec3266685486f727dd70f)
- Simplify the square root calculation for Ed25519, reducing unnecessary operations during verification. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a822a0cb3c8466adbcee510a6234c0fe95ff4bfe)
- Fixed the handling of TLS records with byte length 2^14 in s_server, correctly supporting the maximum record length boundary. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/148b592db7ea18e0209078fe313514fb7c7553f5)
- Remove floating-point formatting support in UEFI environments, avoiding the issue that this runtime does not support floating-point formatting. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f59d72f027da90edcccad5cc78c94d3099fadecf)
- Unify the progress callback interface for commands such as dhparam and dsaparam. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e1cd94f2dca4056ce042c62b89c468dffc088033)
- Check the result of OPENSSL_memdup() in UI calls, and safely handle input data when copying fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3f6a12a07f52c55dc3f4b0def42680f589f89ed4)
- Make X509_STORE_CTX_purpose_inherit() correctly handle the case where the default purpose is 0. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4aa82850267defd772ddf74a88d515ef4fb566b8)
- Make the openssl ciphers command respect the -propquery parameter, selecting cipher implementations based on property query restrictions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4ed381736b063284bdbd5d302988617aa4366a3f)
- Check the return value of get_globals() in BIO_new_from_core_bio(), avoiding dereferencing null pointers after global state retrieval failure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7f1cb465c1f0e45bde8c1ee54a37e6f7641c70c6)
- Use the standard progress callback in the genrsa command to display progress during RSA key generation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/261b399fd7b1f4339e6d0fa3ee37b32b81d9d9e0)
- Add the provider module directory to openssl.pc for applications using pkg-config to find module installation paths. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7fde39de848f062d6db45bf9e69439db2100b9bb)
- Stop using the non-assembly constant-time implementation path for AES by default, avoiding sacrificing performance on normal platforms in default builds. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e180bf641ed23010073b0882d63d5dfd48409602)
- Restore the use of var thiswr in do_ssl3_write(), reverting the TLS write regression caused by the previous removal of this variable. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b375a8ac9b672bba8e651c11afd2e0a466563742)
- Update the comment in ssl3_write_pending() to accurately describe the state and retry behavior of pending TLS data. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7c7c3561ebfb26799e2d12b5f9f0826731a6a06b)
- Prefer SOURCE_DATE_EPOCH when generating the copyright year, ensuring reproducible years across different build environments. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/27aca04e13ca8a9bead49de7bc380110ecb7064e)
- Use opt_int_arg() to parse integer options in speed.c, unifying error checking for command-line numeric parameters. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/78212c6472ed3ade565ebcde0330d6eca7785fd6)
- Cache the password IV length in the EVP enc command, reducing repeated queries. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b30b45b7247d056b569e2b5139f8b503d36e646c)
- Cache the password key length in the EVP enc command, reducing repeated queries. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/70f39a487d3f7d976a01e0ee7ae98a82ceeea7a0)
- Replace overly broad length checks with more meaningful public key existence checks. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2c0f7d46b8449423446cfe1e52fc1e1ecd506b62)
- Check for the presence of an OpenSSL 1.1.x runtime, providing a basis for cross-version Engine compatibility decisions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/14db620282bea38dc44479e562cf9bb61a716444)
- Write auxiliary diagnostic messages to bio_err, ensuring command-line error messages go to standard error output. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2a6994cfa08368a710d66caaae4fc07ad35631bf)
- Enable AES-GCM acceleration via AVX-512 vAES and vPCLMULQDQ instructions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/63b996e752ac698186c38177232280e6515d571b)
- Use the C locale in Bash scripts, avoiding locale settings altering text sorting and parsing results. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bd654f7e98e13c0dc3b5c707880b9a77ba9e342f)
- Unify handling of the return status of X509_TRUST_get_by_id, ensuring unknown or invalid trust identifiers follow a consistent error branch. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7b3041eba1c6e177eede0d6311d53a6b9ff58051)
- In VMS builds, copy DECC include prologue/epilogue files to the build directory when generating header files, ensuring separated builds retain compiler header wrapping behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/98b7b74122e66f63c4ec67a74e345c64a55c68db)
- Check the return value of CRYPTO_strdup and stop further processing on allocation failure, avoiding dereferencing null pointers. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/37be6feeebfec87733e5cb4762fc12bebba9f124)
- Prefer SSL_kDHE/SSL_kECDHE names in TLS cipher suite names and internal mappings, reducing reliance on old SSL_kEDH/SSL_kEECDH aliases. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/66914fc024cfe0fec00dc0f2c7bd8a7957da5ec4)
- Optimize the SM4 lookup table implementation for non-assembly build paths, adjusting the table layout used in round transformations to accelerate software implementations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/13ba91cb02479a91b0743d2bf5f5ec7ce42860d0)
- On FreeBSD, switch ASN.1 time conversion to timegm, interpreting time values as UTC, avoiding local timezone effects. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0176fc78d090210cd7e231a7c2c4564464509506)
- s_server calls SSL_sendfile only when the connection actually has KTLS sending enabled; if KTLS is unavailable, it warns and falls back to regular sending. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/aea68b0ddb7113b982ab503bf830d641e8425759)
- Add return value checks for BIO_new calls in multiple command-line programs, reporting errors and cleaning up allocated resources on failure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fa17f5c98783949a702ab9bb1e780c4e9f15566b)
- Add a SHAKE256 EVP XOF digest example, demonstrating configuring variable-length output digests via the EVP interface and reading results. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4c8cdcd1cf74747a80b4f7dd323cd83ea6c985d8)
- Optimize the SM4-GCM Provider update path on ARM, using the CTR32 hardware interface to process blocks, improving authenticated encryption throughput. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/26efd0b37714dd1f3557c6b6c32822fc99fe1d9b)
- Port the bit-sliced AES implementation to AArch64 assembly, adding a corresponding AES acceleration path for ARMv8. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/82551af5146271080edcd2e3077fea643e727379)
- Convert the AArch64 bit-sliced AES assembly source file to a Perl generation script, enabling code generation according to target assembler syntax. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a35c3a9f5be07c4c1bcabdd98ea56ff3ff20b645)
- Protect related calls on platforms lacking pthread_atfork, avoiding configuration or compilation depending on this optional interface's platform capabilities. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5979596247a73d1aec7310e4da0b6023ffd79623)
- Protect ASYNC_set_mem_functions configuration with a read-write lock, and complete async module initialization/cleanup flows, avoiding races when multiple threads concurrently set allocators. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/43ed2429566f27a2fb030316201c0c7af5a2b966)
- Check allocation results for BIO_new_file calls in applications, using unified error handling when file BIO creation fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/625b0990a069a18917341e2f0fbe36327b0883b7)
- When establishing client connections, decide whether to enable TCP_NODELAY based on the protocol of actual address candidates, avoiding using wrong flags when address resolution returns different transport protocols. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/54b6755702309487ea860e1cc3e60ccef4cf7878)
- Check the return value of OPENSSL_sk_new_null, preventing adding elements after stack allocation failure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5266af87379aecb0ae6036dee88c1a0b8083a432)
- Allow openssl enc to print only the key and IV and exit in non-streamable cipher modes, without requiring actual input data. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f4f397a5cb5b6eec3045047b9406a43fdd6d6602)
- Remove the standalone ARIA-CBC example binary target, avoiding building and distributing a demo program no longer needed. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/97896f744d9ee4f2e821e3383caac8e8c5f226cf)
- Always enable fixed-length padding when performing DH derivation with the X9.42 KDF, ensuring derived input uses the canonical fixed-length shared secret. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/01b18775676115945956f4de0eb0cafedaf027ab)
- Make the s390x internal CPU capability detection symbol hidden, avoiding it becoming an unnecessary dynamic library export interface. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/37816ef5757e458be9648481e56bf698ee3bfbb1)
- Use safe math functions to calculate buffer and object sizes, reducing overflow risks in size addition/division. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/330ff7e67d2ecc1c298fe7c4347c2109b4a979de)
- Reduce the block size of sparse array tree nodes, lowering memory usage under sparse key distributions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/514bd51a8cb901a7351ecdc45a680d6aba720b5a)
- TLS 1.3 clients reject duplicate HelloRetryRequest, avoiding the same handshake being repeatedly retried by the server. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d204a50b898435fbf937316d5693008cebf62eef)
- Disable the ChaCha20 PowerPC five-way interleaved path for shorter inputs, avoiding performance regressions on short buffers. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9c140a33663f319ad4000a6a985c3e14297c7389)
- openssl req and openssl x509 can now output RSA-PSS public key moduli, using the same modulus printing handling as regular RSA keys. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e4cdcb8bc44250aa4e0893dc4a7d64668f0fb949)
- Before KTLS takes over receive encryption, check for unprocessed TLS records in the buffer, avoiding handing data not yet parsed by OpenSSL directly to the kernel. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/85773128d0e80cd8dcc772a6931d385b8cf4acd1)
- Adjust TLS 1.3 record parsing to be compatible with KTLS receive offload, avoiding repeated OpenSSL decryption and verification of data already decrypted by the kernel. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a5fb9605329fb939abb536c1604d44a511741624)
- Allow setting a zero-length SM2 Dist ID as a context parameter, correctly handling empty identifiers. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2904d0a2ae0ec6ce23d5cec66ce8c7bdb005d4e5)
- When exporting keys in the EC Provider, allow exporting other parameters along with domain parameters, no longer rejecting legitimate parameter export requests when no key pair is selected. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e20af37d063514c27567c64e975fa5b3208707a9)
- Introduce an ASIMD-based SM4 acceleration implementation for ARM, covering vectorized processing paths for SM4 block, CBC, and related modes. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4908787f21f4f5fa24b721ed3ebbc4d3e93ef70c)
- Check the result of EVP_KDF_fetch, and do not continue creating or initializing derivation contexts when KDF fetching fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/02119faee397565525151eb2ce39c424d129d287)
- The FIPS Provider imports only named curve parameters, avoiding bringing explicit domain parameters into the FIPS module. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/638c3a28af45bd81a1c90b81efd8e10449eace1b)
- openssl prime validates numeric parameters before performing prime calculations, rejecting invalid input before entering the computation flow. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3b74fdcf1d5eb311e44b7eaa293df6caf54ae70b)
- Adjust locale initialization and cleanup in the FIPS Provider, keeping algorithm name/string handling behaving as expected under different locales. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c5e7de5dee9995cbc7247e37ccd0a6ddd1f7db56)
- macOS 10.7 and 10.8 no longer call unavailable or unreliable __atomic_* builtins, avoiding runtime issues on older systems. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d39de4792dbdb6ab5f78c79d52d0210b44584538)
- Use the .s extension on IA-64 assembler input files, conforming to the platform's assembly build rules. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6009997abd2594d5a7c0606176f404190922b74d)
- Clean up unused local variables in X509_print_ex, eliminating compiler warnings. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/36699c12d37c5bef000cbe3d9b4b2b89bee4e17e)
- SipHash_Final returns failure when the context is not initialized or the output length is zero, preventing reading uninitialized state and generating invalid tags. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/650b142c2e4c1d57868bdbbe1f7f4549ee77f8eb)
- Fix context copying in the SipHash Provider, making copies fully replicate algorithm state without recreating and overwriting initialization data. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/905fec4f4d6bb8a978476cbce0f293ffc683b5fd)
- Prefer GNU library initialization mechanisms over platform-specific ones, unifying related static initialization paths. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2dc3a4a4a57eca0d9bebd87234c7d682506188fc)
- Fix context copying in the Poly1305 Provider, making dup operations fully replicate algorithm state, key, and counter. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bbe909d00e9a593bd5954dfca4d3020467977565)
- AArch64 CPU detection assembly switches to .inst encoding for probe instructions, improving compatibility across different assemblers. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4d63eaf99b4c546fede9a732c2693d0b84641cf9)
- Initialize locale before converting EVP public key names to types, ensuring case-insensitive name lookups work correctly. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e560655f72dc27bcea973c6abfe99af75d313ad7)
- The HTTP client checks the expected Content-Type only when the response status is 200 OK, avoiding treating error status responses as normal content type failures. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e3477d3e5ccd971da3d8a90a7d5096b47372d288)
- Add an SVE-based ChaCha20 implementation for AArch64, using scalable vector instructions to accelerate encryption. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b1b2146ded9ce5a84c62f30c6c4a922b449f6c90)
- c_rehash no longer invokes openssl via shell command concatenation, reducing filename interpretation risks and avoiding shell escaping issues. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7c33270707b568c524a8ef125fe611a8872cb5e8)
- openssl dhparam prints a warning when using generated parameter mode while ignoring -in, avoiding users mistakenly thinking the input file participated in calculations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6d952291762246f6533e19ca413277390db4aae2)
- Allow s_client to configure both HTTP proxy and STARTTLS protocols simultaneously, no longer mistakenly setting proxy parameters as mutually exclusive with STARTTLS. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/802cacf34f2db9111becb4f0d3aa00460df13a19)
- When a parent-child library context or Provider points to itself, do not accumulate parent object references, avoiding self-references preventing resource release. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4da7663b02bf05542830e85db6f74cf90daf1f49)
- Update .gitignore to ignore new build or test artifacts, preventing them from entering version control status. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ac23650c1e53658227436aecc8de03a7ac3d1b9a)
- Update the GOST engine implementation and associated interfaces to adapt to current OpenSSL Provider/algorithm interface changes. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6d4f63469ed5a0f6e7ccde189e8a6d2bb1d2b26a)
- Use the OPENSSL_USE_IPV6 build switch to control IPv6 address code, rather than relying solely on whether the system defines AF_INET6. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/836bb0890dc4d139215824cc9ac35591361f8117)
- Initialize OpenSSL before case-insensitive algorithm name matching in EVP_PKEY_Q_keygen, ensuring locale and comparison functions are ready. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b807c2fbab2128cf3746bb2ebd51cbe3bb6914a9)
- Add assertions for reusing EVP digest algorithm contexts, confirming the reused digest implementation pointer is valid. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/221d65ba534d23a240ccadd0c2679b222aae35b1)
- Remove no longer needed internal error string release functions, simplifying error module initialization and cleanup logic. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1c8787d5e0b01bedfc3cbe5eab5b85290221d8c1)
- Align IV and temporary buffers in the Provider password context to meet the data alignment requirements of vector/assembly implementations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2787a709c984d3884e1726383c2f2afca428d795)
- Revert the extra OPENSSL_init_crypto call in int_ctx_new to avoid premature or repeated initialization. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/615525bd4d6bbc56601fbdc82e7ac20344f48872)
- Attempt locale initialization on each OPENSSL_strcasecmp call to ensure recovery after previous initialization failures. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f505be999f00232702aeb6918e4a1ffa0b9b588b)
- Add the --release parameter to git describe in the release script to determine version identifiers according to release version rules. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/22dbb176deef7d9a80f5c94f57a4b518ea935f50)
- Apply the AES-GCM eight-way unrolling optimization to Neoverse N2 and identify this CPU model to enable the corresponding acceleration path. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9224a407f9bb4c2af087ecf6e691c9027b594ec0)
- The x86 assembly generator generates endbr32 instructions based on the __CET__ compilation option, supporting indirect branch protection for CET-enabled 32 bits. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/272138795ffa63d5811e985f98cac94acf658dbe)
- HP-UX builds no longer include the non-existent sys/select.h, fixing header file errors on that system. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/737e849fd938a943cad207cde1c711e961f92294)
- Revert the IA-64 assembly file suffix adjustment, restoring the .s assembly convention required by the platform toolchain. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/debd9210061206be1b5b6e957033c1b85ac540e1)
- Generate preprocessed assembly files for ChaCha20 and Poly1305 for IA-64 builds, completing the assembly generation process for the corresponding architecture. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/db24ed54302ed458d41ad85093e4189db4b2120b)
- Include required generator scripts as dependencies when generating configuration templates, so that build files are correctly regenerated after template changes. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/42b4a8ab961e3d3be4abffe3aea458448b2e47b4)
- Call ossl_provider_free only after Provider reference count initialization, avoiding freeing a Provider that has not yet acquired a reference. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c4ed6f6f0ee700e0473def049659061dd52fd3fc)
- CONF_modules_unload returns failure when the module finish callback fails, no longer reporting incomplete module cleanup as success. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/697d0b5ba146c232f5b2aa87f4e847a5495c1735)
- Check the return value of ossl_parse_property, stopping method registration or query flow when property expression parsing fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4fa5ed5ce5c345eaeaec8b86eda265add467f941)
- Return export failure when EC Provider parameter construction fails, no longer falsely reporting success when OSSL_PARAM_BLD_to_param has not succeeded. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7d6aad832b4cebb181c53ab80a3f61dc8549be08)
- Add assertions to confirm that the entry in the property definition cache is the first definition of the algorithm, promptly detecting corrupted cache state. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5f4b3db624a83b812f23412e698ffd9c4284f87a)
- Property string parsing checks the return value of ossl_property_value, stopping and reporting errors when property value parsing fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f91568eb50e847d0db2441fd9b9c5ffc8c4fe934)
- Check the result of BIO_new and always free the temporary private key when downgrade copying EVP_PKEY fails, fixing resource leaks and null pointer usage. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d8732803c493cba7a863c5c16da62ee9d611c5ca)
- The default Provider falls back to the general multi-prime key generation implementation when the RSA exponent is less than 65537, avoiding the new key generation path not supporting that exponent. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/27c1cfd7653b7204af3301f93ccd2a3decfc309b)
- When the DH private key does not provide the q parameter, determine the valid range based on the p parameter, no longer directly judging the private key as invalid. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0615cedecda7ed18300db48b0bb56cec6d3527bd)
- Fix documentation comments to clarify when the HPNS random number implementation uses EGD and the role of rand_egd. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/93ed4b5fb40a8ece9d9c67041c4187d63dbfbd51)
- Expose the SSL internal time acquisition function to other SSL submodules, unifying the use of replaceable time sources. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d6bfdf6789f65b1b503f0cdd56010705f7c632d0)
- Improve diagnostic information when setting supported groups fails, indicating invalid or unavailable group names and the reason for failure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ce8822b7e5f4fdf836677faee336a5cf996d4363)
- Change strncpy for writing attribute strings into buffers to memcpy with known length, avoiding unnecessary padding and boundary misuse. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5ad3e76c23576b2e216463bfe43d005a3e09defc)
- Make the decoded-from-explicit flag pass through with explicit EC parameters during key import/export, preserving parameter source information. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/95a6fbdf0d112582b9ad56f8d42ec92b1ec4787d)
- Strengthen the DER Writer's check for invalid RSA-PSS saltlen values, preventing encoding of invalid parameters. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/08f876d0dea184b071a5aded4c55317e5a63c80e)
- Fix handling when the return value of multi-prime RSA key pair validation is non-boolean, so callers no longer misinterpret any non-zero value as success. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/518f1ee81d5a6910365ef404888d0e119a87fd81)
- Use OPENSSL_zalloc to allocate EVP_PBE_CTL table entries, ensuring that uninitialized fields of new control entries default to zero. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3211266aa23253ce8af2b98c4fd94a12a4afa7e4)
- Return NULL and preserve error state when KDF context memory allocation fails, fixing invalid contexts caused by missing return in constructors. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7260709e9ef155c8b3fccaa32e8ba496a3059905)
- Improve the format of ITAV information printed by the cmp command, correctly presenting information type and value. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d9650648821aadabf2d9f3de321f344230b13a4a)
- The Windows export definition file uses the DLL product name as the base name, ensuring correct matching of export symbol files for different Provider DLLs. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5cc9ab5cf51137daf6d2d57718f56316dcb62744)
- Use pragma comment(lib) only under MSVC, avoiding other compilers treating MSVC link directives as syntax errors. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/695cb63c744bab090144a86949b68324ee3094d6)
- Add comments for CMP HTTP keep-alive, explaining client and server behavior during connection reuse. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/93d9d6097685dc29e654db15c091c550aef16d5b)
- Reset the output length before each EVP_PKEY_derive call, avoiding the previous derived length value affecting the result. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ab8d56d05b773e499c86be874fd3f11f5950213c)
- Adjust code style in X509 verification implementation, unifying conditional branches and error handling formats. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c633b973f669948a22bb5e3e3085f54d8d00a90a)
- Adjust code style in X509 purpose-related implementation without changing certificate purpose determination rules. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a2db4e6cd6478c3ae633d9919d0a88f1eb5678f7)
- Check whether EVP_MD is NULL when SSL handles certificate-related paths, avoiding null pointer dereference when the digest is unavailable. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b740012f77aed97cb4b3cd8a4f1fb2f668542795)
- Organize conditional branches and error handling formats in the Base64 BIO implementation to improve code readability. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f95fec293800b47e5eff0f08daefcf1397a24423)
- Fix the issue pointed out by Coverity where a null pointer could still be dereferenced after a null check. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f80910390cb882f346fe59c9803fc914b9c367c2)
- Make EVP_PKEY_is_a series algorithm type check functions accept NULL and safely return not matching on empty input. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ee8db8c5fb5b091f48d29914126d35a7e29cdcf2)
- Check function return values pointed out by Coverity, and do not continue subsequent object operations on failure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/358103b4a651ab3f392f088d86cd30469dccce2e)
- Fix a null pointer dereference reported by Coverity, ensuring the pointer is still valid before reading object fields. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f913c3cd7e22eecbcc8f84b72c645081fa37fdf4)
- Reserve method storage locations before constructing Provider methods, avoiding cache conflicts when multiple algorithm instances register concurrently. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e1eafe8c87612a94552e9ad5df56c489cb6f0ff2)
- Use the rev8 byte reversal instruction on the RISC-V Zbkb extension, consistent with the existing Zbb path. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/48b6776678d794406c625dcb5767102b73081962)
- Use a separate function to centrally obtain GCM operation function pointers, unifying implementation selection across platforms. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/92c9086e5c2b63606cd28a7f13f09b9ff35a0de3)
- Fix the check-after-dereference issue reported by Coverity, avoiding null pointer access caused by state changes after pointer validation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/93429fc0ce9468242a463ff5878cd53b97e7f13f)
- Remove unnecessary quotes around $(CC) in the VC-common target, fixing Windows Visual C build command parsing. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9c90a0737b809048b56dfaa9e64b0a35d6609271)
- CMS_final checks and propagates the result of CMS_dataFinal, avoiding reporting CMS generation success when content digest/signature finalization fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b03756130dadb3732b460a6efd930f1b226acdad)
- Check the return value of SMIME_crlf_copy, stopping CMS/SMIME content processing when newline conversion fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/67c0460b89cc1b0644a1a59af78284dfd8d720af)
- Reject negative IV lengths, avoiding invalid cipher parameters entering cache or buffer length calculations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/83ab43da0c9f67c5069605552b1332ca5fadecf1)
- Fix the null check order issue pointed out by Coverity, verifying non-null before dereferencing the object. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/76ad9ae6fa459af0bd804c01d3d681ec02cddb4b)
- Adjust conditional compilation macros to recognize the AIX xlclang frontend, ensuring the compiler handles platform implementations in the correct branch. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/df1e33bc8ae67573a3f3488eff82e02fc0310203)
- The s390 Provider EC signature implementation explicitly sets the signature length, avoiding EVP calls being unable to know the actual length of the generated signature. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bbedc052973b1c2fab7d7fb891d02aea393ff579)
- Adjust the property method cache refresh strategy to avoid overly deterministic reuse of stale query results under specific execution orders. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/56d4ff6cd7fc200943197dff65146a8864b7df98)
- Unify the format of the X509_VERIFY_PARAM default table for easy viewing and maintenance of default values for each parameter. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1a68a3e42142a2c188f4b69c7337438c89502143)
- Use OSSL_TIME to update TLS session timeout calculations, unifying time values and correctly handling time differences. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/340fe504e42e3e4b6399caff165097cedc994c5e)
- Declare settings and options parameters in recordmethod.h as const, indicating that method implementations must not modify caller configuration. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0c974fc754e4b0525819ca9f6c3e124141b690ad)
- Reject SSL2_VERSION in SSLv3 record headers, avoiding treating non-protocol old version markers as valid SSLv3 records. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/014baa8a6dec5956416baad5af4ddda13bf74341)
- The record layer uses independent message callbacks, avoiding direct dependence on the SSL object's handshake/record callback fields. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3c7b9ef9c56a8066e0e6f4c61bc2ac2648bb1e42)
- Further split TLS connection state into the SSL_CONNECTION object, reducing dependence of handshake and record code on external SSL handle layout. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7a15ed64fa5387dfbf1db391b84ddc7b1bf25571)
- Check the return value of SMIME_crlf_copy, stopping CMS/SMIME content generation when newline normalization fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3a7a539ec542b239efd375f63da070a5230f4ae0)
- Fix encryption/decryption regressions caused by GCM refactoring, restoring correct handling of mode initialization and updates. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/51ccad3f40e5f000da8364b1bb4bddd41657c96e)
- Fix null pointer crash in PEM_write when the output object is empty or encoding fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/db1a505ced696b104b03a072079e663cceecf692)
- Implement AES-GCM-SIV as defined in RFC 8452, providing key derivation, synthetic IV, and authenticated encryption/decryption functionality. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b0a9042e0f1e6bc18d1d945771903d0132766909)
- Fix the return value source of EVP_CIPHER_CTX_get_iv_length, ensuring it reports the current IV length of the context. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4566dae7236b5c90364e963fd02b2ee533e0d712)
- Set the actual signature length in s390 Provider EC signatures, satisfying the output length convention of EVP signature calls. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/efc84eacb7a500306c7cb55e4e2d707dfd9d1ac1)
- When the old 32 VC++ does not support the InterlockedOr64 intrinsic, switch to a manual lock for compatibility with Windows XP targets. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b85ebc4b279ff0abe81c3a64eafc4f3c6c00605e)
- Define OSSL_TIME as a structure to uniformly represent time values and reserve fields for cross-platform clock conversion. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e8a557dc3c1ed16faff4aeb39268f8f5a3f8b81d)
- Fix address parsing out-of-bounds/invalid access in BIO_ADDR found under AddressSanitizer. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/709d4be78f64a8ba0707fb5682b90039e848dad4)
- Add RISC-V Zbb/Zbkb rotation assembly for DES, using bit-manipulation extensions to accelerate round functions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6a9255054b345026bc847ddad72f2da93f30ce4c)
- dhparam supports Provider property query parameters to specify attributes for selecting the DH parameter generation implementation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b134300a342476398c11c19af602d7b2aa6b7f8a)
- dgst signing operations support Provider property queries, obtaining digest/signature algorithms by attributes. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c63e8637fd79c826b3c438cf99cf7f1b293e8318)
- genrsa supports Provider property queries, selecting the RSA key generation Provider according to configuration. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c92c3dfb99485eb2cfb840e92bd0ece8cdd72d0c)
- Make CRYPTO_mem_debug_push and CRYPTO_mem_debug_pop return 0 on success, conforming to the interface return convention. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0e55c3ab8d702ffc897c9beb51d19b14b7896182)
- Revert the previous rollback of fixed-length PPC Montgomery multiplication, re-enabling the optimized implementation after fixing the issue. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d040a1b9a028e89f0a33b36fb99f0151d2fdd4c3)
- Fix the skip condition for the multiblock test to avoid incorrectly skipping when the test prerequisites are met. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/eb7a5cc3454174094c0c09f1d00aec464ce0f786)
- UBSan builds always add -DPEDANTIC to enable stricter compilation diagnostics for detecting undefined behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7b7ad9e578470fe2b20db230638cfc20e3acf252)
- Release temporary big numbers allocated in BN_rand_range during random range calculation, fixing a memory leak. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/35bcac131ce5605c504d48a077f33f69660b660c)
- Add a code signing certificate verification purpose for verifying certificate chains according to code signing policy. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/293ab820812b3979161c5f018c2e753bcd3b11a4)
- Migrate the record reading logic of ssl3_read_n into the new record layer, unifying data reading and buffering. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2093428834151ea4788aa773b5aa2d35e0bbc90a)
- Migrate the record layer encryption/decryption implementations for TLS 1.0, 1.1, and 1.2 to the new record layer. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/56233ba8574c01b3912cf662335fedaabc7faec2)
- Migrate SSLv3 reading code to the new read record layer interface. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3f42f41ad19c631287386fd8d58f9e02466c5e3f)
- Fix negative return value handling reported by Coverity, avoiding using failure status as a valid length or index. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1d1537067304b8c8d87b2df393363b40370ad640)
- Set the modified flag after modifying X509_REQ_INFO data to ensure re-encoding of changed fields when serializing the request. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/39d356e084f6a4e48decf0644961255e6777b071)
- Set the object modified flag after successful ASN1_item_sign or ASN1_item_sign_ctx to ensure the encoded object reflects the new signature data. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9249a34b076df9a9d55ab74ab465d336980cae6a)
- Propose the OSSL_CRYPTO_ALLOC attribute marking scheme to describe memory allocation function behavior to the compiler. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e1035957eba1e6ebdefd0e18dcbad5cbfa7a969a)
- Use __STDC_VERSION__ to determine the C standard version instead of relying on the compiler-specific __STRICT_ANSI__ macro. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b1104a3a2dd4351af85cf48f677691a414ffc3a2)
- Withdraw most build changes that generate crypto objects only on demand, restoring reliable build dependency generation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/800869b84e027a63c1dd0c77cdb95407edf45734)
- Modify libssl to use OSSL_TIME, unifying the data type for TLS timeout and session time calculations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f0131dc04a39afcb1629f5bec2814ef3a4925bbf)
- Restore EVP_PKEY_eq compatibility with the 3.0.0 FIPS Provider, correctly comparing public key objects it provides. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c342004e07fd2c03a672f79353d13554fe0ffdaf)
- When the TLS 1.3 ticket key callback returns 0, do not send NewSessionTicket, respecting the callback's rejection of ticket generation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3e93c5fe1eab677500448e18e4274b26e4b246ae)
- Use an enumeration type to represent the result of Provider method constructors, clearly indicating success, failure, and skip states. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/67ec6d2b747810db609330003dcf08c8c584105b)
- Uniformly read input files in binary format to avoid PEM/text auto-detection altering original input bytes. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4689fe1bfd390db591ad5ab5479f06b52ac6f337)
- Check whether sk_SSL_CIPHER_value returns a non-null object to prevent crashes from null cipher suite entries. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/630d31219b343d2654ab03d2e2c7884e764936ab)
- Correctly identify and handle retransmitted ClientHello to avoid QUIC/TLS handshakes entering an incorrect state due to duplicate initial messages. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/81926c91567cd5d11eec38b9980438f45b276d72)
- Document that the maximum return value of BIO_ctrl_pending is SIZE_MAX, clarifying its size_t length upper bound. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c6be0aa8ac3c172ad998ce33f392143312bfe760)
- Create write-side record layer methods and objects, and have the SSL write path execute through this object. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2b71b042202d11854801682d48ccf4e4e34cd5cf)
- The write-side record layer uses the record layer's own msg_callback, no longer relying on the message callback field in the SSL object. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/310590139e45116d86627dcc85e83f2e3fcbb6b4)
- Migrate the write record BIO reference from the wbio field of SSL_CONNECTION to the record layer bio field. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b5cf81f7c9775d2502730ba126893ce8af4db90e)
- Fix return values of write record layer methods so success, retry, and failure states are accurately conveyed according to calling conventions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/320145d5b3a11492427fe1cab9ca4de52402c72d)
- Fix boundary conditions for negative return values when the record layer handles length queries, not continuing to use negative numbers for record length calculations on failure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/02719d5c4c1e64350b4dddb17e703864809e130a)
- Add initial design for QUIC congestion control API, defining congestion control state, feedback, and algorithm extension interfaces. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c6d5f343336532a7aba4368099b0631a457194a6)
- Rename multi in the OCSP command to n_responders, clarifying it represents the number of parallel OCSP responders. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bafe524b5ce425105ac321f9fffa23e2d5b06845)
- Set the object modified flag after successful ASN.1 signing, ensuring signature state changes are recognized by subsequent encoding. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2f7e61b8b21ed472a3667b8922843851f94a3d93)
- Add NULL parameter checks for X509 and X509_REQ operations, and unify code style and error returns. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/31fbf119f396cf67e808cb95e28302dbe45174cb)
- Revert most Makefile changes that generate crypto objects on demand, restoring stable dependency generation rules. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/29d82bd95b6ef3428c7e776221612a083994219d)
- Sync the VMS openssl.cnf example with the latest configuration changes, updating the generated configuration file. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/651255941c49a5089dfc011f2abd636433da8b82)
- Add helper functions for OSSL_TIME such as time comparison and addition, and complete the time API documentation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8ae74c5bc091e7388c082f090c1fde992c31320f)
- Unify session timeout and certificate time calculations in libssl to use OSSL_TIME. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cffb65f2ff85f19418ed121275901674824e52ca)
- Fix the logic in QUIC QRL when handling Handshake packets, ensuring handshake frames are correctly parsed and state is advanced. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/dfb4efe070804485392354c3062f10a8672d3e2d)
- Fix the OSSL_TIME platform fallback type definition in e_os.h to avoid compilation failures on platforms without native clocks. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fda21497fcde0e1036986921052a05dd430f9918)
- Parenthesize signal arguments when passing to kill, ensuring the wrapper passes the complete argument list when resending signals to child processes. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bd07cc1c7e3ca38689e59868b5945dc223235a49)
- Make FIPS tests select expected values based on Provider version, compatible with different versions of the FIPS Provider. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d250e8563fa400fd3d9b93cff609c7503149b908)
- Add trace_api test to verify trace channel setup, query, and output behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c62a9cd720eccdbb388890ee4a36801d01315be4)
- Add direct error reporting for X509 verification parameter handling to facilitate locating default value parsing failures. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f2ec24c9e7c3df55fba97336594a5e815c342b01)
- Add AES key wrap EVP example demonstrating wrapping a key into ciphertext and its unwrapping process. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9167a47f78159b0578bc032401ab1d66e14eecdb)
- Remove the FIPS build skip condition for SM2 tests, running available tests in FIPS configurations as well. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1ec0acf264652bd981e95842723e5414d634cd93)
- Add a cache scenario in the QUIC overview where multiple connection IDs map to the same connection. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8b5424eae5577809264e73a229fcc4c384611fae)
- Move record version calculation out of the record layer, with protocol methods setting the record version based on handshake negotiation results. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/435d88d70813825533c8789faa71c6287e0d43c9)
- Migrate the empty fragment policy state need_empty_fragments into the record layer, which is responsible for sending the policy. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/91fe8ff02a323eddb0404f975d5c9a03c024593b)
- Remove the record layer's dependency on the SSL_CONNECTION_TREAT_AS_TLS13 macro, reducing coupling of the record layer to handshake state. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7ca61d63e99726ef7874b88b96892dae75f51156)
- Migrate record padding adjustment entirely to the record layer, so that layer uniformly applies application-configured padding. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/aca70ca81c6fcf38554aa95a3a2c75e1eeb1a085)
- Migrate the block_padding support capability check entirely into the record layer, ensuring padding policy is determined by the record layer. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b6f7519bc4b645809b3dcf97478fabbb3037f3e2)
- Check whether the negotiated key share curve is allowed for the current protocol version, rejecting key exchange groups incompatible with the version. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/247b8e52527ed4facd9ff07cdef0df819193c0c3)
- Fix negative loop bounds reported by Coverity, avoiding erroneous iteration when encountering invalid counts. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9ab57f29c78d8d69b6ba9c579521594d7170ca44)
- Extract the dtls_write_records function, centrally responsible for DTLS record construction and sending. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/88bf978eb1766bec720c198deabe8d0a5de157bb)
- DTLS writing uses the record layer's own buffer, removing dependence on the SSL old record layer buffer. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bf04cbfafe77ddc67f1a9c06ffb044f9bf44057c)
- Fix return values of dtls_write_records so write success, retry, and error states conform to record layer conventions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4cdd198ec204a4c2ec6b3ec728ebcc8af04abc86)
- DTLS also uses the common tls_write_records entry, reducing duplication between TLS and DTLS write flows. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/602ee1f672a41f984e8923ad7430ca51ca42abde)
- Use WPACKET to construct record content in the DTLS write record flow, unifying length encoding and buffer management. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/248a9bf21ad5a61d911765964e2758e0da3c554c)
- Make DTLS writes call standard record layer methods, removing temporary compatibility implementations and connecting to the shared record layer interface. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b9e37f8f573de1951655f6d8684f2f65ffc6905b)
- The maximum record overhead is calculated by the record layer, ensuring that DTLS fragmentation and buffer sizes include the correct header, MAC, and padding lengths. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4f428e86d8bc68f95446eef96129c6ad98b57104)
- Merge TLS/DTLS sequence number increment logic to avoid inconsistent handling of epoch and counter between the two implementations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bed07b187506ded20ef39dcbed56dc323ae44ff4)
- Upgrade pyca/cryptography test dependency to 38.0.2, adapting to its API and test updates. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/efd59f7a37bf1f9034b62b67f730c25dff0e8d8e)
- Use BIO_free_all when releasing bio_err to correctly clean up chains that may consist of multiple BIOs. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a73bdc24e14760413a65d478d7c88356b4b95bb5)
- Return NULL immediately when BIO_new_fp fails, and do not continue constructing filter chains that depend on that BIO. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fb03e6145961005a6db011d2f36660d2eed734e2)
- Check the end of the rule string when parsing TLS cipher rule strings to avoid reading beyond the rule_str buffer. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/428511ca66670e169a0e1b12e7540714b0be4cf8)
- Restore the allocation and release behavior of SSL_alloc_buffers and SSL_free_buffers for SSL connection buffers. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7eb39ecb299db3eade11946f9385f5dee1d458d3)
- Adjust the random number seed collection method on OpenVMS to reduce dependence on specific operating system versions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7056dc9c50baa4af5152c625c4735806d51c67cd)
- Ensure the PVK KDF implementation can use SIZE_MAX, fixing compilation issues on platforms that do not define this macro. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8df9f34384cf1a9b8bc8748ea594b07fb5525899)
- Improve the DSO_MALLOC macro definition on OpenVMS so that it works correctly with the OpenVMS compiler and memory interface. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/89d72311327735ef15c804d2adea84a0fb1bfa0a)
- Update FIPS 140-3 self-test known-answer test vectors and test types to match the new standard's self-test classification. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a11064c83b58f9e1b3741704a11cfec2d91aac0e)
- Apply the SM4 optimized implementation to Kunpeng-920, enabling the acceleration path on this ARM server processor. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/88c53cf17d21b06b05043af49af3498665357a6f)
- Release the DRBG of the global default context before destroying engines, avoiding crashes due to destructor order when the random Provider depends on engines. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a88e97fcace01ecf557b207f04328a72df5110df)
- Keep the input/output buffers required for pipeline encryption/decryption valid until the end of the EVP_Cipher call, avoiding references to invalid memory in asynchronous or pipelined processing. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3961af375e1522a3d37d2af8628bff43103ab4f5)
- Reorganize assembly generation rules to unify assembler inputs, generated files, and build dependency handling across architectures. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b8d3cf0954737a9665e3b2bff25bc689a5114010)
- IA-64 SHA-512 assembly scripts ignore case when determining assembly file names, compatible with different file system naming conventions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/119b7b5f2ad7efcf273f395e7633747f56ff3f95)
- Assert that the total record size does not exceed the configured MTU before writing DTLS records, preventing oversized datagrams. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/351ad225b3758f96a5875eb11ac3acda006a1c00)
- For openssl dgst calls in the form of built-in hash names, automatically infer the digest algorithm name based on argv[0]. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1e5780dbc79dab14c1ec1584313755fc2fd2cf55)
- Use a unified encryption growth limit macro to ensure the TLS/DTLS write paths use the same record expansion limits. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ecacbc5e3c48901417e8e05bbf1d29df78610607)
- Put 3DES back into the FIPS Provider, provided as a non-approved algorithm and marked with FIPS properties, maintaining compatibility. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a0ea8ac134e8f503876f19bdc04da69e8862f3a7)
- DH parameter generation uses the DH object's libctx, ensuring execution in the caller-specified library context and Provider properties. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/990d280da95d3c955b86f38b01f5b95ea88d42bb)
- Explicitly mark the cmac-aes256 example as AES-256-CBC CMAC, explaining the block cipher mode used for CMAC. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9270f67059e0291a2ef73acfba5a4ac54f732ef9)
- Cast socklen_t to size_t in socket length assertions to avoid signed/unsigned comparison warnings. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/71faab72b8b49819d8bcf065b039d1d840e8b76c)
- Define platform thread functions in threads_lib.c only on Unix platforms, avoiding linking to non-existent native thread symbols on other systems. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/523e54c15cfadb8c19f6a181b9a69aabd9f58453)
- DJGPP builds no longer check whether timeval is negative, adapting to the platform's timeval representation and time conversion method. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d8bcd64170e8b6fb66da293a95ff21b25d1a357e)
- Use OSSL_PARAM_construct_uint32 to set max_early_data, ensuring the parameter type width matches the protocol field. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1e065a15119520e13a2d68d003c4c06869208a32)
- Connect QUIC_RSTREAM to the RX depacketizer so that decoded STREAM frames enter the corresponding receive stream. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a17c713a7ad394b08646bbb0f0ba1a16e1cc8799)
- Resolve the TODO about alert dispatch in ssl3_dispatch_alert, completing upper-layer alert handling directly from the record layer result. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/732435026b0141063084fb68c076bc1c9fd9bee8)
- Pass the selection parameter of key export all the way to the Provider export function, ensuring only the requested key parts are exported. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/98642df4ba886818900ab7e6b23703544e6addd4)
- Change uint_set from a standalone array implementation to a generic list type, reusing list operations and simplifying set maintenance. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c5ca718003e69ea0ef98392ce0abd4b6bfedeac8)
- QUIC send record layer uses list.h to manage pending objects, unifying linked list insertion and deletion implementations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e32fc5ad0ea1a2d69f12d9208f2de489f7ee9737)
- QUIC ACK manager uses list.h to manage acknowledgment and loss state objects, reducing custom linked list code. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/dead13551c6661d34af3e5ddf1bc53c9efdb5647)
- QUIC ACK and send queues use the generic list.h linked list interface, unifying node allocation, insertion, and deletion. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3fb172ef0a635c2e705d3d1cb58624cfc6afd502)
- Define SIZE_MAX on platforms that use OSSL_SSIZE_MAX, fixing compilation failures when relying on this boundary constant. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1a298b00112e50718acc1fdd40b9bce482112cbf)
- DH_check and DH_check_params use the libctx associated with the DH object to perform prime checks, following the caller's Provider context. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7c639f0b8e97b8290b9f935e83d5e948614c5bf7)
- Improve speed's parsing of piped input, handling chunked arrival and end-of-input states to avoid swallowing or misreading parameters. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/18af4d154cc563a5b02409215a576276caece0f4)
- Disable atomic reference counting in builds without threads, avoiding reliance on atomic implementations on platforms that do not support threads. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b484c6268ce38ccbc1bf5ee95bbd36f76fba994f)
- PEM_read_bio_PrivateKey_ex allows key input where the public key is optional, fixing the old code that forced reading a public key. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/adb408dc791e83f59f3a86bd90d8e804c814ac30)
- Update the pyca-cryptography submodule to 38.0.4 to synchronize tests and compatibility fixes. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9fba7d261ac970e43e80dc41b11f2655a1b530b0)
- No longer modify the thread's cancel state/type, avoiding library functions overriding the caller's thread cancellation policy. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/14c593e0034ddb9ca68f4a8e06b251afa127c6d0)
- ossl_kdf_ctx_create checks whether the obtained KDF is NULL, and no longer dereferences a NULL Provider method when the algorithm is unavailable. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0510f79265bf18fea5f13c1391a12397339db8b7)
- Supplement the implementation and documentation content missing due to the removal of CMP TODO comments, ensuring related CMP functionality still has corresponding handling. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/084d3afd26cc20b41241b70b6c709b76d2a334a5)
- Unbuffer before reading passwords from standard input, ensuring interactive input is not delayed or lost due to stdin buffering. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/efec0f4611ee854f2b0b3da0c135e839bf8e7d04)
- CMS and PKCS#7 RSA decryption no longer use the old Bleichenbacher workaround, relying on the implicit rejection protection at the RSA layer. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/056dade341d2589975a3aae71f81c8d7061583c7)
- AIX builds no longer compile the ChaCha20 assembly implementation that is only applicable to Power10, avoiding assembler not supporting related instructions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/abfc152126616d6f7c1cb1b9cbe8def9f18a1a96)
- AIX builds no longer compile Power10-specific AES-GCM assembly, avoiding assembly syntax failures on that platform. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/50d9b2b5f1236f185b2e360b6f4d640e75ddb07f)
- Fix issues reported by Coverity, adding return value or object lifetime checks. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5e42118de2c8001b3b5fa0cae138950d5b2e1cf1)
- Update FIPS module build instructions, adding configuration, checksum, and installation steps. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e8241fb6fe4e73bb337c9068e5a2421948ee40ad)
- Solaris builds no longer explicitly link libpthread, using thread symbols provided by the platform libc for compatibility with modern Solaris. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f5b06306b7727f830407c639f8a7bf1698f8aaa5)
- EC keymgmt checks the return value of BN_CTX_get, exiting immediately if temporary big number allocation fails, without continuing curve parameter calculations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/467b0492c1e597857b30b91ed72605387aa9825b)
- Clear temporary buffers that store private key derivation information during deterministic DSA nonce generation, preventing residual nonce intermediate values. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/177d433bda2ffd287d676bc53b549b6c246973e6)
- OSSL_PARAM_set_BN writes at least one data byte for zero values, ensuring zero BIGNUM can be correctly written to parameters. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c2ae89148343750e420b72ef1b709ebbc16e47b8)
- OSSL_PARAM_BLD allocates at least one byte for BIGNUM, avoiding zero-length parameters for zero-valued objects. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c455f87aebf245814ba58d6a398b45ca4e80d1d7)
- Split macro definition checks from usage conditions to avoid referencing undefined macros in the same conditional expression. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a509b97d2c4efd96e231913d49544ac7cb36b51b)
- Mark EdDSA as a non-approved algorithm in the FIPS Provider, avoiding FIPS properties incorrectly indicating it as approved. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9fa553247874728cee8ca0ece9aaed476eb0f303)
- QUIC receive stream no longer sends MAX_STREAM_DATA after receiving the final size, avoiding increasing the receive window for an already ended stream. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c4abf9ebb023248046604588692f50a9eee5d6b8)
- QUIC TX no longer manages BIO reference counts; the underlying connection/network layer is responsible for BIO ownership. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cdd3f7323613aaaf316b0b2d3a7700fbc602c8ef)
- QUIC_RSTREAM_free allows NULL to be passed, facilitating safe calls in unified cleanup paths. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/21247795c0c981299efd02bd1dc0034e4c008f67)
- QUIC record layer allows reinstallation of keys for the Initial encryption level, supporting continued sending and receiving after retry or rebuilding the Initial state on the same connection. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b2c94b93994bc079ed3aa7f700adc7782bd0bb64)
- Fix the actual behavior of QUIC TXP discarding encryption levels to match the interface documentation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/df038685644eb1bc4618f678b52fc22f0101235f)
- QUIC null handshake layer allows setting transport parameters later, facilitating prototype flows to fill in negotiated data after connection creation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/462d81dd73480fe349594d2b65698ddc84583d32)
- Include errno.h in sockets.h on necessary platforms to ensure error codes like EINTR are defined. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/16b220dde3ffa0cfaf71dc3bd37a35187f8099fb)
- Implement QUIC_CHANNEL, combining the TLS channel, send/receive record layers, and connection-level state into a single connection processing object. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f538b42155283879d1a55708292105437a96700d)
- Complete integration of QUIC_CHANNEL with the receive depacketizer, dispatching decoded frames to the corresponding connection by channel. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3a37c9235de465fe8d557b32f0178bfad0c09908)
- Add QUIC front-end I/O API, providing design for QUIC read/write, tick, and close interfaces on SSL connection objects. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/22d53c88838d5899383af6955ae62ca4094308c3)
- Integrate the QUIC front-end API with the underlying QUIC connection operations, enabling application calls to enter the underlying connection operations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/03bacce81e3d3b3caba6d3c30edb548d0f5bc364)
- Pause correctly when delivering packets after the TLS secret is delivered, avoiding duplicate or missing data packets during recovery. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4e64437a5fdf5c8ff1b5c2cede6c358a19a28e85)
- Ensure the QUIC front-end API holds a BIO reference and releases it when the connection is destroyed, fixing the BIO lifecycle error. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d1ac77b1a50b070aa55384f4c5eff3df71adb2c7)
- Initialize local variables in QUIC TXP to fix potential reads of unassigned states. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/24c1be5cff94d6d92d78a11c6584deb7047b4ab6)
- Only reprocess pending received data after the RX secret is set, preventing QUIC_CHANNEL from repeatedly parsing without decryption keys. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/92282a17c9959bc61e012e93517320df1ec8ace8)
- Pump the demux at most once per reactor tick, preventing a single connection from repeatedly consuming input or starving other connections in the same event processing round. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3bf4dc8c2106982d4ae6ada0650383e60f96d6e6)
- Maintain QUIC receive and send encryption level states separately, allowing read and write key transitions to proceed independently. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/45ecfc9b52b2d1c9a810cefafe0e8bdd403b6b66)
- Fix the implementation of SSL_tick and SSL_get_tick_timeout to correctly report the next scheduled processing deadline and tick result. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fbe2573d3b54dcaab1eab3401f2948a0f01ee49a)
- Correct erroneous descriptions in QUIC_CHANNEL comments regarding secrets, record layer, and input processing. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9bbc5b54b0f0c64d21eaea35ee3f9722aa77a56e)
- Adjust QUIC handshake advancement logic to correctly handle states where the TLS handshake is not ready and application read/write calls. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ca41f6b7e974e7a8e814705cbf693bd9ea3a10cc)
- Use ossl_assert to check QUIC internal invariants, detecting state corruption earlier in debug builds. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/79534440c5ff2ab0a6233457531e903fbe2968b7)
- Fix the QUIC front-end API's condition for SSL_read completion, reporting read completion only when required application data is received or an explicit state is reached. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3f0c310b80626f6286022f30a7a280b3306587ca)
- The pkey command implicitly performs public key checks when -pubin is specified, rejecting invalid public key input. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3b1c0c8f3cd66e80f81a9b7c9810bdada39363f2)
- bin2bn returns a BIGNUM with value zero directly for zero-length input, avoiding accessing the data pointer on empty input. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1b24b5a1b43c2af0a6c1cb2d196f5132ee723488)
- Expand the TLS write buffer size to reserve space for the Explicit IV, preventing IV writes from exceeding the buffer in modes like CBC. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/626618a09d057db6eee34c3fdd81525b9e3cbc68)
- Restore X25519 and X448 as FIPS-approved algorithms, updating the key exchange algorithm flags in the FIPS Provider. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8948b5749410084ed1dfabf17a90df65efcf0f82)
- Create SSL objects for QUIC-TLS, enabling QUIC channels to reuse TLS handshake and key derivation logic. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a7f41885b368c7fb63e52aadaa0a5b5bd239b876)
- Replace the QUIC prototype null handshake layer with a real TLS handshake layer, starting the standard TLS 1.3 handshake required by QUIC. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2723d705b55bd0c3f1806ff42b9eed379cfee4c0)
- Do not compile sparse_array.o into libssl in no-shared builds, fixing duplicate symbols or object dependencies in static linking. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/aea9b0e4b6c35c7f90b2e5a3475084500488775d)
- Add AES instruction-based SM4 optimization for ARMv8, using vector AES instructions to accelerate SM4 round transformations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c007203b94b6921ebc8103cb7ae51af554c86afe)
- Apply key/certificate/CRL loading helper functions to improve diagnostics, clearly indicating when the required object type is not found. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fedab100a4b8f4c3b81de632f29c159fb46ac3f2)
- Do not dereference the data union member of PKCS7 objects when content is not set, avoiding crashes on empty content objects. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/80253dbdc92bec584f4a9866b43f8674156d838a)
- The Decoder rejects DSA public keys lacking parameters when decoding, ensuring output DSA public keys have necessary domain parameters. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/604247bf75571c1c3fb6a1723346c61acd957221)
- Check the return value of BIO_set_md in PKCS#7 signature and verification chains, stopping operations when digest BIO configuration fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6eebe6c0238178356114a96a7858f36b24172847)
- Restore EdDSA as an approved algorithm in the FIPS Provider, updating Provider registration and related FIPS status. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/09627a8ceb69e19d2855b36228f44a3660af177a)
- Declare DSA types internally in no-deprecated builds, keeping DSA internal code compilable when deprecated APIs are disabled. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/03fd2dcb3431898e54e24b3021e9106257e0fdf0)
- Arm NEON assembly no longer uses LDR pseudo-instructions that Clang cannot handle correctly for loading constants, using compatible constant loading forms instead. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a97ca33f83cc6201fe11d6c76a035c05dbaaf5bc)
- Supplement HPE NonStop documentation on static/dynamic linking restrictions, clarifying supported library linking methods. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7b2625274f5d5ec90aee522ec4e4f3aa08fa5b70)
- Apply AES-GCM eight-way unrolling and EOR3 optimization to Neoverse V2, enabling its dedicated ARM instruction path. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/513e103f14e8473fb6810aa216ab3fb7b724ca5d)
- Use sparse_array.c only in shared libssl, avoiding duplicate inclusion of internal objects in static library targets. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cd870db16348d0d09cb05b7393cf9281509c7795)
- Call OPENSSL_init_crypto only for algorithm fetches on the default libctx, avoiding global default configuration initialization triggered by custom libctx fetches. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7a6a0baa591e3d04831ed0f468c72dc45feba452)
- Revert the change that re-marked EdDSA as a FIPS-approved algorithm, restoring its unapproved status. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/759ab5984eb981f2dd165979a7abb950ddad81ae)
- Remove duplicate forward declaration of SSL_CONNECTION, avoiding declaring the same type twice in internal headers. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d6cf4b59a0f3c32d61828ee82c193494e13ff969)
- QUIC no longer sends the cipher suite list repeatedly in the TLS 1.3 ClientHello, avoiding violations of QUIC TLS cipher suite handling requirements. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d518854cef2acc8bdc510746898f153ad628d4dc)
- SSL_add_dir_cert_subjects_to_stack skips subdirectories when traversing certificate directories, reading only certificate files within them. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1dc35d44f355a7371a1ff8a457586938cc7b168a)
- Explicitly specify .machine any in PPC64 P-521 assembly, allowing the assembler to parse source files with a portable instruction set. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a6d7093a1dc890493d3577c738e729c0265d7b40)
- Tidy up AArch64 CPU feature detection, simplifying hardware capability detection code and conditional branches. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/52a38144b019cfda6b0e5eaa0aca88ae11661a26)
- VMS platform also uses POSIX thread interfaces, enabling thread configuration instead of treating it as a non-threaded system. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ac21c1780a63a8d9a3a6217eb52fe0d188fa7655)
- Datagram BIO uses BIO_ADDR_sockaddr and corresponding length interfaces to construct socket addresses, avoiding inconsistent manual length conversions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a8681703d48d062c2fc1736179218063275f8e33)
- Add Keccak XOF acceleration for s390x, enabling SHAKE output to be generated by CPACF hardware instructions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/de13699370183ab565f548267afa57e25a921ca9)
- Unify handling of the pubin flag in dsa, ec, rsa, and pkey applications, ensuring consistent meaning of public key input options. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/091fef4936da93deee585dadd994144b330485d4)
- Change adrl to add in ARMv7 bit-sliced AES assembly, compatible with assemblers that do not support adrl. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/27093ba73372935fe4ef91d0a45ce6ea90a1ac8e)
- Adjust RSA keymgmt rsa_has checks to separately verify whether the requested public key, private key, and parameter components exist. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a3207163ef3d30658a41a9c9e3750ca4c5b16677)
- RISC-V GCM assembly now uses riscv.pm to generate registers and instructions, unifying assembly syntax conversion. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/75623ed8d01555e7c719f3b1c598e555c0878da0)
- Argon2 implementation uses e_os2.h for platform types instead of directly including stdint.h, improving cross-platform compatibility. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/46ce0854db51e373ab6ed4982431349107cd9b6d)
- openssl enc -help displays the default PBKDF2 iteration count, avoiding user misunderstanding of password derivation strength. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/dc43f080c5d60ef76df4087c1cf53a4bbaad93bd)
- QUIC forwards TLS-specific control commands to the internal SSL object for handling, supporting reuse of existing TLS ctrl options. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f8ffab0d52fc5e37d92c99e4463d76174e869930)
- Associate QUIC RX and TX objects with the libctx that created the connection, ensuring their algorithm and property queries use the caller's Provider environment. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c2212dc19eb280e22bda7d0538b23eef0be040e9)
- CMP certificate status handling releases certStatus before error jumps, fixing a memory leak. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c9c99018a887bfac1fe5a5ae6dcd8a5647494504)
- Fix Coverity-reported use-after-free, avoiding access to certificate status objects after release in CMP error paths. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7c45b7cbb04e297c3342fcc50bf7b0a9e36df1dd)
- Argon2 no longer relies on the UINT64_C macro that some compilers lack, using portable constant representations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4b738c1ac945a3f1a985df79ff8c37a23d2f2fe0)
- Initialize the datagram time field in QUIC demux, avoiding undefined time values in timeout and packet ordering logic. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/29fb7f087994b5cffe1613da25bb8c3231e59e15)
- Limit the maximum number of nodes in the X.509 policy tree, avoiding excessive memory and CPU consumption when validating malicious policy constraints. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3a81370f75b832102e9969533a25ca53fe0b254e)
- macOS builds no longer compile Power10-specific AES-GCM assembly, avoiding Apple assembler not recognizing POWER instruction syntax. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/175645a1a695017a312155a2c1d864ad8bff4eaa)
