- Add a manual lock implementation for 32-bit VC++ 2010 lacking the InterlockedOr64 intrinsic, compatible with older x86 Windows compilers. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8bdc3708964814ea0b7002df020fbd459e3a813f)
- Release existing extension buffers before setting TLS supported groups, fixing a memory leak when setting the group list repeatedly. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/62ea5ffa7c8882ba90b26ab1deb0d977dcb5165c)
- HTTP test server improves diagnostics for port occupation and other errors, reporting reasons for listen failures. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/46032426e42238ca8662b98752f9bc8d44512f29)
- Legacy EC key translation supports OSSL_PKEY_PARAM_EC_PUB_X/Y requests, correctly exporting public key affine coordinate parameters. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9adbce74933b87dd4fe776b70fef55f2f468f5f7)
- Perform policy checks even when the leaf certificate has EXFLAG_INVALID_POLICY set, avoiding invalid policy flags being ignored. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e4142ec43bcc08ffdb090580e24c24a7da302a32)
- Limit the digest algorithms usable by FIPS HMAC DRBG and Hash DRBG, rejecting weak digests that do not meet FIPS requirements. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f553c0f0dd24f037f31d971a99a1ffe7a11f64e6)
- Make fipsinstall recognize DRBG digest restriction options and write them into Provider configuration. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b345dbed28701f8aab06b0271603186127499928)
- Place FIPS Provider configuration function declarations in a dedicated header, isolating module-internal option interfaces. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/30ab774770a7e8547b0d6363b63a73cc80f33a7b)
- Ensure libcrypto static archives contain only one OPENSSL_armcap_P definition, avoiding duplicate symbols at link time. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7b508cd1e18f04d509af4df6c2ab4709c4389d19)
- Change ARM SM4 capability detection to check AArch64 architecture macros, avoiding incorrect use of the maximum ARM architecture version. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/09cb8718fd65dc7126247808cb96b05147bb923f)
- QUIC reactor allows releasing the reactor mutex while waiting on a condition variable, enabling other threads to submit events and wake it. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c019e1efe9b5dbb43c52f516e76b3f535158aaae)
- Mark QUIC functions that must hold a lock when called, clarifying the synchronization prerequisites for each interface. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d7b1faddab2817c6b73f18f84f8ad6cc9d2f563a)
- Add QUIC Thread Assist Core, enabling protocol background threads to advance handshake, timeout, and network events. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9f7acf071c363ed8cb5012e122e1e60447b45c78)
- Add QUIC Thread Assisted Mode frontend, allowing users to choose background threads to drive QUIC I/O. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f2f7c4f15ab1d8dc36b668877253c0e497da8ca6)
- Switch QUIC thread synchronization to OpenSSL mutex, replacing read-write locks for mutual exclusion operations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ffce2946c7f59ad14ffeeef16a82bf7f04e8cd9c)
- Allow overriding the QUIC_CHANNEL time source, facilitating deterministic control of PTO, idle timeout, and retransmission timing in tests. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b212d554e70930d8ebe425e535b0c3621b961541)
- Acquire the corresponding lock before datagram injection API operations on QUIC connections, avoiding concurrent modification of receive state with the background reactor. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5129e59494cb057bf8f744d7a5d390efa7914c67)
- Update build.info dependencies and source file declarations to ensure QUIC-related objects compile correctly per configuration. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/652fbb62a3de29f8c9103c66ac2bc27ac44b032a)
- Use the correct condition variable wait function, ensuring the mutex is properly released and reacquired during thread waits. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/712360631ff95b412883fbcd56dd44752d427565)
- Fix Coverity-reported null pointer dereference by checking object validity before waiting on threads or using callback contexts. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bbe9d2de6c643a2c6758fae4274c307943a59624)
- DTLS buffers application data records from the next epoch, delivering them after the key epoch switch. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5c476976ab8ef057ddbd8f110249d7c796a7f1b1)
- Suppress misleading warnings when /dev/crypto open fails with errno ENXIO, accommodating platforms where the device does not exist. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1636b355ade558d01f5a826494264ca31d6aeeb7)
- Do not call OPENSSL_init_crypto inside RUN_ONCE callbacks, avoiding initialization lock reentrancy deadlocks. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/540c2d175d3c7c28bb969a74f6fe0396f0addc1a)
- Clean up temporary buffers after EVP_PKEY_get_bn_param returns large numbers, avoiding residual copies of sensitive parameters. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/34e4a962bca998cc2d6eb4be721153fbde2f4c35)
- Ignore SIGPIPE when the client endpoint abruptly closes the connection, handling write errors instead of terminating the process by signal. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f309b3f6087db6c83126f8f227f1fc4984cf24b1)
- Allow the TLS record layer to partially release read records, retaining unconsumed portions for subsequent reads. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7a4e109ebe5af83bad6447889e43ac2612375070)
- Make `openssl dgst -list` also list digest algorithms that are currently not fetchable, helping users understand available algorithm names. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7eab7680ee61c64b2ae7acd9dd199ab6734f3d1f)
- Replace pointer bit operations in NIST large number code with conditional expressions, preserving original selection logic and eliminating non-portable pointer tricks. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/326af4ad171b849ba1e76fd425d8f337718c4108)
- Enable Control Flow Guard for Windows build options, so generated binaries use platform-provided control flow integrity protection. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1adc45b1ded7072d1566addeab227efa81b6c947)
- Revert the change enabling Control Flow Guard in Windows builds to restore buildability of affected targets under that configuration. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d748a1c6ac264ba74815e34c8d7a2ca478eb8b04)
- Fix an abnormal control flow path in s_client, ensuring connection processing failures do not fall into incorrect subsequent flows. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6799fc2409823939cde5b4a0da909e16ef78d3a8)
- Change s_client substring detection to find a single delimiter character, avoiding unnecessary string matching when only locating a character. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a80840c663e3409203b0235764e53d8624f74cb8)
- Make HKDF, KBKDF, and SSKDF share parameter handling functions, unifying parsing behavior for common parameters across these KDFs. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/345b42be90448523a335b9369452ea1159a1282a)
- Adjust OCSP command prompt text to clarify that the relevant parameter accepts a certificate list rather than a single certificate. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/87943b933e52ffe59c7ba929ccaaccbe49a7be9e)
- Fix `SSL_rstate_string*()` status mapping, restoring API return values to existing status semantics. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/73bac6e28014bfecc322c67aa8b09077e34da299)
- Adjust QUIC return status when using `SSL_rstate_string*()` to reflect the QUIC handshake phase instead of applying inapplicable TLS status. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/57582450318e955632d8fb09f42bd90f2ed5d3b4)
- Sort candidate stacks before X.509 lookup, ensuring the underlying stack lookup algorithm meets ordered data prerequisites. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3ef5b6009767aeff2fea47144af36cd13bd19d6d)
- Read HTTP proxy environment variables using `ossl_safe_getenv()`, following secure environment variable access policies to avoid trusting unsafe variables in restricted execution environments. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e7cbb09fdf8d835bd0d88b4b288edfd525be569c)
- Sort candidate stacks before ASN.1 identifier lookup, avoiding lookup failures due to unordered stacks. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0feb90ba6093a59dcea0279d699169f604600d49)
- Sort candidate stacks before PBE algorithm lookup, ensuring stack search meets ordered conditions and stably returns matches. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fb7a7f099ecbabbf65c42856ec9fb9d898b15907)
- Sort provider stacks before provider lookup, avoiding incorrect lookup results due to unsorted data. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/07f9c81d20f2c972dd454e2343634586d3aa88a1)
- Sort relevant stacks before X.509 policy tree and directory lookups, enabling binary-search-dependent lookup paths to work correctly. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/efe0222f5c9e07167aeac80d4d5e3d67aa8f1f36)
- Allow the TLS record layer to release zero-length handshake fragment records, avoiding empty fragments lingering and blocking subsequent handshake processing. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c20d923b46641030cb2946a1922ee344b9d27e43)
- Add NewReno QUIC congestion controller, implementing send rate control based on acknowledgments, loss, and congestion window adjustments. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f68fd1cebcc8dfbd57e3c1c7afd2bf1a5b26cbe9)
- Connect the QUIC channel to the NewReno congestion controller by default, making actual connections use the congestion window to limit sent data. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f6f45c55ea0f597387005c429beba80d76484389)
- Use safe multiplication in NewReno window calculations, preventing integer overflow when multiplying high-value parameters. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d235f657f89a6cdce93806bb4e965dfd865a7a13)
- QUIC handshake no longer sends empty renegotiation_info SCSV, avoiding carrying irrelevant extension markers in QUIC that does not use TLS renegotiation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1eef26bd8924058b9ba0e52786b6afab80db23a9)
- Copy minimum and maximum protocol versions only when SSL_CTX and SSL use the same method type, avoiding bringing incompatible method version limits into the connection. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4f373a9773efa63fdb73f3972f13ab78b9342b70)
- When the random number generator has no configured random source, allow using entropy explicitly provided by the caller, avoiding initialization failure due to ignoring that entropy. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/56547da9d3fa24f54b439497d322b12beb004c80)
- Suppress irrelevant warnings when loading CSR via `-vfyopt`, avoiding misreporting options applicable to certificate verification as CSR loading errors. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a75f707fcaaed5c9b26e0ddfc0e0529957a11a1d)
- Output `openssl req -verify` verification results to standard error stream, avoiding verification diagnostics mixing into request data in standard output. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c154f537c34c80e42915f32e97c6ba90d9fd4037)
- Tighten DTLS version identification rules, accepting only valid DTLS version encodings supported by the implementation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/861cd8964bfeb955408e93048d118e1826e12d0c)
- Revert the configuration change that switched Windows OneCore builds to `/APPCONTAINER`, restoring original UWP-compatible build settings. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/639e3970d0f1022c9cd15c4ff45845d7e9d65bbc)
- Refuse to initiate QUIC connections when ALPN is not provided, because QUIC TLS handshake must negotiate an application protocol. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d98f4212b778e3b6b7c15b9fce0a3531f59777a2)
- Fix CMP application TLS hostname selection logic, ensuring it uses the correct name whether the host address includes a port or not. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/30b9a6ec89d97152b5a564b3acf3a94ee57185a7)
- Restrict the RSA bit-length algorithm name check scope in the `speed` tool, avoiding incorrectly including non-RSA algorithm names in RSA benchmark parameter parsing. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/316d5a982b2534af2238af3560db8fa103a9169a)
- Enhance the SSL object unpacking interface in QUIC dispatch, enabling callers to safely obtain the underlying QUIC object and handle non-QUIC SSL objects. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e88cdb8eb7b719803aaaef853db16abf3a4e73d1)
- Determine QUIC client or server identity based on SSL method type, rather than relying on `SSL_set_connect_state()`/`SSL_set_accept_state()` status settings. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/dfb9ae14a44251553d4f4046ff1eb41608b3cca4)
- Create the QUIC channel immediately when creating the QUIC application layer object, ensuring connection state is available for subsequent API use after object initialization. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/23c047090cde899059fb7489e1a35124ca7b5e8a)
- Clarify the purpose of receiving transport parameters in the QUIC channel, preparing for implementation that saves sending-direction transport parameters. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/54562e899c5394505417adc1e0c6410f6a0677f3)
- Save sending-direction transport parameters required for initial flow control in the QUIC channel, enabling correct initialization of local sending quotas based on negotiated values. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0815b725a83da10f60c60d679a88b616da01cecf)
- Change the QUIC channel from handling a fixed number of streams to supporting any number of streams, avoiding connection stream count limitations due to internal single-stream assumptions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/26ad16ea84c58d91375491c0872e43dc27915b4a)
- Make the QUIC channel handle streams newly created by the peer, allowing remote-opened streams to enter the local receive flow. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f20fdd16d817a095f58f9c016044abef24e50e58)
- Delay creation of the default QUIC stream object until the application actually needs that stream, allocating resources then. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/21c80696e51c2b183dad3b19aeb50fe26920f0aa)
- When receiving a QUIC stream with a larger number, implicitly create lower-numbered stream states required by the protocol, avoiding missing entries in the subsequent stream acceptance queue. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/999e13f40eda5a2ca39d1efb407b96f81d2b9535)
- Fix QUIC handling of `STOP_SENDING` frames, making streams requested to stop sending end their sending direction per protocol. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b89c81e43b88c48d7cb5ce48665bab6c36ae02ac)
- Unify recording of application error codes corresponding to QUIC `STOP_SENDING` and `RESET_STREAM` events, ensuring upper layers query the error reason received by the protocol. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b6fc2294a1a5bd6053647afea02180147018112b)
- Reclaim ended QUIC stream states, avoiding the connection retaining stream objects with no activity. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0847e63ee5d58d824390aadcbcf10281c45900c4)
- Send appropriate `STOP_SENDING` or `RESET_STREAM` when releasing QUIC stream objects, notifying the peer to stop data transmission in the corresponding direction. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9aaafc26e0f301fe07d7141dc4a575ef9a4eb4d9)
- Fix QUIC test server logic for handling FIN when data has not yet been read, avoiding FIN being missed or the connection incorrectly remaining open. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/228940168529ba7c10b86934849b19818f79f74e)
- Enable the QUIC test server to query `STOP_SENDING` and `RESET_STREAM` states, facilitating test verification of stream close reasons. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f0e22d1be8a66106932f6f7c069087372ff33789)
- Fix reference counting statistics in multi-stream QUIC scenarios, ensuring references for the connection and multiple streams are not double-counted or omitted. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9cab4bd52396275338a027c02b3a52fbcede6aa5)
- Clarify switching rules between the default QUIC stream during connection creation and explicit stream operations, avoiding application misjudgment of default stream state. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3a61a96c1e43694199f5ec0887d0ad21a4650e3f)
- Remove unsupported floating-point parameter types in UEFI builds, avoiding parameter library dependence on platform-unavailable floating-point capabilities. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2c500578fc68871eca7fabc7ee36b4027891671b)
- Update FIPS module checksum to match changes in source file lists and module content. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ab17dd8fa3db3e1be82dabfc9fde5dc6181e3f49)
- Explicitly set default values when OAEP digest parameters are not yet initialized, avoiding RSA-OAEP decryption using uninitialized digest configuration. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c5aa719502f1ef456b27347e5f7b15c07817da4e)
- Remove the last dependency on `SHLIB_EXT` and clean up the no longer needed shared library extension configuration variable. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bc89c9f97735703f49f528d3c943de0b22ec33d7)
- Fix the logic in the QUIC stream manager for calculating the maximum number of streams, so that the correct limits are applied based on endpoint role and stream direction. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e8142d2ce8383329e6a71639d154191c1515ba55)
- Fix the stream count mode in QUIC flow control so that the receive quota and the number of allowed streams are correctly calculated according to the protocol direction. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3dde3435abab4524e62af31d87bd795543822e54)
- Ensure that all stream-related frames on the QUIC receive path create the corresponding stream state as needed, avoiding frames being dropped when they arrive before an explicit stream object. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/dea57ecf3d0729abb964bfc1ff687b2cbb9845de)
- Extend QUIC frame tracking to cover the sending direction, so that debug output also shows frames sent by the local endpoint. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/45454cccf8172b5a2d7c1342067a1d8dc8396fc9)
- Use the `%llu` format specifier to output `uint64_t` values, avoiding mismatches between integer formats and actual types across platforms. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7802170f7c1d2b89c2610b7affddb3d1b26fc87d)
- Add a new internal `ssl.h` header file to centrally declare types and interfaces shared within the SSL implementation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/674b61ebd982d6a6564ac1f90d8cde22371564bc)
- Write the `malloc` attribute macro in double-underscore form to avoid conflicts with identifiers in compiler or system headers. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0bf7e94c10f1b00510b8a36cdcbedc02a66468be)
- Allow QUIC connections to use the DTLSv1-compatible API, so that applications calling these legacy generic event interfaces can continue to drive QUIC. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2f90ea3daef94ab9806ae20eab1f37986d53eade)
- Use a read lock instead of a write lock when reading EX_CALLBACK data, allowing concurrent reads and reducing lock contention for callback lookups. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6d15357aeb893c6e8b4c7a8188c18f4db54c0612)
- Optimize lock operations when retrieving RSA blinding state, reducing write lock overhead on read-only paths. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f53479f98a2f2a6149192c5e3ef4ddf0926dceba)
- When the requested sleep time exceeds one second, directly call the system `sleep()` to avoid extra overhead from loop waiting. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2631a941469864a35258130082096876e7243225)
- Compute algorithm parameters for legacy RSA-PSS keys in libcrypto, so that legacy call paths also get correct PSS digest and salt length configurations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3410a72dce57651e08d5d2143409cde0205a8f3b)
- Fix protocol consistency handling for QUIC connection IDs, ensuring that connection ID lengths and frame fields comply with the QUIC specification. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/985429f4f4423de71cae270330586da990e6797f)
- Make `SMIME_crlf_copy()` check for null pointer arguments and return an error, avoiding crashes when null input or output pointers are passed. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/23450cfb9204615e97467e8be6a709141523a59e)
- Improve CMP command option warnings so that prompts for improper parameter usage more clearly indicate the problem. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6ed117b32c40992d3211b65cfe1b9aec23652a7d)
- Enhance lifetime management of CMP HTTP callback parameter pointers, avoiding access to freed contexts in asynchronous or subsequent callbacks. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/dea5e2632ca7f3ab48f947359501a7e3f28db178)
- Explain how the CMP mock server generates certificates to be issued based on request templates, making it easier for test code maintainers to understand the source of certificate fields. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5def4bbb4be5477146a0fbb4f14ee02df026419c)
- Update `.gitignore` to exclude generated files that should not be under version control. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/944ee2c30b1e87d4636aeef2ea9bf1adc647e0d9)
- No longer acquire a write lock when reading elements from the stack, reducing lock contention for read-only access. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/80935bf5ad309bf6c03591acf1d48fe1db57b78f)
- Limit the size of OBJECT IDENTIFIER that `OBJ_obj2txt()` can convert, avoiding abnormal memory or output processing caused by oversized identifiers. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d63b3e7959e79f98d60760a739f7876dc5adc838)
- Change the `struct_ref` reference count of ENGINE to atomic operations, avoiding data races in concurrent reference management. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e568d64f9fd3505454704f333bc1e58286f3419d)
- Make `ENGINE_pkey_asn1_find_str()` use a read lock for algorithm lookup, reducing write lock contention on the ENGINE registry for query-only scenarios. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b8fa5be5506e43b405c9a3ecc3d65c77044777be)
- Work around false positives from MemorySanitizer for the RDRAND engine, making test results for the random instruction path more reliable. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2c4124a3a1373036141ee8f07fdd5806cab12aeb)
- Define RC4 capability flags in libcrypto and legacy libraries, so that configurations and implementations relying on these flags can correctly identify RC4 support. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/58e8af4cecd23dbea2e6b061ab68190b38d64145)
- Check the return value of `CRYPTO_atomic_add()` to ensure that reference counting is not processed as successful when the atomic update fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7efc073dd7ddaed732c35e84efc865463db7ffbc)
- Add dual Apache and BSD-2-Clause license declarations to the RISC-V GCM assembly implementation, clarifying the licensing of the code. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/33523d6d669f192c2551c04f9df906d97abf6989)
- Add dual Apache and BSD-2-Clause license declarations to the RISC-V AES assembly implementation, clarifying the licensing of the code. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6181a333670199d20fb60028807edba44899e8d5)
- In the installation documentation, explain how to pin shared library dependencies during static builds, avoiding loading mismatched library versions at runtime. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ce451fb86141fedad607bd68840639b06616047e)
- In builds where `no-deprecated` is not enabled, make `RAND_poll()` trigger random number source polling and reseeding. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cc343d047c147e0a395fb101efbe9dedf458aa17)
- Use memory order operations instead of locks to manage FIPS state flags, reducing synchronization overhead on state read/write paths. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8e9ca334528e0a923c4deb0af250a60510974be0)
- Only record the ACK deadline when an ACK can actually be sent, avoiding QUIC incorrectly scheduling timers for acknowledgments that cannot be sent. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ca711651c19530b54f0dd6f7ff6b24b5c8d016a2)
- Add a QUIC utility function to determine whether a packet number is included in the acknowledgment range of an ACK frame. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/dfd8176f2556ca7ad0029012a048a3adc840bca5)
- Make the QUIC ACK manager provide a query for the largest acknowledged packet number, allowing sending and key update logic to determine acknowledgment progress. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/81b400cf900c530e170a1488222191c5568f6b2d)
- Make the QUIC sending path provide a query for the next packet number to be used, allowing upper layers to determine packet number boundaries when performing key updates. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/007f9e99ea92989d304584803e693fc90fb6af94)
- Add an ACK sending callback to the QUIC sending path, so that the channel layer can synchronously update related state when an ACK is sent. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8f9c9213a1ba034de3140a0d0c0c3b1e46afe457)
- Fix the way the QUIC sending path uses time callbacks, so that send scheduling and timeout calculations follow the application-provided clock. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b98c38d40a9d27a155d88208640430fffa47e28c)
- Fix key update handling in the QUIC channel, so that sending and receiving phases switch correctly according to the protocol. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8a65e7a529020b50716f08acc82816b95765914b)
- Strictly validate the relationship between packet numbers acknowledged in QUIC ACKs and the sending key phase, rejecting acknowledgments that cross disallowed phases. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c93f766860cd4e13aea7253c2d807f6048aa635e)
- Enforce receive packet forgery limits in the QUIC channel, stopping acceptance of suspicious packets when the protocol threshold is reached. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/48120ea5e3648a581ec8011594641178d85b17c4)
- Use ACK information to optimize QUIC key update timing, reducing unnecessary waits during rotation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/37ba2bc72281c196534e265c34be94beb760393e)
- Notify the QUIC ACK manager upon handshake confirmation, so that it starts scheduling acknowledgment frames according to post-handshake rules. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/29a541fe3643921462997856c46998f9b99f440f)
- Return immediately when the name mapping table is empty, avoiding dereferencing a null table and crashing. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d808fa015132d63f06f555649bc4b3f0e2e5124e)
- Update the README copyright year to 2023. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7197abddb891933f52ec84dafb41b685d4a1d122)
- In TLS 1.3 HelloRetryRequest, no longer request invalid key exchange groups, avoiding the server generating retry requests for groups that the client cannot negotiate. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7a949ae5f1799a6629cf6deb44ae0f38455a73dd)
- Unify the document author list format, placing "Authors" after "The OpenSSL Project". [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c5f55a4605a56655b2706c72388c1d59141fd243)
- In OSSL_STORE and PKCS#12 import processes, first check whether the file has a MAC to be verified, then decide whether to prompt for a password, avoiding unnecessary prompts for files that do not require MAC verification. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7a520619c997146639f42ce8595162ac34c2ad41)
- Fix the issue of truncated input during `pkeyutl` decryption, ensuring that the complete ciphertext is passed to the decryption algorithm. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/849450746f38a5658ef783abb0a8c79ae2861464)
- When the configuration file name is empty, `CONF_modules_load_file_ex()` no longer attempts to open an empty path, avoiding erroneous loading of the current directory or unrelated errors. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8b7d5ea7dd602eb7c2c4bc5ad45489dc5fc711f6)
- Ensure that `SSL_connect()` in blocking mode actually waits when the handshake is not yet complete, instead of returning early to the caller. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fb4a2bba7115d1d1d5ac0ab8829e9659199ef77d)
- Set the ping deadline to infinite before starting a QUIC connection, avoiding erroneous timeouts when there are no pending probes to send. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/27195689a8e123be356209d90000f49def13a0b9)
- Disable the stitched AES-GCM implementation on PPC32, avoiding the use of an inapplicable combined assembly path on that platform. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/44957a49329135163a4138b1877ccf7f899d19b9)
- Fix the return value handling of `ossl_config_int()` in UEFI environments, ensuring that configuration integer parsing status is correctly propagated. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/500e479db1beae5fa5691d40b866329d2fdc62e7)
- Replace scattered reference count values with structured reference count objects, unifying atomic increment/decrement and lifetime checking methods. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/99fd5b2b103f701151f4eb3fe0500ae0388e5136)
- Migrate ENGINE object reference counting to the structured atomic counting interface, unifying concurrent reference management. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e3620700a7d7dd772508768f51e892788e39a950)
- Migrate BIO object reference counting to the structured atomic counting interface, ensuring that BIO lifetime updates use consistent atomic operations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a22d1966bb230c335602ff79ca3356137d16b3e6)
- Migrate DH object reference counting to the structured atomic counting interface, unifying concurrent reference management. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9015cbb6eb7a50b04352d625e3907dfbb70684d0)
- Migrate DSA object reference counting to the structured atomic counting interface, unifying concurrent reference management. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/495e6d3b6266176b92ea20dbc6541ca724fa07ff)
- Migrate DSO object reference counting to the structured atomic counting interface, unifying concurrent reference management. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/aaab365c5afb950b9ffaa2916635a18e0d34fa98)
- Migrate ASN.1 object reference counting to the structured atomic counting interface, ensuring safe sharing and release of related objects. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/420ad86a0e35ddbd65dae7e9458e36223af5f140)
- Migrate EC object reference counting to the structured atomic counting interface, unifying lifecycle management of elliptic curve key objects. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1353736b3e6f33a9f6e47f837c5de05cc0dd3647)
- Migrate ECX object reference counting to the structured atomic counting interface, unifying reference management for objects like X25519/X448. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/99b7beafd212144fc3c77a6c09fc48f06f245c29)
- Migrate provider object reference counting to the structured atomic counting interface, unifying concurrent loading and unloading management of providers. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8752694bad830a91ba508451b220c23a99f182f1)
- Migrate RSA object reference counting to the structured atomic counting interface, ensuring atomicity of reference updates for shared key objects. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/97937cfcd8b5a011dd54e74eb2cc3cc26a533b10)
- Migrate OSSL_STORE object reference counting to the structured atomic counting interface, unifying lifecycle management of storage contexts. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2a1f467cb9e00d7b6c437443d6414370f3e6ff40)
- Migrate TLS object reference counting to the structured atomic counting interface, unifying concurrent reference management for SSL contexts and connections. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/43a07d6dd44cc9594a6cfecc464b69a7c4142d5f)
- Migrate QUIC object reference counting to the structured atomic counting interface, unifying concurrent lifecycle management for connection and stream objects. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4eecc6aa5dee91b7ac1b8a40ab07bc6bc5930a5d)
- Migrate encoder object reference counting to the structured atomic counting interface, unifying sharing and release behavior for encoder instances. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7d6ab1210603c23a4f2cbfb5c542726afe3b08cc)
- Migrate legacy provider object reference counting to the structured atomic counting interface, unifying lifecycle management for legacy algorithm providers. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7599d17d9385a7fd7489b81dfe560d319931f125)
- Migrate X.509 object reference counting to the structured atomic counting interface, unifying concurrent lifecycle management for certificates and verification objects. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a903a132a4256d34f20cb2f7636247b41fd85965)
- Migrate EVP object reference counting to the structured atomic counting interface, unifying reference management for keys, algorithm contexts, and other objects. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6be83ac172aac93b49ae0b847fd5ac9de6ab3ff5)
- Add index files generated by the language server to `.gitignore` to prevent editor artifacts from entering version control. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9e87e4e8ac2c5c75eae0ef1d4208e2aa12ff57dc)
- Make `SSL_OP_CLEANSE_PLAINTEXT` also clear QUIC sent stream data, preventing sent application plaintext from remaining in the buffer. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6ba2edb7143472e306cbb4cbee9bae3094bc01ef)
- Clear data in QUIC send/receive ring buffers when releasing them, reducing the time application plaintext remains in memory. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/292c9df2662b6bd54fea233964d908de5c63db7a)
- Add QUIC handshake connection identification capability, allowing applications to determine whether an SSL connection object is carrying a QUIC TLS handshake. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/43788fb3ac7221a699e56c38c1e9b8b4f8de4071)
- Update SSL option handling logic so QUIC connections only accept allowed options while maintaining TLS-compatible semantics. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/18ca1c8fc074e5b0fe52c91c52ade23e1f14cd0e)
- Clarify the dual-license declaration for RISC-V AES and GCM assembly code, specifying that both implementations are licensed under Apache and BSD-2-Clause. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3e76b388521ccafddd8ba19d9ab62c8d25e83e0c)
- Skip compiling `quic_thread_assist.c` when QUIC thread assistance is disabled, allowing the corresponding build option to correctly remove that implementation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1a2a0e1dc88bf905a7997e12de08a4b45f9db53c)
- Initialize the URL parser's port number output to 0, so URLs without an explicit port return a deterministic default field value. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ba189e0a4bdea86b6142da36adc7f054b6f08c6e)
- Fix AES-SIV mode ignoring empty associated data items, so zero-length AAD provided by the caller is also included in parameter sequence processing. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c426c281cfc23ab182f7d7d7a35229e7db1494d9)
- Optimize the PKEY decoder to reduce duplicate work in the decoding path and improve efficiency when loading keys. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/32d3c3abf3b74df1d9ebe562ba90f4dc3bdf2d4f)
- Validate the allowed stream states for STREAM frames per RFC 9000 Section 3.3, rejecting sending or receiving such frames in disallowed states. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/01715f2b41cac2e02663056d99901457db9b3eab)
- Model final data size for the QUIC stream manager and correctly handle `STOP_SENDING`, preserving the protocol-required final offset when reception terminates. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/418e122cd43c29c795de1e1af666d3ad5e4e99e9)
- Clarify that retransmitting QUIC stream frames does not retransmit original stream data, defining the retransmission semantics and data recovery boundaries of this implementation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c407d5e568ee0755d0c96c09a832af9760349c00)
- Validate the final size of QUIC `RESET_STREAM`, ensuring the received final offset is consistent with received data and flow control limits. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2cc0e2dddfe02c5fad74524bc975051021413ea5)
- Fix final size handling for QUIC `RESET_STREAM`, ensuring both sending and receiving sides record stream termination offsets per protocol. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7e3fa44f2445b5cbf6a6bf5ebf3cf96a40775951)
- Clarify the semantics of `is_inflight` in the QUIC ACK manager, specifying that this flag indicates whether a packet is still in flight and affects congestion state. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/85bbef270c1d15ec34e152c13f41ec0c298f5459)
- Suppress duplicate packet numbers per RFC 9000 Section 12.3, preventing the same QUIC packet from being counted twice in receive state. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/dfe5e7fa987c0e79c165a677d6572a04105528e3)
- Limit packet number ranges per RFC 9000 Section 12.3, rejecting QUIC packet numbers exceeding the protocol-allowed maximum. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5a1b1d2be3854581171addfe48bd6457a88c76b3)
- Limit the frame types that can be sent in different packet number spaces per RFC 9000 Section 12.5, avoiding enqueueing frames in disallowed spaces. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8d2e353df48c141305327c43226aeb0d9a7e5aa8)
- Mask application data when sending application-level `CONNECTION_CLOSE` frames per protocol, avoiding erroneous leakage or encoding of disallowed close information. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/96fa10f36fce9016491f587e4c3032ff90adcdf7)
- Generate `MAX_STREAM_DATA` per RFC 9000 Section 13.3, ensuring advertised stream receive credit reflects current flow control state. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/22f21fbdd6ed7032f28d4f22ef1abc98e1c5d325)
- Enforce checking of packet header reserved bits at the QUIC protocol layer, declaring packets invalid when non-zero reserved bits are received. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/08cb9a83277e5cd847742c048345fb6c9daf8170)
- Prohibit server Initial packets from carrying tokens per RFC 9000 Section 17.2.2, validating server initial packet format. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fd0d593220dd259ab9d327782eae28a07a537712)
- Implement QUIC long header packet processing constraints as specified in RFC 9000 Section 17.2.5.1. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/212616ed098bcf1190b6f687b234393b33168ba9)
- Enforce QUIC maximum stream data size limits per RFC 9000 Section 19.8, rejecting data exceeding negotiated limits. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/283938fca59a7930a28e748e8ab7c2d15281c681)
- Implement QUIC stream frame processing and validation rules as specified in RFC 9000 Section 19.7. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b5b40c4e183bf9a00ed086b72aa16369172a0054)
- Handle `STREAM_DATA_BLOCKED` frames per RFC 9000 Section 19.13, reporting states where the sender is blocked by flow control. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f084a8f7615c64cb55cb9de5669025eaa50eef6a)
- Handle `STREAMS_BLOCKED` frames per RFC 9000 Section 19.14, reporting that the peer cannot create more streams due to stream count limits. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f80e61b61f660e721d5cc7325a1a2bacbc7f34a6)
- Centralize and enhance QUIC ACK-eliciting determination logic, so whether a frame requires acknowledgment is judged by unified rules. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6cdb672d0f65aa82044cedb9a96d46fa7da865f7)
- Validate `NEW_CONNECTION_ID` frame fields and sequence numbers per RFC 9000 Section 19.15, rejecting connection ID updates that do not conform to the protocol. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5cc73695df371bac2769bebb0ef2cc70665c486e)
- Validate `RETIRE_CONNECTION_ID` frames per RFC 9000 Section 19.16, ensuring retired connection ID sequences are valid. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f37befa0480ec5d8362a5894e610a676987215b7)
- Validate the length and field combinations of QUIC `preferred_address` transport parameters, rejecting malformed preferred addresses. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/54bd1f24d48c668c125f59e4c63bf9af3fb2f954)
- Implement connection migration constraints from RFC 9000 Section 9.6, ensuring connection addresses and path states are handled per protocol during migration. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3ffb7d104f618262175283f26275b8be61e27467)
- Stop processing subsequent QUIC frames after connection termination, preventing closed connections from continuing to change protocol state. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8a6a00e3b8108c51e0facf30d2942176e72ad079)
- Validate the current state of QUIC send streams before sending data, rejecting writes to streams that are closed or not allowed to send. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/abfe3d5128b4c04f4bf288d03833481dbe52ee4c)
- Validate receive stream state before reading QUIC data, avoiding reads on terminated or unreadable streams. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e0bd282517f5853f2cfc73e90fc04be7a70dfd66)
- Integrate `DATA_SENT` state into the QUIC state machine, making state transitions after sending data consistent with the stream state model. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c068f4d1e91e04b9da5c430b1e18c190c2460aad)
- Switch QUIC send streams to `DATA_SENT` after actually sending stream data, correctly reflecting that they have started sending data. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7c88302b0120c22339a283947409b17bd349b075)
- Fix stream retirement timing for QUIC FIN and RESET, ensuring stream state is cleaned up per protocol after send end or reset. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/08d4b7eb7dac5d1c4d270f60d421a20e5df3c39a)
- Update QUIC stream manager API documentation, explaining state transitions, final size, and stream termination behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b3695154b5cfd8ac528b8c2794d2dc446899caae)
- Use receive stream state to indicate whether FIN has retired, removing the duplicate `recv_fin_retired` flag to avoid inconsistency between two states. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5ed3a435d5f84e296330595985c7adb2575ecba5)
- Update generated files to keep build output in sync with QUIC stream state changes. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bac3f4da5579887d6e203020e83298676cc982ba)
- Correctly initialize the maximum ACK delay value for QUIC channels, avoiding undefined timing states when parameters are not negotiated. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f13868def28ee532631a1dec0322a3ff51b3d7c8)
- Stop generating QUIC packets that fill the MTU during congestion control over-probing, avoiding probe data exceeding allowed send limits. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d56b564b25f9cafacdd57ac43b8b6618202047a5)
- Do not record QUIC packets that are not counted as in-flight data in the congestion controller, avoiding incorrectly increasing the byte count in the network. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/427a02ad0a71a50c9be125d860a84d4e07d09f1e)
- Correctly handle QUIC packets that do not qualify as in-flight, making their sending, acknowledgment, and congestion accounting consistent with packet type rules. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/178c104de68ebf981ae1813a3e0548bbb1051a75)
- Update ARM capability detection macros, enabling the implementation only when SHA-3 is supported and hardware acceleration actually provides performance benefits. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ba9472c1c121b13e48f7c198d3fe9871a86e664c)
- Fix QUIC forced PING generation logic, ensuring PING is sent when probing is needed and pending frames are not incorrectly lost. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1e2e683aa289849483ea9d48e9fcdda9559028ec)
- Remove unreachable code in `bio_print.c`, cleaning up output logic that never executes. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8ae4b236347d82226b6d86e02a9717e6a51d58a0)
- Check the result of `X509_STORE_lock()` to avoid accessing storage objects after lock failure in certificate lookup. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bc5d9cc8711e86d5c25b81c58dfae531536e61fc)
- Skip subsequent property checks when q in DH parameters is clearly invalid, avoiding unnecessary computation after certain failure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1c16253f3c3a8d1e25918c3f404aae6a5b0893de)
- Include error reporting declarations in the reference counting header file, ensuring error APIs are correctly accessible in reference counting failure paths. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bdcaa47ddb86bfef8bbdebc02a7b72df49920b6d)
- Correct database-related spelling. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ccb2f3080d84a271f17458a60e0d7ccd77929e95)
- Detect LSX capability on LoongArch via `getauxval(AT_HWCAP)`, enabling vector AES implementation based on actual CPU features. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c612289b77c37f7295d5af0d0e6b6c04e6ba727c)
- Remove unused `sending` parameter in key derivation functions, simplifying the internal interface. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fbd23b929609c0b2fe22da97ac349fae5a385027)
- Add a mechanism for the QUIC stream manager to track streams that can be processed by shutdown flush, supporting sending remaining stream data during shutdown. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b864110a82096c6b824406a3f8686a5099ea17c4)
- Make the QUIC test server still read data already received in streams after connection termination, avoiding buffered data being discarded on close events. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5904a0a71f5730c2f1e3028e092ea986b603081d)
- Ensure QUIC clock advancement suppression is not used in blocking mode, avoiding blocking reads/writes waiting forever due to test-controlled clocks. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cae02d2b0a650f07d29c9072a159b70035ae6c07)
- Implement shutdown flush for the QUIC application layer, flushing stream data that can still be sent before connection close. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8a2e9abac8dbdab154461484a19261daf05926f7)
- Block disallowed QUIC API operations during shutdown flush to prevent applications from concurrently changing the state of streams that are being closed. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6d6b3a032dfac23207f475b5bfe692e290f83c85)
- Make `openssl speed` measure both RSA encryption/decryption and signing/verification, no longer reporting only signing operations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0195df8baa12ac2f1364f55db09ba7fabb67df93)
- Unify the output format of the speed tool for displaying operations per unit time, making benchmark results of different algorithms easier to compare. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/eb2ff04cc75934fed2d8b6f3085d262978ae6033)
- Make `PEM_read_bio_Parameters()` no longer ask for a password, because PEM parameter files do not contain private key content that requires decryption. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0d0791eedff7f0747503d816184810aa093f523e)
- Continue advancing the TLS handshake even on empty write operations, avoiding handshake state stagnation when callers write with zero length. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/33f6ad1724b2f32a370d01b61ef12120a75d8049)
- Ensure that calling `SSL_has_pending()` before connection establishment also returns a valid result, avoiding query errors due to uninitialized connection state. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c31f06120fa8411da3cd779dfe881325204745ac)
- Continue calling the QUIC TLS tick after the TLS handshake completes, ensuring that QUIC TLS events required after the handshake are also processed. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f85d343208bd944b88891feba200259b4cd0ba7c)
- Correctly mark the QUIC TLS SSL object as shutdown, so that its shutdown state can be queried through the generic SSL API. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f219abef51fd47fa5945d23bbdc379778e512dc5)
- Adjust code indentation to conform to project style. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bdb1f6b74486daa1971b928528109a4c67cf2eb9)
- Replace magic numbers with HelloRetryRequest enumeration constants, avoiding handshake state comparisons that rely on hard-to-understand numeric values. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5ac7ee4d5a38e4f163ed6a7c9c283d45038625a8)
- Add conformance notes for QUIC to RFC 9000 Section 19.19, documenting corresponding frame processing constraints. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/73ef6e6f0f14c2cf2231ee72f3d98757310f1e31)
- Document the MUST requirements of RFC 9000 Section 10.1, clarifying the retransmission behavior that QUIC loss detection implementations must follow. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b056e9fcf58502f6bff513768b38f82c42059e7c)
- Use safe fused multiply-divide functions to compute QUIC ratios, avoiding intermediate overflow caused by safe multiplication followed by ordinary division. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/24ae2d79d57dd60f2617502d751d0ef78c571307)
- Complete QUIC conformance requirements per RFC 9000 Section 10.2.1, restricting data sending behavior under congestion. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6b3b5f9d28d267f7c63be859d8617bf1205e4d68)
- Complete QUIC conformance handling per RFC 9000 Section 10.2.2, ensuring appropriate actions are taken per protocol after persistent congestion. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/afe4a7978d2cff7852b46e8f23218ec6c41b8bf0)
- Implement the rate limiting requirements of RFC 9000 Section 10.2.1, limiting QUIC probing or sending rates to comply with congestion control rules. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/50e76846bf2d431d431b0b026f63d0b708d6e960)
- Fix a code style detail to avoid unnecessary formatting differences. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d11b901b0b19d2044d15cf4e5a7a41e7e2d9acfa)
- In persistent congestion scenarios, discard packets instead of closing the connection per RFC 9000 Section 10.2.3, making recovery behavior follow specification requirements. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bed20874870da6cf5fa60734964072504563fdac)
- Use defined constants instead of magic numbers in the QUIC implementation, ensuring protocol thresholds are centrally defined and clearly understood. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/029ddd1eadd3218017de5e6256363d09b7e015f8)
- Fix the `no-autoload-config` option so that this configuration again prevents OpenSSL from automatically loading configuration files. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cb8e64131e7ce230a9268bdd7cc4664868ff0dc9)
- When importing and exporting decoded keys, no longer use zero values to represent selection sets, avoiding confusion between zero and "unspecified selection". [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2acb0d363c0032b5b97c4f6596609f40bd7d842f)
- Make the NIST P-521 field square and multiplication wrapper functions static, avoiding link conflicts caused by exporting internal symbols. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3e47a286dc3274bda72a196c3a4030a1fc8302f1)
- Implement a Solinas fast reduction strategy based on 56-bit limbs for P-384, improving the efficiency of finite field operations for this curve. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/01d901e470d9e035a3bd78e77b9438a4cc0da785)
- Pass the retry flag in BIO flush control operations, so that flush can correctly return retry status to the caller when a retryable condition is encountered. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bcbc7d60679b79fa4347e33c865306dce41ed985)
- Allow the QUIC sending path to force padding for `PATH_RESPONSE`, so that path validation responses meet specific packet length requirements. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c5cb85b6651256fcdd0cf15c14f4d082f73c1abb)
- Send the correct TLS alert when QUIC transport parameters are not received, avoiding reporting missing parameters as an error category. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3ad5711e484736c7383b43d03f83e5700e589dfa)
- Correctly discard handshake data in the QUIC Initial packet space, avoiding leakage of Initial encryption level data into subsequent processing. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3eb0f9a7027c635b7c162f936ecb76d95146c62e)
- Enforce monotonically increasing packet numbers during QUIC key updates, rejecting duplicate or regressing packet numbers due to key phase switches. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0c1cc36bbb3b29a43cf08572b1176e5ee8e37ce2)
- Apply flow control limits to QUIC CRYPTO streams, ensuring handshake data is also subject to receive credit constraints. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/098914d0b768c090d443a46b66e4875969bee1e6)
- Implement the QUIC CRYPTO frame encoding/decoding and processing requirements specified in RFC 9000 Section 19.6. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/67e72ed575f6be1a29a8be8f785b1a1150588b44)
- Avoid using the C++ reserved word `template` as a function parameter name, so that header files and implementations can be included by C++ compilers. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/420a0874db8a6b0070d4820e81e82bf48412d7da)
- Expose the QUIC connection close reason from the channel layer to the application layer API, allowing applications to read the peer's close reason. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/40c8c756c86fc17751b989426aa66fb33319c4ca)
- Fix static analysis issues in QUIC frame processing tests, avoiding invalid accesses when constructing or freeing frames in tests. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/565d2987cd99a32249050b666d052d963b248d75)
- Correctly handle negative IV lengths in the QUIC receive path, avoiding reading buffers after converting negative values to oversized lengths. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b538ae4fbf1d9c800d2ed1cc6c317b36572ec7bb)
- Correctly reject negative IV lengths in the QUIC send path, avoiding illegal lengths participating in encrypted packet construction. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a2d4915ab2730797f97c90a127084a668733c96c)
- Check the return value of `block_until_pred()` in QUIC shutdown, avoiding treating a failed wait as a successful shutdown. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/23406e304f4d2406e6aa51c44b8f8dc2612e63fc)
- Correctly handle return values from QUIC test server calls, ensuring invalid results are not used after request failures. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f540b6b4f6608fa5edfa2ec77fce6d3c92bb9a1f)
- Adjust default receive flow control parameters for QUIC channels, so that default buffering and receive credits match expected data traffic. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/89b0948e534c1522e7d00b60cf55f5ada0bb39da)
- No longer call assertion macros on the return pointer of `bn_wexpand()`, handling it according to its real failure semantics to avoid invalid pointer checks. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/69b9a992961c27ac6d0f0bec259806ac953a81d4)
- Provide a compatible definition of `SSL_OP_CISCO_ANYCONNECT` in internal headers when the public header does not define it. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f7b2942c041ee803557a009a4554760c56484c9d)
- Reject unexpected CertificateRequest received after QUIC handshake as `PROTOCOL_VIOLATION`, conforming to QUIC rules prohibiting this message. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b644a9323f0060e27b3e45101856dc9e3bec0ac4)
- Preserve the flag that the object is performing QUIC when clearing QUIC TLS state, avoiding subsequent SSL operations being incorrectly treated as ordinary TLS. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0f2add9e8d4c1dc09848ea12aaad2eb4c5358bf2)
- Require NewSessionTicket with the early_data extension to provide a valid maximum early data value, rejecting tickets with missing or invalid credits. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/04c7fb53e0437f83e2476e5d55a1af61959fadf5)
- Prohibit sending TLS KeyUpdate messages in QUIC, because QUIC uses its own key update mechanism rather than TLS post-handshake messages. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/50a0af2e41ea61a79c19c17f9e87541e283ba8bf)
- Check the return value of `i2d_X509_NAME()`, ensuring that hash computation does not continue when X.509 name encoding fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/945fde53a3db5011940a059fd1407b81197c9e14)
- Delay releasing the previous BIO, avoiding premature destruction of the BIO when subsequent reads still need to use it. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/92e3f43aec553145b4d4cbb4dbd3df9658a05bb4)
- Check the return value of `ASN1_STRING_set()`, avoiding the SPKAC command continuing to output invalid objects after setting strings fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/20baa24f9f3997a96db7cd176a6ef609afe80cea)
- Check the result of acquiring the store lock when looking up X.509 certificates by subject, avoiding continued access to the certificate store after lock failure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6404d064b8012a2c353603a3b3effa6289313d61)
- Fix handling when the QUIC receive buffer is empty but FIN has been received, so that the read side can still observe stream end. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/72622c0b9637667cfef3692e5a63b90d637f0c72)
- Delay discarding QUIC Initial encryption level state, ensuring it remains available when still needed during connection establishment. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b6125b54ededb83ca930174718157d73561523ad)
- Update the ping deadline when receiving QUIC packets, avoiding unnecessary PINGs sent due to old probe timers when there is active traffic on the connection. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8fd32a0eda994527668a1e19a29ca9c85b4a35d8)
- Make RSA-PSS keys also support query conversion of payload parameters, ensuring the provider parameter reading interface is compatible with the RSA control interface. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cf712830b7b5a20a768a1fc5f78dc48841b7617f)
- Allow setting RSA-PSS keys via `EVP_PKEY_assign()`, and correctly recognize their signing capabilities in `EVP_PKEY_can_sign()`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e2972982c64f3f1ac10b3ebe1086d99ec67631bd)
- Avoid generating duplicate QUIC `CONNECTION_CLOSE` frames, preventing close frames from being re-queued on every scheduling round during connection close. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0b31072e086dc9a53f62cec60ea8d565320e640a)
- Fix diagnostics produced by the assembler when including LoongArch architecture headers, so that related assembly targets can build normally. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/84a0b1b169197e3afdadcdafc9fea65361ff672f)
- Add basic handling of version negotiation packets for QUIC, enabling endpoints to respond to or recognize the version negotiation process. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/777a8a7f5d5b80919da906cdaf8825f502bcad4e)
- Fix QUIC application layer handling of different connection modes, ensuring blocking and non-blocking APIs operate as configured. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c5b882a80b9f5811e45e29f4492bf335e870eb35)
- Properly initialize receive event fields for unencrypted QUIC packets, avoiding version negotiation and other packets using uninitialized state. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/413a427c2a7743474f57d5799f42de5357ceace2)
- Fix handling of PING frames in the QUIC receive path, ensuring probe frames trigger corresponding acknowledgment behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/de85ec03f5c6044fae8f2d1812d59aab0687b12a)
- Remove the unused `read_iv` member from the record layer structure, cleaning up invalid state fields. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/20a54aa21fc34ded577daf0bc91808b68a3b3c95)
- Specify the VC perlasm scheme for Win64 assembly configuration in Configure, so that Windows assembly code is generated with the correct toolchain. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a2608e4bc430d6216bbf36f50a29278e8759103a)
- Update generated files to incorporate multi-stream wait interface and test changes. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ade3baa6629b152185383605fb14d7b09483b409)
- Make the SM2 ARMv8 assembly script adopt the argument parsing logic of the NIST P-256 assembly script, ensuring consistent command-line generation parameter handling. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8a7f30ef67d59f808610275e8bc1723510cf062b)
- Make `OPENSSL_init_crypto()` load configuration into the initial global default library context, so that provider and algorithm configurations in the default context take effect at initialization. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ecb6cdf02a302af18fe4bc20097a9ea3177f897c)
- Enhance internal boundary checks in the QUIC ring buffer, rejecting erroneous read/write sequences to prevent buffer state corruption. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/60421893a286bb9eb7fb7c2454b84af9778ffca4)
- Remove the redundant parentheses in `ktls_configure_crypto()` and organize the conditional code structure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/da57c0eaf22c390f9b38c42ca1bd7daca4effd2f)
- Change the internal helper function in BIO connect to static to avoid exporting unnecessary global symbols. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ed28cb8140f1de81eca0f90b169af2b783abfe16)
- Adjust the SSL BIO helper functions to correctly configure QUIC BIO, avoiding the behavior of chaining ordinary TLS BIOs for QUIC. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/573f16c99719c9439a66a82fa256662d7cd32d47)
- Automatically configure the underlying QUIC BIO as non-blocking mode, meeting the operational requirements of QUIC event-driven I/O. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0818c17007bbda000e9c6329a1104d09cc614517)
- Add capability negotiation for datagram BIO so that users can query operations such as non-blocking and peer address supported by the BIO. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1bd35edc6603523953db24321df10d41c7a37923)
- Fix the `BIO_CTRL_DGRAM_GET_LOCAL_ADDR_ENABLE` behavior of the datagram pair BIO so that the local address query switch takes effect correctly according to the control command. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c20b78d59960c523c4de02e7bd62fcd4c0a5a4f7)
- Change `TLS1_FLAGS_QUIC` to an internal private definition to avoid applications depending on implementation detail flags. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d492e34351ae49e899a7c66f1882703a4fedced2)
- Add comments to internal flags to explain their purpose and setting timing. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d5c3f4b2dba0202c589d1d733e88e392794dce41)
- Before checking the number of QUIC ACK ranges, first confirm that the input data is sufficient to avoid out-of-bounds reads when parsing truncated frames. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a31601cc3ffca7de688aabcd34d83ff2c4496e17)
- Reuse allocated memory when processing ACK frames to reduce repeated allocation overhead when continuously parsing acknowledgment ranges. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8c792b0ccd41657d9972efbcc997a0c39d49121f)
- Adjust the interface judgment in the QUIC application layer that only allows connection objects to be called, ensuring that connection-specific APIs are not misused by stream objects. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/56df4cf24fad554e173d950a79a516e730096055)
- Replace `ssl3_get_message()` with generic TLS message header and message body reading functions to unify the handshake message parsing path across TLS versions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5318c012885a5382eadbf95aa9c1d35664bca819)
- Add more header file compatibility handling for the VMS C compiler to avoid build failures caused by differences in platform header declarations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d94bee8e9be2eb9e7e659a29c42586fe2f90f5a6)
- Include the internal integer constant header file in the QUIC congestion control file to ensure that the fixed-width constants used have correct declarations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cfbdc5dd14bf9fc969c9eb76216ab59a4ae64ba4)
- Use `.quad` instead of `.dword` in ARMv8 assembly to ensure that the 64 bit constant is generated according to the instruction format supported by the target assembler. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/852438adc0c9cb29b9cbdb5adab727230f40effb)
- Continue to supplement header file compatibility declarations required by the VMS C compiler to fix missing definitions in platform builds. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e0f4976ac634e8c74231e45f865457edab834324)
- Improve the warning content when `parse_name()` skips invalid RDN input, clearly indicating the ignored name field. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/49e097344ba51a8b25016794d482813b9c1e137f)
- Allow `OSSL_PARAM_BLD_push_BN_pad()` to accept an empty BIGNUM, supporting the expression of unset large number parameters with empty values. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2ce79d97e338c8eaacf67ce2e1a1b0fb1c639f11)
- Update generated files so that the above QUIC test and interface changes are reflected in the build artifacts. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e97c6c3cdf8b4f69e58e8cd610236ae474b9a97f)
- Explain that `BIO_set_accept_name()` should pass `*` when accepting any network interface, clarifying the configuration method for wildcard listening addresses. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b0da24bd2dc64e3a01df24e01aba37fe4c269230)
- Exclude `poll.h` in NonStop builds because this platform does not provide this header file. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/aff99225f946d8f538b5e0cb95fc65d5cd36b99b)
- Remove the regex `/r` modifier that requires Perl 5.14, so that the build script continues to be compatible with the Perl 5.11 supported by the project. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d30695ba4de494a8554c05afbfc0f984315a6306)
- Fix the function definition format error so that declarations and implementations conform to C syntax. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e22ebb893e2f44bd08f69f9ce4ccfc5e4d2990e2)
- Make the legacy BLAKE2 EVP structure reuse the base BLAKE2 implementation, unifying algorithm computation logic and reducing duplicate code. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/df9ecd2ef3907ec0a7bf9c54d9273d5342329bf9)
- Add a deletion API for OSSL_STORE, allowing applications to delete specified objects or resources from the storage backend. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0a8807b4a838ec6e6a84b2a28781e821ede90480)
- Make `OPENSSL_buf2hexstr()` use the default separator constant to unify the hexadecimal string output format. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/861027ffd06019baf82148837e30a992ca9b055e)
- Improve the security and readability of `SSL_get_shared_ciphers()`, ensuring more robust output buffer handling and cipher suite list copying. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2743594d73e65c38375c619e89ec62579e2c24a9)
- Allow providing a passphrase callback when opening OSSL_STORE, so that encrypted storage objects can use caller-defined password input logic. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/96e678087de25c4bb19ef01492bd04002c3fe315)
- Make `openssl enc` output "bad decrypt" only when decryption fails, avoiding the use of incorrect decryption error messages in the encryption path. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0e138b7b591f160a50aff22f662254d1b39c9cac)
- Add `SSL_net_read_desired()` and `SSL_net_write_desired()` for QUIC/TLS/DTLS, allowing applications to query whether the network layer currently needs to read or write. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3432157ba1e0e29bab8bdd31d7ae728930e57c42)
- Add `SSL_get_rpoll_descriptor()` and `SSL_get_wpoll_descriptor()` to the QUIC application layer, providing read/write polling descriptors usable in event loops. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f26244336f2a1b0d3040fe1db5d1024ec40e0b8b)
- Add poll descriptor query functions to socket BIO, allowing callers to directly obtain the underlying socket polling object from the BIO. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b79e73cfba2f17cc810bde3c50a2a1d4f03b8cae)
- Fix the null pointer dereference reported by Coverity, ensuring safe return on null object paths. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/be01f609f98a8930f2c91b813715e515a88f4d54)
- No longer link the sparse array object file into libssl, avoiding the SSL library containing an unused internal sparse array implementation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/34d36cdcbdde2e402446994e8384404ca98bfb3f)
- Update generated files so that source changes enter the corresponding build targets. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/746b95cc38d7c85a406ee34b609069c3da616e08)
- Fix the issue of dereferencing a pointer before null check, checking whether the object is null before reading members. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/15410839c668f97b5c03ee1a1bc1a2bf4315715f)
- Fix risky function calls flagged by static analysis, avoiding unvalidated input entering high-risk processing paths. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/eaf08794398ac3caaadffcfd670854bf51f610fa)
- Fix use-after-free where the object is accessed after being freed, ensuring that object contents are not read after the error branch terminates. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6bd07949e54f9958eb8a0f9a597ceb3910753ab0)
- Ensure that the Unix Makefile template always defines and runs `md-nits`, so that documentation checks are not skipped due to missing build configuration items. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0c74339c730dc74f9f7aeac866f02c780f39b3a9)
- Update the object cross-reference generation artifacts to include the ECDSA/SHA-3 mapping. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/de4aa81faaaddcacf0608166406d07bcd641e92b)
- Fix the macro definition comment, clarifying that the deprecated item belongs to 3.1 rather than 3.2. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6f66602eaa0a034847e9b1347c108b82c60b41d7)
- Store the decoded DTLS record version in `rec_version`, allowing subsequent record layer logic to use the actual version field. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4b5b2239d8752f8dd413872057c57ef9e1a1a591)
- Make DTLS records undergo the post-process phase after record layer processing, updating the same record state as TLS. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1d3f266446eb5b12a4162804536b7356024977e3)
- Improve the detection logic for traditional private keys provided by ENGINE, so that old key methods can be correctly recognized by EVP. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2b74e75331a27fc89cad9c8ea6a26c70019300b5)
- Restore the existing output semantics of `EVP_PKEY_print_private()` for private key data, avoiding incompatible changes in output content. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1296c2ec7866a4f2f4d210432c771142e8de33a0)
- Avoid overwriting existing FFC parameters during DH/DSA initialization, preserving the finite field parameters passed by the caller. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/706512ecbc31585d447b53c3aa89acdf6951f996)
- Pass the DTLS record version to the record layer message callback, allowing the callback to interpret tracked records based on the version. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b31597d989f422a0d341be4946d4d64a9251047f)
- Display the record version in the `DTLSv1_listen()` diagnostic output to help troubleshoot DTLS version negotiation and client connection issues. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5f79670f7b9b0354a5f6ccac9474f7a12f86407e)
- Embed the base datagram BIO state into the SCTP datagram BIO structure, reducing duplicate maintenance of common datagram state fields. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4bad474746472f08b0247b5afa81ddc71df98d5f)
- Use the real clock for the timeout wait function of condition variables, ensuring correct calculation of operating system timeout periods. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2b8d81534479b161dda063477272363fb2caef08)
- Ensure that `struct timeval` is defined when `ssl.h` is included, avoiding incomplete type errors under different header file orders. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6cf23ce54d4b9e3b11c8b4807c62ecba27e3aa16)
- Use standard C `srand()` to initialize the random seed in Windows builds, avoiding dependence on the unavailable POSIX `srandom()`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3b107b86ca7d1c6309bc7071ead59acb8c098f3b)
- Fix the handling of paths containing spaces in the Unix Makefile template, so that commands are correctly generated when source and build paths contain spaces. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/499184145d22df59a9c53e530a5ccfdd07753614)
- Explain in the PKCS7 signer structure documentation the confusing aspects of the digest algorithm field name. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e15891b477fe9c3d3dc6f331812c9e8afc48dc05)
- Ensure that the object identifier lock is allocated before using it, avoiding access to a null lock during concurrent initialization. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cd920f8fa1bb603a620bea697027f5573fadc12e)
- Skip empty values when constructing multi-key BIGNUM parameters, avoiding the generation of invalid parameter items. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/15a39e7025e0ed4e31664c499894006e41582068)
- No longer require the RSA parameter setting interface to provide CRT values, being compatible with old keys that do not contain CRT parameters. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2647726bd3ca63dc5f07ae3f10e16dff35d95626)
- Output a clear error message when the application cannot open the store, indicating that the open operation failed. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/edc2b6e3b1950ab0fb71e2d7dca0836b43a9ec3b)
- Remove the ASN.1 header file dependency from the common internal header file, reducing unrelated coupling. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/80f32964a5388f5a313ced88f17a41f17794e369)
- Explicitly include the required ASN.1 header file in the CMS source file, ensuring that structure definitions are visible at the point of use. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fa9e6ad46860ea92aa2e1ba997b20c6dff76b42c)
- Ignore QUIC Retry packets that arrive too late, avoiding old Retry restarting an already advanced handshake. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/56e303259ed48884c914fe24b354e9cc7b7532c3)
- Do not include the ping deadline in the QUIC tick deadline when there is no sending capability, avoiding invalid wakeups. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/098f27f9ef8be2a418f76896ee3c824e8709fcf7)
- Defer two QUIC pending tasks to avoid describing unimplemented capabilities as current behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8e520d2714abf4c6254ceec24b57f238433541ee)
- Round up when converting nanoseconds to `timeval`, avoiding truncation causing timer deadlines to be too early. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c239bc9e960b866093dbc666c78c78935233750c)
- Ensure `ERR_STATE` is consistent after saving or restoring error state, without leaving partially updated data. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/94300d8de224e2135e75439e6b9c63eb7ad61fdf)
- Apply key or IV length parameters to the EVP context early when present, avoiding algorithm initialization missing caller configuration. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f3a7e6c057b5054aa05710f3d528b92e3e885268)
- Record the amount of data sent during send retry, avoiding duplicate or missing stream data after recovery. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b9b9f4886f87abd39535721243d4297fd45e558a)
- Create hierarchical demo Makefile, supporting individual build of example subdirectories and unified top-level management. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/66f4782f1452d6fbfab78822b340a99aaeacc2f0)
- Fix `speed` handling of asynchronous job counts for KEM and signature algorithms. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/df5f419b14de9ff47082c42f2a2db6557ceca84f)
- Do not wait for `select()` when there is data to write, avoiding send stalling due to waiting for readable events. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/95420a2500fe0d96fb44cf7d826a156433c50589)
- Adjust QUIC handshake encryption buffer size to avoid insufficient capacity or over-allocation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/29f633840df49f29e71a57cc9682d9f3703bfe3b)
- Improve QUIC ping deadline management to avoid scheduling invalid probes when there is no send requirement. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c7ed5e4697a71012e0a2d9dd5eaf997754ae5156)
- Link libatomic for riscv32 to satisfy runtime library dependencies for atomic operations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/492bccfc0aca055638407bba44671141ffe7111a)
- Fix the constant used when comparing name flags in `x509_print_ex()` to ensure correct selection of name printing format. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d7f20c79920ba0e74a775bb145bfa9d4a3606492)
- Refresh README project overview and documentation entry points. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4da702b61e7a2180eda4de5a922c68a7c0b48f51)
- Update example list and run instructions in demos README. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7380e6b54b141eb05893c9a107ad7c5b6196c97a)
- Do not immediately declare packet loss after loss detection timer triggers, avoiding premature retransmission. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9d67bd5ffd9514b791917804f7b839a5b3fb6da2)
- Unify QUIC ping and idle deadline calculation rules to avoid boundary inconsistencies. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e401723baf89b1f201a59fdd679f7ae6b5c123a2)
- Check pointer before freeing QUIC_TLS to avoid crash when cleaning up null object. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e99c771985cbaf6ae55912b581d115f5097fe2fd)
- Let QUIC application layer automatically adjust write buffer to adapt to connection send requirements. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/96040e539e5ecdf1002e737cb29042f9a065460d)
- Generate QUIC ACK frame only when packet number space needs acknowledgment. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4febab7d808c2c746ded9424ceff4163bdae3278)
- Let TLS example accept target host and port from command line. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/151af35f560a696ddb2793e4b8a5e675dc5994ac)
- Document build and run instructions for TLS example in README. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/70de52650d9aeab475745edcfa268112ed2a3eff)
- Let QUIC example accept target host and port from command line. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3b60efa109a4637bf30d8d2b6067a5ea7151eff3)
- Update run instructions for QUIC example in README. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f7f40db44bf045389ee7d2f60e4416374dc3f2e8)
- Set clear error reason for missing QUIC transport parameter extension. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3c7c4866464cfb872b91ba204e3c64d4da9e2fdf)
- Reject integer QUIC transport parameters with trailing bytes. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cecc05c2935ef2c93753f126b71103bc6c0c2c7a)
- Preserve original data length when generating QUIC STREAM frames to ensure correct offset calculation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/61a468bfaff43893763f555511300f069ae78746)
- Enhance input protection for DH public key validation and key generation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d73028b75b416ccabdc267553dcce241e831eaf3)
- Improve implementation and documentation details of QUIC HTTP/3 demo. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0f96c6e39ad6f60c3166b8356fa46756b20226d0)
- Correctly calculate padding length for QUIC ACK-only packets. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/68ed191f6b6541631dd8e298017e83b9b16d5413)
- Check return value of provider insertion into `openssl list` list, report error on insertion failure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/26997d66059432e1fa5bf946249a0bf6086dd716)
- Use `fcntl(F_GETFL)` instead of `BIO_sock_nbio()` to query socket status on NonStop. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0ef3e1305db3f77bb0b0702c9b45bb39d165f28a)
- CMS example outputs signer's signingTime attribute. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7dc833c2f659dfcd7ea4af951f045f93a0dbe30c)
- Calculate remaining space in QUIC packet based on authentication tag length to avoid overestimating payload capacity. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2aba9548c4a0a3f8359ab5476df3ad58f1cbcf06)
- Zero out handshake fragment buffer before allocation to avoid uninitialized data residue. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1ea038bfa2726ad1bfbc220c8955c0fead9393d5)
- Update generated files to synchronize build artifacts. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/18f594209a141532ae07e90c13f77aa73cebd8b3)
